`README.md`:

```md
# dsh-ios — DeepSeek 官方源码的 iOS 越狱本地部署 App

把 [DeepSeek 官方 agent harness](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness)（`@deepseek-ai/dsh`）
打成一个可在**越狱 iPhone / iPad** 上一键本地部署的 App，交付 **未签名 `.ipa`**。

```
┌──────────────────────────── Dsh.app ────────────────────────────┐
│  SwiftUI 壳：部署/链接/工作区/日志/自检/代理/后台保活            │
│  Runtime/node   iPhoneOS arm64 Node 26（官方源码交叉编译）       │
│  Runtime/dsh    DeepSeek 官方运行树（commit fcc0ad4，零修改）    │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
        │ posix_spawn                         │ NWListener 中转
        ▼                                     ▼
  node --jitless dsh web            0.0.0.0:3081 ──▶ 127.0.0.1:3080
  --host 127.0.0.1 --port 3080       （同 Wi-Fi 任意设备可访问）
```

## 交付物

| 产物 | 说明 | 下载 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `dsh-ios-unsigned.ipa` | 未签名，172 MB（TrollStore / Sideloadly 安装） | [build-21](https://github.com/mzydgnh2/dsh-ios/releases/download/build-21/dsh-ios-unsigned.ipa) |
| `dsh-ios-ldid.ipa` | ad-hoc ldid 签名 + 越狱 entitlements（可直放 `/Applications` 或 `/var/jb/Applications`） | [build-21](https://github.com/mzydgnh2/dsh-ios/releases/download/build-21/dsh-ios-ldid.ipa) |

全部 [Releases](https://github.com/mzydgnh2/dsh-ios/releases)。每次 CI 全绿自动发布。

## 功能

- **一键部署**：装好打开即自动准备目录 → 自检 Node → 启动官方 `dsh web` → 交付链接
- **双链接**：本机 `http://127.0.0.1:3080/?token=…` + 局域网 `http://<设备IP>:3081/?token=…`（含二维码）
- **工作区/容器**：新建 / 重命名 / 删除 / 查看体积；就是 `Documents/Workspaces` 下的独立目录，Web UI 里直接可选中
- **可调试**：实时日志（stdout+stderr，可过滤/导出）、11 项自检、端口/参数/代理/堆上限可调
- **稳**：崩溃自动拉起（限 3 次）、局域网 IP 变化自动重启、后台静音保活（可关）
- **官方到底**：DeepSeek 代码零修改，CI 校验 `git status --porcelain` 为空；App 只做容器

不支持（也做不到）的：**本地跑模型权重**（V3/R1 数百 GB，需官方 API）、非越狱设备（无法 spawn 子进程）、
pwsh 工具、插件市场在线安装。详见 [docs/ARCHITECTURE.md](docs/ARCHITECTURE.md#能力边界重要别踩坑)。

## 流水线

| # | 工作流 | 运行环境 | 产物 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | `node-ios-runtime.yml` | macOS | iPhoneOS arm64 Node 26（官方 nodejs.org 源码 + iOS 补丁集） |
| 2 | `deepseek-runtime.yml` | Linux | 官方源码构建的 `@deepseek-ai/dsh` 生产依赖树 |
| 3 | `build-ipa.yml` | macOS | `Payload/Dsh.app` → 两个 `.ipa` + Release |
| — | `swift-check.yml` | macOS | App 源码编译快速反馈 |

## 供应链（可追溯）

- DeepSeek：`deepseek-harness` @ `fcc0ad462172522aaf5c93a7b2e01f6b9f0b0159`（= `0.2.2-rc.1`），**零修改**
- Node.js：`nodejs.org/dist/v26.13.0` 官方源码包（sha256 固定），仅 iOS 交叉编译适配补丁
  （`runtime/node-ios/patches/`，来源与许可见 `runtime/node-ios/NOTICE.md`）
- iOS 壳：本仓库 `apple/`（MIT）

> 必须说明：Apple 不提供任何 iOS 版 Node，`nodejs/node` 的 `configure.py` 里 `ios` 只是从未被维护的占位。
> 因此 Node 运行时这一层不得不使用 iOS 交叉编译补丁集；DeepSeek 本身没有任何替代实现。

## 许可

本仓库自有代码 MIT。DeepSeek 部分遵循其 MIT 许可；Node.js 部分遵循 Node.js 许可。
```

`apple/Resources/dsh-shell.js`:

```js
#!/usr/bin/env node
/*
 * dsh-shell — a POSIX-ish shell implemented entirely inside one Node process.
 *
 * The app runs inside an iOS sandbox where fork(2) is denied, so mksh/dash can
 * only run their own builtins: every pipe, subshell, command substitution and
 * external program fails with "can't fork" / "Operation not permitted".
 *
 * Rather than shelling out, this shell implements the commands itself and runs
 * pipelines in-process: each stage's stdout is a string handed to the next
 * stage. Nothing forks, so it works inside the sandbox.
 *
 * Invocation:  bash -c "<command>"        (also -lc, -l, plain args)
 * Exit codes follow the last pipeline's status.
 */
'use strict';

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');
const os = require('os');

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ helpers */

const enc = s => Buffer.from(String(s), 'utf8');

function fail(message, code = 1) {
  return { out: '', err: message.endsWith('\n') ? message : message + '\n', code };
}

function ok(out = '') {
  return { out, err: '', code: 0 };
}

function errnoMessage(error, target) {
  const code = error && error.code;
  if (code === 'ENOENT') return `${target}: No such file or directory`;
  if (code === 'EACCES' || code === 'EPERM') return `${target}: Permission denied`;
  if (code === 'EISDIR') return `${target}: Is a directory`;
  if (code === 'ENOTDIR') return `${target}: Not a directory`;
  if (code === 'ENOTEMPTY') return `${target}: Directory not empty`;
  return `${target}: ${error && error.message ? error.message : String(error)}`;
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------ word expansion */

function globToRegExp(pattern) {
  let re = '^';
  for (const ch of pattern) {
    if (ch === '*') re += '[^/]*';
    else if (ch === '?') re += '[^/]';
    else re += ch.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&');
  }
  return new RegExp(re + '$');
}

function expandGlob(word) {
  if (!/[*?]/.test(word)) return [word];
  const dir = path.dirname(word);
  const base = path.basename(word);
  const absolute = word.startsWith('/');
  const root = dir === '.' ? (absolute ? '/' : process.cwd()) : dir;
  let entries;
  try {
    entries = fs.readdirSync(root);
  } catch {
    return [word];
  }
  const re = globToRegExp(base);
  const hits = entries
    .filter(name => re.test(name))
    .filter(name => {
      try {
        return fs.existsSync(path.join(root, name));
      } catch {
        return false;
      }
    })
    .sort()
    .map(name => {
      const full = path.join(root, name);
      if (dir === '.') return name;
      return dir.endsWith('/') ? dir + name : dir + '/' + name;
    });
  return hits.length > 0 ? hits : [word];
}

function expandTilde(word) {
  if (word === '~') return process.env.HOME || os.homedir();
  if (word.startsWith('~/')) return (process.env.HOME || os.homedir()) + word.slice(1);
  return word;
}

/* --------------------------------------------------------------- tokenizer */

const OPERATORS = ['&&', '||', ';;', '|', ';', '>', '>>', '<', '2>', '2>>', '&'];

function tokenize(input) {
  const tokens = [];
  let current = '';
  let quote = null;
  let i = 0;

  const pushWord = () => {
    if (current.length > 0) {
      tokens.push({ type: 'word', value: current });
      current = '';
    }
  };

  while (i < input.length) {
    const ch = input[i];

    if (quote === "'") {
      if (ch === "'") quote = null;
      else current += ch;
      i += 1;
      continue;
    }

    if (quote === '"') {
      if (ch === '"') {
        quote = null;
      } else if (ch === '\\' && i + 1 < input.length) {
        current += input[i + 1];
        i += 1;
      } else {
        current += ch;
      }
      i += 1;
      continue;
    }

    if (ch === '#' && current === '') break; // comment
    if (ch === "'" || ch === '"') {
      quote = ch;
      i += 1;
      continue;
    }
    if (ch === '\\' && i + 1 < input.length) {
      current += input[i + 1];
      i += 1;
      continue;
    }
    if (ch === ' ' || ch === '\t') {
      pushWord();
      i += 1;
      continue;
    }
    if (ch === '\n') {
      pushWord();
      tokens.push({ type: 'op', value: ';' });
      i += 1;
      continue;
    }

    const two = input.slice(i, i + 2);
    if (OPERATORS.includes(two)) {
      pushWord();
      tokens.push({ type: 'op', value: two });
      i += 2;
      continue;
    }
    if (OPERATORS.includes(ch) && ch !== '&') {
      pushWord();
      tokens.push({ type: 'op', value: ch });
      i += 1;
      continue;
    }
    current += ch;
    i += 1;
  }
  pushWord();
  return tokens;
}

/* -------------------------------------------------------------- expansion */

function substitute(ctx, text) {
  // $(cmd) and `cmd`
  let out = '';
  let i = 0;
  while (i < text.length) {
    if (text.startsWith('$(', i)) {
      let depth = 1;
      let j = i + 2;
      while (j < text.length && depth > 0) {
        if (text[j] === '(') depth += 1;
        else if (text[j] === ')') depth -= 1;
        j += 1;
      }
      const inner = text.slice(i + 2, j - 1);
      const result = ctx.run(inner);
      out += result.out.replace(/\n+$/, '');
      i = j;
      continue;
    }
    if (text[i] === '`') {
      const j = text.indexOf('`', i + 1);
      if (j === -1) {
        out += text[i];
        i += 1;
        continue;
      }
      out += ctx.run(text.slice(i + 1, j)).out.replace(/\n+$/, '');
      i = j + 1;
      continue;
    }
    if (text[i] === '$') {
      if (text[i + 1] === '{') {
        const j = text.indexOf('}', i + 2);
        if (j !== -1) {
          const expr = text.slice(i + 2, j);
          out += expandBraced(ctx, expr);
          i = j + 1;
          continue;
        }
      }
      const rest = text.slice(i + 1);
      const match = /^([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*|[0-9@#?$!*-])/.exec(rest);
      if (match) {
        out += lookupVar(ctx, match[1]);
        i += 1 + match[1].length;
        continue;
      }
    }
    out += text[i];
    i += 1;
  }
  return out;
}

function lookupVar(ctx, name) {
  if (name === '?') return String(ctx.lastStatus);
  if (name === '#') return String(ctx.positional.length);
  if (name === '$') return String(process.pid);
  if (name === '0') return 'bash';
  if (/^[0-9]+$/.test(name)) return ctx.positional[Number(name) - 1] || '';
  if (name === '*') return ctx.positional.join(' ');
  if (name === '@') return ctx.positional.join(' ');
  return ctx.env[name] !== undefined ? String(ctx.env[name]) : '';
}

function expandBraced(ctx, expr) {
  const fallback = /^([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*)(:?[-=])(.*)$/.exec(expr);
  if (fallback) {
    const [, name, op, rest] = fallback;
    const value = ctx.env[name];
    const empty = value === undefined || (op.startsWith(':') && value === '');
    if (empty) {
      ctx.env[name] = substitute(ctx, rest);
      return ctx.env[name];
    }
    return value;
  }
  const slice = /^([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*):?([#%])(.*)$/.exec(expr);
  if (slice) {
    const [, name, op, pattern] = slice;
    const value = ctx.env[name] || '';
    const isPrefix = op === '#';
    if (pattern.startsWith('*')) {
      const target = pattern.slice(1);
      const idx = value.indexOf(target);
      if (idx === -1) return value;
      return isPrefix ? value.slice(idx) : value.slice(0, idx + target.length);
    }
    const target = pattern.endsWith('*') ? pattern.slice(0, -1) : pattern;
    const idx = value.indexOf(target);
    if (idx === -1) return value;
    return isPrefix ? value.slice(idx) : value.slice(0, idx);
  }
  const length = /^#([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*)$/.exec(expr);
  if (length) return String((ctx.env[length[1]] || '').length);
  const plain = /^([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*)$/.exec(expr);
  if (plain) return lookupVar(ctx, plain[1]);
  return '';
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------- commands */

function needArgs(name, args, minimum) {
  if (args.length < minimum) return fail(`${name}: missing operand`);
  return null;
}

function resolveFile(ctx, target) {
  if (target.startsWith('/')) return target;
  return path.join(ctx.cwd, target);
}

function readInput(ctx, stdin) {
  return stdin !== undefined ? stdin : '';
}

function splitLines(text) {
  return text.length === 0 ? [] : text.replace(/\n$/, '').split('\n');
}

const commands = {
  echo(ctx, args) {
    let newline = true;
    let interpret = false;
    let index = 0;
    while (index < args.length && /^-[neE]+$/.test(args[index])) {
      if (args[index].includes('n')) newline = false;
      if (args[index].includes('e')) interpret = true;
      index += 1;
    }
    let text = args.slice(index).join(' ');
    if (interpret) {
      text = text.replace(/\\n/g, '\n').replace(/\\t/g, '\t').replace(/\\r/g, '\r').replace(/\\\\/g, '\\');
    }
    return ok(text + (newline ? '\n' : ''));
  },

  printf(ctx, args) {
    if (args.length === 0) return fail('printf: usage: printf format [arguments]');
    const format = args[0];
    const values = args.slice(1);
    let valueIndex = 0;
    let out = '';
    for (let i = 0; i < format.length; i += 1) {
      const ch = format[i];
      if (ch !== '%') {
        if (ch === '\\' && i + 1 < format.length) {
          const next = format[i + 1];
          const map = { n: '\n', t: '\t', r: '\r', '\\': '\\', '0': '\0' };
          if (map[next] !== undefined) {
            out += map[next];
            i += 1;
            continue;
          }
        }
        out += ch;
        continue;
      }
      const spec = format.slice(i + 1, i + 2);
      i += 1;
      if (spec === '%') {
        out += '%';
        continue;
      }
      const value = values[valueIndex++] ?? '';
      if (spec === 's') out += value;
      else if (spec === 'd' || spec === 'i') out += String(parseInt(value, 10) || 0);
      else if (spec === 'f') out += String(parseFloat(value) || 0);
      else if (spec === 'x') out += (parseInt(value, 10) || 0).toString(16);
      else out += spec;
    }
    return ok(out);
  },

  pwd(ctx) {
    return ok(ctx.cwd + '\n');
  },

  cd(ctx, args) {
    const target = args[0] ?? (ctx.env.HOME || os.homedir());
    const resolved = resolveFile(ctx, expandTilde(target));
    try {
      const stat = fs.statSync(resolved);
      if (!stat.isDirectory()) return fail(`cd: not a directory: ${target}`);
      ctx.cwd = fs.realpathSync(resolved);
      ctx.env.PWD = ctx.cwd;
      return ok();
    } catch (error) {
      return fail(`cd: ${errnoMessage(error, target)}`);
    }
  },

  true() {
    return ok();
  },

  false() {
    return { out: '', err: '', code: 1 };
  },

  exit(ctx, args) {
    const code = args.length > 0 ? parseInt(args[0], 10) : ctx.lastStatus;
    ctx.exitCode = Number.isFinite(code) ? code : 0;
    ctx.exitRequested = true;
    return { out: '', err: '', code: ctx.exitCode };
  },

  ls(ctx, args) {
    const flags = new Set();
    const targets = [];
    for (const arg of args) {
      if (arg.startsWith('-') && arg.length > 1) for (const ch of arg.slice(1)) flags.add(ch);
      else targets.push(arg);
    }
    const showAll = flags.has('a');
    const long = flags.has('l');
    const listOne = target => {
      const resolved = resolveFile(ctx, target);
      let stat;
      try {
        stat = fs.statSync(resolved);
      } catch (error) {
        return { out: '', err: errnoMessage(error, target) + '\n', code: 2 };
      }
      let out = '';
      let err = '';
      let code = 0;
      if (stat.isDirectory()) {
        let entries = [];
        try {
          entries = fs.readdirSync(resolved).filter(name => showAll || !name.startsWith('.')).sort();
        } catch (error) {
          return { out: '', err: errnoMessage(error, target) + '\n', code: 2 };
        }
        if (targets.length > 1) out += `${target}:\n`;
        for (const name of entries) {
          if (long) {
            const entryPath = path.join(resolved, name);
            let entryStat;
            try {
              entryStat = fs.lstatSync(entryPath);
            } catch {
              continue;
            }
            const kind = entryStat.isDirectory() ? 'd' : entryStat.isSymbolicLink() ? 'l' : '-';
            out += `${kind}${(entryStat.mode & 0o777).toString(8).padStart(3, '0')} ${String(entryStat.size).padStart(10)} ${name}\n`;
          } else {
            out += name + '\n';
          }
        }
      } else if (long) {
        out += `-${(stat.mode & 0o777).toString(8).padStart(3, '0')} ${String(stat.size).padStart(10)} ${target}\n`;
      } else {
        out += target + '\n';
      }
      return { out, err, code };
    };
    if (targets.length === 0) targets.push('.');
    const results = targets.map(listOne);
    return {
      out: results.map(r => r.out).join(''),
      err: results.map(r => r.err).join(''),
      code: results.some(r => r.code !== 0) ? results.find(r => r.code !== 0).code : 0
    };
  },

  cat(ctx, args, stdin) {
    if (args.length === 0) return ok(readInput(ctx, stdin));
    let out = '';
    let err = '';
    let code = 0;
    for (const target of args) {
      if (target === '-') {
        out += readInput(ctx, stdin);
        continue;
      }
      try {
        out += fs.readFileSync(resolveFile(ctx, target), 'utf8');
      } catch (error) {
        err += errnoMessage(error, target) + '\n';
        code = 1;
      }
    }
    return { out, err, code };
  },

  head(ctx, args, stdin) {
    let count = 10;
    const files = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < args.length; i += 1) {
      if (args[i] === '-n') count = parseInt(args[++i], 10) || 10;
      else if (/^-\d+$/.test(args[i])) count = parseInt(args[i].slice(1), 10);
      else files.push(args[i]);
    }
    const text = files.length > 0
      ? files.map(f => { try { return fs.readFileSync(resolveFile(ctx, f), 'utf8'); } catch { return ''; } }).join('')
      : readInput(ctx, stdin);
    return ok(splitLines(text).slice(0, count).map(l => l + '\n').join(''));
  },

  tail(ctx, args, stdin) {
    let count = 10;
    const files = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < args.length; i += 1) {
      if (args[i] === '-n') count = parseInt(args[++i], 10) || 10;
      else if (/^-\d+$/.test(args[i])) count = parseInt(args[i].slice(1), 10);
      else files.push(args[i]);
    }
    const text = files.length > 0
      ? files.map(f => { try { return fs.readFileSync(resolveFile(ctx, f), 'utf8'); } catch { return ''; } }).join('')
      : readInput(ctx, stdin);
    const lines = splitLines(text);
    return ok(lines.slice(Math.max(0, lines.length - count)).map(l => l + '\n').join(''));
  },

  wc(ctx, args, stdin) {
    const text = args.length > 0
      ? args.map(f => { try { return fs.readFileSync(resolveFile(ctx, f), 'utf8'); } catch { return ''; } }).join('\n')
      : readInput(ctx, stdin);
    const lines = text === '' ? 0 : text.split('\n').filter((l, i, a) => !(i === a.length - 1 && l === '')).length;
    const words = text.trim() === '' ? 0 : text.trim().split(/\s+/).length;
    return ok(`${String(lines).padStart(7)} ${String(words).padStart(7)} ${String(Buffer.byteLength(text)).padStart(7)}\n`);
  },

  grep(ctx, args, stdin) {
    const flags = { i: false, v: false, n: false, c: false, l: false, r: false, E: false };
    const rest = [];
    for (const arg of args) {
      if (arg.startsWith('-') && arg.length > 1 && !/^-?\d+$/.test(arg)) {
        for (const ch of arg.slice(1)) if (flags[ch] !== undefined) flags[ch] = true;
      } else rest.push(arg);
    }
    if (rest.length === 0) return fail('grep: usage: grep [options] pattern [file...]');
    const patternText = rest.shift();
    let regex;
    try {
      regex = new RegExp(patternText, flags.i ? 'i' : '');
    } catch {
      return fail(`grep: invalid pattern: ${patternText}`);
    }
    const targets = rest;
    const sources = [];
    if (targets.length === 0) sources.push({ name: '(standard input)', text: readInput(ctx, stdin) });
    else {
      for (const target of targets) {
        const resolved = resolveFile(ctx, target);
        try {
          const stat = fs.statSync(resolved);
          if (stat.isDirectory()) {
            if (!flags.r) {
              sources.push({ name: target, text: '', error: `${target}: Is a directory` });
              continue;
            }
            const walk = dir => {
              for (const name of fs.readdirSync(dir)) {
                const full = path.join(dir, name);
                let childStat;
                try { childStat = fs.statSync(full); } catch { continue; }
                if (childStat.isDirectory()) walk(full);
                else sources.push({ name: full, text: fs.readFileSync(full, 'utf8') });
              }
            };
            walk(resolved);
          } else {
            sources.push({ name: target, text: fs.readFileSync(resolved, 'utf8') });
          }
        } catch (error) {
          sources.push({ name: target, text: '', error: errnoMessage(error, target) });
        }
      }
    }
    let out = '';
    let err = '';
    let matched = 0;
    const prefixFor = source => (targets.length > 1 ? source.name + ':' : '');
    for (const source of sources) {
      if (source.error) {
        err += source.error + '\n';
        continue;
      }
      const lines = splitLines(source.text);
      for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i += 1) {
        const hit = regex.test(lines[i]);
        if (hit === flags.v) continue;
        matched += 1;
        if (flags.l) {
          out += source.name + '\n';
          break;
        }
        if (flags.c) continue;
        out += (flags.n ? i + 1 + ':' : '') + prefixFor(source) + lines[i] + '\n';
      }
      if (flags.c) out += prefixFor(source) + matched + '\n';
      if (flags.c) matched = 0;
    }
    return { out, err, code: matched > 0 || out.length > 0 ? 0 : 1 };
  },

  find(ctx, args) {
    const roots = [];
    let namePattern = null;
    let typeFilter = null;
    for (let i = 0; i < args.length; i += 1) {
      if (args[i] === '-name') namePattern = args[++i];
      else if (args[i] === '-type') typeFilter = args[++i];
      else if (args[i] === '-maxdepth') i += 1;
      else roots.push(args[i]);
    }
    if (roots.length === 0) roots.push('.');
    const regex = namePattern ? globToRegExp(namePattern) : null;
    let out = '';
    let err = '';
    for (const root of roots) {
      const resolved = resolveFile(ctx, root);
      const walk = (dir, prefix) => {
        let entries;
        try {
          entries = fs.readdirSync(dir).sort();
        } catch (error) {
          err += errnoMessage(error, dir) + '\n';
          return;
        }
        for (const name of entries) {
          const full = path.join(dir, name);
          const display = prefix === '' ? path.join(root, name).replace(/^\.\//, '') : path.join(prefix, name);
          let stat;
          try { stat = fs.lstatSync(full); } catch { continue; }
          const isDir = stat.isDirectory();
          const nameOk = !regex || regex.test(name);
          const typeOk = !typeFilter || (typeFilter === 'd' ? isDir : typeFilter === 'f' ? stat.isFile() : true);
          if (nameOk && typeOk) out += display + '\n';
          if (isDir) walk(full, display);
        }
      };
      try {
        const stat = fs.statSync(resolved);
        if (stat.isDirectory()) walk(resolved, '');
        else out += root + '\n';
      } catch (error) {
        err += errnoMessage(error, root) + '\n';
      }
    }
    return { out, err, code: err ? 1 : 0 };
  },

  mkdir(ctx, args) {
    let parents = false;
    const targets = [];
    for (const arg of args) {
      if (arg === '-p') parents = true;
      else targets.push(arg);
    }
    const missing = needArgs('mkdir', targets, 1);
    if (missing) return missing;
    let err = '';
    for (const target of targets) {
      try {
        fs.mkdirSync(resolveFile(ctx, target), { recursive: parents });
      } catch (error) {
        err += errnoMessage(error, target) + '\n';
      }
    }
    return { out: '', err, code: err ? 1 : 0 };
  },

  rmdir(ctx, args) {
    const missing = needArgs('rmdir', args, 1);
    if (missing) return missing;
    let err = '';
    for (const target of args) {
      try {
        fs.rmdirSync(resolveFile(ctx, target));
      } catch (error) {
        err += errnoMessage(error, target) + '\n';
      }
    }
    return { out: '', err, code: err ? 1 : 0 };
  },

  rm(ctx, args) {
    let recursive = false;
    let force = false;
    const targets = [];
    for (const arg of args) {
      if (arg === '-rf' || arg === '-fr' || arg === '-R' || arg === '-r') {
        recursive = true;
        if (arg.includes('f')) force = true;
      } else if (arg === '-f') force = true;
      else targets.push(arg);
    }
    const missing = needArgs('rm', targets, 1);
    if (missing && !force) return missing;
    let err = '';
    for (const target of targets) {
      try {
        fs.rmSync(resolveFile(ctx, target), { recursive, force });
      } catch (error) {
        err += errnoMessage(error, target) + '\n';
      }
    }
    return { out: '', err, code: err ? 1 : 0 };
  },

  cp(ctx, args) {
    const operands = args.filter(a => !a.startsWith('-'));
    const recursive = args.some(a => /-[a-zA-Z]*[rR]/.test(a));
    if (operands.length < 2) return fail('cp: missing destination file operand');
    const destination = resolveFile(ctx, operands.pop());
    let err = '';
    for (const source of operands) {
      try {
        const from = resolveFile(ctx, source);
        const stat = fs.statSync(from);
        if (stat.isDirectory() && !recursive) {
          err += `cp: ${source}: Is a directory\n`;
          continue;
        }
        let target = destination;
        if (fs.existsSync(destination) && fs.statSync(destination).isDirectory()) {
          target = path.join(destination, path.basename(from));
        }
        fs.cpSync(from, target, { recursive });
      } catch (error) {
        err += errnoMessage(error, source) + '\n';
      }
    }
    return { out: '', err, code: err ? 1 : 0 };
  },

  mv(ctx, args) {
    const operands = args.filter(a => !a.startsWith('-'));
    if (operands.length < 2) return fail('mv: missing destination file operand');
    const destination = resolveFile(ctx, operands.pop());
    let err = '';
    for (const source of operands) {
      try {
        const from = resolveFile(ctx, source);
        let target = destination;
        if (fs.existsSync(destination) && fs.statSync(destination).isDirectory()) {
          target = path.join(destination, path.basename(from));
        }
        fs.renameSync(from, target);
      } catch (error) {
        err += errnoMessage(error, source) + '\n';
      }
    }
    return { out: '', err, code: err ? 1 : 0 };
  },

  touch(ctx, args) {
    const missing = needArgs('touch', args, 1);
    if (missing) return missing;
    let err = '';
    for (const target of args) {
      try {
        const resolved = resolveFile(ctx, target);
        const now = new Date();
        if (fs.existsSync(resolved)) fs.utimesSync(resolved, now, now);
        else fs.writeFileSync(resolved, '');
      } catch (error) {
        err += errnoMessage(error, target) + '\n';
      }
    }
    return { out: '', err, code: err ? 1 : 0 };
  },

  chmod(ctx, args) {
    if (args.length < 2) return fail('chmod: missing operand');
    const mode = parseInt(args[0], 8);
    if (!Number.isFinite(mode)) return fail(`chmod: invalid mode: ${args[0]}`);
    let err = '';
    for (const target of args.slice(1)) {
      try {
        fs.chmodSync(resolveFile(ctx, target), mode);
      } catch (error) {
        err += errnoMessage(error, target) + '\n';
      }
    }
    return { out: '', err, code: err ? 1 : 0 };
  },

  stat(ctx, args) {
    const targets = args.filter(a => !a.startsWith('-'));
    const missing = needArgs('stat', targets, 1);
    if (missing) return missing;
    let out = '';
    let err = '';
    for (const target of targets) {
      try {
        const resolved = resolveFile(ctx, target);
        const stat = fs.lstatSync(resolved);
        out += `  File: ${target}\n  Size: ${stat.size}\tBlocks: ${Math.ceil(stat.size / 512)}\tIO Block: 4096\n`;
        out += `  Mode: ${(stat.mode & 0o777).toString(8)}\tType: ${stat.isDirectory() ? 'directory' : 'regular file'}\n`;
        out += `Modify: ${stat.mtime.toISOString()}\n`;
      } catch (error) {
        err += errnoMessage(error, target) + '\n';
      }
    }
    return { out, err, code: err ? 1 : 0 };
  },

  du(ctx, args) {
    const targets = args.filter(a => !a.startsWith('-'));
    if (targets.length === 0) targets.push('.');
    let out = '';
    for (const target of targets) {
      const resolved = resolveFile(ctx, target);
      let bytes = 0;
      const walk = dir => {
        for (const name of fs.readdirSync(dir)) {
          const full = path.join(dir, name);
          try {
            const stat = fs.lstatSync(full);
            if (stat.isDirectory()) walk(full);
            else bytes += stat.size;
          } catch { /* ignore */ }
        }
      };
      try {
        const stat = fs.statSync(resolved);
        if (stat.isDirectory()) walk(resolved);
        else bytes = stat.size;
        out += `${Math.max(1, Math.ceil(bytes / 1024))}\t${target}\n`;
      } catch (error) {
        out += '';
      }
    }
    return ok(out);
  },

  sort(ctx, args, stdin) {
    let text = '';
    const files = args.filter(a => !a.startsWith('-'));
    if (files.length > 0) for (const f of files) {
      try { text += fs.readFileSync(resolveFile(ctx, f), 'utf8'); } catch { /* ignore */ }
    } else text = readInput(ctx, stdin);
    const reverse = args.some(a => /-[a-zA-Z]*r/.test(a));
    const numeric = args.some(a => /-[a-zA-Z]*n/.test(a));
    let lines = splitLines(text);
    lines.sort(numeric ? (a, b) => parseFloat(a) - parseFloat(b) : (a, b) => (a < b ? -1 : a > b ? 1 : 0));
    if (reverse) lines.reverse();
    return ok(lines.map(l => l + '\n').join(''));
  },

  uniq(ctx, args, stdin) {
    const text = readInput(ctx, stdin);
    const lines = splitLines(text);
    const out = [];
    for (const line of lines) {
      if (out.length === 0 || out[out.length - 1] !== line) out.push(line);
    }
    return ok(out.map(l => l + '\n').join(''));
  },

  cut(ctx, args, stdin) {
    const delimiter = (args.find(a => a.startsWith('-d')) || '-d\t').slice(2) || '\t';
    const fieldSpec = (args.find(a => a.startsWith('-f')) || '').slice(2);
    if (!fieldSpec) return fail('cut: [-f list] [-d char]');
    const fields = fieldSpec.split(',').map(n => parseInt(n, 10));
    const text = readInput(ctx, stdin);
    const out = splitLines(text).map(line => {
      const parts = delimiter === ' ' ? line.split(/\s+/) : line.split(delimiter);
      return fields.map(n => parts[n - 1] ?? '').join(delimiter);
    });
    return ok(out.map(l => l + '\n').join(''));
  },

  sed(ctx, args, stdin) {
    const script = args.find(a => !a.startsWith('-'));
    if (!script) return fail('sed: missing script');
    const text = readInput(ctx, stdin);
    const subst = /^s(.)(.*?)\1(.*?)\1([gi]*)$/.exec(script);
    if (subst) {
      const [, , pattern, replacement, flags] = subst;
      let regex;
      try { regex = new RegExp(pattern, flags.includes('g') ? 'g' : ''); } catch { return fail('sed: invalid regex'); }
      const out = splitLines(text).map(line => line.replace(regex, replacement));
      return ok(out.map(l => l + '\n').join(''));
    }
    if (script === 'p') return ok(text);
    if (script === 'd') return ok('');
    return fail(`sed: unsupported script: ${script}`);
  },

  date() {
    return ok(new Date().toString() + '\n');
  },

  env(ctx) {
    return ok(Object.entries(ctx.env).map(([k, v]) => `${k}=${v}`).join('\n') + '\n');
  },

  which(ctx, args) {
    let out = '';
    for (const name of args) {
      const found = commands[name] ? `builtin:${name}` : null;
      out += (found || `${name} not found`) + '\n';
    }
    return { out, err: '', code: 0 };
  },

  basename(ctx, args) {
    if (args.length === 0) return fail('basename: missing operand');
    return ok(path.basename(args[0]) + '\n');
  },

  dirname(ctx, args) {
    if (args.length === 0) return fail('dirname: missing operand');
    return ok(path.dirname(args[0]) + '\n');
  },

  realpath(ctx, args) {
    let out = '';
    let err = '';
    for (const target of args) {
      try { out += fs.realpathSync(resolveFile(ctx, target)) + '\n'; }
      catch (error) { err += errnoMessage(error, target) + '\n'; }
    }
    return { out, err, code: err ? 1 : 0 };
  },

  sleep(ctx, args) {
    const seconds = parseFloat(args[0] || '0');
    const until = Date.now() + seconds * 1000;
    while (Date.now() < until) { /* busy wait: no timers inside a sync shell */ }
    return ok();
  },

  sha256sum(ctx, args) {
    const crypto = require('crypto');
    let out = '';
    let err = '';
    for (const target of args) {
      try {
        const data = fs.readFileSync(resolveFile(ctx, target));
        out += `${crypto.createHash('sha256').update(data).digest('hex')}  ${target}\n`;
      } catch (error) {
        err += errnoMessage(error, target) + '\n';
      }
    }
    return { out, err, code: err ? 1 : 0 };
  },

  test(ctx, args) { return testBuiltin(ctx, stripBrackets(args)); },
  '['(ctx, args) { return testBuiltin(ctx, stripBrackets(args)); },

  export(ctx, args) {
    for (const arg of args) {
      const eq = arg.indexOf('=');
      if (eq === -1) continue;
      ctx.env[arg.slice(0, eq)] = arg.slice(eq + 1);
    }
    return ok();
  },

  unset(ctx, args) {
    for (const name of args) delete ctx.env[name];
    return ok();
  },

  set(ctx, args) {
    if (args.length === 0) return ok(Object.entries(ctx.env).map(([k, v]) => `${k}=${v}`).join('\n') + '\n');
    return ok();
  },

  source(ctx, args) {
    if (args.length === 0) return fail('source: missing file');
    try {
      return ctx.run(fs.readFileSync(resolveFile(ctx, args[0]), 'utf8'));
    } catch (error) {
      return fail(`source: ${errnoMessage(error, args[0])}`);
    }
  },

  '.': (ctx, args) => commands.source(ctx, args),

  type(ctx, args) {
    let out = '';
    for (const name of args) out += (commands[name] ? `${name} is a shell builtin` : `${name}: not found`) + '\n';
    return ok(out);
  },

  jobs() { return ok(); },
  alias() { return ok(); },
  unalias() { return ok(); },
  umask() { return ok('0022\n'); },
  wait() { return ok(); },
  times() { return ok('0m0.000s 0m0.000s\n0m0.000s 0m0.000s\n'); },
  read() { return ok(); },
  eval(ctx, args) { return ctx.run(args.join(' ')); },
  shift(ctx, args) {
    const count = parseInt(args[0] || '1', 10);
    ctx.positional = ctx.positional.slice(count);
    return ok();
  },
  ':' () { return ok(); }
};

function stripBrackets(args) {
  if (args.length > 0 && args[args.length - 1] === ']') return args.slice(0, -1);
  return args;
}

function testBuiltin(ctx, args) {
  const truth = value => {
    if (args.length === 1) return value !== '';
    return value !== '';
  };
  try {
    if (args.length === 0) return { out: '', err: '', code: 1 };
    if (args[0] === '!') return { out: '', err: '', code: testBuiltin(ctx, args.slice(1)).code === 0 ? 1 : 0 };
    if (args.length === 1) return { out: '', err: '', code: truth(args[0]) ? 0 : 1 };
    if (args.length === 2) {
      const [op, operand] = args;
      let result = false;
      if (op === '-e') result = fs.existsSync(resolveFile(ctx, operand));
      else if (op === '-f') result = fs.existsSync(resolveFile(ctx, operand)) && fs.statSync(resolveFile(ctx, operand)).isFile();
      else if (op === '-d') result = fs.existsSync(resolveFile(ctx, operand)) && fs.statSync(resolveFile(ctx, operand)).isDirectory();
      else if (op === '-s') result = fs.existsSync(resolveFile(ctx, operand)) && fs.statSync(resolveFile(ctx, operand)).size > 0;
      else if (op === '-r' || op === '-w') result = fs.existsSync(resolveFile(ctx, operand));
      else if (op === '-z') result = operand === '';
      else if (op === '-n') result = operand !== '';
      return { out: '', err: '', code: result ? 0 : 1 };
    }
    const [left, op, right] = args;
    const numeric = (a, b, fn) => fn(parseFloat(a), parseFloat(b));
    let result = false;
    switch (op) {
      case '=': case '==': result = left === right; break;
      case '!=': result = left !== right; break;
      case '-eq': result = numeric(left, right, (a, b) => a === b); break;
      case '-ne': result = numeric(left, right, (a, b) => a !== b); break;
      case '-lt': result = numeric(left, right, (a, b) => a < b); break;
      case '-le': result = numeric(left, right, (a, b) => a <= b); break;
      case '-gt': result = numeric(left, right, (a, b) => a > b); break;
      case '-ge': result = numeric(left, right, (a, b) => a >= b); break;
      default: result = false;
    }
    return { out: '', err: '', code: result ? 0 : 1 };
  } catch {
    return { out: '', err: '', code: 1 };
  }
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ executor */

function createContext(env) {
  return {
    env: { ...env },
    cwd: process.cwd(),
    positional: [],
    lastStatus: 0,
    exitCode: 0,
    exitRequested: false,
    run(source) {
      const result = execute(this, source);
      this.lastStatus = result.code;
      return result;
    }
  };
}

function splitTop(tokens) {
  const segments = [];
  let current = [];
  for (const token of tokens) {
    if (token.type === 'op' && [';', '&&', '||', '&'].includes(token.value)) {
      segments.push({ tokens: current, operator: token.value });
      current = [];
    } else {
      current.push(token);
    }
  }
  if (current.length > 0) segments.push({ tokens: current, operator: null });
  return segments;
}

function expandWord(ctx, word) {
  const substituted = substitute(ctx, word);
  const tilde = expandTilde(substituted);
  return expandGlob(tilde);
}

function expandWords(ctx, words) {
  const out = [];
  for (const word of words) out.push(...expandWord(ctx, word));
  return out;
}

function runPipeline(ctx, stages) {
  let stdin;
  let lastResult = { out: '', err: '', code: 0 };
  for (const stage of stages) {
    lastResult = runStage(ctx, stage, stdin);
    stdin = lastResult.out;
  }
  return lastResult;
}

function runStage(ctx, stage, stdin) {
  const words = [];
  const redirects = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < stage.length; i += 1) {
    const token = stage[i];
    if (token.type === 'op' && ['>', '>>', '<', '2>', '2>>'].includes(token.value)) {
      const target = stage[i + 1];
      if (!target || target.type !== 'word') return fail('syntax error: redirection without target', 2);
      redirects.push({ op: token.value, target: target.value });
      i += 1;
      continue;
    }
    if (token.type === 'word') words.push(token.value);
  }
  if (words.length === 0) return { out: '', err: '', code: 0 };

  const expanded = expandWords(ctx, words);
  if (expanded.length === 0) return { out: '', err: '', code: 0 };
  const name = expanded[0];
  const args = expanded.slice(1);

  let input = stdin;
  for (const redirect of redirects.filter(r => r.op === '<')) {
    try {
      input = fs.readFileSync(resolveFile(ctx, redirect.target), 'utf8');
    } catch (error) {
      return fail(`${redirect.target}: ${errnoMessage(error, redirect.target)}`);
    }
  }

  const handler = commands[name];
  let result;
  if (handler) {
    result = handler(ctx, args, input);
  } else {
    result = fail(`${name}: command not found (this shell runs everything in-process; see 调试 → 终端说明)`, 127);
  }

  for (const redirect of redirects.filter(r => r.op === '>' || r.op === '>>')) {
    try {
      const target = resolveFile(ctx, redirect.target);
      if (redirect.op === '>>') fs.appendFileSync(target, result.out);
      else fs.writeFileSync(target, result.out);
      result = { ...result, out: '' };
    } catch (error) {
      result = { ...result, err: result.err + errnoMessage(error, redirect.target) + '\n', code: 1 };
    }
  }
  for (const redirect of redirects.filter(r => r.op === '2>' || r.op === '2>>')) {
    try {
      const target = resolveFile(ctx, redirect.target);
      if (redirect.op === '2>>') fs.appendFileSync(target, result.err);
      else fs.writeFileSync(target, result.err);
      result = { ...result, err: '' };
    } catch (error) {
      result = { ...result, err: result.err + errnoMessage(error, redirect.target) + '\n', code: 1 };
    }
  }
  return result;
}

function execute(ctx, source) {
  const tokens = tokenize(source);
  if (tokens.length === 0) return { out: '', err: '', code: 0 };
  const segments = splitTop(tokens);
  let output = '';
  let errors = '';
  let status = 0;

  for (const segment of segments) {
    const stageTokens = segment.tokens;
    if (stageTokens.length === 0) continue;

    // `if`, `for`, `while` blocks are executed verbatim through the same parser
    // only when they are simple; anything complex is reported instead of
    // silently mis-executing.
    const first = stageTokens[0];
    if (first.type === 'word' && ['if', 'for', 'while', 'until', 'case', '{'].includes(first.value)) {
      errors += `bash: compound command "${first.value}" is not supported by the in-app shell; run its body directly\n`;
      status = 2;
      continue;
    }

    // split pipeline stages on '|'
    const stages = [];
    let current = [];
    for (const token of stageTokens) {
      if (token.type === 'op' && token.value === '|') {
        stages.push(current);
        current = [];
      } else current.push(token);
    }
    stages.push(current);

    const result = runPipeline(ctx, stages);
    output += result.out;
    errors += result.err;
    status = result.code;
    ctx.lastStatus = status;

    if (segment.operator === '&&' && status !== 0) break;
    if (segment.operator === '||' && status === 0) break;
    if (ctx.exitRequested) break;
  }

  if (ctx.exitRequested) status = ctx.exitCode;
  return { out: output, err: errors, code: status };
}

/* --------------------------------------------------------------------- main */

function parseArgs(argv) {
  let command = null;
  const rest = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < argv.length; i += 1) {
    const arg = argv[i];
    if (arg === '-c') {
      command = argv[i + 1] ?? '';
      i += 1;
      continue;
    }
    if (/^-[a-zA-Z]+$/.test(arg) && !command) continue; // -l, -e, -x, ...
    rest.push(arg);
  }
  return { command, rest };
}

function main() {
  const { command, rest } = parseArgs(process.argv.slice(2));
  const ctx = createContext(process.env);
  ctx.positional = rest;

  let source = command;
  if (source === null) {
    if (rest.length > 0) {
      try {
        source = fs.readFileSync(path.resolve(rest[0]), 'utf8');
      } catch (error) {
        process.stderr.write(`bash: ${rest[0]}: ${errnoMessage(error, rest[0])}\n`);
        process.exit(127);
      }
    } else {
      source = fs.readFileSync(0, 'utf8');
    }
  }

  const result = execute(ctx, source);
  if (result.out) process.stdout.write(result.out);
  if (result.err) process.stderr.write(result.err);
  process.exit(result.code);
}

main();

```

`apple/Resources/web-compat.js`:

```js
/* DeepSeek for iOS — WebKit compatibility shim.
 * Served by the harness itself so every browser gets it, and also injected
 * into the in-app WKWebView for subframes/workers that load before us.
 */

    (function () {
      'use strict';
      var g = typeof globalThis !== 'undefined' ? globalThis : window;

      // --- AbortSignal (Safari 17.4+) -------------------------------------
      if (g.AbortSignal) {
        if (!g.AbortSignal.any) {
          g.AbortSignal.any = function (signals) {
            var controller = new AbortController();
            var list = Array.prototype.slice.call(signals || []);
            for (var i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {
              var s = list[i];
              if (!s) continue;
              if (s.aborted) { controller.abort(s.reason); return controller.signal; }
            }
            list.forEach(function (s) {
              s.addEventListener('abort', function () { controller.abort(s.reason); }, { once: true });
            });
            return controller.signal;
          };
        }
        if (!g.AbortSignal.timeout) {
          g.AbortSignal.timeout = function (ms) {
            var controller = new AbortController();
            setTimeout(function () {
              try { controller.abort(new DOMException('signal timed out', 'TimeoutError')); }
              catch (e) { controller.abort(); }
            }, ms);
            return controller.signal;
          };
        }
        if (!g.AbortSignal.abort) {
          g.AbortSignal.abort = function (reason) {
            var controller = new AbortController();
            controller.abort(reason);
            return controller.signal;
          };
        }
      }

      // --- Array / String / Object ----------------------------------------
      if (!Array.prototype.at) {
        Object.defineProperty(Array.prototype, 'at', {
          value: function (n) {
            n = Math.trunc(n) || 0;
            if (n < 0) n += this.length;
            return (n < 0 || n >= this.length) ? undefined : this[n];
          }, writable: true, configurable: true
        });
      }
      if (!String.prototype.at) {
        Object.defineProperty(String.prototype, 'at', {
          value: function (n) {
            n = Math.trunc(n) || 0;
            if (n < 0) n += this.length;
            return (n < 0 || n >= this.length) ? undefined : this.charAt(n);
          }, writable: true, configurable: true
        });
      }
      ['findLast', 'findLastIndex'].forEach(function (name) {
        if (Array.prototype[name]) return;
        Object.defineProperty(Array.prototype, name, {
          value: function (fn, thisArg) {
            for (var i = this.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
              if (fn.call(thisArg, this[i], i, this)) return name === 'findLast' ? this[i] : i;
            }
            return name === 'findLast' ? undefined : -1;
          }, writable: true, configurable: true
        });
      });
      ['toSorted', 'toReversed', 'toSpliced', 'with'].forEach(function (name) {
        if (Array.prototype[name]) return;
        Object.defineProperty(Array.prototype, name, {
          value: function () {
            var copy = Array.prototype.slice.call(this);
            if (name === 'toSorted') return copy.sort.apply(copy, arguments);
            if (name === 'toReversed') return copy.reverse();
            if (name === 'toSpliced') return copy.splice.apply(copy, arguments), copy;
            var idx = arguments[0], val = arguments[1];
            if (idx < 0) idx += copy.length;
            copy[idx] = val;
            return copy;
          }, writable: true, configurable: true
        });
      });
      if (!Object.hasOwn) {
        Object.hasOwn = function (obj, key) { return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(obj, key); };
      }
      if (!Object.groupBy) {
        Object.groupBy = function (items, fn) {
          var out = Object.create(null);
          var i = 0;
          for (var item of items) {
            var key = fn(item, i++);
            (out[key] = out[key] || []).push(item);
          }
          return out;
        };
      }
      if (!Map.groupBy) {
        Map.groupBy = function (items, fn) {
          var out = new Map();
          var i = 0;
          for (var item of items) {
            var key = fn(item, i++);
            if (!out.has(key)) out.set(key, []);
            out.get(key).push(item);
          }
          return out;
        };
      }
      if (!Promise.withResolvers) {
        Promise.withResolvers = function () {
          var resolve, reject;
          var promise = new Promise(function (res, rej) { resolve = res; reject = rej; });
          return { promise: promise, resolve: resolve, reject: reject };
        };
      }
      if (!URL.canParse) {
        URL.canParse = function (input, base) {
          try { new URL(input, base); return true; } catch (e) { return false; }
        };
      }
      if (!globalThis.requestIdleCallback) {
        globalThis.requestIdleCallback = function (cb) {
          return setTimeout(function () {
            cb({ didTimeout: false, timeRemaining: function () { return 8; } });
          }, 1);
        };
        globalThis.cancelIdleCallback = function (id) { clearTimeout(id); };
      }
      if (g.crypto && !g.crypto.randomUUID) {
        g.crypto.randomUUID = function () {
          var bytes = new Uint8Array(16);
          g.crypto.getRandomValues(bytes);
          bytes[6] = (bytes[6] & 0x0f) | 0x40;
          bytes[8] = (bytes[8] & 0x3f) | 0x80;
          var hex = Array.prototype.map.call(bytes, function (b) {
            return ('0' + b.toString(16)).slice(-2);
          }).join('');
          return hex.slice(0, 8) + '-' + hex.slice(8, 12) + '-' + hex.slice(12, 16) + '-' +
                 hex.slice(16, 20) + '-' + hex.slice(20);
        };
      }
    })();
```

```swift
import SwiftUI

// MARK: - App entry

@main
struct DshApp: App {
    @StateObject private var settings: AppSettings
    @StateObject private var harness: HarnessController
    @Environment(\.scenePhase) private var scenePhase

    init() {
        let s = AppSettings()
        _settings = StateObject(wrappedValue: s)
        _harness = StateObject(wrappedValue: HarnessController(settings: s))
    }

    var body: some Scene {
        WindowGroup {
            RootView()
                .environmentObject(settings)
                .environmentObject(harness)
                .preferredColorScheme(nil)
                .onAppear {
                    // One-tap deploy: a fresh install boots the harness by itself.
                    if settings.autoStart && harness.state == .idle {
                        harness.deploy(auto: true)
                    }
                }
                .onChange(of: scenePhase) { phase in
                    harness.handleScenePhase(phase)
                }
        }
    }
}

// MARK: - Paths

enum Paths {
    static var bundleRuntime: URL { Bundle.main.bundleURL.appendingPathComponent("Runtime", isDirectory: true) }
    static var nodeBinary: URL { bundleRuntime.appendingPathComponent("node") }
    static var dshTree: URL { bundleRuntime.appendingPathComponent("dsh", isDirectory: true) }
    static var dshBin: URL { dshTree.appendingPathComponent("lib/bin.js") }

    static var documents: URL {
        FileManager.default.urls(for: .documentDirectory, in: .userDomainMask)[0]
    }
    static var dshHome: URL { documents.appendingPathComponent("dsh-home", isDirectory: true) }
    static var workspaces: URL { documents.appendingPathComponent("Workspaces", isDirectory: true) }
    static var logsDir: URL { documents.appendingPathComponent("logs", isDirectory: true) }
    static var tmp: URL { documents.appendingPathComponent("tmp", isDirectory: true) }
    static var homeDir: URL { documents.appendingPathComponent("home", isDirectory: true) }
    /// Node is copied here when the app bundle's executable bit did not survive
    /// installation: an app bundle is read-only, so it cannot be fixed in place.
    static var binDir: URL { documents.appendingPathComponent("bin", isDirectory: true) }
    static var nodeCopy: URL { binDir.appendingPathComponent("node") }
    /// Third-party model provider overlay passed to the launcher via --patch.
    static var modelOverlay: URL { documents.appendingPathComponent("model-overlay.yml") }
    /// Generated at package time: the `standard` preset without its dead
    /// workflow-ptc subtree. Only used when `--dump-config` accepts it.
    static var presetOverlay: URL { bundleRuntime.appendingPathComponent("ios-preset-overlay.yml") }

    static func ensureDirectories() throws {
        for dir in [dshHome, workspaces, logsDir, tmp, homeDir, binDir] {
            try FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: dir, withIntermediateDirectories: true)
        }
    }
}

// MARK: - Settings

final class AppSettings: ObservableObject {
    private let store = UserDefaults.standard

    @Published var port: Int { didSet { store.set(port, forKey: "port") } }
    @Published var lanEnabled: Bool { didSet { store.set(lanEnabled, forKey: "lanEnabled") } }
    @Published var lanPort: Int { didSet { store.set(lanPort, forKey: "lanPort") } }
    @Published var autoStart: Bool { didSet { store.set(autoStart, forKey: "autoStart") } }
    @Published var keepAlive: Bool { didSet { store.set(keepAlive, forKey: "keepAlive") } }
    @Published var backgroundKeepAlive: Bool { didSet { store.set(backgroundKeepAlive, forKey: "backgroundKeepAlive") } }
    @Published var extraNodeFlags: String { didSet { store.set(extraNodeFlags, forKey: "extraNodeFlags") } }
    @Published var proxyURL: String { didSet { store.set(proxyURL, forKey: "proxyURL") } }
    @Published var maxOldSpaceMB: Int { didSet { store.set(maxOldSpaceMB, forKey: "maxOldSpaceMB") } }
    /// Drops `--jitless`. Only useful on chips where V8 can obtain executable
    /// memory on iOS; it is what WebAssembly (and therefore PTC mode) needs.
    /// Off by default because a bad guess crashes the harness on start.
    @Published var experimentalJIT: Bool { didSet { store.set(experimentalJIT, forKey: "experimentalJIT") } }

    init() {
        port = store.object(forKey: "port") as? Int ?? 3080
        lanEnabled = store.object(forKey: "lanEnabled") as? Bool ?? true
        lanPort = store.object(forKey: "lanPort") as? Int ?? 3081
        autoStart = store.object(forKey: "autoStart") as? Bool ?? true
        keepAlive = store.object(forKey: "keepAlive") as? Bool ?? true
        backgroundKeepAlive = store.object(forKey: "backgroundKeepAlive") as? Bool ?? true
        extraNodeFlags = store.string(forKey: "extraNodeFlags") ?? "--jitless"
        proxyURL = store.string(forKey: "proxyURL") ?? ""
        maxOldSpaceMB = store.object(forKey: "maxOldSpaceMB") as? Int ?? 1024
        experimentalJIT = store.object(forKey: "experimentalJIT") as? Bool ?? false
    }

    var nodeFlags: [String] {
        extraNodeFlags.split(separator: " ").map(String.init).filter { !$0.isEmpty }
    }
}

// MARK: - Log store

enum LogStream: String { case out = "out", err = "err", app = "app" }

struct LogLine: Identifiable {
    let id = UUID()
    let date = Date()
    let stream: LogStream
    let text: String
}

final class LogStore: ObservableObject {
    @Published private(set) var lines: [LogLine] = []
    private let limit = 6000
    private let queue = DispatchQueue(label: "dsh.logstore")
    /// Authoritative copy, only ever touched on `queue`. Publishing is a
    /// snapshot to the main thread; reading `lines` here would race and drop
    /// bursts of startup lines.
    private var buffer: [LogLine] = []
    private var flushScheduled = false

    func append(_ text: String, _ stream: LogStream) {
        queue.async {
            self.buffer.append(LogLine(stream: stream, text: text))
            if self.buffer.count > self.limit {
                self.buffer.removeFirst(self.buffer.count - self.limit)
            }
            guard !self.flushScheduled else { return }
            self.flushScheduled = true
            self.queue.asyncAfter(deadline: .now() + 0.1) {
                let snapshot = self.buffer
                self.flushScheduled = false
                DispatchQueue.main.async { self.lines = snapshot }
            }
        }
    }

    func clear() {
        queue.async {
            self.buffer.removeAll()
            DispatchQueue.main.async { self.lines = [] }
        }
    }

    func dump() -> String {
        queue.sync {
            buffer.map { "[\(Self.formatter.string(from: $0.date))] \($0.stream.rawValue.uppercased()) \($0.text)" }
                .joined(separator: "\n")
        }
    }

    static let formatter: DateFormatter = {
        let f = DateFormatter()
        f.dateFormat = "HH:mm:ss.SSS"
        return f
    }()
}
```
