```swift
import SwiftUI
import WebKit

/// In-app browser for the harness UI, with a console bridge so failures are
/// visible in the app log instead of a blank screen.
struct BrowserView: View {
    @EnvironmentObject var harness: HarnessController
    @State private var reloadToken = UUID()
    @State private var currentURL = ""

    var body: some View {
        NavigationView {
            Group {
                if let url = harness.localURL {
                    WebView(url: url,
                            reloadToken: reloadToken,
                            onLog: { message in harness.logs.append(message, .err) })
                        .id(url.absoluteString)
                } else {
                    VStack(spacing: 12) {
                        Image(systemName: "safari").font(.system(size: 42)).foregroundColor(.secondary)
                        Text("先到「部署」页一键启动").font(.headline)
                        Text("启动成功后这里会自动加载 DeepSeek 的 Web UI。")
                            .font(.footnote).foregroundColor(.secondary)
                        if let url = harness.localURL {
                            Text(url.absoluteString)
                                .font(.system(size: 10, design: .monospaced))
                                .multilineTextAlignment(.center)
                        }
                    }.padding()
                }
            }
            .navigationTitle("DeepSeek")
            .navigationBarTitleDisplayMode(.inline)
            .toolbar {
                ToolbarItemGroup(placement: .navigationBarTrailing) {
                    Button(action: { reloadToken = UUID(); harness.logs.append("网页：手动重新加载", .app) }) {
                        Image(systemName: "arrow.clockwise")
                    }
                    if let url = harness.localURL {
                        Button(action: { UIApplication.shared.open(url) }) { Image(systemName: "safari") }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        .navigationViewStyle(.stack)
    }
}

struct WebView: UIViewRepresentable {
    let url: URL
    let reloadToken: UUID
    let onLog: (String) -> Void

    func makeUIView(context: Context) -> WKWebView {
        let controller = WKUserContentController()
        // Console bridge first: it must wrap console before the app's bundle runs.
        controller.addUserScript(WKUserScript(source: WebView.consoleBridge,
                                             injectionTime: .atDocumentStart,
                                             forMainFrameOnly: false))
        controller.addUserScript(WKUserScript(source: WebCompat.source,
                                             injectionTime: .atDocumentStart,
                                             forMainFrameOnly: false))
        controller.add(context.coordinator, name: "dshios")

        let config = WKWebViewConfiguration()
        config.userContentController = controller
        config.allowsInlineMediaPlayback = true
        config.websiteDataStore = .default()

        let webView = WKWebView(frame: .zero, configuration: config)
        webView.navigationDelegate = context.coordinator
        webView.allowsBackForwardNavigationGestures = true
        if #available(iOS 16.4, *) { webView.isInspectable = true }

        context.coordinator.attach(webView: webView, log: onLog)
        webView.load(URLRequest(url: url))
        return webView
    }

    func updateUIView(_ webView: WKWebView, context: Context) {
        guard context.coordinator.lastToken != reloadToken else { return }
        context.coordinator.lastToken = reloadToken
        webView.load(URLRequest(url: url))
    }

    func makeCoordinator() -> Coordinator { Coordinator() }

    final class Coordinator: NSObject, WKNavigationDelegate, WKScriptMessageHandler {
        var lastToken: UUID?
        private weak var webView: WKWebView?
        private var log: ((String) -> Void)?
        private var retried = false

        func attach(webView: WKWebView, log: @escaping (String) -> Void) {
            self.webView = webView
            self.log = log
        }

        // MARK: JS -> app log

        func userContentController(_ controller: WKUserContentController,
                                   didReceive message: WKScriptMessage) {
            guard let text = message.body as? String else { return }
            log?("网页 JS ▸ " + text.prefix(400))
        }

        // MARK: navigation

        func webView(_ webView: WKWebView, didFinish navigation: WKNavigation!) {
            log?("网页已加载：\(webView.url?.absoluteString ?? "-")")
            webView.evaluateJavaScript("""
            (function () {
              var text = document.body ? (document.body.innerText || '') : '';
              return 'title=' + (document.title || '') + ' textLen=' + text.length +
                     ' nodes=' + (document.body ? document.body.querySelectorAll('*').length : 0);
            })();
            """) { value, error in
                if let error = error {
                    self.log?("网页自检失败：\(error.localizedDescription)")
                } else if let value = value as? String {
                    self.log?("网页自检：\(value)")
                }
            }
        }

        func webView(_ webView: WKWebView,
                     didFailProvisionalNavigation navigation: WKNavigation!,
                     withError error: Error) {
            log?("网页加载失败（provisional）：\(error.localizedDescription)")
            retryOnce(webView)
        }

        func webView(_ webView: WKWebView, didFail navigation: WKNavigation!, withError error: Error) {
            log?("网页加载失败：\(error.localizedDescription)")
            retryOnce(webView)
        }

        private func retryOnce(_ webView: WKWebView) {
            guard !retried, let url = webView.url ?? webView.backForwardList.currentItem?.url else { return }
            retried = true
            log?("网页：重试一次")
            DispatchQueue.main.asyncAfter(deadline: .now() + 1.5) {
                webView.load(URLRequest(url: url))
            }
        }
    }

    /// Captures page-level errors so a blank screen is diagnosable from the app.
    static let consoleBridge = """
    (function () {
      if (window.__dshiosBridgeInstalled) { return; }
      window.__dshiosBridgeInstalled = true;
      var budget = 25;
      function send(kind, text) {
        if (budget <= 0) { return; }
        budget--;
        try { window.webkit.messageHandlers.dshios.postMessage(kind + ' ' + text); } catch (e) {}
      }
      try {
        var originalError = console.error.bind(console);
        console.error = function () {
          send('[console.error]', Array.prototype.map.call(arguments, function (a) {
            try { return (a && a.stack) ? a.stack : String(a); } catch (e) { return '<unprintable>'; }
          }).join(' '));
          originalError.apply(null, arguments);
        };
      } catch (e) {}
      window.addEventListener('error', function (event) {
        var target = event.target;
        if (target && target !== window && target.tagName) {
          send('[resource.error]', target.tagName + ' ' + (target.src || target.href || ''));
        } else {
          send('[window.error]', (event.message || '') + ' @' + (event.filename || '') + ':' + (event.lineno || 0));
        }
      }, true);
      window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection', function (event) {
        send('[unhandledrejection]', String(event.reason));
      });
    })();
    """
}

```

`apple/Sources/CrashLog.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation
import UIKit

// File-scope state and capture-free handlers: Swift only converts capture-free
// functions into the C function pointers NSSetUncaughtExceptionHandler and
// signal(2) require.

private var crashFD: Int32 = -1

private func crashWrite(_ text: String) {
    guard crashFD >= 0 else { return }
    _ = text.withCString { write(crashFD, $0, strlen($0)) }
}

private func crashExceptionHandler(_ exception: NSException) {
    let frames = exception.callStackSymbols.prefix(30).joined(separator: "\n")
    crashWrite("NSException \(exception.name.rawValue): \(exception.reason ?? "-")\n\(frames)\n")
}

private func crashSignalHandler(_ sig: Int32) {
    crashWrite("fatal signal \(sig)\n")
    signal(sig, SIG_DFL)
    raise(sig)
}

/// Minimal crash recorder.
///
/// "It just quits" reports are useless without a reason, and a jailbroken
/// install gives us no crash log we can reach from the app. We record what a
/// process is allowed to record — uncaught Objective-C exceptions (what SwiftUI
/// and UIKit misuse usually raise) and the usual fatal signals — and the
/// diagnostics export includes it.
enum CrashLog {

    static let fileName = "crash.log"
    static var url: URL { Paths.logsDir.appendingPathComponent(fileName) }

    static func install() {
        try? FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: Paths.logsDir, withIntermediateDirectories: true)
        if !FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: url.path) {
            FileManager.default.createFile(atPath: url.path, contents: nil)
        }
        crashFD = open(url.path, O_WRONLY | O_APPEND | O_CREAT, 0o644)

        NSSetUncaughtExceptionHandler(crashExceptionHandler)
        for sig in [SIGABRT, SIGSEGV, SIGBUS, SIGILL, SIGFPE, SIGTRAP] {
            signal(sig, crashSignalHandler)
        }
    }

    /// Tail of the crash log, embedded in the diagnostics text.
    static func tail(maxBytes: Int = 8000) -> String {
        guard let text = try? String(contentsOf: url, encoding: .utf8), !text.isEmpty else { return "" }
        return text.count > maxBytes ? String(text.suffix(maxBytes)) : text
    }
}

```

`apple/Sources/HarnessController.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation
import SwiftUI
import UIKit
import Network
import Combine
import AVFoundation

enum HarnessState: Equatable {
    case idle
    case preparing
    case starting
    case running
    case stopping
    case failed(String)

    var label: String {
        switch self {
        case .idle: return "未部署"
        case .preparing: return "准备中…"
        case .starting: return "启动中…"
        case .running: return "运行中"
        case .stopping: return "正在停止…"
        case .failed: return "失败"
        }
    }

    var isBusy: Bool {
        switch self {
        case .preparing, .starting, .stopping: return true
        default: return false
        }
    }
}

struct CheckResult: Identifiable {
    let id = UUID()
    let title: String
    let ok: Bool
    let detail: String
}

/// Owns the whole lifecycle: the harness child process, its log stream, the
/// delivered URLs, the LAN relay and the diagnostics.
final class HarnessController: ObservableObject {

    @Published private(set) var state: HarnessState = .idle
    @Published private(set) var localURL: URL?
    @Published private(set) var lanURL: URL?
    @Published private(set) var launchToken: String?
    @Published private(set) var pid: Int32 = 0
    @Published private(set) var startedAt: Date?
    @Published private(set) var nodeVersion: String = "—"
    @Published private(set) var nodeExecutable: URL?
    @Published private(set) var effectivePort: Int = 0
    @Published private(set) var pluginIssues: [String] = []
    @Published private(set) var toolErrors: [String] = []
    @Published private(set) var startupLogPath: String = ""
    @Published private(set) var lastError: String?
    @Published private(set) var lanInterface: String = "—"
    @Published var checks: [CheckResult] = []
    @Published var autoRestartCount: Int = 0

    let logs = LogStore()
    private let settings: AppSettings
    private let process = NodeProcess()
    private var relay: TCPRelay?
    private var pathMonitor: NWPathMonitor?
    private var currentLANIP: String?
    private var pollTimer: Timer?
    private var startDeadline: Date?
    private var stoppingIntentionally = false
    private let keeper = BackgroundKeeper()
    private var diagnosticsBundle: [CheckResult] = []
    private var jitlessFallbackUsed = false
    private var platformSpoofUsable: Bool?
    private var overlayCache: [String: Bool] = [:]
    private var shimLoaderUsable: Bool?
    private var launchTime = Date()

    init(settings: AppSettings) {
        self.settings = settings
        process.onLine = { [weak self] line, stream in self?.handle(line: line, stream: stream) }
        process.onExit = { [weak self] code in self?.handleExit(code) }
        logs.append("DeepSeek for iOS 已就绪（官方源码 \(UpstreamInfo.commitShort)）", .app)
        CrashLog.install()
        NotificationCenter.default.addObserver(forName: UIApplication.willTerminateNotification,
                                               object: nil, queue: .main) { [weak self] _ in
            self?.killChildNow()
        }
    }

    // MARK: - Public control

    func deploy(auto: Bool = false) {
        guard !state.isBusy, state != .running else { return }
        setState(.preparing)
        lastError = nil
        DispatchQueue.global(qos: .userInitiated).async { [weak self] in
            guard let self = self else { return }
            do {
                try Paths.ensureDirectories()
                let executable = try self.prepareNodeExecutable()
                let version = try self.probeNode(at: executable)
                self.runPlatformPreflight(executable: executable)
                DispatchQueue.main.async {
                    self.nodeExecutable = executable
                    self.nodeVersion = version
                    self.start()
                }
            } catch {
                DispatchQueue.main.async {
                    self.fail(error.localizedDescription)
                }
            }
        }
    }

    func start() {
        guard process.pid == 0 else { return }
        setState(.starting)
        stoppingIntentionally = false

        // A previous child survives app relaunch (iOS kills the app, not the
        // process group). If it is still bound, this instance would fail to get
        // `webServer` and the harness would report "plugins did not activate".
        killStaleProcess()
        killOrphanRuntimes()
        _ = waitForPortFree(settings.port, timeout: 4)

        // A stale listener must never block the app: an orphan started by an
        // older build recorded no pid, so it cannot be matched — bind the next
        // free port instead of failing.
        var chosen = settings.port
        if !portFree(chosen) {
            guard let fallbackPort = nextFreePort(from: settings.port, avoiding: [settings.lanPort]) else {
                fail("从 \(settings.port) 起找不到空闲端口。请在「调试」里手动换一个端口。")
                return
            }
            logs.append("端口 \(settings.port) 被占用，本次改用 \(fallbackPort)（可到「调试」页改回）", .app)
            chosen = fallbackPort
        }
        effectivePort = chosen

        currentLANIP = NetworkInfo.primaryIPv4()
        do {
            try Paths.ensureDirectories()
            // Publish the app's folders into the harness' workspace registry so
            // they show up in the Web UI's workspace list.
            prepareShells()
            syncWorkspaces(restartIfRunning: false)
            let args = try buildArguments()
            let env = buildEnvironment()
            logs.append("启动: node " + args.joined(separator: " "), .app)
            launchTime = Date()
            try process.launch(executable: Paths.nodeBinary.path,
                               args: args,
                               environment: env,
                               workingDirectory: Paths.workspaces.path)
            pid = process.pid
            UserDefaults.standard.set(Int(process.pid), forKey: "childPid")
            startedAt = Date()
            startDeadline = Date().addingTimeInterval(150)

            if settings.lanEnabled, let ip = currentLANIP {
                currentLANIP = ip
                startRelay(lanIP: ip)
            } else if settings.lanEnabled {
                logs.append("未检测到局域网 IPv4 地址（未连 Wi‑Fi？），暂时只有本机链接", .app)
                lanInterface = "无"
            }
            startPathMonitor()
            schedulePoll()
            if settings.backgroundKeepAlive { keeper.start() }
        } catch {
            fail(error.localizedDescription)
        }
    }

    func stop() {
        stoppingIntentionally = true
        setState(.stopping)
        pollTimer?.invalidate(); pollTimer = nil
        relay?.stop(); relay = nil
        process.terminate()
        DispatchQueue.main.asyncAfter(deadline: .now() + 0.6) {
            self.process.shutdown()
            self.pid = 0
            self.startedAt = nil
            self.localURL = nil
            self.lanURL = nil
            self.setState(.idle)
            self.logs.append("已停止", .app)
        }
    }

    func restart() {
        stop()
        DispatchQueue.main.asyncAfter(deadline: .now() + 1.2) { self.start() }
    }

    func handleScenePhase(_ phase: ScenePhase) {
        switch phase {
        case .background:
            if !settings.backgroundKeepAlive { keeper.stop() }
        case .active:
            if settings.backgroundKeepAlive && state == .running { keeper.start() }
            if state == .running { verifyAlive() }
        default:
            break
        }
    }

    private func verifyAlive() {
        guard let url = localURL else { return }
        var request = URLRequest(url: url)
        request.timeoutInterval = 5
        URLSession.shared.dataTask(with: request) { _, response, error in
            if error != nil || response == nil {
                DispatchQueue.main.async {
                    self.logs.append("前台恢复后探测发现服务无响应，尝试重启", .app)
                    if self.settings.keepAlive { self.restart() }
                }
            }
        }.resume()
    }

    // MARK: - Runtime checks

    /// Resolves the node executable that will actually run.
    ///
    /// The bundle copy is preferred, but installers (Sideloadly, AltStore,
    /// `installipa`, repacking tools, some TrollStore paths) routinely drop the
    /// executable bit of non-code resources. The bundle is read-only, so the bit
    /// cannot be restored there — the binary is copied into the writable
    /// container instead and made executable there.
    @discardableResult
    private func prepareNodeExecutable() throws -> URL {
        let fm = FileManager.default
        guard fm.fileExists(atPath: Paths.dshBin.path) else {
            throw RuntimeError.missing("App 内缺少 DeepSeek 运行树（Runtime/dsh/lib/bin.js）。安装包不完整，请重新安装完整 ipa。")
        }
        guard fm.fileExists(atPath: Paths.nodeBinary.path) else {
            throw RuntimeError.missing("App 内缺少 Node 运行时（Runtime/node）。安装包不完整，请重新安装完整 ipa。")
        }

        // Fast path: the bundled binary is runnable as-is.
        if fm.isExecutableFile(atPath: Paths.nodeBinary.path) {
            logs.append("Node 可执行：使用 bundle 内二进制", .app)
            return Paths.nodeBinary
        }

        // Slow path: materialise a copy we are allowed to chmod.
        logs.append("bundle 内 Node 丢了可执行位（安装工具常见行为），复制到可写目录后修复…", .app)
        let dest = Paths.nodeCopy
        do {
            try fm.createDirectory(at: Paths.binDir, withIntermediateDirectories: true)
            let attr = try? fm.attributesOfItem(atPath: Paths.nodeBinary.path)
            let sourceSize = (attr?[.size] as? NSNumber)?.intValue ?? -1
            let existingSize = (try? fm.attributesOfItem(atPath: dest.path))?[.size] as? NSNumber
            let reusable = existingSize?.intValue == sourceSize && fm.isExecutableFile(atPath: dest.path)
            if !reusable {
                if fm.fileExists(atPath: dest.path) { try fm.removeItem(at: dest) }
                try fm.copyItem(at: Paths.nodeBinary, to: dest)
                try fm.setAttributes([.posixPermissions: 0o755], ofItemAtPath: dest.path)
            }
            guard fm.isExecutableFile(atPath: dest.path) else {
                throw RuntimeError.missing("复制后的 Node 仍不可执行：\(dest.path)")
            }
            logs.append("Node 已修复到可写目录：\(dest.path)", .app)
            return dest
        } catch let error as RuntimeError {
            throw error
        } catch {
            throw RuntimeError.missing("无法把 Node 复制到可写目录（\(error.localizedDescription)）。"
                + "如果 App 被沙盒限制（Sideloadly/AltStore 安装），请改用 TrollStore 安装。")
        }
    }

    /// One-shot self test of the resolved executable, with the failure classified
    /// so the UI can say what to actually do about it.
    private func probeNode(at executable: URL) throws -> String {
        let (code, output) = NodeProcess.run(executable: executable.path,
                                             args: ["--version"],
                                             environment: buildEnvironment(),
                                             timeout: 30)
        if code == 0 {
            let version = output.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
            logs.append("Node 运行时: \(version)（\(executable.lastPathComponent)）", .app)
            return version
        }
        let detail = output.isEmpty ? "无输出" : output
        switch code {
        case -1:
            throw RuntimeError.missing("系统拒绝启动 Node（Operation not permitted）。这几乎总是**沙盒限制**："
                + "Sideloadly / AltStore / 自签 7 天证书安装的 App 无法创建子进程。请用 TrollStore 安装，"
                + "或把 App 放到 /Applications（rootfull）或 /var/jb/Applications（rootless）并用 ldid 签名。")
        case -9, -11:
            throw RuntimeError.missing("Node 被系统杀死（signal \(-code)），通常是**签名不被接受**。"
                + "请安装 dsh-ios-ldid.ipa，或用 TrollStore 重新安装。\n\(detail)")
        case -2:
            throw RuntimeError.missing("Node 启动超时（30 秒）。设备可能内存不足，或二进制与本机不匹配。\n\(detail)")
        default:
            throw RuntimeError.missing("Node 自检失败（exit \(code)）：\n\(detail)")
        }
    }

    /// Rebuilds the writable node copy, then re-tests it. Bound to the UI button.
    func repairRuntime() {
        let fm = FileManager.default
        try? fm.removeItem(at: Paths.nodeCopy)
        logs.append("已清除可写目录中的 Node 副本，重新准备…", .app)
        do {
            let executable = try prepareNodeExecutable()
            nodeExecutable = executable
            nodeVersion = try probeNode(at: executable)
            lastError = nil
            setState(.idle)
            logs.append("运行时修复完成，可以点「一键部署并启动」", .app)
        } catch {
            fail(error.localizedDescription)
        }
        runDiagnostics()
    }

    private func buildEnvironment() -> [String: String] {
        var env = ProcessInfo.processInfo.environment
        env["HOME"] = Paths.homeDir.path
        env["TMPDIR"] = Paths.tmp.path
        env["DSH_HOME"] = Paths.dshHome.path
        env["PWD"] = Paths.workspaces.path
        env["LANG"] = "en_US.UTF-8"
        env["LC_ALL"] = "en_US.UTF-8"
        env["TERM"] = "xterm-256color"
        env["NO_COLOR"] = "1"
        env["NODE_OPTIONS"] = ""
        // Load the N-API addon straight from the bundle instead of a tmp copy.
        env["NARB_DISABLE_NATIVE_CACHE"] = "1"
        // The launcher execs node with the in-process shell script; both paths
        // travel through the environment so the launcher stays argument-free.
        env["DSH_SHELL_NODE"] = Paths.nodeBinary.path
        env["DSH_SHELL_JS"] = Paths.bundleRuntime.appendingPathComponent("shell/dsh-shell.js").path
        // The shipped web profile reads the deployment-wide sandbox mode from
        // this variable (`mode: process.env.DSH_PERMISSION_MODE ?? 'workspace-write'`).
        // iOS has no confinement backend, so tools run under the documented
        // full-access mode instead of failing closed on every call.
        env["DSH_PERMISSION_MODE"] = "danger-full-access"
        env["PATH"] = [Paths.binDir.path, Paths.bundleRuntime.path, "/var/jb/usr/bin", "/var/jb/bin",
                       "/usr/bin", "/bin", "/usr/sbin", "/sbin", "/usr/local/bin"].joined(separator: ":")
        let models = ModelConfig.load()
        if !models.deepseekKey.isEmpty { env["DEEPSEEK_API_KEY"] = models.deepseekKey }
        if models.thirdPartyEnabled && !models.thirdPartyKey.isEmpty {
            env[ModelConfig.envThirdParty] = models.thirdPartyKey
        }
        if !settings.proxyURL.isEmpty {
            env["HTTP_PROXY"] = settings.proxyURL
            env["HTTPS_PROXY"] = settings.proxyURL
            env["ALL_PROXY"] = settings.proxyURL
        }
        return env
    }

    private func buildArguments() throws -> [String] {
        var args: [String] = []
        // The iOS port of V8 has no JIT; the interpreter keeps memory W^X-clean.
        let wantsJitless = (!jitlessFallbackUsed || settings.extraNodeFlags.contains("--jitless"))
            && !settings.experimentalJIT
        if wantsJitless { args.append("--jitless") }
        if settings.maxOldSpaceMB > 0 {
            args.append("--max-old-space-size=\(settings.maxOldSpaceMB)")
        }
        for flag in settings.nodeFlags where flag != "--jitless" && !flag.hasPrefix("--max-old-space-size") {
            args.append(flag)
        }
        // Report darwin so the harness' native layer accepts us; only enabled
        // once the preflight proves both native modules load.
        if platformSpoofUsable == true, let preload = try? RuntimeShims.ensure() {
            args.append("--require")
            args.append(preload.path)
        }
        args.append(Paths.dshBin.path)

        // Launcher flags first (`--profile`/`--patch`), then the web app's own.
        args.append("--profile")
        args.append("web")
        // One overlay carries the iOS sandbox pass-through runner (iOS has no
        // bwrap/Landlock/Seatbelt, and the sandbox plugin fails closed without a
        // runner) plus the user's third-party model provider when configured.
        let models = ModelConfig.load()
        var patches: [URL] = []
        if let overlay = try? IOSOverlay.writeOverlay(models: models) { patches.append(overlay) }
        var droppedPresetOverlay = false
        if FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: Paths.presetOverlay.path) {
            if overlayUsable(patches + [Paths.presetOverlay]) {
                patches.append(Paths.presetOverlay)
            } else {
                droppedPresetOverlay = true
            }
        }
        for patch in patches {
            args.append("--patch")
            args.append(patch.path)
        }
        if models.thirdPartyEnabled { logs.append("已注入 overlay：沙箱旁路 + 第三方模型网关", .app) }
        else { logs.append("已注入 overlay：iOS 沙箱旁路 runner", .app) }
        if droppedPresetOverlay { logs.append("预设覆盖不可用（已跳过），标准模式可能仍显示加载失败", .err) }
        args.append("--no-open")
        args.append("--host")
        args.append("127.0.0.1")
        args.append("--port")
        args.append("\(effectivePort)")
        if settings.lanEnabled, let ip = currentLANIP {
            args.append("--trusted-host")
            args.append(ip)
            args.append("\(ip):\(settings.lanPort)")
        }
        return args
    }

    // MARK: - LAN relay

    private func startRelay(lanIP: String) {
        guard settings.lanPort != effectivePort else {
            logs.append("局域网端口 \(settings.lanPort) 与本机端口相同，已跳过中转（请在「调试」里改成不同端口）", .err)
            lanInterface = "端口冲突"
            return
        }
        relay?.stop()
        let relay = TCPRelay(listenPort: UInt16(settings.lanPort), targetPort: UInt16(effectivePort))
        relay.onEvent = { [weak self] message in
            DispatchQueue.main.async { self?.logs.append(message, .app) }
        }
        do {
            try relay.start()
            self.relay = relay
            lanInterface = "\(lanIP) → \(settings.lanPort)"
            logs.append("局域网链接已准备：http://\(lanIP):\(settings.lanPort)/", .app)
        } catch {
            logs.append("局域网中转启动失败：\(error.localizedDescription)", .err)
            lanInterface = "失败"
        }
    }

    private func startPathMonitor() {
        pathMonitor?.cancel()
        let monitor = NWPathMonitor()
        monitor.pathUpdateHandler = { [weak self] _ in
            DispatchQueue.main.asyncAfter(deadline: .now() + 2) {
                guard let self = self, self.state == .running else { return }
                let ip = NetworkInfo.primaryIPv4()
                guard ip != self.currentLANIP else { return }
                self.logs.append("局域网地址变化：\(self.currentLANIP ?? "无") → \(ip ?? "无")，重启以更新信任主机", .app)
                self.currentLANIP = ip
                if self.settings.keepAlive { self.restart() }
            }
        }
        monitor.start(queue: DispatchQueue(label: "dsh.path"))
        pathMonitor = monitor
    }

    // MARK: - Output handling

    private func handle(line: String, stream: LogStream) {
        logs.append(line, stream)
        guard localURL == nil else { return }
        if let url = Self.extractURL(from: line, port: effectivePort) {
            adopt(url: url)
        }
    }

    private func adopt(url: URL) {
        localURL = url
        if let items = URLComponents(url: url, resolvingAgainstBaseURL: false)?.queryItems,
           let token = items.first(where: { $0.name == "token" })?.value {
            launchToken = token
        }
        if settings.lanEnabled, let ip = currentLANIP {
            var components = URLComponents(url: url, resolvingAgainstBaseURL: false)
            components?.host = ip
            components?.port = settings.lanPort
            lanURL = components?.url
        }
        setState(.running)
        logs.append("部署成功 · 本地链接 \(url.absoluteString)", .app)
        DispatchQueue.main.asyncAfter(deadline: .now() + 3) { self.refreshPluginHealth() }
        DispatchQueue.main.asyncAfter(deadline: .now() + 12) { self.refreshToolErrors() }
        if let lan = lanURL { logs.append("部署成功 · 局域网链接 \(lan.absoluteString)", .app) }
    }

    static func extractURL(from line: String, port: Int) -> URL? {
        let pattern = "https?://[^\\s\"'`<>]+"
        guard let regex = try? NSRegularExpression(pattern: pattern) else { return nil }
        let range = NSRange(line.startIndex..., in: line)
        let matches = regex.matches(in: line, range: range).compactMap { match -> String? in
            guard let r = Range(match.range, in: line) else { return nil }
            return String(line[r])
        }
        let cleaned = matches.map { $0.trimmingCharacters(in: CharacterSet(charactersIn: ".,);")) }
        if let withToken = cleaned.first(where: { $0.contains("token=") }) { return URL(string: withToken) }
        if let onPort = cleaned.first(where: { $0.contains("127.0.0.1:\(port)") || $0.contains("localhost:\(port)") }) {
            return URL(string: onPort)
        }
        return nil
    }

    private func schedulePoll() {
        pollTimer?.invalidate()
        pollTimer = Timer.scheduledTimer(withTimeInterval: 2.0, repeats: true) { [weak self] timer in
            guard let self = self else { timer.invalidate(); return }
            if self.state == .running { timer.invalidate(); return }
            if let deadline = self.startDeadline, Date() > deadline {
                timer.invalidate()
                self.fail("启动超时（150 秒内没有拿到 Web UI 地址）。请到「日志」查看 node 输出，或点「调试 → 复制诊断文本」。")
                self.process.terminate(force: true)
                self.process.shutdown()
                self.pid = 0
                return
            }
            self.probeHTTP()
        }
    }

    private func probeHTTP() {
        guard let url = URL(string: "http://127.0.0.1:\(effectivePort)/") else { return }
        var request = URLRequest(url: url)
        request.timeoutInterval = 3
        URLSession.shared.dataTask(with: request) { [weak self] _, response, _ in
            guard let self = self else { return }
            guard let http = response as? HTTPURLResponse else { return }
            if http.statusCode == 200 || http.statusCode == 401 || http.statusCode == 403 {
                DispatchQueue.main.async {
                    if self.localURL == nil {
                        self.logs.append("端口 \(self.effectivePort) 已响应（HTTP \(http.statusCode)），但还没解析到带 token 的链接；请查看日志中的 URL 行", .app)
                    }
                }
            }
        }.resume()
    }

    private func handleExit(_ code: Int32) {
        let wasRunning = (state == .running)
        pid = 0
        UserDefaults.standard.removeObject(forKey: "childPid")
        startedAt = nil
        localURL = nil
        lanURL = nil
        pollTimer?.invalidate(); pollTimer = nil
        relay?.stop(); relay = nil
        if stoppingIntentionally {
            setState(.idle)
            return
        }
        // If the runtime rejects --jitless (older builds), retry once without it
        // instead of dying on a flag mismatch.
        let recent = logs.lines.suffix(12).map { $0.text }.joined(separator: "\n")
        let flagProblem = recent.contains("bad option") || recent.contains("--jitless") && recent.contains("not allowed")
        if flagProblem && !jitlessFallbackUsed && Date().timeIntervalSince(launchTime) < 20 {
            jitlessFallbackUsed = true
            logs.append("检测到 --jitless 不被支持，去掉该参数重试", .app)
            let stripped = settings.extraNodeFlags
                .split(separator: " ").map(String.init).filter { $0 != "--jitless" }
            settings.extraNodeFlags = stripped.joined(separator: " ")
            DispatchQueue.main.asyncAfter(deadline: .now() + 1) { self.start() }
            return
        }
        process.flushPending()
        lastError = "harness 进程退出（code \(code)）"
        logs.append("harness 进程退出，code=\(code)", .err)
        if settings.keepAlive && autoRestartCount < 3 {
            autoRestartCount += 1
            setState(.failed("进程退出，第 \(autoRestartCount) 次自动重启…"))
            DispatchQueue.main.asyncAfter(deadline: .now() + 2) { self.start() }
        } else {
            setState(.failed("进程退出（code \(code)）"))
            if wasRunning { logs.append("已达自动重启上限，请检查日志", .err) }
        }
    }

    // MARK: - Diagnostics

    func runDiagnostics() {
        var results: [CheckResult] = []
        let fm = FileManager.default
        let resolved = nodeExecutable ?? Paths.nodeBinary
        results.append(CheckResult(title: "App 内 Node 二进制",
                                   ok: fm.fileExists(atPath: Paths.nodeBinary.path),
                                   detail: Paths.nodeBinary.path
                                         + (fm.isExecutableFile(atPath: Paths.nodeBinary.path) ? "" : "（缺可执行位，需复制修复）")))
        results.append(CheckResult(title: "实际使用的 Node",
                                   ok: fm.isExecutableFile(atPath: resolved.path),
                                   detail: resolved.path))
        results.append(CheckResult(title: "DeepSeek 运行树（官方源码构建）",
                                   ok: fm.fileExists(atPath: Paths.dshBin.path),
                                   detail: Paths.dshBin.path))
        let (vcode, vout) = NodeProcess.run(executable: resolved.path,
                                            args: ["--version"],
                                            environment: buildEnvironment(),
                                            timeout: 30)
        results.append(CheckResult(title: "node --version", ok: vcode == 0, detail: vout))
        let (jcode, jout) = NodeProcess.run(executable: resolved.path,
                                            args: ["--jitless", "-e", "process.stdout.write('jitless-ok')"],
                                            environment: buildEnvironment(),
                                            timeout: 40)
        results.append(CheckResult(title: "--jitless 解释器可用",
                                   ok: jcode == 0 && jout.contains("jitless-ok"),
                                   detail: jout.isEmpty ? "exit \(jcode)" : jout))
        let (_, evalOut) = NodeProcess.run(executable: resolved.path,
                                           args: ["-e", "require('node:sqlite');process.stdout.write('sqlite-ok')"],
                                           environment: buildEnvironment(),
                                           timeout: 40)
        results.append(CheckResult(title: "内置模块加载（node:sqlite）",
                                   ok: evalOut.contains("sqlite-ok"),
                                   detail: evalOut))

        results.append(CheckResult(title: "DSH_HOME 可写",
                                   ok: isWritable(Paths.dshHome),
                                   detail: Paths.dshHome.path))
        results.append(CheckResult(title: "工作区根目录可写",
                                   ok: isWritable(Paths.workspaces),
                                   detail: Paths.workspaces.path))
        let probePort = state == .running ? effectivePort : settings.port
        results.append(CheckResult(title: "本机端口 \(probePort)",
                                   ok: portFree(probePort) || state == .running,
                                   detail: state == .running ? "运行中（占用属正常）" : (portFree(probePort) ? "空闲" : "被占用")))
        results.append(CheckResult(title: "局域网地址",
                                   ok: NetworkInfo.primaryIPv4() != nil,
                                   detail: NetworkInfo.primaryIPv4() ?? "未连接 Wi‑Fi"))
        results.append(CheckResult(title: "本地链接",
                                   ok: localURL != nil,
                                   detail: localURL?.absoluteString ?? "尚未取得（部署成功后自动填充）"))
        let spawnHint: String
        if pid > 0 { spawnHint = "已成功 spawn node (pid \(pid))" }
        else if vcode == -1 { spawnHint = "被拒绝（EPERM）：App 处于沙盒中，请用 TrollStore 安装" }
        else if vcode == -9 || vcode == -11 { spawnHint = "被系统杀死（signal \(-vcode)）：签名不被接受，请装 dsh-ios-ldid.ipa" }
        else if vcode == 0 { spawnHint = "可以 spawn 子进程" }
        else { spawnHint = "失败（exit \(vcode)）" }
        results.append(CheckResult(title: "越狱环境（可 spawn 子进程）", ok: vcode == 0, detail: spawnHint))
        checks = results
    }

    private func isWritable(_ url: URL) -> Bool {
        let probe = url.appendingPathComponent(".write-test")
        do {
            try "ok".write(to: probe, atomically: true, encoding: .utf8)
            try FileManager.default.removeItem(at: probe)
            return true
        } catch { return false }
    }

    private func portFree(_ port: Int) -> Bool {
        let fd = socket(AF_INET, SOCK_STREAM, 0)
        guard fd >= 0 else { return true }
        defer { close(fd) }
        var addr = sockaddr_in()
        addr.sin_family = sa_family_t(AF_INET)
        addr.sin_port = in_port_t(UInt16(port).bigEndian)
        addr.sin_addr.s_addr = inet_addr("127.0.0.1")
        var one: Int32 = 1
        setsockopt(fd, SOL_SOCKET, SO_REUSEADDR, &one, socklen_t(MemoryLayout<Int32>.size))
        let result = withUnsafePointer(to: &addr) { ptr -> Int32 in
            ptr.withMemoryRebound(to: sockaddr.self, capacity: 1) { bind(fd, $0, socklen_t(MemoryLayout<sockaddr_in>.size)) }
        }
        return result == 0
    }

    /// Verifies that reporting `darwin` actually lets both native modules load,
    /// before any of that is enabled — a wrong platform report must never be able
    /// to break the boot.
    private func runPlatformPreflight(executable: URL) {
        guard platformSpoofUsable == nil else { return }
        guard let preload = try? RuntimeShims.ensure() else {
            platformSpoofUsable = false
            logs.append("原生兼容预检：跳过（无法写入预载脚本）", .err)
            return
        }
        let (code, output) = NodeProcess.run(
            executable: executable.path,
            args: ["--require", preload.path, RuntimeShims.preflightFile.path, Paths.dshTree.path],
            environment: buildEnvironment(),
            timeout: 25)
        let text = output.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
        if code == 0 && text.contains("preflight-ok") {
            platformSpoofUsable = true
            logs.append("原生兼容预检通过（\(text)）：flock 与 require-builtin 均可用", .app)
        } else {
            platformSpoofUsable = false
            logs.append("原生兼容预检未通过（exit \(code)）：\(text)。将以 ios 平台启动，发送消息时 flock 会报错。", .err)
        }
    }

    /// Validates a patch set with the launcher itself (`--dump-config`), so a
    /// generated overlay can never stop the harness from booting.
    private func overlayUsable(_ patches: [URL]) -> Bool {
        let key = patches.map { $0.lastPathComponent }.joined(separator: "+")
        if let cached = overlayCache[key] { return cached }
        var args = ["--jitless", Paths.dshBin.path, "--profile", "web"]
        for patch in patches {
            args.append("--patch")
            args.append(patch.path)
        }
        args.append("--dump-config")
        let (code, output) = NodeProcess.run(executable: (nodeExecutable ?? Paths.nodeBinary).path,
                                             args: args,
                                             environment: buildEnvironment(),
                                             timeout: 45)
        let ok = code == 0
        overlayCache[key] = ok
        if ok {
            logs.append("overlay 校验通过：\(patches.map { $0.lastPathComponent }.joined(separator: ", "))", .app)
        } else {
            logs.append("overlay 校验失败（exit \(code)）：\(output.suffix(300))", .err)
        }
        return ok
    }

    /// `bash` is not on PATH inside this app (the jailbreak bootstrap keeps it
    /// under /var/jb, and the harness may scrub the environment). Expose whatever
    /// shell the device has through `Documents/bin`, which is first on PATH.
    private func prepareShells() {
        let fm = FileManager.default
        try? fm.createDirectory(at: Paths.binDir, withIntermediateDirectories: true)

        // Ships two POSIX shells; whichever actually runs here gets exposed as
        // `sh`/`bash` on PATH. dash segfaults on some devices while a control
        // program runs, so the choice is made by an actual self test, not by
        // which file we happened to ship.
        try? fm.createDirectory(at: Paths.binDir, withIntermediateDirectories: true)
        var candidates: [URL] = []

        // In-process shell: the sandbox denies fork(2), so any real shell can
        // only run its own builtins. This one implements the commands itself in
        // Node and runs pipelines as strings, so it needs no child processes.
        // `sh-launch` and the SEA build were both attempts to reach node from a
        // child process; the sandbox denies that ("Operation not permitted"), so
        // only self-contained shells remain. sh-c (fork-free, written in C) is
        // first because it needs no child process at all.

        for name in ["sh-c", "sh-mksh", "sh-dash"] {
            let bundled = Paths.bundleRuntime.appendingPathComponent("bin/\(name)")
            if fm.fileExists(atPath: bundled.path) {
                candidates.append(bundled)
                // A container copy covers installs that strip the bundle's exec bit.
                let copy = Paths.binDir.appendingPathComponent(name)
                let size = ((try? fm.attributesOfItem(atPath: bundled.path))?[.size] as? NSNumber)?.intValue ?? -1
                let installed = ((try? fm.attributesOfItem(atPath: copy.path))?[.size] as? NSNumber)?.intValue
                if installed != size || !fm.isExecutableFile(atPath: copy.path) {
                    try? fm.removeItem(at: copy)
                    if let data = try? Data(contentsOf: bundled) { try? data.write(to: copy) }
                    try? fm.setAttributes([.posixPermissions: 0o755], ofItemAtPath: copy.path)
                }
                candidates.append(copy)
            }
        }

        for candidate in candidates {
            let (code, output) = NodeProcess.run(executable: candidate.path,
                                                 args: ["-c", "echo shell-ok"],
                                                 environment: buildEnvironment(),
                                                 timeout: 20)
            if code == 0 && output.contains("shell-ok") {
                let sh = Paths.binDir.appendingPathComponent("sh")
                let bash = Paths.binDir.appendingPathComponent("bash")
                try? fm.removeItem(at: sh)
                try? fm.createSymbolicLink(atPath: sh.path, withDestinationPath: candidate.path)
                try? fm.removeItem(at: bash)
                try? fm.createSymbolicLink(atPath: bash.path, withDestinationPath: candidate.path)
                logs.append("shell: 使用 \(candidate.lastPathComponent)（\(candidate.path)）并自检通过", .app)
                return
            }
            let why = code < 0 ? "被信号 \(-code) 终止" : "exit \(code)"
            logs.append("shell: \(candidate.lastPathComponent) 自检失败（\(why)）：\(output)", .err)
        }
        logs.append("shell: 内置 shell 都不工作，回退到系统 shell 探测", .err)

        let bashCandidates = ["/var/jb/usr/bin/bash", "/var/jb/bin/bash", "/usr/bin/bash", "/bin/bash",
                              "/var/jb/usr/local/bin/bash"]
        let shCandidates = ["/var/jb/usr/bin/sh", "/var/jb/bin/sh", "/usr/bin/sh", "/bin/sh", "/var/jb/usr/bin/dash"]

        func firstExecutable(_ candidates: [String]) -> String? {
            candidates.first { fm.isExecutableFile(atPath: $0) }
        }
        func link(_ name: String, to target: String) {
            let url = Paths.binDir.appendingPathComponent(name)
            if let existing = try? fm.destinationOfSymbolicLink(atPath: url.path), existing == target { return }
            try? fm.removeItem(at: url)
            try? fm.createSymbolicLink(atPath: url.path, withDestinationPath: target)
        }

        if let bash = firstExecutable(bashCandidates) {
            link("bash", to: bash)
            logs.append("shell: bash → \(bash)", .app)
        } else if let sh = firstExecutable(shCandidates) {
            // No bash on this bootstrap: expose the POSIX shell under both names
            // so `spawn bash -c ...` at least starts (bash-only syntax may fail).
            link("sh", to: sh)
            link("bash", to: sh)
            logs.append("shell: 只有 POSIX shell（\(sh)），已同时映射为 bash（仅 -c 兼容）", .err)
        } else {
            logs.append("shell: 设备上找不到 bash/sh，bash 工具将报 ENOENT", .err)
        }
        if let sh = firstExecutable(shCandidates) { link("sh", to: sh) }
    }

    /// Control experiment: if a five-line program also fails, no external
    /// binary can run here and the terminal simply cannot work on this device.
    private func diagnoseExecutables() {
        let hello = Paths.bundleRuntime.appendingPathComponent("bin/hello")
        guard FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: hello.path) else { return }
        let (code, output) = NodeProcess.run(executable: hello.path,
                                             args: [],
                                             environment: buildEnvironment(),
                                             timeout: 10)
        if code == 0 && output.contains("hello-ok") {
            logs.append("exec 自检：最小可执行程序可以运行 → 问题只在 shell 二进制本身", .app)
        } else {
            let why = code < 0 ? "被信号 \(-code) 终止" : "exit \(code)"
            logs.append("exec 自检：最小可执行程序也失败（\(why)）→ 这台设备不允许执行外部二进制，终端无解", .err)
        }
    }

    /// The in-process shell ships as a real executable: iOS refuses to exec a
    /// script ("Operation not permitted") but happily runs a signed Mach-O from
    /// the bundle, and Node's SEA build gives us exactly that (shell logic baked
    /// into a copy of the runtime, so nothing is forked).
    private func installInProcessShell() -> URL? {
        // SEA build is no longer shipped (it aborted on the self-built runtime);
        // the launcher above replaces it.
        nil
    }

    /// Publishes the app's folders into the harness registry. The harness reads
    /// that file once at startup, so a live server must be restarted to see new
    /// workspaces — done automatically when `restartIfRunning` is set.
    func syncWorkspaces(restartIfRunning: Bool) {
        let registry = Paths.dshHome.appendingPathComponent("storages/workspace.json")
        do {
            let changed = try WorkspaceRegistryWriter.sync(root: Paths.workspaces, registryFile: registry)
            guard changed > 0 else { return }
            if changed >= 1000 {
                logs.append("已修正 harness 里工作区路径为 realpath 形式（\(changed - 1000) 条）", .app)
            } else {
                logs.append("已把 \(changed) 个 App 工作区登记到 harness", .app)
            }
            if restartIfRunning, state == .running {
                logs.append("为了让网页立刻看到新工作区，自动重启 harness", .app)
                restart()
            }
        } catch {
            logs.append("工作区登记失败：\(error.localizedDescription)", .err)
        }
    }

    // MARK: - Process hygiene

    /// Kills the child recorded by a previous app run, so a relaunch never has
    /// two harnesses fighting over the same port.
    private func killStaleProcess() {
        let recorded = UserDefaults.standard.integer(forKey: "childPid")
        guard recorded > 0 else { return }
        if kill(pid_t(recorded), 0) == 0 {
            logs.append("发现上次遗留的 harness 进程 pid=\(recorded)，先结束它", .app)
            kill(-pid_t(recorded), SIGTERM)
            kill(pid_t(recorded), SIGTERM)
            for _ in 0..<20 {
                if kill(pid_t(recorded), 0) != 0 { break }
                Thread.sleep(forTimeInterval: 0.1)
            }
            if kill(pid_t(recorded), 0) == 0 {
                kill(-pid_t(recorded), SIGKILL)
                kill(pid_t(recorded), SIGKILL)
                Thread.sleep(forTimeInterval: 0.3)
            }
        }
        UserDefaults.standard.removeObject(forKey: "childPid")
    }

    private func killChildNow() {
        guard process.pid > 0 else { return }
        process.terminate(force: true)
        UserDefaults.standard.removeObject(forKey: "childPid")
    }

    private func nextFreePort(from start: Int, avoiding reserved: [Int] = []) -> Int? {
        for candidate in (start + 1)...(start + 40) where candidate < 65000 {
            if reserved.contains(candidate) { continue }
            if portFree(candidate) { return candidate }
        }
        return nil
    }

    /// Kills every process whose executable lives inside this app bundle —
    /// i.e. harness instances left behind by a previous install or an older
    /// build that did not record a pid. libproc is resolved with dlsym so the
    /// binary needs no link-time dependency on it.
    private func killOrphanRuntimes() {
        typealias ListPidsFn = @convention(c) (UnsafeMutableRawPointer?, Int32) -> Int32
        typealias PidPathFn = @convention(c) (Int32, UnsafeMutableRawPointer?, UInt32) -> Int32
        guard let handle = dlopen(nil, RTLD_NOW),
              let listSym = dlsym(handle, "proc_listallpids"),
              let pathSym = dlsym(handle, "proc_pidpath") else { return }
        let listPids = unsafeBitCast(listSym, to: ListPidsFn.self)
        let pidPath = unsafeBitCast(pathSym, to: PidPathFn.self)
        let selfPid = getpid()

        var capacity = 4096
        var pids = [pid_t](repeating: 0, count: capacity)
        let written = pids.withUnsafeMutableBytes { buffer -> Int32 in
            listPids(buffer.baseAddress, Int32(buffer.count))
        }
        guard written > 0 else { return }
        let count = min(Int(written) / MemoryLayout<pid_t>.size, capacity)
        capacity = count

        let bundleRoot = Bundle.main.bundleURL.standardizedFileURL.path
        var killed = 0
        for index in 0..<capacity {
            let candidate = pids[index]
            guard candidate > 1, candidate != selfPid else { continue }
            var path = [CChar](repeating: 0, count: 4096)
            let length = pidPath(candidate, &path, UInt32(path.count))
            guard length > 0 else { continue }
            let executable = String(cString: path)
            guard executable.hasPrefix(bundleRoot), executable.hasSuffix("Runtime/node") else { continue }
            kill(candidate, SIGKILL)
            killed += 1
        }
        if killed > 0 {
            logs.append("清理了 \(killed) 个遗留的 harness 进程", .app)
            Thread.sleep(forTimeInterval: 0.5)
        }
    }

    private func waitForPortFree(_ port: Int, timeout: TimeInterval) -> Bool {
        let deadline = Date().addingTimeInterval(timeout)
        while Date() < deadline {
            if portFree(port) { return true }
            Thread.sleep(forTimeInterval: 0.25)
        }
        return portFree(port)
    }

    /// Tool failures happen inside the harness, so neither the app log nor the
    /// WebView console sees them: they are only written into the session log.
    /// Scan the newest session files and lift the error text out.
    func refreshToolErrors() {
        let root = Paths.dshHome.appendingPathComponent("sessions", isDirectory: true)
        let fm = FileManager.default
        guard let walker = fm.enumerator(at: root,
                                         includingPropertiesForKeys: [.contentModificationDateKey],
                                         options: [.skipsHiddenFiles]) else { return }
        var files: [URL] = []
        for case let url as URL in walker where url.pathExtension == "jsonl" {
            files.append(url)
        }
        let newest = files.sorted {
            let a = (try? $0.resourceValues(forKeys: [.contentModificationDateKey]).contentModificationDate) ?? .distantPast
            let b = (try? $1.resourceValues(forKeys: [.contentModificationDateKey]).contentModificationDate) ?? .distantPast
            return a > b
        }.prefix(3)

        var found: [String] = []
        for file in newest {
            guard let text = try? String(contentsOf: file, encoding: .utf8) else { continue }
            for line in text.split(separator: "\n").reversed() where found.count < 8 {
                guard line.contains("rror") else { continue }
                guard let range = line.range(of: "\"error\"") ?? line.range(of: "isError") else { continue }
                let start = line.index(range.lowerBound, offsetBy: -80, limitedBy: line.startIndex) ?? line.startIndex
                let end = line.index(start, offsetBy: 320, limitedBy: line.endIndex) ?? line.endIndex
                let snippet = line[start..<end].replacingOccurrences(of: "\\n", with: " ")
                found.append(String(snippet))
            }
        }
        toolErrors = Array(Set(found)).sorted()
        if !toolErrors.isEmpty {
            logs.append("工具错误：\(toolErrors.count) 条（见「调试」页）", .err)
        }
    }

    // MARK: - Harness health

    func newestStartupLog() -> URL? {
        let dir = Paths.dshHome.appendingPathComponent("logs", isDirectory: true)
        guard let files = try? FileManager.default.contentsOfDirectory(at: dir,
                                                                     includingPropertiesForKeys: [.contentModificationDateKey],
                                                                     options: [.skipsHiddenFiles]) else { return nil }
        return files.filter { $0.lastPathComponent.hasPrefix("startup-") }
            .sorted { a, b in
                let da = (try? a.resourceValues(forKeys: [.contentModificationDateKey]).contentModificationDate) ?? .distantPast
                let db = (try? b.resourceValues(forKeys: [.contentModificationDateKey]).contentModificationDate) ?? .distantPast
                return da > db
            }
            .first
    }

    /// Reads the harness' own startup report and turns it into a short list:
    /// the Web UI degrades silently when a plugin fails, so this is the only
    /// place the reason is written down.
    func refreshPluginHealth() {
        guard let url = newestStartupLog(),
              let text = try? String(contentsOf: url, encoding: .utf8) else {
            return
        }
        startupLogPath = url.path
        let lines = text.split(separator: "\n", omittingEmptySubsequences: false).map(String.init)
        var issues: [String] = []

        for (index, raw) in lines.enumerated() {
            let line = raw.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces)
            guard line.hasPrefix("Package: ") else { continue }
            let package = String(line.dropFirst("Package: ".count))
            var id = package
            var back = index - 1
            while back >= 0, lines[back].trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces).isEmpty { back -= 1 }
            if back >= 0 { id = lines[back].trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces) }
            var reason = "加载失败"
            if index + 1 < lines.count, lines[index + 1].contains("failed to import") {
                reason = "模块导入失败"
            }
            issues.append("\(id) — \(reason)（\(package)）")
        }

        if let tableStart = lines.firstIndex(where: { $0.contains("did not activate") }) {
            for raw in lines[(tableStart + 1)...].prefix(12) {
                let line = raw.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces)
                if line.isEmpty || line.hasPrefix("Full diagnostics") { break }
                if line.hasPrefix("Plugin") { continue }
                issues.append("未激活：\(line)")
            }
        }

        let unique = Array(Set(issues)).sorted()
        if !unique.isEmpty {
            pluginIssues = unique
            logs.append("插件健康：\(unique.count) 项异常，详见 startup 日志", .err)
        }
    }

    /// Plain text diagnostics, small enough to paste into a chat: the app log
    /// plus the harness' own startup reports (the only place plugin failures
    /// are written down).
    func diagnosticsText() -> String {
        var body = """
        DeepSeek for iOS 诊断包
        App 版本: \(Bundle.main.infoDictionary?["CFBundleShortVersionString"] as? String ?? "?")
        上游 commit: \(UpstreamInfo.commit)
        状态: \(state.label)
        Node: \(nodeVersion) @ \(nodeExecutable?.path ?? "-")
        PID: \(pid)
        本机链接: \(localURL?.absoluteString ?? "-")
        局域网链接: \(lanURL?.absoluteString ?? "-")
        插件异常: \(pluginIssues.isEmpty ? "无" : pluginIssues.joined(separator: " | "))
        \(pluginIssues.isEmpty ? "" : "")

        \(toolErrors.isEmpty ? "" : "===== 工具错误（会话记录）=====\n" + toolErrors.joined(separator: "\n---\n") + "\n\n")
        \(CrashLog.tail().isEmpty ? "" : "===== 崩溃日志 =====\n" + CrashLog.tail() + "\n")

        ===== 应用日志 =====
        \(logs.dump())
        """
        let dir = Paths.dshHome.appendingPathComponent("logs", isDirectory: true)
        if let files = try? FileManager.default.contentsOfDirectory(at: dir,
                                                                   includingPropertiesForKeys: [.contentModificationDateKey],
                                                                   options: [.skipsHiddenFiles]) {
            let recent = files.filter { $0.lastPathComponent.hasPrefix("startup-") }
                .sorted { a, b in
                    let da = (try? a.resourceValues(forKeys: [.contentModificationDateKey]).contentModificationDate) ?? .distantPast
                    let db = (try? b.resourceValues(forKeys: [.contentModificationDateKey]).contentModificationDate) ?? .distantPast
                    return da > db
                }
                .prefix(3)
            for file in recent {
                body += "\n\n===== harness 启动日志：\(file.lastPathComponent) =====\n"
                let text = (try? String(contentsOf: file, encoding: .utf8)) ?? "(无法读取)"
                // Keep the paste manageable: the tail carries the failure report.
                body += text.count > 20000 ? String(text.suffix(20000)) : text
            }
        }
        return body
    }

    /// One shareable file version of {@link diagnosticsText}.
    func exportDiagnostics() -> URL? {
        let name = "dsh-diagnostics-\(Int(Date().timeIntervalSince1970)).txt"
        let url = Paths.logsDir.appendingPathComponent(name)
        do {
            try diagnosticsText().write(to: url, atomically: true, encoding: .utf8)
            return url
        } catch { return nil }
    }

    // MARK: - Helpers

    func exportLogs() -> URL? {
        let name = "dsh-log-\(Int(Date().timeIntervalSince1970)).txt"
        let url = Paths.logsDir.appendingPathComponent(name)
        var body = "DeepSeek for iOS 日志\n上游 commit: \(UpstreamInfo.commit)\n状态: \(state.label)\nPID: \(pid)\n本地链接: \(localURL?.absoluteString ?? "-")\n局域网链接: \(lanURL?.absoluteString ?? "-")\nNode: \(nodeVersion)\n---\n"
        body += logs.dump()
        do {
            try body.write(to: url, atomically: true, encoding: .utf8)
            return url
        } catch {
            return nil
        }
    }

    private func setState(_ new: HarnessState) {
        DispatchQueue.main.async { self.state = new }
    }

    private func fail(_ message: String) {
        lastError = message
        logs.append("错误：\(message)", .err)
        setState(.failed(message))
        relay?.stop(); relay = nil
    }
}

enum RuntimeError: LocalizedError {
    case missing(String)
    var errorDescription: String? {
        switch self {
        case let .missing(text): return text
        }
    }
}

enum UpstreamInfo {
    static var commit: String {
        let bundled = Paths.bundleRuntime.appendingPathComponent("UPSTREAM_COMMIT")
        if let text = try? String(contentsOf: bundled, encoding: .utf8) {
            let trimmed = text.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
            if !trimmed.isEmpty { return trimmed }
        }
        if let declared = Bundle.main.infoDictionary?["DshUpstreamCommit"] as? String, !declared.isEmpty {
            return declared
        }
        return "fcc0ad4"
    }
    static var commitShort: String { String(commit.prefix(7)) }
}

/// Keeps the process alive when the app leaves the foreground by playing a
/// silent loop. Off by default? No: on by default, and switchable in settings.
final class BackgroundKeeper {
    private var player: AVAudioPlayer?

    func start() {
        guard player == nil else { if player?.isPlaying == false { player?.play() }; return }
        do {
            let session = AVAudioSession.sharedInstance()
            try session.setCategory(.playback, mode: .default, options: [.mixWithOthers])
            try session.setActive(true)
            let url = try writeSilence()
            let player = try AVAudioPlayer(contentsOf: url)
            player.numberOfLoops = -1
            player.volume = 0
            player.play()
            self.player = player
        } catch {
            player = nil
        }
    }

    func stop() {
        player?.stop()
        player = nil
        try? AVAudioSession.sharedInstance().setActive(false, options: [.notifyOthersOnDeactivation])
    }

    private func writeSilence() throws -> URL {
        let url = Paths.tmp.appendingPathComponent("silence.wav")
        if FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: url.path) { return url }
        try FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: Paths.tmp, withIntermediateDirectories: true)
        let sampleRate: UInt32 = 8000
        let seconds: UInt32 = 1
        let dataSize = sampleRate * seconds * 2
        var data = Data()
        func le32(_ v: UInt32) { var x = v.littleEndian; withUnsafeBytes(of: &x) { data.append(contentsOf: $0) } }
        func le16(_ v: UInt16) { var x = v.littleEndian; withUnsafeBytes(of: &x) { data.append(contentsOf: $0) } }
        data.append(contentsOf: Array("RIFF".utf8)); le32(36 + dataSize)
        data.append(contentsOf: Array("WAVEfmt ".utf8)); le32(16); le16(1); le16(1)
        le32(sampleRate); le32(sampleRate * 2); le16(2); le16(16)
        data.append(contentsOf: Array("data".utf8)); le32(dataSize)
        data.append(Data(repeating: 0, count: Int(dataSize)))
        try data.write(to: url)
        return url
    }
}
```

```swift
import SwiftUI

// MARK: - Root

struct RootView: View {

            LogsView().tabItem { Label("日志", systemImage: "text.alignleft") }.tag(2)

// MARK: - Home

struct HomeView: View {
    @EnvironmentObject var harness: HarnessController
    @EnvironmentObject var settings: AppSettings

    var body: some View {
        NavigationView {
            ScrollView {
                VStack(spacing: 16) {
                    statusCard
                    actions
                    if harness.state == .running {
                        linksCard
                        usageCard
                    }
                    if case let .failed(message) = harness.state {
                        failureCard(message)
                    }
                    if !harness.pluginIssues.isEmpty {
                        pluginIssueCard
                    }
                    runtimeCard
                }
                .padding(16)
            }
            .background(Color(UIColor.systemGroupedBackground).ignoresSafeArea())
            .navigationTitle("DeepSeek · 本地部署")
        }
        .navigationViewStyle(.stack)
    }

    private var statusCard: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 12) {
            HStack(spacing: 10) {
                ZStack {
                    Circle().fill(statusColor.opacity(0.15)).frame(width: 44, height: 44)
                    Image(systemName: statusIcon)
                        .font(.system(size: 20, weight: .semibold))
                        .foregroundColor(statusColor)

                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 2) {

                metric("Node", harness.nodeVersion)
                Divider().frame(height: 26)
                metric("端口", "\(harness.effectivePort > 0 ? harness.effectivePort : settings.port)")
            }
            .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
        }
        .padding(16)
        .background(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 16).fill(Color(UIColor.secondarySystemGroupedBackground)))
    }

    private var actions: some View {
        VStack(spacing: 10) {
            if harness.state == .running {
                HStack(spacing: 10) {
                    Button(action: { harness.restart() }) {
                        Label("重启", systemImage: "arrow.clockwise")
                            .frame(maxWidth: .infinity).padding(.vertical, 14)
                    }
                    .buttonStyle(FilledButtonStyle(color: Color(red: 0.29, green: 0.42, blue: 0.94)))
                    Button(action: { harness.stop() }) {
                        Label("停止", systemImage: "stop.fill")
                            .frame(maxWidth: .infinity).padding(.vertical, 14)
                    }
                    .buttonStyle(FilledButtonStyle(color: .gray))
                }
            } else {
                Button(action: { harness.deploy() }) {
                    Label("一键部署并启动", systemImage: "play.fill")
                        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity).padding(.vertical, 16)
                }
                .buttonStyle(FilledButtonStyle(color: Color(red: 0.29, green: 0.42, blue: 0.94)))
                .disabled(harness.state.isBusy)
            }
        }
    }

    private var linksCard: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 14) {
            Text("交付链接").font(.headline)
            if let local = harness.localURL {
                linkRow(title: "本机链接", url: local, tint: .blue)
            }
            if let lan = harness.lanURL {
                linkRow(title: "局域网链接", url: lan, tint: .green)
            } else if settings.lanEnabled {
                Text("未取得局域网地址：请确认已连接 Wi‑Fi。").font(.caption).foregroundColor(.secondary)
            } else {
                Text("局域网访问已在「调试」里关闭。").font(.caption).foregroundColor(.secondary)
            }
        }
        .padding(16)
        .background(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 16).fill(Color(UIColor.secondarySystemGroupedBackground)))

        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 8) {
            Text("下一步").font(.headline)
            Text("1. 打开「网页」页 → 设置 (Settings) → Models → 填入 DeepSeek API Key\n2. 点 Choose workspace → 选择 Workspaces 下的目录\n3. 开始对话")
                .font(.footnote)
                .foregroundColor(.secondary)
            Text("模型由 DeepSeek 官方 API 提供，harness 本身完全跑在本机。")
                .font(.caption2).foregroundColor(.secondary)
        }
        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, alignment: .leading)
        .padding(16)
        .background(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 16).fill(Color(UIColor.secondarySystemGroupedBackground)))
    }

    private func failureCard(_ message: String) -> some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 8) {
            Label("部署失败", systemImage: "exclamationmark.triangle.fill")
                .font(.headline).foregroundColor(.red)
            Text(message).font(.footnote)
            Text("到「日志」页查看 node 的原始输出，或在「调试」页运行自检。")
                .font(.caption).foregroundColor(.secondary)
        }
        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, alignment: .leading)
        .padding(16)
        .background(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 16).fill(Color.red.opacity(0.08)))
    }

    private var pluginIssueCard: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 8) {
            Label("部分插件未激活（\(harness.pluginIssues.count)）", systemImage: "exclamationmark.circle.fill")
                .font(.headline).foregroundColor(.orange)
            ForEach(harness.pluginIssues.prefix(6), id: \.self) { issue in
                Text("• " + issue).font(.caption2).foregroundColor(.secondary)
            }
            if harness.pluginIssues.count > 6 {
                Text("…还有 \(harness.pluginIssues.count - 6) 项").font(.caption2).foregroundColor(.secondary)
            }
            Button {
                UIPasteboard.general.string = harness.diagnosticsText()
                UIImpactFeedbackGenerator(style: .light).impactOccurred()
            } label: {
                Label("复制插件诊断（含 harness 启动日志）", systemImage: "doc.on.clipboard")
                    .font(.subheadline)
            }
            Text("这些插件对应的功能会显示为不可用。上面的按钮会把 harness 自己的启动报告复制出来。")
                .font(.caption2).foregroundColor(.secondary)
        }
        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, alignment: .leading)
        .padding(16)
        .background(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 16).fill(Color.orange.opacity(0.10)))
    }

    private var runtimeCard: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 6) {
            Text("运行环境").font(.headline)
            Text("• DeepSeek：官方源码 \(UpstreamInfo.commitShort)，零修改")
            Text("• Node.js：iPhoneOS arm64，解释器模式（--jitless）")
            Text("• 数据目录：Documents/dsh-home、Documents/Workspaces")
        }
        .font(.caption)
        .foregroundColor(.secondary)
        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, alignment: .leading)
        .padding(16)
        .background(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 16).fill(Color(UIColor.secondarySystemGroupedBackground)))
    }

    private func linkRow(title: String, url: URL, tint: Color) -> some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 6) {
            HStack {
                Text(title).font(.subheadline).bold().foregroundColor(tint)
                Spacer()
                Button(action: {
                    UIPasteboard.general.string = url.absoluteString
                    UIImpactFeedbackGenerator(style: .light).impactOccurred()
                }) { Image(systemName: "doc.on.doc") }
                Button(action: { UIApplication.shared.open(url) }) { Image(systemName: "arrow.up.right.square") }
            }
            Text(url.absoluteString)
                .font(.system(.caption, design: .monospaced))
                .textSelection(.enabled)
                .lineLimit(3)
        }
    }

    private func metric(_ title: String, _ value: String) -> some View {
        VStack(spacing: 2) {
            Text(value).font(.system(.footnote, design: .monospaced)).lineLimit(1).minimumScaleFactor(0.7)
            Text(title).font(.caption2).foregroundColor(.secondary)
        }
        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
    }

    private var statusColor: Color {
        switch harness.state {
        case .running: return .green
        case .failed: return .red
        case .idle: return .gray
        default: return .orange
        }
    }

    private var statusIcon: String {
        switch harness.state {
        case .running: return "checkmark.seal.fill"
        case .failed: return "xmark.octagon.fill"
        case .idle: return "circle.dashed"
        default: return "hourglass"
        }
    }

    private var statusSubtitle: String {
        switch harness.state {
        case .running:
            if let start = harness.startedAt {
                let f = DateFormatter(); f.dateFormat = "HH:mm:ss"
                return "自 \(f.string(from: start)) 起运行 · \(harness.lanURL != nil ? "本机 + 局域网" : "仅本机")"
            }
            return "运行中"
        case .idle: return "点击下面的按钮自动完成部署"
        case .failed: return "见下方错误信息"
        default: return "请稍候"
        }
    }
}

struct FilledButtonStyle: ButtonStyle {
    let color: Color
    func makeBody(configuration: Configuration) -> some View {
        configuration.label
            .font(.headline)
            .foregroundColor(.white)
            .background(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 14).fill(color.opacity(configuration.isPressed ? 0.75 : 1)))
    }
}
```

`apple/Sources/IOSOverlay.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation

/// iOS has no confinement backend: no bwrap, no Landlock, no Seatbelt.
///
/// `dsh-sandbox-local` fails closed on an unknown platform, so every tool
/// execution would raise `SANDBOX_UNAVAILABLE`. The upstream plugin supports a
/// `runnerCommand`, so we point it at a tiny pass-through runner: it consumes
/// the profile arguments and `exec`s the command unchanged. This keeps the
/// plugin (and therefore sandboxPolicy / workflow-ptc / every tool that wants a
/// policy) alive, at the cost of running tools unconfined — which is what the
/// jailbroken app process already does.
enum IOSOverlay {

    static let runnerName = "no-sandbox-runner.sh"
    static let runnerFailureSignature = "deepseek-ios-runner: fatal"

    static var runnerPath: URL { Paths.binDir.appendingPathComponent(runnerName) }

    static let runnerScript = """
    #!/bin/sh
    # DeepSeek for iOS: iOS has no bwrap/Landlock/Seatbelt backend.
    # Skip the profile arguments the caller passes and exec the command as-is.
    while [ "$#" -gt 0 ]; do
      if [ "$1" = "--" ]; then shift; break; fi
      shift
    done
    if [ "$#" -eq 0 ]; then
      echo "deepseek-ios-runner: fatal: empty argv" >&2
      exit 125
    fi
    exec "$@"
    """

    /// Writes (or repairs) the pass-through runner and returns its path.
    @discardableResult
    static func ensureRunner() throws -> URL {
        let fm = FileManager.default
        try fm.createDirectory(at: Paths.binDir, withIntermediateDirectories: true)
        let url = runnerPath
        let existing = try? String(contentsOf: url, encoding: .utf8)
        if existing != runnerScript {
            if fm.fileExists(atPath: url.path) { try fm.removeItem(at: url) }
            try runnerScript.write(to: url, atomically: true, encoding: .utf8)
        }
        if !fm.isExecutableFile(atPath: url.path) {
            try fm.setAttributes([.posixPermissions: 0o755], ofItemAtPath: url.path)
        }
        return url
    }

    /// Builds the single overlay file handed to the launcher with `--patch`:
    /// the sandbox runner, plus the user's third-party model provider when set.
    static func writeOverlay(models: ModelConfig) throws -> URL {
        let runner = try ensureRunner()
        var yaml = "# Generated by DeepSeek for iOS. Passed to the harness with --patch;\n"
        yaml += "# upstream source is untouched.\n"
        yaml += "- id: sandbox\n"
        yaml += "  config:\n"
        yaml += "    runnerCommand:\n"
        yaml += "      - \(quote(runner.path))\n"
        yaml += "    runnerFailureSignatures:\n"
        yaml += "      - \(quote(runnerFailureSignature))\n"

        // "PTC 模式" executes model-written TypeScript through Node's built-in
        // type stripping (amaro/SWC), which is WebAssembly — unavailable under
        // `--jitless`, and iOS cannot enable JIT for this Node build. Every tool
        // call routed through PTC therefore dies with "WebAssembly is not
        // supported in this environment". "创造模式" composes plugins the same
        // way. Both are disabled through the profile overlay (configuration, not
        // a source change) so the remaining preset — direct tool calls, no code
        // execution — becomes the default.
        // PTC is enabled again: with the experimental JIT switch on (the device
        // reports WebAssembly working), its TypeScript code-run path is usable.
        // If JIT is off, PTC tool calls fail with a wasm error — that is a
        // runtime choice, not a load-time failure.
        yaml += "- id: preset-cordis\n  disabled: true\n"

        if models.thirdPartyEnabled {
            try models.validate()
            yaml += "- id: llm-pi-ai\n"
            yaml += "  config:\n"
            yaml += "    providers:\n"
            yaml += "      \(models.providerID):\n"
            yaml += "        apiKeyEnv: \(ModelConfig.envThirdParty)\n"
            yaml += "        api: \(models.apiProtocol)\n"
            yaml += "        baseURL: \(quote(models.baseURL.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces)))\n"
            yaml += "        name: \(quote(models.displayName.isEmpty ? models.providerID : models.displayName))\n"
            yaml += "        models:\n"
            let modalities = [models.inputText ? "text" : nil, models.inputImage ? "image" : nil]
                .compactMap { $0 }
            let levels = models.reasoningLevels
                .split(whereSeparator: { $0 == "," || $0 == " " })
                .map { $0.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces) }
                .filter { !$0.isEmpty }
            for model in models.parsedModels {
                yaml += "          - id: \(model)\n"
                yaml += "            name: \(quote(model))\n"
                if models.contextWindow > 0 { yaml += "            contextWindow: \(models.contextWindow)\n" }
                if models.maxTokens > 0 { yaml += "            maxTokens: \(models.maxTokens)\n" }
                if !modalities.isEmpty {
                    yaml += "            input: [\(modalities.joined(separator: ", "))]\n"
                }
                if !levels.isEmpty {
                    yaml += "            reasoningEfforts:\n"
                    for level in levels { yaml += "              \(level): true\n" }
                }
            }
        }

        let url = Paths.modelOverlay
        try yaml.write(to: url, atomically: true, encoding: .utf8)
        return url
    }

    private static func quote(_ value: String) -> String {
        "\"" + value.replacingOccurrences(of: "\\", with: "\\\\")
                    .replacingOccurrences(of: "\"", with: "\\\"") + "\""
    }
}
```
