`apple/Sources/ModelConfig.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation
import Security

// MARK: - Keychain (secrets never touch the plist)

enum Keychain {
    private static let service = "com.dshios.dsh"

    static func set(_ value: String, for account: String) {
        delete(account)
        guard !value.isEmpty else { return }
        let query: [String: Any] = [
            kSecClass as String: kSecClassGenericPassword,
            kSecAttrService as String: service,
            kSecAttrAccount as String: account,
            kSecValueData as String: Data(value.utf8),
            kSecAttrAccessible as String: kSecAttrAccessibleAfterFirstUnlock,
        ]
        SecItemAdd(query as CFDictionary, nil)
    }

    static func get(_ account: String) -> String {
        let query: [String: Any] = [
            kSecClass as String: kSecClassGenericPassword,
            kSecAttrService as String: service,
            kSecAttrAccount as String: account,
            kSecReturnData as String: true,
            kSecMatchLimit as String: kSecMatchLimitOne,
        ]
        var item: CFTypeRef?
        guard SecItemCopyMatching(query as CFDictionary, &item) == errSecSuccess,
              let data = item as? Data,
              let text = String(data: data, encoding: .utf8) else { return "" }
        return text
    }

    static func delete(_ account: String) {
        let query: [String: Any] = [
            kSecClass as String: kSecClassGenericPassword,
            kSecAttrService as String: service,
            kSecAttrAccount as String: account,
        ]
        SecItemDelete(query as CFDictionary)
    }
}

// MARK: - Model configuration

/// Model access is configured in the app instead of the Web UI:
///
/// * the official DeepSeek provider already reads `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY` from the
///   environment (it is the `apiKeyEnv` in the shipped web profile), so the key
///   is passed as an env var and never lands on disk;
/// * a third-party / OpenAI-compatible gateway is expressed as a `llm-pi-ai`
///   provider in a patch overlay, which the launcher accepts via `--patch`
///   (upstream documented mechanism — no source is modified).
struct ModelConfig {
    static let deepseekAccount = "deepseek.api-key"
    static let thirdPartyAccount = "thirdparty.api-key"
    static let envThirdParty = "DSH_THIRD_PARTY_KEY"

    var deepseekKey: String = ""
    var thirdPartyEnabled: Bool = false
    var providerID: String = "custom"
    var displayName: String = "Custom"
    var baseURL: String = ""
    var apiProtocol: String = "openai-completions"
    var modelIDs: String = ""
    var contextWindow: Int = 0
    var maxTokens: Int = 0
    var inputText: Bool = true
    var inputImage: Bool = false
    var reasoningLevels: String = ""
    var thirdPartyKey: String = ""

    static let protocols = ["openai-completions", "openai-responses", "anthropic-messages"]

    static func load() -> ModelConfig {
        let defaults = UserDefaults.standard
        var config = ModelConfig()
        config.deepseekKey = Keychain.get(deepseekAccount)
        config.thirdPartyKey = Keychain.get(thirdPartyAccount)
        config.thirdPartyEnabled = defaults.bool(forKey: "thirdPartyEnabled")
        config.providerID = defaults.string(forKey: "thirdPartyProviderID") ?? "custom"
        config.displayName = defaults.string(forKey: "thirdPartyDisplayName") ?? "Custom"
        config.baseURL = defaults.string(forKey: "thirdPartyBaseURL") ?? ""
        config.apiProtocol = defaults.string(forKey: "thirdPartyProtocol") ?? "openai-completions"
        config.modelIDs = defaults.string(forKey: "thirdPartyModels") ?? ""
        config.contextWindow = defaults.integer(forKey: "thirdPartyContextWindow")
        config.maxTokens = defaults.integer(forKey: "thirdPartyMaxTokens")
        config.inputText = defaults.object(forKey: "thirdPartyInputText") as? Bool ?? true
        config.inputImage = defaults.object(forKey: "thirdPartyInputImage") as? Bool ?? false
        config.reasoningLevels = defaults.string(forKey: "thirdPartyReasoning") ?? ""
        return config
    }

    func save() {
        let defaults = UserDefaults.standard
        Keychain.set(deepseekKey, for: ModelConfig.deepseekAccount)
        Keychain.set(thirdPartyKey, for: ModelConfig.thirdPartyAccount)
        defaults.set(thirdPartyEnabled, forKey: "thirdPartyEnabled")
        defaults.set(providerID, forKey: "thirdPartyProviderID")
        defaults.set(displayName, forKey: "thirdPartyDisplayName")
        defaults.set(baseURL, forKey: "thirdPartyBaseURL")
        defaults.set(apiProtocol, forKey: "thirdPartyProtocol")
        defaults.set(modelIDs, forKey: "thirdPartyModels")
        defaults.set(contextWindow, forKey: "thirdPartyContextWindow")
        defaults.set(maxTokens, forKey: "thirdPartyMaxTokens")
        defaults.set(inputText, forKey: "thirdPartyInputText")
        defaults.set(inputImage, forKey: "thirdPartyInputImage")
        defaults.set(reasoningLevels, forKey: "thirdPartyReasoning")
    }

    var isUsable: Bool {
        !deepseekKey.isEmpty || (thirdPartyEnabled && !baseURL.isEmpty && !parsedModels.isEmpty)
    }

    var parsedModels: [String] {
        modelIDs.split(whereSeparator: { $0 == "," || $0 == " " || $0 == "\n" })
            .map { $0.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces) }
            .filter { !$0.isEmpty }
    }

    func validate() throws {
        guard thirdPartyEnabled else { return }
        let id = providerID.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces)
        guard !id.isEmpty, id.range(of: "^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9._-]*$", options: .regularExpression) != nil else {
            throw ModelConfigError.invalid("Provider ID 只能用小写字母、数字、`-`、`_`、`.`")
        }
        let url = baseURL.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces)
        guard url.hasPrefix("http://") || url.hasPrefix("https://"), !url.contains(" ") else {
            throw ModelConfigError.invalid("Base URL 必须以 http:// 或 https:// 开头")
        }
        guard !parsedModels.isEmpty else {
            throw ModelConfigError.invalid("至少填一个模型 id")
        }
        for model in parsedModels where model.range(of: "^[A-Za-z0-9._:/-]+$", options: .regularExpression) == nil {
            throw ModelConfigError.invalid("模型 id 含非法字符：\(model)")
        }
    }

    /// Writes the `llm-pi-ai` overlay for the third-party gateway.
    /// Returns nil when no third-party provider is configured.
    func writeOverlay(to url: URL) throws -> URL? {
        try validate()
        guard thirdPartyEnabled else {
            if FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: url.path) {
                try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: url)
            }
            return nil
        }
        var yaml = "# Generated by DeepSeek for iOS from the in-app model settings.\n"
        yaml += "# Passed to the harness with --patch; no upstream source is modified.\n"
        yaml += "- id: llm-pi-ai\n"
        yaml += "  config:\n"
        yaml += "    providers:\n"
        yaml += "      \(providerID):\n"
        yaml += "        apiKeyEnv: \(ModelConfig.envThirdParty)\n"
        yaml += "        api: \(apiProtocol)\n"
        yaml += "        baseURL: \(baseURL.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces))\n"
        yaml += "        name: \(displayName.isEmpty ? providerID : displayName)\n"
        yaml += "        models:\n"
        for model in parsedModels {
            yaml += "          - id: \(model)\n"
        }
        try yaml.write(to: url, atomically: true, encoding: .utf8)
        return url
    }
}

enum ModelConfigError: LocalizedError {
    case invalid(String)
    var errorDescription: String? {
        switch self {
        case let .invalid(text): return text
        }
    }
}

```

`apple/Sources/NodeProcess.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation

/// posix_spawn wrapper. `Process` is unavailable on iOS, so the harness is
/// launched directly with posix_spawn, in its own process group, with pipes for
/// stdout/stderr.
///
/// Note: on Darwin `posix_spawn_file_actions_t`/`posix_spawnattr_t` are opaque
/// pointers, and the C API takes `UnsafeMutablePointer<T?>`, so the value is kept
/// as an optional and addressed with `&`.
final class NodeProcess {

    private(set) var pid: pid_t = 0
    var onLine: ((String, LogStream) -> Void)?
    var onExit: ((Int32) -> Void)?

    private var outSource: DispatchSourceRead?
    private var errSource: DispatchSourceRead?
    private var outBuffer = Data()
    private var errBuffer = Data()

    // MARK: - One-shot helper

    /// Runs a short-lived command and returns (exitCode, combinedOutput).
    static func run(executable: String,
                    args: [String],
                    environment: [String: String],
                    timeout: TimeInterval = 20) -> (Int32, String) {
        let out = Pipe()
        let err = Pipe()

        var fa: posix_spawn_file_actions_t? = nil
        posix_spawn_file_actions_init(&fa)
        defer { posix_spawn_file_actions_destroy(&fa) }
        posix_spawn_file_actions_adddup2(&fa, out.fileHandleForWriting.fileDescriptor, STDOUT_FILENO)
        posix_spawn_file_actions_adddup2(&fa, err.fileHandleForWriting.fileDescriptor, STDERR_FILENO)

        var attr: posix_spawnattr_t? = nil
        posix_spawnattr_init(&attr)
        defer { posix_spawnattr_destroy(&attr) }
        posix_spawnattr_setflags(&attr, Int16(POSIX_SPAWN_SETPGROUP))

        var argv: [UnsafeMutablePointer<CChar>?] = ([executable] + args).map { strdup($0) }
        argv.append(nil)
        var envp: [UnsafeMutablePointer<CChar>?] = environment.map { strdup("\($0.key)=\($0.value)") }
        envp.append(nil)
        defer {
            for p in argv where p != nil { free(p) }
            for p in envp where p != nil { free(p) }
        }

        var childPid: pid_t = 0
        let rc = posix_spawn(&childPid, executable, &fa, &attr, &argv, &envp)
        guard rc == 0 else { return (-1, "spawn failed: \(String(cString: strerror(rc)))") }
        out.fileHandleForWriting.closeFile()
        err.fileHandleForWriting.closeFile()

        let deadline = Date().addingTimeInterval(timeout)
        var outData = Data()
        var errData = Data()
        while Date() < deadline {
            let o = out.fileHandleForReading.availableData
            if !o.isEmpty { outData.append(o) }
            let e = err.fileHandleForReading.availableData
            if !e.isEmpty { errData.append(e) }
            var status: Int32 = 0
            if waitpid(childPid, &status, WNOHANG) == childPid {
                outData.append(out.fileHandleForReading.availableData)
                errData.append(err.fileHandleForReading.availableData)
                // Negative code = killed by that signal (e.g. -9 = SIGKILL),
                // which is how iOS reports "signature/permission refused".
                let code = (status & 0x7f) == 0 ? (status >> 8) & 0xff : -(status & 0x7f)
                let text = String(data: outData, encoding: .utf8) ?? ""
                let errText = String(data: errData, encoding: .utf8) ?? ""
                return (code, (text + errText).trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines))
            }
            Thread.sleep(forTimeInterval: 0.02)
        }
        kill(childPid, SIGKILL)
        return (-2, "timeout after \(Int(timeout))s")
    }

    // MARK: - Long running

    func launch(executable: String,
                args: [String],
                environment: [String: String],
                workingDirectory: String) throws {
        var outPipe: [Int32] = [0, 0]
        var errPipe: [Int32] = [0, 0]
        guard pipe(&outPipe) == 0 else { throw NodeProcessError.spawnFailed(errno, "pipe()") }
        guard pipe(&errPipe) == 0 else { throw NodeProcessError.spawnFailed(errno, "pipe()") }

        var fa: posix_spawn_file_actions_t? = nil
        posix_spawn_file_actions_init(&fa)
        defer { posix_spawn_file_actions_destroy(&fa) }
        posix_spawn_file_actions_adddup2(&fa, outPipe[1], STDOUT_FILENO)
        posix_spawn_file_actions_adddup2(&fa, errPipe[1], STDERR_FILENO)
        posix_spawn_file_actions_addclose(&fa, outPipe[0])
        posix_spawn_file_actions_addclose(&fa, errPipe[0])

        var attr: posix_spawnattr_t? = nil
        posix_spawnattr_init(&attr)
        defer { posix_spawnattr_destroy(&attr) }
        posix_spawnattr_setflags(&attr, Int16(POSIX_SPAWN_SETPGROUP))

        var argv: [UnsafeMutablePointer<CChar>?] = ([executable] + args).map { strdup($0) }
        argv.append(nil)
        var envp: [UnsafeMutablePointer<CChar>?] = environment.map { strdup("\($0.key)=\($0.value)") }
        envp.append(nil)
        defer {
            for p in argv where p != nil { free(p) }
            for p in envp where p != nil { free(p) }
        }

        // iOS has no posix_spawn_file_actions_addchdir_np, but a spawned child
        // inherits the parent's working directory, so move this thread's cwd
        // around the spawn and restore it immediately afterwards.
        let previousDirectory = FileManager.default.currentDirectoryPath
        _ = workingDirectory.withCString { chdir($0) }

        var childPid: pid_t = 0
        let rc = posix_spawn(&childPid, executable, &fa, &attr, &argv, &envp)
        _ = previousDirectory.withCString { chdir($0) }
        close(outPipe[1])
        close(errPipe[1])
        guard rc == 0 else {
            close(outPipe[0]); close(errPipe[0])
            throw NodeProcessError.spawnFailed(rc, String(cString: strerror(rc)))
        }
        pid = childPid

        outSource = makeSource(fd: outPipe[0], stream: .out)
        errSource = makeSource(fd: errPipe[0], stream: .err)

        let watched = childPid
        DispatchQueue.global(qos: .userInitiated).async { [weak self] in
            var status: Int32 = 0
            while waitpid(watched, &status, 0) == -1 && errno == EINTR {}
            let code = (status & 0x7f) == 0 ? (status >> 8) & 0xff : -1
            DispatchQueue.main.async { self?.onExit?(code) }
        }
    }

    private func makeSource(fd: Int32, stream: LogStream) -> DispatchSourceRead {
        let src = DispatchSource.makeReadSource(fileDescriptor: fd,
                                                queue: DispatchQueue(label: "dsh.pipe.\(stream.rawValue)"))
        src.setEventHandler { [weak self] in
            guard let self = self else { return }
            var buf = [UInt8](repeating: 0, count: 65536)
            let n = read(fd, &buf, buf.count)
            guard n > 0 else { return }
            self.consume(Data(bytes: buf, count: n), stream: stream)
        }
        src.setCancelHandler { close(fd) }
        src.resume()
        return src
    }

    private func consume(_ data: Data, stream: LogStream) {
        if stream == .out {
            outBuffer.append(data)
            flush(&outBuffer, stream: stream)
        } else {
            errBuffer.append(data)
            flush(&errBuffer, stream: stream)
        }
    }

    private func flush(_ buffer: inout Data, stream: LogStream) {
        while let idx = buffer.firstIndex(of: 0x0a) {
            let lineData = buffer[buffer.startIndex..<idx]
            buffer.removeSubrange(buffer.startIndex...idx)
            if let text = String(data: lineData, encoding: .utf8) {
                let clean = text.replacingOccurrences(of: "\u{1b}[[0-9;]*m",
                                                      with: "",
                                                      options: .regularExpression)
                DispatchQueue.main.async { [weak self] in self?.onLine?(clean, stream) }
            }
        }
        if buffer.count > 1024 * 256, let text = String(data: buffer, encoding: .utf8) {
            buffer.removeAll()
            DispatchQueue.main.async { [weak self] in self?.onLine?(text, stream) }
        }
    }

    func terminate(force: Bool = false) {
        guard pid > 0 else { return }
        let target = pid
        kill(-target, force ? SIGKILL : SIGTERM)
        kill(target, force ? SIGKILL : SIGTERM)
        if !force {
            DispatchQueue.global().asyncAfter(deadline: .now() + 3) {
                kill(-target, SIGKILL)
                kill(target, SIGKILL)
            }
        }
        pid = 0
    }

    /// Emits whatever is still buffered without a trailing newline, so a
    /// process that hangs mid-line still shows what it printed.
    func flushPending() {
        if !outBuffer.isEmpty, let text = String(data: outBuffer, encoding: .utf8) {
            outBuffer.removeAll()
            DispatchQueue.main.async { [weak self] in self?.onLine?(text, .out) }
        }
        if !errBuffer.isEmpty, let text = String(data: errBuffer, encoding: .utf8) {
            errBuffer.removeAll()
            DispatchQueue.main.async { [weak self] in self?.onLine?(text, .err) }
        }
    }

    func shutdown() {
        outSource?.cancel(); outSource = nil
        errSource?.cancel(); errSource = nil
    }

    var isRunning: Bool { pid > 0 }
}

enum NodeProcessError: LocalizedError {
    case spawnFailed(Int32, String)

    var errorDescription: String? {
        switch self {
        case let .spawnFailed(code, message):
            return "启动 node 失败（\(code): \(message)）。请确认 App 未被沙盒限制（越狱环境）且二进制可执行。"
        }
    }
}

```

`apple/Sources/RuntimeShims.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation

/// iOS cannot be spelled to the harness' native layer.
///
/// Two official pieces refuse to run here:
/// * `@deepseek-ai/node-addon-system/flock` throws on any platform except
///   linux/darwin ("flock is not supported on ios-arm64") — and the JSONL
///   session store needs it for every write;
/// * `node-addon-require-builtin` ships prebuilts for darwin/linux/win32 only.
///
/// Apple ships no Node.js, so neither has an iOS build upstream. iOS *is* Darwin:
/// same Mach-O/dyld, same libSystem, and it has the POSIX `flock(2)` syscall. So
/// we compile both official modules for iPhoneOS arm64 (see `runtime/`) and ship
/// them under their **darwin** package names, then report `process.platform` as
/// `darwin` from a tiny CommonJS preload.
///
/// Nothing upstream is modified, and the preload is only enabled when a preflight
/// proves both native modules load. If it fails, the app starts exactly as
/// before instead of hanging.
enum RuntimeShims {

    static var directory: URL { Paths.binDir.appendingPathComponent("shims", isDirectory: true) }
    static var preloadFile: URL { directory.appendingPathComponent("ios-platform.cjs") }
    static var preflightFile: URL { directory.appendingPathComponent("preflight.cjs") }

    static let preloadSource = """
    'use strict';
    // iOS is Darwin: same loader, same libSystem, same flock(2). The harness'
    // native bindings only accept linux/darwin, so report darwin and ship iOS
    // builds of those bindings under the darwin platform package names.
    try {
      Object.defineProperty(process, 'platform', {
        value: 'darwin', enumerable: true, configurable: true, writable: false,
      });
    } catch (error) {
      // Reporting the real platform is better than failing to start.
    }
    """

    /// Loads exactly the two native modules the harness loads, through the same
    /// resolution it uses, before we commit to the preload.
    static let preflightSource = """
    'use strict';
    const path = require('node:path');
    const { createRequire } = require('node:module');
    try {
      const tree = process.argv[2];
      if (!tree) throw new Error('missing tree argument');
      const req = createRequire(path.join(tree, 'lib', 'bin.js'));
      const requireBuiltin = req('node-addon-require-builtin');
      const info = requireBuiltin.getBindingInfo();
      const manifest = req.resolve('@deepseek-ai/node-addon-system-darwin-arm64/package.json');
      const flock = req(path.join(path.dirname(manifest), 'bin', 'system.node'));
      if (typeof flock.tryLock !== 'function') throw new Error('flock binding has no tryLock');
      process.stdout.write('preflight-ok ' + info.backend + ' ' + info.abi);
    } catch (error) {
      process.stdout.write('preflight-failed ' + (error && error.message));
      process.exit(3);
    }
    """

    static func ensure() throws -> URL {
        let fm = FileManager.default
        try fm.createDirectory(at: directory, withIntermediateDirectories: true)
        for (url, source) in [(preloadFile, preloadSource), (preflightFile, preflightSource)] {
            if (try? String(contentsOf: url, encoding: .utf8)) != source {
                if fm.fileExists(atPath: url.path) { try fm.removeItem(at: url) }
                try source.write(to: url, atomically: true, encoding: .utf8)
            }
        }
        return preloadFile
    }
}
```

`apple/Sources/SettingsView.swift`:

```swift
import SwiftUI
import UIKit

struct SettingsView: View {
    @EnvironmentObject var settings: AppSettings
    @EnvironmentObject var harness: HarnessController
    @State private var portText: String = ""
    @State private var lanPortText: String = ""
    @State private var showResetConfirm = false
    @State private var deepseekKey = ""
    @State private var thirdPartyEnabled = false
    @State private var providerID = "custom"
    @State private var providerDisplayName = "Custom"
    @State private var baseURL = ""
    @State private var apiProtocol = "openai-completions"
    @State private var modelIDs = ""
    @State private var contextWindow = ""
    @State private var maxTokens = ""
    @State private var thirdPartyKey = ""
    @State private var inputText = true
    @State private var inputImage = false
    @State private var reasoningLevels = ""
    @State private var modelMessage: String?
    @State private var diagnosticsURL: URL?

    var body: some View {
        NavigationView {
            Form {
                Section(header: Text("服务"),
                         footer: Text("端口改动会在重启后生效。harness 只监听 127.0.0.1，局域网通过 App 内置中转提供。")) {
                    HStack {
                        Text("本机端口")
                        Spacer()
                        TextField("3080", text: $portText)
                            .keyboardType(.numberPad)
                            .multilineTextAlignment(.trailing)
                            .frame(width: 90)
                            .onSubmit { applyPorts() }
                    }
                    Toggle("开启局域网访问", isOn: $settings.lanEnabled)
                    if settings.lanEnabled {
                        HStack {
                            Text("局域网端口")
                            Spacer()
                            TextField("3081", text: $lanPortText)
                                .keyboardType(.numberPad)
                                .multilineTextAlignment(.trailing)
                                .frame(width: 90)
                                .onSubmit { applyPorts() }
                        }
                        HStack {
                            Text("当前地址")
                            Spacer()
                            Text(NetworkInfo.primaryIPv4() ?? "未连接 Wi‑Fi")
                                .font(.system(.footnote, design: .monospaced))
                                .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                        }
                    }
                    Toggle("启动后自动部署", isOn: $settings.autoStart)
                    Toggle("崩溃自动重启（最多 3 次）", isOn: $settings.keepAlive)
                    Toggle("后台保活（静音播放，耗电）", isOn: $settings.backgroundKeepAlive)
                }

                Section(header: Text("运行时"),
                         footer: Text("iOS 版 V8 没有 JIT，--jitless 让解释器运行在纯 W^X 内存上；一般不要删除。")) {
                    HStack {
                        Text("额外 node 参数")
                        Spacer()
                    }
                    TextField("--jitless", text: $settings.extraNodeFlags)
                        .font(.system(.footnote, design: .monospaced))
                        .autocapitalization(.none)
                        .disableAutocorrection(true)
                    Stepper("V8 堆上限 \(settings.maxOldSpaceMB) MB", value: $settings.maxOldSpaceMB, in: 256...4096, step: 256)
                    Toggle("实验性 JIT（去掉 --jitless）", isOn: $settings.experimentalJIT)
                    Text("iOS 上 V8 默认拿不到可执行内存，所以用解释器模式。打开后会尝试 JIT：若成功，WebAssembly 可用、PTC 模式可用；若失败，harness 会启动即崩，关掉开关再重启即可。")
                        .font(.caption2).foregroundColor(.secondary)
                    HStack {
                        Text("Node 版本")
                        Spacer()
                        Text(harness.nodeVersion).foregroundColor(.secondary)
                    }
                }

                Section(header: Text("网络代理"),
                         footer: Text("如需通过代理访问 DeepSeek API，填 http://host:port 或 socks5://host:port。")) {
                    TextField("留空=不使用代理", text: $settings.proxyURL)
                        .autocapitalization(.none)
                        .disableAutocorrection(true)
                        .font(.system(.footnote, design: .monospaced))
                }

                Section(header: Text("模型（在 App 内配置）"),
                         footer: Text("DeepSeek 官方：只填 API Key（以环境变量 DEEPSEEK_API_KEY 注入，不落盘）。\n第三方 / OpenAI 兼容网关：填 Base URL、协议与模型 id，App 会用官方 --patch overlay 机制注入，不改动 DeepSeek 源码。改完点保存，然后回「部署」页重启。")) {
                    SecureField("DeepSeek API Key（sk-…）", text: $deepseekKey)
                        .autocapitalization(.none)
                        .disableAutocorrection(true)
                    Toggle("启用第三方 / 自定义模型网关", isOn: $thirdPartyEnabled)
                    if thirdPartyEnabled {
                        TextField("Provider ID（小写，如 mygw）", text: $providerID)
                            .autocapitalization(.none)
                            .disableAutocorrection(true)
                        TextField("显示名（如 My Gateway）", text: $providerDisplayName)
                        TextField("Base URL（https://gateway/v1）", text: $baseURL)
                            .autocapitalization(.none)
                            .disableAutocorrection(true)
                            .keyboardType(.URL)
                        Picker("API 协议", selection: $apiProtocol) {
                            ForEach(ModelConfig.protocols, id: \.self) { Text($0).tag($0) }
                        }
                        TextField("模型 id（逗号分隔，可加多个）", text: $modelIDs)
                            .autocapitalization(.none)
                            .disableAutocorrection(true)
                        HStack {
                            Text("上下文窗口")
                            Spacer()

                            .foregroundColor(.red)

                    }
                }

                portText = "\(settings.port)"
                lanPortText = "\(settings.lanPort)"
                let models = ModelConfig.load()
                deepseekKey = models.deepseekKey
                thirdPartyEnabled = models.thirdPartyEnabled
                providerID = models.providerID
                providerDisplayName = models.displayName
                baseURL = models.baseURL
                apiProtocol = models.apiProtocol
                modelIDs = models.modelIDs
                contextWindow = models.contextWindow > 0 ? String(models.contextWindow) : ""
                maxTokens = models.maxTokens > 0 ? String(models.maxTokens) : ""
                inputText = models.inputText
                inputImage = models.inputImage
                reasoningLevels = models.reasoningLevels
                thirdPartyKey = models.thirdPartyKey
            }
            .alert("重置 DeepSeek 数据？", isPresented: $showResetConfirm) {
                Button("重置", role: .destructive) {
                    harness.stop()
                    try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: Paths.dshHome)
                    try? Paths.ensureDirectories()
                    harness.logs.append("已重置 DSH_HOME", .app)
                }
                Button("取消", role: .cancel) {}
            } message: {
                Text("会删除会话、配置与模型设置（含 API Key），工作区文件不动。")
            }
        }
        .navigationViewStyle(.stack)
    }

    private func saveModelConfig() {
        var models = ModelConfig()
        models.deepseekKey = deepseekKey.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces)
        models.thirdPartyEnabled = thirdPartyEnabled
        models.providerID = providerID.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces)
        models.displayName = providerDisplayName.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces)
        models.baseURL = baseURL.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces)
        models.apiProtocol = apiProtocol
        models.modelIDs = modelIDs
        models.contextWindow = Int(contextWindow) ?? 0
        models.maxTokens = Int(maxTokens) ?? 0
        models.inputText = inputText
        models.inputImage = inputImage
        models.reasoningLevels = reasoningLevels
        models.thirdPartyKey = thirdPartyKey.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces)
        do {
            try models.validate()
            models.save()
            _ = try models.writeOverlay(to: Paths.modelOverlay)
            modelMessage = "已保存 · 回「部署」页点重启生效"
            harness.logs.append("模型配置已保存（DeepSeek \(models.deepseekKey.isEmpty ? "未填" : "已填")，第三方 \(models.thirdPartyEnabled ? "开启" : "关闭")）", .app)
        } catch {
            modelMessage = error.localizedDescription
        }
    }

    private func applyPorts() {
        if let p = Int(portText), p > 1024, p < 65535 { settings.port = p }
        if let lp = Int(lanPortText), lp > 1024, lp < 65535 { settings.lanPort = lp }
        portText = "\(settings.port)"
        lanPortText = "\(settings.lanPort)"
    }
}
```

`apple/Sources/TCPRelay.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation
import Network

/// A tiny TCP forwarder: listens on 0.0.0.0:listenPort and pipes every
/// connection to 127.0.0.1:targetPort, where the harness actually listens.
///
/// The harness refuses wildcard binds (it only accepts one concrete local
/// address), so LAN access is provided by this relay instead of binding the
/// harness itself to the network. HTTP and WebSocket both survive verbatim
/// because the byte stream is piped both ways without interpretation.
final class TCPRelay {

    private let listenPort: UInt16
    private let targetPort: UInt16
    private var listener: NWListener?
    private let queue = DispatchQueue(label: "dsh.relay")
    var onEvent: ((String) -> Void)?

    private var bridges: [ObjectIdentifier: Bridge] = [:]

    init(listenPort: UInt16, targetPort: UInt16) {
        self.listenPort = listenPort
        self.targetPort = targetPort
    }

    func start() throws {
        stop()
        let params = NWParameters.tcp
        params.allowLocalEndpointReuse = true
        params.acceptLocalOnly = false
        guard let port = NWEndpoint.Port(rawValue: listenPort) else {
            throw RelayError.badPort
        }
        let listener = try NWListener(using: params, on: port)
        listener.stateUpdateHandler = { [weak self] state in
            switch state {
            case .ready:
                self?.onEvent?("局域网中转已就绪：0.0.0.0:\(self?.listenPort ?? 0) → 127.0.0.1:\(self?.targetPort ?? 0)")
            case let .failed(error):
                self?.onEvent?("局域网中转失败：\(error.localizedDescription)")
            default:
                break
            }
        }
        listener.newConnectionHandler = { [weak self] connection in
            self?.accept(connection)
        }
        listener.start(queue: queue)
        self.listener = listener
    }

    func stop() {
        listener?.cancel()
        listener = nil
        queue.sync {
            for (_, bridge) in self.bridges { bridge.close() }
            self.bridges.removeAll()
        }
    }

    private func accept(_ incoming: NWConnection) {
        let params = NWParameters.tcp
        let target = NWConnection(host: NWEndpoint.Host("127.0.0.1"),
                                  port: NWEndpoint.Port(rawValue: targetPort) ?? 3080,
                                  using: params)
        let bridge = Bridge(client: incoming, server: target) { [weak self] key in
            self?.queue.async { self?.bridges.removeValue(forKey: key) }
        }
        bridges[ObjectIdentifier(incoming)] = bridge
        bridge.start(queue: queue)
    }

    enum RelayError: LocalizedError {
        case badPort
        var errorDescription: String? { "局域网端口无效" }
    }
}

private final class Bridge {
    private let client: NWConnection
    private let server: NWConnection
    private let onClose: (ObjectIdentifier) -> Void
    private let queue = DispatchQueue(label: "dsh.relay.bridge")
    private var closed = false

    init(client: NWConnection, server: NWConnection, onClose: @escaping (ObjectIdentifier) -> Void) {
        self.client = client
        self.server = server
        self.onClose = onClose
    }

    func start(queue: DispatchQueue) {
        client.stateUpdateHandler = { [weak self] state in
            if case .failed = state { self?.close() }
            if case .cancelled = state { self?.close() }
        }
        server.stateUpdateHandler = { [weak self] state in
            if case .failed = state { self?.close() }
            if case .cancelled = state { self?.close() }
        }
        client.start(queue: queue)
        server.start(queue: queue)
        pump(from: client, to: server)
        pump(from: server, to: client)
    }

    private func pump(from source: NWConnection, to sink: NWConnection) {
        source.receive(minimumIncompleteLength: 1, maximumLength: 65536) { [weak self] data, _, isComplete, error in
            guard let self = self, !self.closed else { return }
            if let data = data, !data.isEmpty {
                sink.send(content: data, completion: .contentProcessed { sendError in
                    if sendError != nil { self.close(); return }
                    if !isComplete { self.pump(from: source, to: sink) }
                    else { self.close() }
                })
            } else if isComplete || error != nil {
                self.close()
            } else {
                self.pump(from: source, to: sink)
            }
        }
    }

    func close() {
        guard !closed else { return }
        closed = true
        client.cancel()
        server.cancel()
        onClose(ObjectIdentifier(client))
    }
}

```

`apple/Sources/WebCompat.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation

/// WebKit compatibility shim.
///
/// The shim itself is `Resources/web-compat.js`: the same file is served by the
/// harness (injected into the frontend's `index.html` at packaging time) so
/// **every** browser gets it — Safari on iOS older than 17.4 lacks
/// `AbortSignal.any`, which makes every client `fetch` throw, and that is what
/// keeps the workspace list stuck on "正在加载工作区…".
///
/// This type injects the same script into the in-app `WKWebView`, so the
/// embedded browser works even if the served copy is ever missing.
enum WebCompat {

    static let source: String = {
        if let url = Bundle.main.url(forResource: "web-compat", withExtension: "js"),
           let text = try? String(contentsOf: url, encoding: .utf8) {
            return text
        }
        return """
        (function () {
          if (window.AbortSignal && !AbortSignal.any) {
            AbortSignal.any = function (signals) {
              var c = new AbortController();
              for (var i = 0; i < signals.length; i++) {
                if (signals[i] && signals[i].aborted) { c.abort(signals[i].reason); return c.signal; }
              }
              signals.forEach(function (s) {
                if (s) s.addEventListener('abort', function () { c.abort(s.reason); }, { once: true });
              });
              return c.signal;
            };
          }
        })();
        """
    }()
}
```

```swift
import SwiftUI
import UIKit

// MARK: - Workspaces (containers)

struct WorkspacesView: View {
    @EnvironmentObject var harness: HarnessController
    @StateObject private var store = WorkspaceStore()
    @State private var newName = ""
    @State private var showCreate = false
    @State private var pendingDelete: WorkspaceStore.Workspace?
    @State private var renaming: WorkspaceStore.Workspace?
    @State private var renameText = ""

    var body: some View {
        NavigationView {
            List {
                Section(header: Text("容器根目录")) {
                    VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 4) {
                        Text("Documents/Workspaces")
                            .font(.system(.caption, design: .monospaced))
                        Text("harness 以这里作为工作目录，Web UI 里 Choose workspace 就能看到下面的目录。")
                            .font(.caption2).foregroundColor(.secondary)
                    }
                }
                Section(header: Text("工作区（\(store.items.count)）")) {
                    if store.items.isEmpty {
                        Text("还没有工作区").foregroundColor(.secondary)
                    }
                    ForEach(store.items) { item in
                        NavigationLink(destination: WorkspaceFilesView(root: item.url)) {
                            VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 4) {
                                HStack {
                                    Image(systemName: "folder.fill").foregroundColor(item.fromHarness ? .green : .blue)
                                    Text(item.name).font(.body)
                                    if item.fromHarness {
                                        Text("网页登记").font(.caption2)
                                            .padding(.horizontal, 6).padding(.vertical, 2)
                                            .background(Capsule().fill(Color.green.opacity(0.15)))
                                    }
                                    Spacer()
                                    Text(WorkspaceStore.humanBytes(item.bytes))
                                        .font(.caption2).foregroundColor(.secondary)
                                }
                                Text("\(item.fileCount) 个文件 · \(WorkspacesView.dateText(item.modified))")
                                    .font(.caption2).foregroundColor(.secondary)
                            }
                            .contentShape(Rectangle())
                        }
                        .contextMenu {
                            Button { UIPasteboard.general.string = item.url.path } label: { Label("复制路径", systemImage: "doc.on.doc") }
                            Button { renaming = item; renameText = item.name } label: { Label("重命名", systemImage: "pencil") }
                            Button(role: .destructive) { pendingDelete = item } label: { Label("删除", systemImage: "trash") }
                        }
                    }
                }
                if let error = store.lastError {
                    Section { Text(error).foregroundColor(.red).font(.caption) }
                }
            }
            .listStyle(.insetGrouped)
            .navigationTitle("工作区")
            .toolbar {
                ToolbarItem(placement: .navigationBarTrailing) {
                    Button { newName = ""; showCreate = true } label: { Image(systemName: "plus") }
                }
                ToolbarItem(placement: .navigationBarLeading) {
                    Button { store.reload() } label: { Image(systemName: "arrow.clockwise") }
                }
            }
            .alert("新建工作区", isPresented: $showCreate) {
                TextField("名称，例如 project-a", text: $newName)
                Button("创建") {
                    if store.create(name: newName) != nil { harness.syncWorkspaces(restartIfRunning: true) }
                    newName = ""
                }
                Button("取消", role: .cancel) {}
            }
            .alert("重命名", isPresented: Binding(get: { renaming != nil }, set: { if !$0 { renaming = nil } })) {
                TextField("新名称", text: $renameText)
                Button("保存") { if let item = renaming { store.rename(item, to: renameText) }; renaming = nil }
                Button("取消", role: .cancel) { renaming = nil }
            }
            .alert("删除工作区？", isPresented: Binding(get: { pendingDelete != nil }, set: { if !$0 { pendingDelete = nil } })) {
                Button("删除", role: .destructive) { if let item = pendingDelete { store.delete(item) }; pendingDelete = nil }
                Button("取消", role: .cancel) { pendingDelete = nil }
            } message: {
                Text("目录及其中的全部文件都会被删除，无法恢复。")
            }
            .onAppear { store.ensureDefault(); store.reload() }
        }
        .navigationViewStyle(.stack)
    }

    static func dateText(_ date: Date) -> String {
        let f = DateFormatter()
        f.dateFormat = "MM-dd HH:mm"
        return f.string(from: date)
    }
}

// MARK: - Logs

struct LogsView: View {
    @EnvironmentObject var harness: HarnessController
    @State private var autoScroll = true
    @State private var filter = ""
    @State private var shareURL: URL?

    private var visible: [LogLine] {
        let all = harness.logs.lines
        guard !filter.isEmpty else { return all }
        return all.filter { $0.text.localizedCaseInsensitiveContains(filter) }
    }

    var body: some View {
        NavigationView {
            VStack(spacing: 0) {
                HStack(spacing: 8) {
                    Image(systemName: "magnifyingglass").foregroundColor(.secondary)
                    TextField("过滤关键字", text: $filter)
                        .textFieldStyle(.roundedBorder)
                        .autocapitalization(.none)
                        .disableAutocorrection(true)
                    Toggle("跟随", isOn: $autoScroll).labelsHidden()
                    Text(autoScroll ? "跟随" : "定格").font(.caption2).foregroundColor(.secondary)
                }
                .padding(.horizontal, 12).padding(.vertical, 8)

                ScrollViewReader { proxy in
                    List {
                        ForEach(visible) { line in
                            HStack(alignment: .top, spacing: 6) {
                                Text(LogStore.formatter.string(from: line.date))
                                    .font(.system(size: 10, design: .monospaced))
                                    .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                                Text(line.text)
                                    .font(.system(size: 11, design: .monospaced))
                                    .foregroundColor(color(for: line))
                                    .textSelection(.enabled)
                            }
                            .listRowInsets(EdgeInsets(top: 2, leading: 8, bottom: 2, trailing: 8))
                            .id(line.id)
                        }
                    }
                    .listStyle(.plain)
                    .onChange(of: harness.logs.lines.count) { _ in
                        if autoScroll, let last = visible.last {
                            withAnimation(.linear(duration: 0.1)) { proxy.scrollTo(last.id, anchor: .bottom) }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            .navigationTitle("运行日志")
            .navigationBarTitleDisplayMode(.inline)
            .toolbar {
                ToolbarItemGroup(placement: .navigationBarTrailing) {
                    Button {
                        if let url = harness.exportLogs() { shareURL = url }
                    } label: { Image(systemName: "square.and.arrow.up") }
                    Button { harness.logs.clear() } label: { Image(systemName: "trash") }
                }
            }
            .sheet(item: Binding(get: { shareURL.map(ShareItem.init) }, set: { _ in shareURL = nil })) { item in
                ShareSheet(items: [item.url])
            }
        }
        .navigationViewStyle(.stack)
    }

    private func color(for line: LogLine) -> Color {
        switch line.stream {
        case .err: return .red
        case .app: return .secondary
        case .out: return .primary
        }
    }
}

struct ShareItem: Identifiable {
    let url: URL
    var id: String { url.path }
}

struct ShareSheet: UIViewControllerRepresentable {
    let items: [Any]
    func makeUIViewController(context: Context) -> UIActivityViewController {
        UIActivityViewController(activityItems: items, applicationActivities: nil)
    }
    func updateUIViewController(_ controller: UIActivityViewController, context: Context) {}
}
```

`apple/Sources/WorkspaceFilesView.swift`:

```swift
import SwiftUI

/// File browser for one workspace: browse, rename, edit text, delete one or many.
struct WorkspaceFilesView: View {
    let root: URL
    @State private var current: URL
    @State private var entries: [Entry] = []
    @State private var selecting = false
    @State private var selection = Set<String>()
    @State private var editing: Entry?
    @State private var error: String?
    @State private var newFolder = ""
    @State private var showNewFolder = false
    @State private var confirmDeleteAll = false

    struct Entry: Identifiable, Hashable {
        let url: URL
        let isDirectory: Bool
        let size: Int64
        let modified: Date
        var id: String { url.path }
        var name: String { url.lastPathComponent }
    }

    init(root: URL) {
        self.root = root
        _current = State(initialValue: root)
    }

    var body: some View {
        List {
            if let error = error {
                Section { Text(error).font(.caption).foregroundColor(.red) }
            }
            if entries.isEmpty {
                Text("这个文件夹是空的").foregroundColor(.secondary)
            }
            ForEach(entries) { entry in
                row(entry)
            }
        }
        .listStyle(.plain)
        .navigationTitle(current.lastPathComponent)
        .navigationBarTitleDisplayMode(.inline)
        .toolbar {
            ToolbarItemGroup(placement: .navigationBarTrailing) {
                if selecting {
                    Button("全选") { selection = Set(entries.map { $0.id }) }
                    Button(role: .destructive) { deleteSelected() } label: {
                        Text("删除(\(selection.count))")
                    }
                    .disabled(selection.isEmpty)
                } else {
                    Button { newFolder = ""; showNewFolder = true } label: { Image(systemName: "folder.badge.plus") }
                    Button(selecting ? "完成" : "选择") { selecting.toggle(); selection.removeAll() }
                }
            }
        }
        .safeAreaInset(edge: .bottom) {
            if selecting && !entries.isEmpty {
                Button(role: .destructive) { confirmDeleteAll = true } label: {
                    Label("删除全部（\(entries.count) 项）", systemImage: "trash")
                        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity).padding(.vertical, 12)
                }
                .background(Color(UIColor.secondarySystemGroupedBackground))
            }
        }
        .alert("新建文件夹", isPresented: $showNewFolder) {
            TextField("名称", text: $newFolder)
            Button("创建") { createFolder() }
            Button("取消", role: .cancel) {}
        }
        .alert("删除全部内容？", isPresented: $confirmDeleteAll) {
            Button("删除", role: .destructive) { deleteAll() }
            Button("取消", role: .cancel) {}
        } message: {
            Text("会删除当前文件夹下的全部 \(entries.count) 项，无法恢复。")
        }
        .sheet(item: $editing) { entry in
            FileEditorSheet(url: entry.url) { reload() }
        }
        .onAppear(perform: reload)
    }

    @ViewBuilder
    private func row(_ entry: Entry) -> some View {
        HStack {
            if selecting {
                Image(systemName: selection.contains(entry.id) ? "checkmark.circle.fill" : "circle")
                    .foregroundColor(selection.contains(entry.id) ? .blue : .secondary)
            }
            Image(systemName: entry.isDirectory ? "folder.fill" : "doc.text")
                .foregroundColor(entry.isDirectory ? .blue : .secondary)
            VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 2) {
                Text(entry.name).lineLimit(1)
                Text(subtitle(entry)).font(.caption2).foregroundColor(.secondary)
            }
            Spacer()
        }
        .contentShape(Rectangle())
        .onTapGesture {
            if selecting {
                if selection.contains(entry.id) { selection.remove(entry.id) } else { selection.insert(entry.id) }
            } else if entry.isDirectory {
                current = entry.url
                reload()
            } else {
                editing = entry
            }
        }
        .contextMenu {
            Button { editing = entry } label: { Label(entry.isDirectory ? "查看" : "打开", systemImage: "eye") }
            Button(role: .destructive) { delete([entry]) } label: { Label("删除", systemImage: "trash") }
        }
    }

    private func subtitle(_ entry: Entry) -> String {
        let size = ByteCountFormatter.string(fromByteCount: entry.size, countStyle: .file)
        let formatter = DateFormatter()
        formatter.dateFormat = "MM-dd HH:mm"
        return entry.isDirectory ? formatter.string(from: entry.modified) : "\(size) · \(formatter.string(from: entry.modified))"
    }

    private func reload() {
        let fm = FileManager.default
        do {
            let urls = try fm.contentsOfDirectory(at: current,
                                                  includingPropertiesForKeys: [.isDirectoryKey, .fileSizeKey, .contentModificationDateKey],
                                                  options: [.skipsHiddenFiles])
            entries = urls.compactMap { url in
                guard let values = try? url.resourceValues(forKeys: [.isDirectoryKey, .fileSizeKey, .contentModificationDateKey]) else { return nil }
                return Entry(url: url,
                             isDirectory: values.isDirectory ?? false,
                             size: Int64(values.fileSize ?? 0),
                             modified: values.contentModificationDate ?? Date())
            }
            .sorted { a, b in
                if a.isDirectory != b.isDirectory { return a.isDirectory }
                return a.name.localizedStandardCompare(b.name) == .orderedAscending
            }
            error = nil
        } catch {
            entries = []
            self.error = error.localizedDescription
        }
    }

    private func createFolder() {
        let name = WorkspaceStore.sanitize(newFolder)
        guard !name.isEmpty else { return }
        do {
            try FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: current.appendingPathComponent(name), withIntermediateDirectories: true)
            reload()
        } catch {
            self.error = error.localizedDescription
        }
    }

    private func delete(_ targets: [Entry]) {
        for entry in targets where entry.url.standardizedFileURL != root.standardizedFileURL {
            try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: entry.url)
        }
        selection.removeAll()
        reload()
    }

    private func deleteSelected() {
        delete(entries.filter { selection.contains($0.id) })
    }

    private func deleteAll() {
        delete(entries)
        selecting = false
        confirmDeleteAll = false
    }
}

/// Simple text editor for a file in a workspace.
struct FileEditorSheet: View {
    let url: URL
    let onSave: () -> Void
    @Environment(\.presentationMode) private var presentation
    @State private var text = ""
    @State private var editable = false
    @State private var message: String?

    var body: some View {
        NavigationView {
            VStack(spacing: 0) {
                if editable {
                    TextEditor(text: $text)
                        .font(.system(.body, design: .monospaced))
                        .autocapitalization(.none)
                        .disableAutocorrection(true)
                } else {
                    ScrollView {
                        Text(text).font(.system(.body, design: .monospaced)).frame(maxWidth: .infinity, alignment: .leading).padding()
                    }
                }
                if let message = message {
                    Text(message).font(.caption).foregroundColor(.secondary).padding(.bottom, 6)
                }
            }
            .navigationTitle(url.lastPathComponent)
            .navigationBarTitleDisplayMode(.inline)
            .toolbar {
                ToolbarItem(placement: .cancellationAction) {
                    Button("关闭") { presentation.wrappedValue.dismiss() }
                }
                ToolbarItem(placement: .confirmationAction) {
                    if editable {
                        Button("保存") { save() }
                    } else {
                        Button("编辑") { editable = true }
                    }
                }
            }
            .onAppear(perform: load)
        }
        .navigationViewStyle(.stack)
    }

    private func load() {
        let size = (try? FileManager.default.attributesOfItem(atPath: url.path)[.size] as? NSNumber)?.intValue ?? 0
        if size > 1_000_000 {
            text = "文件较大（\(ByteCountFormatter.string(fromByteCount: Int64(size), countStyle: .file))），不在 App 内编辑。"
            editable = false
            return
        }
        if let content = try? String(contentsOf: url, encoding: .utf8) {
            text = content
            editable = true
        } else {
            text = "不是 UTF-8 文本文件，无法在这里编辑。"
            editable = false
        }
    }

    private func save() {
        do {
            try text.write(to: url, atomically: true, encoding: .utf8)
            message = "已保存"
            onSave()
        } catch {
            message = "保存失败：\(error.localizedDescription)"
        }
    }
}
```

`apple/Sources/Workspaces.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation

/// Local addresses the app can advertise for LAN access.
enum NetworkInfo {

    /// Best guess at the Wi-Fi/Ethernet IPv4 address (en0 first).
    static func primaryIPv4() -> String? {
        var addresses = allIPv4()
        if let en0 = addresses.first(where: { $0.interface == "en0" }) { return en0.address }
        return addresses.first?.address
    }

    static func allIPv4() -> [(interface: String, address: String)] {
        var result: [(String, String)] = []
        var ifaddr: UnsafeMutablePointer<ifaddrs>?
        guard getifaddrs(&ifaddr) == 0, let first = ifaddr else { return [] }
        defer { freeifaddrs(ifaddr) }
        var pointer = first
        while true {
            let flags = Int32(pointer.pointee.ifa_flags)
            let addr = pointer.pointee.ifa_addr
            if let addr = addr, (flags & IFF_UP) == IFF_UP, (flags & IFF_LOOPBACK) == 0,
               addr.pointee.sa_family == UInt8(AF_INET) {
                var host = [CChar](repeating: 0, count: Int(NI_MAXHOST))
                let len = socklen_t(addr.pointee.sa_len)
                if getnameinfo(addr, len, &host, socklen_t(host.count), nil, 0, NI_NUMERICHOST) == 0 {
                    let name = String(cString: pointer.pointee.ifa_name)
                    result.append((name, String(cString: host)))
                }
            }
            guard let next = pointer.pointee.ifa_next else { break }
            pointer = next
        }
        return result
    }
}

/// Workspaces are plain directories under Documents/Workspaces. They double as
/// the harness "container" root (the node process runs with this directory as
/// its cwd), so the Web UI's workspace picker shows exactly what the app lists.
final class WorkspaceStore: ObservableObject {

    struct Workspace: Identifiable, Hashable {
        let id: String
        let url: URL
        var name: String { url.lastPathComponent }
        var fileCount: Int
        var bytes: Int64
        var modified: Date
        /// Registered by the harness (e.g. created in the Web UI) rather than a
        /// directory this app manages under Workspaces/.
        var fromHarness: Bool = false
    }

    @Published private(set) var items: [Workspace] = []
    @Published var lastError: String?

    var root: URL { Paths.workspaces }

    init() { reload() }

    func reload() {
        let fm = FileManager.default
        var found: [Workspace] = []
        if let entries = try? fm.contentsOfDirectory(at: root, includingPropertiesForKeys: [.isDirectoryKey, .contentModificationDateKey], options: [.skipsHiddenFiles]) {
            for url in entries {
                let values = try? url.resourceValues(forKeys: [.isDirectoryKey, .contentModificationDateKey])
                guard values?.isDirectory == true else { continue }
                let stats = Self.stats(of: url)
                found.append(Workspace(id: url.path,
                                       url: url,
                                       fileCount: stats.count,
                                       bytes: stats.bytes,
                                       modified: values?.contentModificationDate ?? Date()))
            }
        }
        // Workspaces the harness knows about (created in the Web UI) also belong
        // in this list: read its registry and surface every existing directory.
        let known = Set(found.map { canonicalPath($0.url.path) })
        for path in registeredWorkspacePaths() where !known.contains(canonicalPath(path.path)) {
            let stats = Self.stats(of: path)
            let values = try? path.resourceValues(forKeys: [.contentModificationDateKey])
            found.append(Workspace(id: path.path,
                                   url: path,
                                   fileCount: stats.count,
                                   bytes: stats.bytes,
                                   modified: values?.contentModificationDate ?? Date(),
                                   fromHarness: true))
        }
        items = found.sorted {
            if $0.fromHarness != $1.fromHarness { return !$0.fromHarness }
            return $0.name.localizedStandardCompare($1.name) == .orderedAscending
        }
    }

    /// Every existing directory path mentioned in the harness' workspace
    /// registry (`DSH_HOME/storages/workspace.json`). Parsed defensively: the
    /// storage format is internal to the harness, so we only lift path-like
    /// strings out of it and never write to that file.
    func registeredWorkspacePaths() -> [URL] {
        let file = Paths.dshHome.appendingPathComponent("storages/workspace.json")
        guard let data = try? Data(contentsOf: file),
              let root = try? JSONSerialization.jsonObject(with: data) else { return [] }
        var paths = Set<String>()
        func walk(_ node: Any) {
            if let text = node as? String {
                if text.hasPrefix("/") { paths.insert(text) }
            } else if let dict = node as? [String: Any] {
                dict.values.forEach(walk)
            } else if let array = node as? [Any] {
                array.forEach(walk)
            }
        }
        walk(root)
        return paths.compactMap { path -> URL? in
            var isDirectory: ObjCBool = false
            guard FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: path, isDirectory: &isDirectory),
                  isDirectory.boolValue else { return nil }
            return URL(fileURLWithPath: path)
        }.sorted { $0.path < $1.path }
    }


    static func stats(of url: URL) -> (count: Int, bytes: Int64) {
        var count = 0
        var bytes: Int64 = 0
        let keys: [URLResourceKey] = [.isRegularFileKey, .fileSizeKey]
        if let walker = FileManager.default.enumerator(at: url, includingPropertiesForKeys: keys, options: [.skipsHiddenFiles]) {
            for case let file as URL in walker {
                guard let v = try? file.resourceValues(forKeys: Set(keys)) else { continue }
                if v.isRegularFile == true {
                    count += 1
                    bytes += Int64(v.fileSize ?? 0)
                }
            }
        }
        return (count, bytes)
    }

    @discardableResult
    func create(name rawName: String, template: Bool = true) -> Workspace? {
        let name = Self.sanitize(rawName)
        guard !name.isEmpty else { lastError = "名称不能为空"; return nil }
        let url = root.appendingPathComponent(name, isDirectory: true)
        guard !FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: url.path) else {
            lastError = "同名工作区已存在"; return nil
        }
        do {
            try FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: url, withIntermediateDirectories: true)
            if template {
                let readme = """
                # \(name)

                这是 App 内置的独立工作区（容器）。DeepSeek 的会话在这个目录里读写文件。

                - 目录位置：Documents/Workspaces/\(name)
                - 可以在「文件」App 里直接放入/取出文件（App 已开启文件共享）
                - 删除工作区会连同其中文件一起删除，请先备份
                """
                try readme.write(to: url.appendingPathComponent("README.md"), atomically: true, encoding: .utf8)
            }
            reload()
            lastError = nil
            return items.first { $0.name == name }
        } catch {
            lastError = error.localizedDescription
            return nil
        }
    }

    func delete(_ workspace: Workspace) {
        do {
            try FileManager.default.removeItem(at: workspace.url)
            reload()
            lastError = nil
        } catch {
            lastError = error.localizedDescription
        }
    }

    func rename(_ workspace: Workspace, to newName: String) {
        let name = Self.sanitize(newName)
        guard !name.isEmpty else { lastError = "名称不能为空"; return }
        let target = root.appendingPathComponent(name, isDirectory: true)
        guard !FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: target.path) else { lastError = "同名工作区已存在"; return }
        do {
            try FileManager.default.moveItem(at: workspace.url, to: target)
            reload()
        } catch {
            lastError = error.localizedDescription
        }
    }

    /// The app no longer seeds a `default` workspace: the user starts empty and
    /// creates what they need.
    func ensureDefault() -> Workspace? { nil }

    static func sanitize(_ name: String) -> String {
        let trimmed = name.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
        let allowed = CharacterSet.alphanumerics.union(CharacterSet(charactersIn: "-_ ."))
        let filtered = trimmed.unicodeScalars.filter { allowed.contains($0) }.map(String.init).joined()
        return filtered.replacingOccurrences(of: "/", with: "-").prefix(48).description
    }

    static func humanBytes(_ bytes: Int64) -> String {
        let f = ByteCountFormatter()
        f.countStyle = .file
        return f.string(fromByteCount: bytes)
    }
}

/// POSIX `realpath`. iOS mounts the data partition at `/private/var` with
/// `/var` as a symlink, and the harness compares the workspace record's `path`
/// against `fs.realpath(cwd)` exactly — an uncanonicalised `/var/...` path never
/// matches, and a session can never attach ("its cwd resolves to ...").
func canonicalPath(_ path: String) -> String {
    guard let resolved = realpath(path, nil) else { return path }
    defer { free(resolved) }
    return String(cString: resolved)
}

/// Writes app workspaces into the harness' own workspace registry
/// (`DSH_HOME/storages/workspace.json`, domain `workspace` v2) so the Web UI
/// lists the folders this app created, and vice versa.
///
/// Only called while the harness is stopped: it owns that file while running and
/// would overwrite our additions on its next flush.
enum WorkspaceRegistryWriter {

    struct Record: Codable {
        var path: String
        var title: String
        var sessionIds: [String]
        var createdAt: String
        var updatedAt: String
    }

    /// Registers every directory under `root` that the registry does not know
    /// yet. Returns how many were added.
    @discardableResult
    static func sync(root: URL, registryFile: URL) throws -> Int {
        let fm = FileManager.default
        let iso = ISO8601DateFormatter()
        iso.formatOptions = [.withInternetDateTime, .withFractionalSeconds]
        let now = iso.string(from: Date())

        var document: [String: Any]
        if let data = try? Data(contentsOf: registryFile),
           let parsed = try? JSONSerialization.jsonObject(with: data) as? [String: Any] {
            document = parsed
        } else {
            document = [
                "unit": ["name": "workspace", "version": 2],
                "global": ["initialized": true, "workspaceIds": [String](),
                           "archivedSessionIds": [String](), "pinnedSessionIds": [String]()],
                "tables": ["workspaces": [String: Any]()],
            ]
        }

        guard var global = document["global"] as? [String: Any],
              var tables = document["tables"] as? [String: Any],
              var workspaces = tables["workspaces"] as? [String: Any] else { return 0 }
        var ids = global["workspaceIds"] as? [String] ?? []

        var knownPaths = Set<String>()
        var repaired = 0
        for (id, value) in workspaces {
            guard var record = value as? [String: Any],
                  let stored = record["path"] as? String else { continue }
            let canonical = canonicalPath(stored)
            if canonical != stored {
                // Earlier builds stored `/var/...`; the harness needs the
                // `/private/var/...` realpath or no session can attach.
                record["path"] = canonical
                record["updatedAt"] = now
                workspaces[id] = record
                repaired += 1
            }
            knownPaths.insert(canonical)
        }

        let entries = (try? fm.contentsOfDirectory(at: root,
                                                  includingPropertiesForKeys: [.isDirectoryKey],
                                                  options: [.skipsHiddenFiles])) ?? []
        var added = 0
        for entry in entries {
            guard (try? entry.resourceValues(forKeys: [.isDirectoryKey]))?.isDirectory == true else { continue }
            let canonical = canonicalPath(entry.path)
            guard !knownPaths.contains(canonical) else { continue }
            let id = UUID().uuidString.lowercased()
            workspaces[id] = [
                "path": canonical,
                "title": entry.lastPathComponent,
                "sessionIds": [String](),
                "createdAt": now,
                "updatedAt": now,
            ]
            ids.append(id)
            knownPaths.insert(canonical)
            added += 1
        }
        guard added > 0 || repaired > 0 else { return 0 }

        global["initialized"] = true
        global["workspaceIds"] = ids
        tables["workspaces"] = workspaces
        document["global"] = global
        document["tables"] = tables
        document["unit"] = ["name": "workspace", "version": 2]

        let data = try JSONSerialization.data(withJSONObject: document, options: [.prettyPrinted, .sortedKeys])
        try fm.createDirectory(at: registryFile.deletingLastPathComponent(), withIntermediateDirectories: true)
        let tmp = registryFile.appendingPathExtension("ios-tmp")
        try data.write(to: tmp, options: .atomic)
        _ = try? fm.removeItem(at: registryFile)
        try fm.moveItem(at: tmp, to: registryFile)
        return added + (repaired > 0 ? 1000 : 0)
    }
}
```

```md
# 上游源码固定（DeepSeek）

本项目的 DeepSeek 功能 **只** 来自以下官方仓库，不含任何自创/第三方实现：

| 项 | 值 |
| --- | --- |
| 仓库 | https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness |
| 名称 | `@deepseek-ai/dsh`（DeepSeek agent harness，"Everything is a Plugin"） |
| 许可 | MIT |
| 固定 commit | `fcc0ad462172522aaf5c93a7b2e01f6b9f0b0159` |
| commit 日期 | 2026-11-04T08:40:00Z |
| 对应版本 | `0.2.2-rc.1`（= 该 commit 的 release merge，npm `latest`） |
| 官网 | https://www.deepseek.com/en/harness/ |

构建方式见 `deepseek/build-runtime.sh`：`git clone` 该 commit → `pnpm install --frozen-lockfile`
→ `pnpm run build` → 用 pnpm 从 workspace 产物装配出可直接运行的 `dsh` 运行树（不改一行源码）。

> 本仓库对 DeepSeek 源码 **零修改**：所有补丁只作用于 Node.js 运行时（Apple 无 iOS 构建）。
```
