`deepseek/build-runtime.sh`:

```sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Build the DeepSeek (dsh) runtime tree for iOS from the pinned OFFICIAL source.
#
# Nothing here modifies DeepSeek source: we clone the pinned upstream commit,
# install with the upstream lockfile, run the upstream build, then let pnpm
# materialise a production tree for @deepseek-ai/dsh and repair any package the
# workspace `link:` overrides leave outside that closure.
#
# Usage: deepseek/build-runtime.sh <prepare|install|build|assemble|prune|verify|all>
set -euo pipefail

ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")/.." && pwd)"
UPSTREAM_REPO="${UPSTREAM_REPO:-https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness.git}"
UPSTREAM_COMMIT="$(sed -n 's/^UPSTREAM_COMMIT=//p' "$ROOT/deepseek/upstream.env" | tr -d '"' | tr -d ' ')"
SRC="${SRC:-$ROOT/deepseek/src}"
OUT="${OUT:-$ROOT/deepseek/out}"
TREE="$OUT/runtime"
DSH="$TREE/dsh"

log() { printf '\033[36;1m==> %s\033[0m\n' "$*"; }

prepare() {
  log "cloning official source $UPSTREAM_REPO @ $UPSTREAM_COMMIT"
  rm -rf "$SRC"; mkdir -p "$(dirname "$SRC")"
  git init -q "$SRC"
  git -C "$SRC" remote add origin "$UPSTREAM_REPO"
  git -C "$SRC" fetch -q --depth 1 origin "$UPSTREAM_COMMIT"
  git -C "$SRC" checkout -q FETCH_HEAD
  local got; got="$(git -C "$SRC" rev-parse HEAD)"
  [ "$got" = "$UPSTREAM_COMMIT" ] || { echo "commit mismatch: $got"; exit 1; }
  log "verified upstream HEAD = $got"
  git -C "$SRC" log -1 --format='%H %cI %s'
  log "working tree must be pristine:"
  git -C "$SRC" status --porcelain | head -20
}

install() {
  log "pnpm install (upstream lockfile)"
  cd "$SRC"
  pnpm install --frozen-lockfile --config.confirmModulesPurge=false
}

build() {
  log "pnpm run build (upstream build script)"
  cd "$SRC"
  pnpm run build
}

# Locates a package directory anywhere in the checkout: the root node_modules,
# pnpm's virtual store, or a workspace package that pnpm only links where used.
locate_pkg() {
  local name="$1" hit
  if [ -e "$SRC/node_modules/$name" ]; then
    echo "$SRC/node_modules/$name"; return 0
  fi
  hit="$(find "$SRC/node_modules/.pnpm" -maxdepth 7 -type d -path "*/node_modules/$name" -print 2>/dev/null | head -1)"
  if [ -n "$hit" ]; then echo "$hit"; return 0; fi
  hit="$(grep -rl --include=package.json -F "\"name\": \"$name\"" "$SRC/packages" "$SRC/vendor" "$SRC/native" "$SRC/apps" 2>/dev/null | head -1)"
  if [ -n "$hit" ]; then dirname "$hit"; return 0; fi
  return 1
}

# Boots `dsh web` once against the current tree; returns 0 when the UI URL
# appears in its output. Plugin import failures are aggregated by the harness,
# so one pass reveals many missing packages at once.
boot_web() {
  local home="$OUT/boot-home" logf="$OUT/boot.log"
  rm -rf "$home"; mkdir -p "$home"
  (
    cd "$DSH"
    HOME="$home" DSH_HOME="$home/.dsh" \
      timeout "${BOOT_BUDGET:-40}" node ./lib/bin.js web --no-open --port 3099 --host 127.0.0.1 >"$logf" 2>&1 || true
  )
  grep -qE "dsh web: https?://" "$logf"
}

# pnpm's deploy closure under `link:` overrides misses workspace packages that
# the bundles import lazily at boot. Repairing against a real boot — rather than
# against `--version` — is what makes the shipped tree actually usable.
repair_closure() {
  local pass m src fixed
  local -a missing
  for pass in $(seq 1 40); do
    if boot_web; then
      log "closure complete after $pass boot(s)"
      return 0
    fi
    mapfile -t missing < <(grep -oE "Cannot find package '[^']+'" "$OUT/boot.log" \
      | sed "s/Cannot find package '//; s/'$//" | sort -u)
    if [ "${#missing[@]}" -eq 0 ]; then
      echo "boot failed without a missing-package report:" >&2
      tail -60 "$OUT/boot.log" >&2
      return 1
    fi
    fixed=0
    for m in "${missing[@]}"; do
      if [ -d "$DSH/node_modules/$m" ] && [ ! -L "$DSH/node_modules/$m" ]; then
        continue
      fi
      if ! src="$(locate_pkg "$m")"; then
        echo "cannot locate $m in the checkout" >&2
        continue
      fi
      src="$(readlink -f "$src")"   # never copy the link itself into the tree
      log "closure repair: $m  <-  ${src#$SRC/}"
      mkdir -p "$(dirname "$DSH/node_modules/$m")"
      rm -rf "$DSH/node_modules/$m"
      # Workspace package dirs contain cyclic pnpm links; keep symlinks as
      # symlinks first (cheap, no cycles) and only dereference as a fallback.
      if ! ( set +e; cp -R "$src" "$DSH/node_modules/$m" 2>/dev/null ); then
        rm -rf "$DSH/node_modules/$m"
        ( set +e; cp -RL "$src" "$DSH/node_modules/$m" 2>/dev/null )
      fi
      if [ -f "$DSH/node_modules/$m/package.json" ]; then
        fixed=$((fixed + 1))
      else
        echo "copy of $m did not produce a package" >&2
      fi
    done
    if [ "$fixed" -eq 0 ]; then
      echo "boot still failing and nothing could be repaired:" >&2
      tail -60 "$OUT/boot.log" >&2
      return 1
    fi
  done
  echo "closure did not converge after 40 boots" >&2
  tail -60 "$OUT/boot.log" >&2
  return 1
}

assemble() {
  log "assembling production tree for @deepseek-ai/dsh (pnpm deploy)"
  cd "$SRC"
  rm -rf "$TREE"; mkdir -p "$TREE"
  pnpm --filter @deepseek-ai/dsh deploy --prod --legacy "$DSH" >/dev/null 2>&1 || \
    pnpm --filter @deepseek-ai/dsh deploy --prod "$DSH"
  du -sh "$DSH"
  log "repairing dependency closure against a real boot"
  repair_closure
}

prune() {
  log "pruning non-runtime weight (maps, TypeScript sources, docs, tests)"
  local before after
  before="$(du -sm "$DSH" | cut -f1)"
  find "$DSH" -type f -name '*.map' -delete
  find "$DSH" -type f -name '*.ts' ! -name '*.d.ts' -delete
  find "$DSH" -type f -name '*.tsbuildinfo' -delete
  find "$DSH" -type d \( -name 'test' -o -name 'tests' -o -name '__tests__' -o -name 'docs' -o -name '.git' \) -prune -exec rm -rf {} + 2>/dev/null || true
  find "$DSH" -type f -name '*.md' -delete
  after="$(du -sm "$DSH" | cut -f1)"
  log "pruned ${before} MB -> ${after} MB"
}

normalize() {
  log "normalizing the tree so it is relocatable (no links escaping it)"
  python3 "$ROOT/deepseek/normalize_tree.py" "$DSH"
}

# Verifies the tree from a *relocated copy*: this is what actually ships, and it
# is the only way to catch links that resolved against the build checkout.
verify() {
  log "verifying from a relocated copy (what the phone will see)"
  local probe="$OUT/relocate-probe"
  rm -rf "$probe"; mkdir -p "$probe"
  cp -a "$DSH" "$probe/dsh"
  (
    cd "$probe/dsh"
    node ./lib/bin.js --version
    echo "--- web --help ---"
    # never pipe node into head: EPIPE + pipefail would fail the gate
    node ./lib/bin.js web --help >web-help.txt 2>&1 || true
    head -6 web-help.txt; rm -f web-help.txt
    echo "--- frontend assets ---"
    find . -path '*dsh-web-frontend/dist/index.html' | head -3
    echo "--- files / size ---"
    find . -type f | wc -l
    du -sh .
  )
  rm -rf "$probe"
  log "verify OK"
}

# Boots the harness for real on the build host: proves the assembled tree (and
# its native addon) actually serves, not just that modules resolve.
smoke() {
  log "smoke test: boot \`dsh web\` (45s budget)"
  local home="$OUT/smoke-home" logf="$OUT/smoke.log"
  rm -rf "$home"; mkdir -p "$home"
  (
    cd "$DSH"
    HOME="$home" DSH_HOME="$home/.dsh" \
      timeout 45 node ./lib/bin.js web --no-open --port 3099 --host 127.0.0.1 >"$logf" 2>&1 || true
  )
  echo "--- smoke log (last 200 lines) ---"
  tail -200 "$logf" || true
  echo "--- startup diagnostics ---"
  find "$home" -name 'startup-*.log' | while IFS= read -r f; do
    echo "== $f"
    tail -150 "$f"
  done
  if grep -q "No usable native binding" "$logf"; then
    echo "smoke: native addon missing for the build platform"; return 1
  fi
  if grep -qE "dsh web: https?://" "$logf"; then
    log "smoke: harness served its UI"
    grep -oE "dsh web: https?://[^ ]+" "$logf" | head -2 | sed -E 's/(token=)[^&]+/\1<redacted>/'
    # The UI still serves with plugins disabled, so the URL alone is not a pass:
    # a plugin that could not import is a feature the user will find missing.
    local failed
    failed="$(grep -cE "failed to import|did not activate" "$logf" || true)"
    if [ "${failed:-0}" != "0" ]; then
      echo "smoke: $failed plugin failure line(s):"
      grep -B2 -E "failed to import|did not activate" "$logf" | head -40
      if grep -q "Cannot find package" "$logf"; then
        echo "smoke: missing packages remain -> not shipping"; return 1
      fi
      echo "smoke: failures are not missing-package related; shipping with a warning"
    fi
    log "smoke OK: harness served its UI"
    return 0
  fi
  echo "smoke: no URL line in the log"; return 1
}

case "${1:-all}" in
  prepare) prepare ;;
  install) install ;;
  build) build ;;
  assemble) assemble ;;
  normalize) normalize ;;
  prune) prune ;;
  smoke) smoke ;;
  verify) verify ;;
  all) prepare; install; build; assemble; normalize; prune; normalize; verify; smoke ;;
  *) echo "unknown subcommand: $1"; exit 2 ;;
esac

```

`deepseek/normalize_tree.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Make the assembled DeepSeek runtime tree relocatable.

pnpm's `link:` overrides (and `deploy`'s handling of them) leave symlinks that
escape the tree and point back into the build checkout, e.g.

    node_modules/@deepseek-ai/cosmokit -> ../../../../../src/vendor/cosmokit

Those resolve while the tree still sits inside the checkout, so a naive
`node lib/bin.js --version` "passes" — and then the tree ships to a phone where
the link dangles and the app dies on launch.

This pass guarantees that every symlink inside the tree resolves inside it:

* links that already stay inside are rewritten as relative links (portable),
* links whose target exists only outside the tree are materialised once under
  `node_modules/<name>` and re-pointed at that internal copy,
* links with no target at all (stale pnpm entries for packages the production
  closure never loads) are dropped and reported.

The caller must boot the tree afterwards (build-runtime.sh does) so that
anything that mattered is caught.

Usage: normalize_tree.py <tree-root>
"""
import os
import shutil
import sys

tree = os.path.realpath(sys.argv[1])
tree_prefix = tree + os.sep


def package_name(link_path: str) -> str:
    return link_path.split("/node_modules/")[-1]


def materialise(src: str, name: str) -> str:
    """Copy `src` to the single internal location `node_modules/<name>`."""
    dest = os.path.join(tree, "node_modules", *name.split("/"))
    if os.path.islink(dest):
        os.remove(dest)
    if not os.path.exists(dest):
        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(dest), exist_ok=True)
        tmp = dest + ".normalize-tmp"
        shutil.rmtree(tmp, ignore_errors=True)
        shutil.copytree(src, tmp, symlinks=False, ignore_dangling_symlinks=True)
        os.rename(tmp, dest)
    return dest


def collect_links() -> list:
    links = []
    for root, dirs, files in os.walk(tree):
        for entry in dirs + files:
            path = os.path.join(root, entry)
            if os.path.islink(path):
                links.append(path)
    return links


def main() -> int:
    made_relative = internalised = dropped = 0
    problems = []

    for path in collect_links():
        # An earlier iteration may have replaced this very path with a real
        # directory (materialise) or removed it (stale drop).
        if not os.path.islink(path):
            continue
        real = os.path.realpath(path)
        if real.startswith(tree_prefix) and os.path.exists(real):
            want = os.path.relpath(real, os.path.dirname(path))
            if os.readlink(path) != want:
                os.remove(path)
                os.symlink(want, path)
                made_relative += 1
            continue

        if os.path.isdir(real):
            was_link = os.path.islink(path)
            dest = materialise(real, package_name(path))
            # materialise() already replaced this very path when it was the link
            # pointing at the external directory; otherwise re-point the link.
            if was_link and os.path.islink(path):
                os.remove(path)
                os.symlink(os.path.relpath(dest, os.path.dirname(path)), path)
            internalised += 1
            continue

        # Nothing to point at: a stale pnpm entry. Dropping it is safe as long
        # as the tree still boots, which the caller verifies right after.
        problems.append((path, os.readlink(path)))
        os.remove(path)
        dropped += 1

    print(f"normalize: {made_relative} relative, {internalised} internalised, {dropped} stale dropped")
    if problems:
        print(f"normalize: dropped {len(problems)} unresolved links (boot verification must still pass)")
        for path, target in problems[:10]:
            print(f"  {path} -> {target}")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

```

`docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`:

```md
# 架构与边界

## 一句话

一个 iOS 壳（SwiftUI）+ 一个 iOS 版 Node 运行时 + 官方 DeepSeek 运行树；
壳负责「起进程 / 给链接 / 管容器 / 做诊断」，其余全是官方的。

```
Dsh.app/
├── Dsh                      SwiftUI 壳（本仓库 apple/）
└── Runtime/
    ├── node                 iPhoneOS arm64 Node 26（官方 nodejs/node 源码交叉编译）
    ├── UPSTREAM_COMMIT      DeepSeek 上游 commit（可追溯）
    ├── BUILD-INFO.txt       构建溯源
    └── dsh/                 官方 @deepseek-ai/dsh 及其依赖闭包
        ├── lib/bin.js       dsh CLI 入口
        ├── node_modules/    完整生产依赖
        └── ...
```

进程模型：

```
[App 进程]  ──posix_spawn──▶  [node --jitless dsh web --host 127.0.0.1 --port 3080]
     │                                     │
     │  NWListener 0.0.0.0:3081 ──pipe──▶ 127.0.0.1:3080   （局域网）
     │  WKWebView ──http──▶ 127.0.0.1:3080                  （本机）
     └── 日志/URL/退出码 ◀── pipe ◀── node stdout/stderr
```

## 为什么必须有两个进程

`dsh web` 是一个长驻 Node 服务（HTTP + WebSocket + 文件系统 + 子任务），
用 InProcess 方式把 Node 静态链进 App 也可以，但那要求 `libnode` 与 App 同签名、
并且和 `--jitless` 的 V8 内存模型绑定；独立进程更简单、更接近官方运行方式，
崩溃隔离也更好（崩溃只影响 node，App 可自动拉起）。

代价：需要越狱环境（无沙盒）才能 `posix_spawn`。这也是「越狱专用」的根本原因。

## 为什么局域网要自己做中转

`dsh web --host` **只接受一个具体的本机地址，明确拒绝 0.0.0.0**（上游设计，避免误暴露）。
同时它有一道 Host 校验：只信任 loopback、监听地址本身、以及 `--trusted-host` 声明的 authority。

所以：harness 只监听 127.0.0.1，局域网由 App 的 NWListener 中转（纯字节管道，WebSocket 升级原样透传），
并把当前 Wi‑Fi IP 通过 `--trusted-host` 告知 harness。IP 变化时自动重启以刷新信任列表。

## 为什么关掉 JIT

iOS 不允许普通的 RWX 内存页。iOS 版 V8 需要 `MAP_JIT` 或 mprotect 的 W^X 翻转才能生成机器码，
在不同 A 系列芯片/越狱组合上行为不一致。`--jitless` 让 V8 只用解释器，完全不申请可执行内存，
兼容性最好；对「HTTP 服务 + 流式 JSON」这种 I/O 密集型负载足够用（比 JIT 慢，但可用）。

调试页可以改 `额外 node 参数`；如果你愿意在你的设备上试 JIT，可自行去掉 `--jitless`（不保证）。

## 能力边界（重要，别踩坑）

| 项 | 状态 |
| --- | --- |
| DeepSeek Web UI 全部功能（会话、模型、Agent、工作区、插件 UI） | ✅ |
| 本机 + 局域网访问、token 链接交付 | ✅ |
| 工作区容器创建/删除/重命名 | ✅ |
| 日志、自检、导出、代理、端口、后台保活 | ✅ |
| bash/终端工具 | ⚠️ 依赖设备自带 `/bin/sh` 与工具集，功能有限 |
| PowerShell 工具 | ❌ iOS 上无 pwsh |
| 插件市场在线安装 | ❌ 需要 pnpm 联网安装；App 内运行树只读 |
| 模型权重本地推理 | ❌ 必须是官方 API（V3/R1 数百 GB，设备放不下） |
| 非越狱设备 | ❌ 无法 spawn 子进程 |

## 供应链

| 组件 | 来源 | 修改 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| DeepSeek harness | `deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness` @ `fcc0ad4`（= 0.2.2-rc.1） | **零修改**（CI 会校验 `git status --porcelain` 为空） |
| Node.js | `nodejs.org/dist/v26.13.0` 官方源码包（sha256 固定） | 仅 iOS 交叉编译适配补丁（`runtime/node-ios/patches/`，MIT） |
| 原生 addon `require-builtin` | `deepseek-ai/dsh-node-addon-require-builtin` @ `a58d359`（= npm 0.1.9） | **零修改**，仅用官方源码为 iPhoneOS arm64 重新编译（`runtime/require-builtin-ios/`） |
| iOS 壳 | 本仓库 `apple/` | — |

### 为什么还要自己编一个 .node

harness 启动时会加载 `node-addon-require-builtin`（N-API）。官方 npm 只发布
darwin / linux / win32 的预编译产物，**没有 iOS**，于是：

```
dsh: host preparation failed: No usable native binding found for
     node-addon-require-builtin-ios-arm64 (auto)
```

我们不是替换它，而是拿官方源码（同一 commit）用 `-target arm64-apple-ios15.0 -bundle`
重新编译了一个 `ios-arm64-napi-v9.node`，按官方平台包的目录形态装进运行树
（`runtime/require-builtin-ios/build.sh`），并 ldid 签名。这是「官方代码、为缺失的平台补编产物」，
与他们的 release 流水线为其他平台做的事完全一致。

Node 侧补丁集来源与许可见 `runtime/node-ios/NOTICE.md`。
不做这一步是不行的：Apple 从不提供任何 iOS 版 Node，`configure.py` 里的 `ios` 目标也从未被上游维护。

```

`docs/BUILD.md`:

```md
# 构建说明（全部在 GitHub Actions，无需 Mac）

四条流水线，按依赖顺序：

## 1) `node-ios-runtime.yml` — Node.js for iPhoneOS arm64

- runner：`macos-15`（Xcode 16.4），超时 360 分钟
- 源码：`https://nodejs.org/dist/v26.13.0/node-v26.13.0.tar.gz`（sha256 固定，见脚本头）
- 步骤：`prepare`（下载+校验+打补丁）→ `configure`（`--dest-os=ios --dest-cpu=arm64`）→ `build`（make，带 ccache/GHA cache）→ `package`（ldid 签名 + 静态审计）
- 产物：`ios-node26-runtime`（`node` 二进制 + manifest + SHA256SUMS + 审计报告）

补丁集在 `runtime/node-ios/patches/nodejs26/`，处理的事：gyp 的 ios flavor、iPhoneOS 目标默认值、
c-ares 的 darwin 配置、crypto 的 macOS-only API 守卫、V8 的 iOS 可执行内存模型等。

**注意**：这条流水线最慢（1–3 小时），跑成功后其 `out/Release` 会被 Actions cache 复用，后续小改很快。

## 2) `deepseek-runtime.yml` — 官方 DeepSeek 运行树

- runner：`ubuntu-latest`，约 10 分钟
- `deepseek/build-runtime.sh`：
  1. `prepare`：`git fetch <pinned commit>` 并校验 HEAD 完全一致，打印 `git status --porcelain`（应为空 → 证明零修改）
  2. `install`：`pnpm install --frozen-lockfile`（上游 lockfile）
  3. `build`：`pnpm run build`（上游构建脚本）
  4. `assemble`：`pnpm deploy` 出生产树，然后**闭包修复**——逐个解决 `Cannot find package 'X'`，
     从 checkout 里定位该包并 dereference 复制进树，直到 `dsh --version` 能跑
  5. `prune`：删 `.map`、`.ts`（保留 `.d.ts`）、`test/`、`docs/`、`*.md`
- 产物：`dsh-runtime.tar.gz` + `provenance.txt`

## 3) `build-ipa.yml` — 组装 App

- job `deepseek-runtime`：同上（每次打包都从源码重建，保证可复现）
- job `node-runtime`：从「1)」最近一次**成功**的运行下载 `ios-node26-runtime`
- job `ipa`（`macos-15`）：
  1. `xcodegen generate`（`apple/project.yml`）
  2. `xcodebuild -sdk iphoneos -arch arm64 CODE_SIGNING_ALLOWED=NO`
  3. 把 `Runtime/node`、`Runtime/dsh`、`UPSTREAM_COMMIT`、`BUILD-INFO.txt` 塞进 `Dsh.app/Runtime/`
  4. 打两个 zip：`Payload/Dsh.app` → `dsh-ios-unsigned.ipa`；再把两个可执行文件 `ldid -S entitlements` → `dsh-ios-ldid.ipa`
  5. 发布 Release（`build-<run_number>`）

## 4) `swift-check.yml` — 只编译 App（约 3 分钟）

改 Swift 时用它快速验证，不用等打包。

---

## 本地复现（可选）

需要 macOS + Xcode 才能出 ipa；DeepSeek 运行树在任意 Linux 都能建：

```sh
bash deepseek/build-runtime.sh all      # prepare/install/build/assemble/prune/verify
node deepseek/out/runtime/dsh/lib/bin.js --version
```

App：

```sh
cd apple && xcodegen generate
xcodebuild -project Dsh.xcodeproj -scheme Dsh -sdk iphoneos -arch arm64 \
  CODE_SIGNING_ALLOWED=NO build
```

## 常见 CI 问题

- **`Cannot find package` 修复循环跑满**：某个包既不在 `node_modules` 也不在 `.pnpm` 里 → 看日志里
  `closure repair:` 行，必要时手动把它加进 `assemble()`。
- **macOS runner 磁盘不足**：`Free disk space` 步骤已清理 Xcode 里的非 iPhoneOS 平台；如果 node 构建
  仍 OOM/ENOSPC，把 `cache_epoch` input 加 1 重跑。
- **artifact 找不到**：`3)` 依赖 `1)` 的成功运行；先确认 `1)` 绿。

```

`docs/DEBUG.md`:

```md
# 调试与排障

## 三层诊断

1. **首页状态卡**：状态 / PID / Node 版本 / 端口
2. **日志页**：node 的 stdout+stderr 原样滚动（20000 行环形缓冲），支持关键字过滤、跟随、导出
3. **调试页 → 运行自检**：11 项检查，逐条给结论

自检项：

| 检查 | 说明 |
| --- | --- |
| App 内 Node 二进制 | `Dsh.app/Runtime/node` 是否存在 |
| DeepSeek 运行树 | `Dsh.app/Runtime/dsh/lib/bin.js` 是否存在 |
| `node --version` | 能否 spawn 子进程（沙盒会在这里失败） |
| `--jitless` 解释器可用 | 执行一小段 JS |
| 内置模块加载 | `require('node:sqlite')` |
| DSH_HOME 可写 / 工作区可写 | 文件系统权限 |
| 端口占用 | 127.0.0.1:3080 是否空闲 |
| 局域网地址 | 是否连了 Wi‑Fi |
| 本地链接 | 是否已拿到 token URL |
| 越狱环境 | 是否成功 spawn 出 node 进程 |

## 常见错误

### 1. 一直停在「启动中」，日志里没有任何 node 输出

**原因**：App 被沙盒限制，`posix_spawn` 被拒（自签 7 天证书 / AltStore 安装的 App 就是这样）。

**解决**：换 TrollStore 安装，或按 `docs/INSTALL.md` 方式 C/D 把 App 放到
`/Applications`（rootfull）或 `/var/jb/Applications`（rootless）并用 ldid 带 entitlements 签名。

### 1.5 `Node 二进制不可执行且无法修正权限：你没有将文件"node"存储到文件夹"Runtime"中的权限`

**原因**：安装工具（Sideloadly / AltStore / 重打包 / 某些 TrollStore 路径）把 `Dsh.app/Runtime/node`
的**可执行位弄丢了**，而 iOS 的 app bundle 是只读的，App 没法在里面 `chmod`。

**新版 App（build-22 起）会自动处理**：把 Node 复制到可写的 `Documents/bin/node` 再 chmod +x。
装新版即可；也可以手动「调试 → 修复运行时（重新准备 Node）」。

**如果随后报 `系统拒绝启动 Node（Operation not permitted）`**：那是**沙盒**问题（子进程被禁），
换用 TrollStore 安装，或把 App 放到 `/Applications`／`/var/jb/Applications` 并用 ldid 带 entitlements 签名。

### 2. `node: bad CPU type in executable` / `Exec format error`

**原因**：二进制不是 iOS arm64，或没被正确签名。

**解决**：确认装的是本仓库 Releases 里的 ipa（内含 iPhoneOS arm64 的 node）。
越狱机器上可以 `file Dsh.app/Runtime/node` 应输出 `Mach-O 64-bit executable arm64`，
`vtool -show-build` 里 `platform IOS`。

### 3. `Cannot find package '…'`

**原因**：打包时运行树依赖闭包不完整（我们用 `pnpm deploy` + 闭包修复，理论上已解决）。

**解决**：重新下载最新 ipa；或看 `deepseek/build-runtime.sh` 的 `assemble` 步骤日志。

### 3.5 `No usable native binding found for node-addon-require-builtin-ios-arm64`

**原因**：运行树里缺 iOS 版原生 addon（打包不完整），或它没被签名/签名被破坏。

**解决**：装本仓库 Releases 里完整 ipa。手动检查：

```sh
APP=/var/jb/Applications/Dsh.app
find $APP/Runtime -name '*ios-arm64*' -o -name 'require_builtin.node'
vtool -show-build $APP/Runtime/dsh/node_modules/node-addon-require-builtin-ios-arm64/prebuilt/ios-arm64-napi-v9.node | grep platform
# 期望 platform IOS
```

### 4. 浏览器打开提示 403 / `Forbidden: untrusted host`

**原因**：harness 有 Host 校验。用局域网 IP 访问时，该 authority 必须出现在 `--trusted-host`。

**解决**：App 会自动把当前 Wi‑Fi IP 加进去。若换了网络（IP 变了），点「重启」；
App 也会用 `NWPathMonitor` 侦测到并自动重启。

### 5. 页面能打开但一直转圈 / WebSocket 连不上

**原因**：中转层没起来，或端口冲突。

**解决**：看日志里「局域网中转已就绪」这一行；换一个局域网端口再重启。

### 6. `--jitless` 相关崩溃、`Out of memory`

**原因**：解释器模式下 JS 执行较慢，内存上限过低。

**解决**：调试页把 V8 堆上限调大（默认 1024MB，最大 4096MB）。

### 7. API 报网络错误（尤其在国内网络）

**解决**：调试页填 HTTP 代理，例如 `http://192.168.1.2:7890`（同一 Wi‑Fi 的电脑上跑代理）。

## 手动验证（越狱终端）

```sh
APP=/var/jb/Applications/Dsh.app      # rootless；rootfull 用 /Applications/Dsh.app
cd $APP/Runtime
./node --version
./node --jitless -e 'console.log(process.version, process.platform, process.arch)'
cd dsh && HOME=/tmp ./node --jitless ./lib/bin.js --version
```

能打印版本号就说明运行时没问题，剩下都是参数/网络问题。

## 日志与数据

- 日志导出：调试页「导出日志」→ 存到 `Documents/logs/dsh-log-*.txt`，走系统分享
- 数据：`Documents/dsh-home`（会话、配置、模型）、`Documents/Workspaces`（工作区）
- 重置：调试页「重置 DeepSeek 数据」（保留工作区）

```

`docs/INSTALL.md`:

```md
# 安装（越狱设备）

产物：`dsh-ios-unsigned.ipa`（未签名）与 `dsh-ios-ldid.ipa`（ad-hoc ldid 签名 + 越狱 entitlements）。
两者内容完全相同，只是签名状态不同。**DeepSeek 本体来自官方源码，App 只是容器。**

支持：iPhone / iPad，arm64，**iOS 15.0 及以上**，rootless（Dopamine、palera1n rootless）、roothide、rootfull（palera1n rootful、unc0ver、checkra1n、Taurine）全兼容。

---

## 方式 A：TrollStore（推荐，rootless / roothide / rootfull 通用）

1. 设备已装 TrollStore（iOS 14.0–16.6.1 / 17.0）。
2. 把 `dsh-ios-unsigned.ipa` 传到设备（AirDrop / 文件 App / `scp`）。
3. 用 TrollStore 打开该 ipa → Install。
4. 桌面出现「DeepSeek」图标，点开即自动部署。

TrollStore 会自己完成签名与 entitlements 注入，无需电脑。

## 方式 B：Sideloadly / AltStore / SideStore（需电脑或自签描述文件）

1. 连接设备，把 `dsh-ios-unsigned.ipa` 拖入 Sideloadly。
2. 填入自己的 Apple ID（免费账号 7 天有效期）。
3. Start 安装。

> 注意：这种方式 App 会被**沙盒限制**，无法 `posix_spawn` node 子进程；而且重打包经常会丢掉
> `Runtime/node` 的可执行位。App 会自动把 node 复制到可写目录修好前者，但**沙盒拦子进程是修不掉的**。
> 若「自检」里 `node --version` 失败、状态卡在「启动中」，说明被沙盒拦了，请改用方式 A/C/D。

## 方式 C：rootless（Dopamine / palera1n rootless）手动安装

```sh
# 在设备上（NewTerm / SSH，root 或 sudo）
unzip -o dsh-ios-ldid.ipa -d /tmp/dshipa
mkdir -p /var/jb/Applications
cp -R /tmp/dshipa/Payload/Dsh.app /var/jb/Applications/
chown -R mobile:mobile /var/jb/Applications/Dsh.app
chmod 755 /var/jb/Applications/Dsh.app/Dsh /var/jb/Applications/Dsh.app/Runtime/node
uicache -a          # 或 uicache -p /var/jb/Applications/Dsh.app
```

如果你只有未签名版，先用 ldid 签一下：

```sh
ldid -S/var/jb/usr/share/dsh-entitlements.plist /var/jb/Applications/Dsh.app/Dsh
ldid -S/var/jb/usr/share/dsh-entitlements.plist /var/jb/Applications/Dsh.app/Runtime/node
```

（entitlements 文件见仓库 `apple/entitlements.plist`；`ldid -S` 不带参数也行，但会拿不到 `platform-application`，
在部分设备上会导致无法创建子进程。）

## 方式 D：rootfull（palera1n rootful / unc0ver / checkra1n）

```sh
unzip -o dsh-ios-ldid.ipa -d /tmp/dshipa
cp -R /tmp/dshipa/Payload/Dsh.app /Applications/
chown -R mobile:mobile /Applications/Dsh.app
chmod 755 /Applications/Dsh.app/Dsh /Applications/Dsh.app/Runtime/node
uicache -a
```

## 方式 E：roothide

roothide 的 bootstrap 路径被隐藏，`/var/jb` 实际映射到随机目录。用 roothide 自带的
`jbctl`/`roothide` 环境变量安装：

```sh
source /var/containers/Bundle/tweaksupport/... # 或使用 roothide 提供的 shell 环境
$JBROOT/usr/bin/uicache -a
```

实践中最省事的是**方式 A（TrollStore）**，它与你用哪种越狱无关。

---

## 首次启动

1. 打开 App → 首页状态应自动走到「运行中」，并显示两个链接。
2. 点「网页」标签页 → 进入 DeepSeek Web UI。
3. 在 Web UI 里 **Settings → Models** 填 DeepSeek API Key（[platform.deepseek.com](https://platform.deepseek.com/)）。
4. 点 **Choose workspace** → 选 `Workspaces` 下任意目录（App「工作区」页可创建/删除）。

> 模型推理由 DeepSeek 官方 API 完成；harness（Agent、工具、会话、Web UI）全部跑在这台设备上。
> 也就是说：**iPhone 上不需要、也不可能本地跑满血 DeepSeek 权重**（V3/R1 是数百 GB 级别）。

## 卸载与数据

- 卸载 App 即删除运行环境；数据在 App 沙盒 `Documents/` 内，随之删除。
- 想保留工作区文件：先到「工作区」页复制路径，或开启「文件」App 访问（已开启 `UIFileSharingEnabled`）。

```

`docs/USAGE.md`:

```md
# 使用说明

## 一键部署

打开 App → 「部署」页 → **一键部署并启动**（首次安装会自动执行）。App 会：

1. 准备数据目录（`Documents/dsh-home`、`Documents/Workspaces`、`Documents/tmp`、`Documents/home`）
2. 用内置的 Node 运行时自检（`node --version`）
3. 以 `--host 127.0.0.1 --port 3080` 启动官方 `dsh web`
4. 从 node 输出里解析出带 launch token 的 URL
5. 若开启局域网：在 `0.0.0.0:<局域网端口>` 起一个 TCP 中转转发到 `127.0.0.1:3080`，
   并把设备 IP 作为 `--trusted-host` 传给 harness（否则 harness 的 Host 校验会 403）
6. 把两条链接显示在首页（可复制 / 直接打开 / 局域网链接可显示二维码）

状态卡片四要素：状态、PID、Node 版本、端口。

## 两个链接

| 链接 | 谁可以访问 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 本机 `http://127.0.0.1:3080/?token=…` | 只有本机 | 在 App 的「网页」页或 Safari 打开 |
| 局域网 `http://<设备IP>:3081/?token=…` | 同一 Wi‑Fi 下的任何设备 | 电脑浏览器 / 另一台手机扫码打开 |

开关在「调试」页：**开启局域网访问**。端口都可改。

> 链接里带一次性 launch token，换成 cookie 后就能直接访问根路径。别把链接发到公开群里。

## 配置模型（必做一次）

1. 「网页」页 → 左下角 **Settings → Models**
2. 粘贴 [platform.deepseek.com](https://platform.deepseek.com/) 的 API Key → Save
3. 立即生效，无需重启

也支持自定义 OpenAI 兼容端点（`docs/user/guide/providers.md`）。

## 工作区 / 容器

「工作区」页是 App 侧的容器管理：

- **新建**：`Documents/Workspaces/<名字>/`，自动写一份 README 说明
- **删除**：连同里面所有文件删除（不可恢复）
- **重命名**、**复制路径**

harness 的 node 进程以 `Documents/Workspaces` 作为工作目录，所以在 Web UI 里点
**Choose workspace** 打开目录浏览器，看到的就是这些容器目录。
选中哪个，Agent 就只能在那个目录里读写（每个会话独立、互不干扰）。

文件进出：App 已开启 `UIFileSharingEnabled`，「文件」App → 我的 iPhone → DeepSeek 里可直接拖入。

## 权限与工具能力

harness 的工具是在**设备上**执行的：

| 工具 | iOS 上能否用 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 文件读写/搜索 | ✅ | 沙盒内 `Documents/Workspaces` |
| 网页搜索/抓取 | ✅ | 需网络 |
| 子任务、计划、Todo、Present | ✅ | 纯 JS |
| bash / 终端 / PowerShell | ⚠️ 部分 | 依赖设备的 `/bin/sh`。越狱设备通常有（Procursus `/var/jb/usr/bin` 已加入 PATH），但 iOS 缺少大量 GNU 工具 |
| 定时任务 | ✅ | 需 App 保持运行 |
| MCP | ✅ | 纯 Node |

## 后台运行

iOS 会挂起后台 App。默认开启「后台保活」（静音音频循环），让服务在锁屏/切后台后继续可用；
耗电会上升，可随时关掉。关掉后：切回前台时 App 会自动重启服务。

## 常见用法

- **给局域网里的电脑用**：部署后复制局域网链接到电脑浏览器，或用二维码手机扫码。
- **换端口**：调试页改端口 → 回部署页点「重启」。
- **看 Agent 在干什么**：Web UI 里点开工具调用；或看 App 的「日志」页。
- **出问题先做的三件事**：看日志 → 运行自检 → 导出日志。

```

`runtime/busybox-ios/build.sh`:

```sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Cross-compile busybox for iPhoneOS arm64 — a real userland (ash + applets).
#
# Built with `allnoconfig` and a curated applet list: the default config pulls in
# Linux-only modules (console-tools, init, networking daemons) that either need
# syscalls iOS lacks or break on Apple's `ar`. Everything the harness' bash tool
# and an agent actually use is enabled below.
#
# Usage: build.sh --artifact <dir>
set -euo pipefail

HERE="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
ROOT="$(cd "$HERE/../.." && pwd)"
BUSYBOX_VERSION="${BUSYBOX_VERSION:-1.36.1}"
IOS_TARGET="${IOS_TARGET:-15.0}"

ARTIFACT=""
if [ "${1:-}" = "--artifact" ]; then ARTIFACT="$(pwd)/${2:?artifact dir required}"; fi
OUT="${OUT_DIR:-$ROOT/.work/busybox-ios}"

log() { printf '\033[36;1m==> %s\033[0m\n' "$*"; }

fetch() {
  mkdir -p "$OUT"
  if [ ! -d "$OUT/src" ]; then
    log "busybox $BUSYBOX_VERSION source"
    curl -fsSL "https://busybox.net/downloads/busybox-$BUSYBOX_VERSION.tar.bz2" | tar xj -C "$OUT"
    mv "$OUT/busybox-$BUSYBOX_VERSION" "$OUT/src"
  fi
}

configure() {
  cd "$OUT/src"
  make distclean >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
  log "defconfig (every symbol gets a line, so it can be toggled by sed)"
  make defconfig >"$OUT/defconfig.log" 2>&1

  log "enabling the userland applets"
  enabled=0
  missing=""
  enable() {
    for name in "$@"; do
      if grep -q "^# ${name} is not set\|^${name}=" .config; then
        sed -i '' "s/^# ${name} is not set/${name}=y/; s/^${name}=m/${name}=y/" .config
        enabled=$((enabled + 1))
      else
        missing="$missing $name"
      fi
    done
  }
  prune() {
    for name in "$@"; do
      sed -i '' "s/^${name}=y/# ${name} is not set/" .config 2>/dev/null || true
    done
  }

  # shell
  enable CONFIG_ASH CONFIG_ASH_BASH_COMPAT CONFIG_ASH_CMDCMD CONFIG_ASH_ECHO \
         CONFIG_ASH_TEST CONFIG_SH_IS_ASH CONFIG_ASH_EXPAND_PRMT
  # archival: keeps the module non-empty and gives tar/gzip
  enable CONFIG_TAR CONFIG_GZIP CONFIG_GUNZIP CONFIG_UNZIP CONFIG_CPIO CONFIG_BZIP2
  # coreutils
  enable CONFIG_LS CONFIG_CAT CONFIG_CP CONFIG_MV CONFIG_RM CONFIG_MKDIR CONFIG_RMDIR \
         CONFIG_TOUCH CONFIG_CHMOD CONFIG_CHOWN CONFIG_LN CONFIG_STAT CONFIG_DU CONFIG_DF \
         CONFIG_HEAD CONFIG_TAIL CONFIG_WC CONFIG_CUT CONFIG_SORT CONFIG_UNIQ CONFIG_TR \
         CONFIG_TEE CONFIG_BASENAME CONFIG_DIRNAME CONFIG_REALPATH CONFIG_READLINK \
         CONFIG_MKTEMP CONFIG_SEQ CONFIG_EXPR CONFIG_PRINTF CONFIG_ECHO CONFIG_TEST \
         CONFIG_TRUE CONFIG_FALSE CONFIG_NOFORK CONFIG_SLEEP CONFIG_DATE CONFIG_SYNC \
         CONFIG_PWD CONFIG_ID CONFIG_WHOAMI CONFIG_UNAME CONFIG_WHICH CONFIG_ENV \
         CONFIG_PRINTENV CONFIG_PS CONFIG_KILL CONFIG_MD5SUM CONFIG_SHA1SUM CONFIG_SHA256SUM \
         CONFIG_MKDIR CONFIG_STTY CONFIG_CHGRP
  # text processing
  enable CONFIG_GREP CONFIG_EGREP CONFIG_FGREP CONFIG_SED CONFIG_AWK CONFIG_DIFF \
         CONFIG_FIND CONFIG_XARGS CONFIG_LESS CONFIG_VI CONFIG_CMP CONFIG_COMM CONFIG_OD \
         CONFIG_HEXDUMP CONFIG_STRINGS CONFIG_PASTE CONFIG_JOIN CONFIG_FOLD CONFIG_EXPAND \
         CONFIG_UNEXPAND CONFIG_SPLIT
  # Only coreutils/shell/findutils/grep/sed/archival/libbb are kept: every
  # applet declared outside them needs headers or syscalls iOS does not have.
  prune CONFIG_ACPID CONFIG_ADDGROUP CONFIG_ADDUSER CONFIG_ADD_SHELL \
        CONFIG_ADJTIMEX CONFIG_ARP CONFIG_ARPING CONFIG_ASCII \
        CONFIG_AWK CONFIG_BBCONFIG CONFIG_BB_SYSCTL CONFIG_BC \
        CONFIG_BEEP CONFIG_BLKDISCARD CONFIG_BLKID CONFIG_BLOCKDEV \
        CONFIG_BOOTCHARTD CONFIG_BRCTL CONFIG_CAL CONFIG_CHAT \
        CONFIG_CHATTR CONFIG_CHCON CONFIG_CHPASSWD CONFIG_CHPST \
        CONFIG_CHRT CONFIG_CHVT CONFIG_CLEAR CONFIG_CMP \
        CONFIG_CONSPY CONFIG_CROND CONFIG_CRONTAB CONFIG_CRYPTPW \
        CONFIG_DC CONFIG_DEALLOCVT CONFIG_DEFAULT_SETFONT_DIR CONFIG_DELGROUP \
        CONFIG_DELUSER CONFIG_DEPMOD CONFIG_DEVFSD CONFIG_DEVFSD_FG_NP \
        CONFIG_DEVFSD_MODLOAD CONFIG_DEVFSD_VERBOSE CONFIG_DEVMEM CONFIG_DIFF \
        CONFIG_DMESG CONFIG_DNSD CONFIG_DNSDOMAINNAME CONFIG_DUMPKMAP \
        CONFIG_ED CONFIG_EJECT CONFIG_ENVDIR CONFIG_ENVUIDGID \
        CONFIG_ETHER_WAKE CONFIG_FAKEIDENTD CONFIG_FALLOCATE CONFIG_FATATTR \
        CONFIG_FBSET CONFIG_FBSPLASH CONFIG_FDFLUSH CONFIG_FDFORMAT \
        CONFIG_FDISK CONFIG_FDISK_SUPPORT_LARGE_DISKS CONFIG_FEATURE_ACPID_COMPAT CONFIG_FEATURE_ADDUSER_TO_GROUP \
        CONFIG_FEATURE_AIX_LABEL CONFIG_FEATURE_AWK_GNU_EXTENSIONS CONFIG_FEATURE_AWK_LIBM CONFIG_FEATURE_BC_INTERACTIVE \
        CONFIG_FEATURE_BC_LONG_OPTIONS CONFIG_FEATURE_BEEP_FREQ CONFIG_FEATURE_BEEP_LENGTH_MS CONFIG_FEATURE_BLKID_TYPE \
        CONFIG_FEATURE_BOOTCHARTD_BLOATED_HEADER CONFIG_FEATURE_BOOTCHARTD_CONFIG_FILE CONFIG_FEATURE_BRCTL_FANCY CONFIG_FEATURE_BRCTL_SHOW \
        CONFIG_FEATURE_CALL_TELINIT CONFIG_FEATURE_CHAT_CLR_ABORT CONFIG_FEATURE_CHAT_IMPLICIT_CR CONFIG_FEATURE_CHAT_NOFAIL \
        CONFIG_FEATURE_CHAT_SEND_ESCAPES CONFIG_FEATURE_CHAT_SWALLOW_OPTS CONFIG_FEATURE_CHAT_TTY_HIFI CONFIG_FEATURE_CHAT_VAR_ABORT_LEN \
        CONFIG_FEATURE_CHECK_NAMES CONFIG_FEATURE_COMPRESS_BBCONFIG CONFIG_FEATURE_CROND_CALL_SENDMAIL CONFIG_FEATURE_CROND_D \
        CONFIG_FEATURE_CROND_DIR CONFIG_FEATURE_CROND_SPECIAL_TIMES CONFIG_FEATURE_DC_BIG CONFIG_FEATURE_DC_LIBM \
        CONFIG_FEATURE_DEFAULT_PASSWD_ALGO CONFIG_FEATURE_DEL_USER_FROM_GROUP CONFIG_FEATURE_DEVFS CONFIG_FEATURE_DIFF_DIR \
        CONFIG_FEATURE_DIFF_LONG_OPTIONS CONFIG_FEATURE_DMESG_PRETTY CONFIG_FEATURE_EJECT_SCSI CONFIG_FEATURE_FANCY_PING \
        CONFIG_FEATURE_FBSET_FANCY CONFIG_FEATURE_FBSET_READMODE CONFIG_FEATURE_FDISK_ADVANCED CONFIG_FEATURE_FDISK_WRITABLE \
        CONFIG_FEATURE_FTPD_ACCEPT_BROKEN_LIST CONFIG_FEATURE_FTPD_AUTHENTICATION CONFIG_FEATURE_FTPD_WRITE CONFIG_FEATURE_FTPGETPUT_LONG_OPTIONS \
        CONFIG_FEATURE_GETOPT_LONG CONFIG_FEATURE_GPT_LABEL CONFIG_FEATURE_HDPARM_GET_IDENTITY CONFIG_FEATURE_HDPARM_HDIO_DRIVE_RESET \
        CONFIG_FEATURE_HDPARM_HDIO_GETSET_DMA CONFIG_FEATURE_HDPARM_HDIO_SCAN_HWIF CONFIG_FEATURE_HDPARM_HDIO_TRISTATE_HWIF CONFIG_FEATURE_HDPARM_HDIO_UNREGISTER_HWIF \
        CONFIG_FEATURE_HTTPD_ACL_IP CONFIG_FEATURE_HTTPD_AUTH_MD5 CONFIG_FEATURE_HTTPD_BASIC_AUTH CONFIG_FEATURE_HTTPD_CGI \
        CONFIG_FEATURE_HTTPD_CONFIG_WITH_SCRIPT_INTERPR CONFIG_FEATURE_HTTPD_DATE CONFIG_FEATURE_HTTPD_ENCODE_URL_STR CONFIG_FEATURE_HTTPD_ERROR_PAGES \
        CONFIG_FEATURE_HTTPD_ETAG CONFIG_FEATURE_HTTPD_GZIP CONFIG_FEATURE_HTTPD_LAST_MODIFIED CONFIG_FEATURE_HTTPD_PORT_DEFAULT \
        CONFIG_FEATURE_HTTPD_PROXY CONFIG_FEATURE_HTTPD_RANGES CONFIG_FEATURE_HTTPD_SETUID CONFIG_FEATURE_HTTPD_SET_REMOTE_PORT_TO_ENV \
        CONFIG_FEATURE_HWCLOCK_ADJTIME_FHS CONFIG_FEATURE_IFCONFIG_BROADCAST_PLUS CONFIG_FEATURE_IFCONFIG_HW CONFIG_FEATURE_IFCONFIG_MEMSTART_IOADDR_IRQ \
        CONFIG_FEATURE_IFCONFIG_SLIP CONFIG_FEATURE_IFCONFIG_STATUS CONFIG_FEATURE_IFUPDOWN_EXTERNAL_DHCP CONFIG_FEATURE_IFUPDOWN_IP \
        CONFIG_FEATURE_IFUPDOWN_IPV4 CONFIG_FEATURE_IFUPDOWN_IPV6 CONFIG_FEATURE_IFUPDOWN_MAPPING CONFIG_FEATURE_INETD_RPC \
        CONFIG_FEATURE_INETD_SUPPORT_BUILTIN_CHARGEN CONFIG_FEATURE_INETD_SUPPORT_BUILTIN_DAYTIME CONFIG_FEATURE_INETD_SUPPORT_BUILTIN_DISCARD CONFIG_FEATURE_INETD_SUPPORT_BUILTIN_ECHO \
        CONFIG_FEATURE_INETD_SUPPORT_BUILTIN_TIME CONFIG_FEATURE_INIT_COREDUMPS CONFIG_FEATURE_INIT_MODIFY_CMDLINE CONFIG_FEATURE_INIT_QUIET \
        CONFIG_FEATURE_INIT_SCTTY CONFIG_FEATURE_INIT_SYSLOG CONFIG_FEATURE_IPCALC_FANCY CONFIG_FEATURE_IPCALC_LONG_OPTIONS \
        CONFIG_FEATURE_IPC_SYSLOG CONFIG_FEATURE_IPC_SYSLOG_BUFFER_SIZE CONFIG_FEATURE_IP_ADDRESS CONFIG_FEATURE_IP_LINK \
        CONFIG_FEATURE_IP_NEIGH CONFIG_FEATURE_IP_RARE_PROTOCOLS CONFIG_FEATURE_IP_ROUTE CONFIG_FEATURE_IP_ROUTE_DIR \
        CONFIG_FEATURE_IP_RULE CONFIG_FEATURE_IP_TUNNEL CONFIG_FEATURE_KILL_DELAY CONFIG_FEATURE_KILL_REMOVED \
        CONFIG_FEATURE_KLOGD_KLOGCTL CONFIG_FEATURE_KMSG_SYSLOG CONFIG_FEATURE_LAST_FANCY CONFIG_FEATURE_LESS_ASK_TERMINAL \
        CONFIG_FEATURE_LESS_BRACKETS CONFIG_FEATURE_LESS_DASHCMD CONFIG_FEATURE_LESS_ENV CONFIG_FEATURE_LESS_FLAGS \
        CONFIG_FEATURE_LESS_LINENUMS CONFIG_FEATURE_LESS_MARKS CONFIG_FEATURE_LESS_MAXLINES CONFIG_FEATURE_LESS_RAW \
        CONFIG_FEATURE_LESS_REGEXP CONFIG_FEATURE_LESS_TRUNCATE CONFIG_FEATURE_LESS_WINCH CONFIG_FEATURE_LOADFONT_PSF2 \
        CONFIG_FEATURE_LOADFONT_RAW CONFIG_FEATURE_LOGREAD_REDUCED_LOCKING CONFIG_FEATURE_LSMOD_PRETTY_2_6_OUTPUT CONFIG_FEATURE_MAKEDEVS_LEAF \
        CONFIG_FEATURE_MAKEDEVS_TABLE CONFIG_FEATURE_MDEV_CONF CONFIG_FEATURE_MDEV_DAEMON CONFIG_FEATURE_MDEV_EXEC \
        CONFIG_FEATURE_MDEV_LOAD_FIRMWARE CONFIG_FEATURE_MDEV_RENAME CONFIG_FEATURE_MDEV_RENAME_REGEXP CONFIG_FEATURE_MESG_ENABLE_ONLY_GROUP \
        CONFIG_FEATURE_MINIX2 CONFIG_FEATURE_MKSWAP_UUID CONFIG_FEATURE_MODPROBE_BLACKLIST CONFIG_FEATURE_MOUNT_CIFS \
        CONFIG_FEATURE_MOUNT_FAKE CONFIG_FEATURE_MOUNT_FLAGS CONFIG_FEATURE_MOUNT_FSTAB CONFIG_FEATURE_MOUNT_HELPERS \
        CONFIG_FEATURE_MOUNT_LABEL CONFIG_FEATURE_MOUNT_NFS CONFIG_FEATURE_MOUNT_OTHERTAB CONFIG_FEATURE_MOUNT_VERBOSE \
        CONFIG_FEATURE_NAMEIF_EXTENDED CONFIG_FEATURE_NETSTAT_PRG CONFIG_FEATURE_NETSTAT_WIDE CONFIG_FEATURE_NOLOGIN \

        CONFIG_FTPPUT CONFIG_HOSTNAME CONFIG_HTTPD CONFIG_IFCONFIG \
        CONFIG_IFDOWN CONFIG_IFENSLAVE CONFIG_IFPLUGD CONFIG_IFUP \
        CONFIG_IFUPDOWN_IFSTATE_PATH CONFIG_INETD CONFIG_IP CONFIG_IPADDR \
        CONFIG_IPCALC CONFIG_IPLINK CONFIG_IPNEIGH CONFIG_IPROUTE \
        CONFIG_IPRULE CONFIG_IPTUNNEL CONFIG_NAMEIF CONFIG_NBDCLIENT \

  log "  requested: $enabled"
  if [ -n "$missing" ]; then log "  not in this config:$missing"; fi

  log "normalising the config"
  yes "" | make oldconfig >"$OUT/oldconfig.log" 2>&1 || true
  echo "    enabled symbols: $(grep -c '=y' .config)"
}

build() {
  local SDK CC
  SDK="$(xcrun --sdk iphoneos --show-sdk-path)"
  CC="$(xcrun --sdk iphoneos -f clang)"
  cd "$OUT/src"
  yes "" | make oldconfig >/dev/null 2>&1 || true

  log "host build tools (wrapper forces the macOS SDK)"
  MACSDK="$(xcrun --sdk macosx --show-sdk-path)"
  HOSTCC_WRAPPER="$OUT/hostcc"
  cat > "$HOSTCC_WRAPPER" <<WRAP
#!/bin/sh
exec "$(xcrun -f clang)" -isysroot "$MACSDK" "\$@"
WRAP
  chmod +x "$HOSTCC_WRAPPER"
  echo "macOS SDK: $MACSDK"
  "$HOSTCC_WRAPPER" -E -x c - </dev/null >/dev/null && echo "host compiler ok"
  make -j1 V=1 HOSTCC="$HOSTCC_WRAPPER" scripts/basic/fixdep >"$OUT/hosttools.log" 2>&1 || {
    echo "---- host tool build failed ----"
    tail -40 "$OUT/hosttools.log"
    exit 1
  }

  log "ar wrapper (Apple's ar rejects an archive with no members)"
  "$(xcrun -f clang)" -c -x c /dev/null -o "$OUT/empty.o"
  AR_WRAPPER="$OUT/arwrap"
  cat > "$AR_WRAPPER" <<WRAP
#!/bin/sh
# busybox builds one built-in.o per module; a module whose applets are all
# disabled has no members, which Apple's ar refuses. Archive a stub instead.
sub="\$1"; shift
archive="\$1"; shift
if [ \$# -eq 0 ]; then
  exec "$(xcrun -f ar)" "\$sub" "\$archive" "$OUT/empty.o"
fi
exec "$(xcrun -f ar)" "\$sub" "\$archive" "\$@"
WRAP
  chmod +x "$AR_WRAPPER"

  log "shimming POSIX calls iOS marks unavailable"
  patch_src() {
    local file="$1" from="$2" to="$3"
    [ -f "$file" ] || return 0
    grep -q "$from" "$file" || return 0
    sed -i '' "s/$from/$to/g" "$file"
    echo "    patched $(basename "$file"): $from -> $to"
  }
  # iOS forbids changing the system clock; busybox' date() only needs to try.
  if [ -f coreutils/date.c ]; then
    sed -i '' '1i\
static int bb_clock_settime_stub(int id, const void *tp) { (void)id; (void)tp; return -1; }
' coreutils/date.c
    patch_src coreutils/date.c 'clock_settime(' 'bb_clock_settime_stub('
  fi

  # Darwin declares inet_ntoa/inet_pton only in <arpa/inet.h>, which Linux gets
  # transitively. Add the include where busybox relies on that.
  if [ -f libbb/xconnect.c ]; then
    grep -q 'arpa/inet.h' libbb/xconnect.c || sed -i '' 's|#include <sys/types.h>|#include <sys/types.h>\
#include <arpa/inet.h>|' libbb/xconnect.c
    echo "    patched xconnect.c: +arpa/inet.h"
  fi

  # sendfile(2) has a different signature on Darwin and no <sys/sendfile.h>;
  # busybox only uses it as a fast path, so make the call fail and let the
  # read/write fallback run.
  if [ -f libbb/copyfd.c ]; then
    sed -i '' '1i\
static long bb_sendfile_stub(int out_fd, int in_fd, long long *offset, unsigned long count) { (void)out_fd; (void)in_fd; (void)offset; (void)count; return -1; }
' libbb/copyfd.c
    patch_src libbb/copyfd.c 'sendfile(' 'bb_sendfile_stub('
  fi

  # awk's system() is unavailable on iOS: report failure instead of spawning.
  if [ -f editors/awk.c ]; then
    sed -i '' '1i\
static int bb_system_stub(const char *cmd) { (void)cmd; return -1; }
' editors/awk.c
    patch_src editors/awk.c 'system(' 'bb_system_stub('
  fi

  log "restricting libs-y to the modules iOS can build"
  python3 - "$PWD/Makefile" <<'PYEOF'
import re, sys
path = sys.argv[1]
text = open(path, encoding='utf-8').read()
keep = ['archival/', 'archival/libarchive/', 'coreutils/', 'coreutils/libcoreutils/',
        'debianutils/', 'findutils/', 'libbb/', 'shell/']
block = 'libs-y\t\t:= \\\n' + ''.join(f'\t\t{k} \\\n' for k in keep)
pattern = re.compile(r'libs-y\t\t:= \\\n(?:\t\t[^\n]*\\\n)*')
text, count = pattern.subn(block, text, count=1)
open(path, 'w', encoding='utf-8').write(text)
print('    libs-y entries kept:', len(keep), '(replacements:', count, ')')
PYEOF

  log "make (single job)"
  make -j1 \
    CC="$CC -target arm64-apple-ios$IOS_TARGET -isysroot $SDK -O2 -Wno-error -I$HERE/compat -include $HERE/compat/ios_compat.h" \
    HOSTCC="$HOSTCC_WRAPPER" \
    AR="$AR_WRAPPER" \
    NM="$(xcrun -f nm)" \
    STRIP="$(xcrun -f strip)" \
    CROSS_COMPILE="" \
    >"$OUT/make.log" 2>&1 || {
      echo "---- last 60 lines ----"
      tail -60 "$OUT/make.log"
      echo "---- first error ----"
      grep -nE "error:|Error [0-9]" "$OUT/make.log" | head -10
      exit 1
    }

  test -f busybox || { echo "busybox not produced"; tail -30 "$OUT/make.log"; exit 1; }
  log "built: $(file busybox)"
  ./busybox 2>&1 | head -2 || true
}

main() {
  fetch
  configure
  build
  if [ -n "$ARTIFACT" ]; then
    rm -rf "$ARTIFACT"; mkdir -p "$ARTIFACT"
    cp "$OUT/src/busybox" "$ARTIFACT/busybox"
    chmod +x "$ARTIFACT/busybox"
    log "artifact: $ARTIFACT"
    ls -l "$ARTIFACT"
    vtool -show-build "$ARTIFACT/busybox" | head -4 || true
  fi
}

main

```

`runtime/busybox-ios/compat/ios_compat.h`:

```h
/* Force-included for every busybox translation unit.
 * Darwin/iOS lacks a few GNU helpers busybox assumes, and marks a handful of
 * POSIX calls unavailable. Keep this header macro-only so it never conflicts
 * with the real declarations. */
#pragma once

/* Never applied to .S files: the compat header is force-included, and one
 * assembly source would otherwise choke on C declarations. */
#ifndef __ASSEMBLER__

/* Darwin still exports inet_aton but no longer declares it. Forward-declare the
 * struct instead of pulling <netinet/in.h> in before the system headers. */
struct in_addr;
extern int inet_aton(const char *cp, struct in_addr *inp);

#ifndef __bswap16
#define __bswap16(x) __builtin_bswap16(x)
#endif
#ifndef __bswap32
#define __bswap32(x) __builtin_bswap32(x)
#endif
#ifndef __bswap64
#define __bswap64(x) __builtin_bswap64(x)
#endif

/* Darwin spells the nanosecond stat fields differently from Linux. */
#ifndef st_atim
#define st_atim st_atimespec
#endif
#ifndef st_mtim
#define st_mtim st_mtimespec
#endif
#ifndef st_ctim
#define st_ctim st_ctimespec
#endif
#ifndef st_birthtim
#define st_birthtim st_birthtimespec
#endif

/* Darwin has no mempcpy (GNU). */
#ifndef mempcpy
#define mempcpy(dst, src, n) ((void *)((char *)memcpy((dst), (src), (n)) + (n)))
#endif

/* Darwin has no setresuid/setresgid: privilege dropping is unsupported here. */
#ifndef setresuid
#define setresuid(r, e, s) ((void)(r), (void)(e), (void)(s), -1)
#endif
#ifndef setresgid
#define setresgid(r, e, s) ((void)(r), (void)(e), (void)(s), -1)
#endif

#ifndef PTHREAD_STACK_MIN
#define PTHREAD_STACK_MIN 16384
#endif

/* Darwin keeps block counts as blkcnt_t but busybox expects 512-byte units. */
#ifndef ST_NBLOCKS
#define ST_NBLOCKS(st) ((st).st_blocks)
#endif

/* Link-layer constants busybox expects from <linux/if_ether.h>. */
#ifndef ETH_ALEN
#define ETH_ALEN 6
#endif
#ifndef ETH_HLEN
#define ETH_HLEN 14
#endif
#ifndef ETH_P_IP
#define ETH_P_IP 0x0800
#endif
#ifndef ETH_P_ARP
#define ETH_P_ARP 0x0806
#endif

#endif /* !__ASSEMBLER__ */

```

`runtime/busybox-ios/compat/mntent.h`:

```h
/* Darwin has no <mntent.h>; busybox only uses it to locate a mount point.
 * Returning "no entries" makes those helpers report failure, which is the
 * correct answer for an app container. */
#pragma once
#include <stdio.h>

struct mntent {
    char *mnt_fsname;
    char *mnt_dir;
    char *mnt_type;
    char *mnt_opts;
    int mnt_freq;
    int mnt_passno;
};

static inline FILE *setmntent(const char *file, const char *mode) { (void)file; (void)mode; return NULL; }
static inline struct mntent *getmntent(FILE *stream) { (void)stream; return NULL; }
static inline struct mntent *getmntent_r(FILE *stream, struct mntent *result, char *buffer, int size) {
    (void)stream; (void)result; (void)buffer; (void)size; return NULL;
}
static inline int endmntent(FILE *stream) { (void)stream; return 0; }
static inline int addmntent(FILE *stream, const struct mntent *entry) { (void)stream; (void)entry; return -1; }
static inline char *hasmntopt(const struct mntent *entry, const char *option) { (void)entry; (void)option; return NULL; }

```

`runtime/busybox-ios/compat/sys/sysmacros.h`:

```h
/* Darwin has no <sys/sysmacros.h>; keep the device-number helpers available. */
#pragma once
#include <sys/types.h>

static inline dev_t bb_makedev(unsigned int major, unsigned int minor) {
    return (dev_t)(((major & 0xff) << 24) | (minor & 0xffffff));
}
static inline unsigned int bb_major(dev_t dev) { return (unsigned int)((dev >> 24) & 0xff); }
static inline unsigned int bb_minor(dev_t dev) { return (unsigned int)(dev & 0xffffff); }

#ifndef makedev
#define makedev(maj, min) bb_makedev((maj), (min))
#endif
#ifndef major
#define major(dev) bb_major(dev)
#endif
#ifndef minor
#define minor(dev) bb_minor(dev)
#endif

```

/* The iOS SDK has no <utmp.h> (no login records). busybox only needs the types
 * to compile; the stub implementations below return "no entries". */
#pragma once

#define UT_LINESIZE 32
#define UT_NAMESIZE 32
#define UT_HOSTSIZE 256

struct utmp {
    short ut_type;
    int ut_pid;
    char ut_line[UT_LINESIZE];
    char ut_id[4];
    char ut_user[UT_NAMESIZE];
    char ut_host[UT_HOSTSIZE];
};

#define EMPTY 0
#define RUN_LVL 1
#define BOOT_TIME 2
#define NEW_TIME 3
#define OLD_TIME 4
#define INIT_PROCESS 5
#define LOGIN_PROCESS 6
#define USER_PROCESS 7
#define DEAD_PROCESS 8

struct utmp *getutent(void);
void setutent(void);
void endutent(void);
int utmpname(const char *file);
struct utmp *getutid(const struct utmp *id);
