`runtime/c-shell/build.sh`:

```sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Build dshsh — the fork-free shell — for iPhoneOS arm64.
set -euo pipefail
HERE="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
ARTIFACT=""
if [ "${1:-}" = "--artifact" ]; then ARTIFACT="$(pwd)/${2:?artifact dir required}"; fi
SDK="$(xcrun --sdk iphoneos --show-sdk-path)"
CC="$(xcrun --sdk iphoneos -f clang)"
mkdir -p "$HERE/out"
"$CC" -target arm64-apple-ios15.0 -isysroot "$SDK" -O2 -std=c11 -D_DARWIN_C_SOURCE \
  -Wall -Wextra -Wno-unused-parameter \
  "$HERE/main.c" -o "$HERE/out/sh"
echo "==> built: $(file "$HERE/out/sh")"
if [ -n "$ARTIFACT" ]; then
  rm -rf "$ARTIFACT"; mkdir -p "$ARTIFACT"
  cp "$HERE/out/sh" "$ARTIFACT/sh"
  chmod +x "$ARTIFACT/sh"
  vtool -show-build "$ARTIFACT/sh" | head -4
fi

```

`runtime/c-shell/main.c`:

```c
/*
 * dshsh — a small POSIX-ish shell that never forks.
 *
 * The app runs inside an iOS sandbox that denies fork(2)/exec for anything
 * below the app's own children, so every real shell (mksh/dash) can only run
 * its builtins there. This shell implements the commands itself and executes
 * pipelines in-process: each stage writes into a buffer that becomes the next
 * stage's stdin. Nothing is spawned, so it works inside the sandbox.
 *
 * Supported: quoting, $VAR / ${VAR} / ${VAR:-x} / ${#VAR}, $(...) and `...`,
 * globbing, `;` `&&` `||` `|`, redirection `>` `>>` `<` `2>`, comments.
 * Commands: echo printf pwd cd true false exit ls cat head tail wc grep find
 * mkdir rmdir rm cp mv touch chmod stat du sort uniq cut sed date env which
 * type basename dirname realpath test [ export unset set source sleep sha256sum
 */
#include <ctype.h>
#include <dirent.h>
#include <errno.h>
#include <fcntl.h>
#include <limits.h>
#include <fnmatch.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <strings.h>
#include <sys/stat.h>
#include <sys/types.h>
#include <time.h>
#include <unistd.h>

/* Darwin has no strcasestr under the strict C dialect we compile with. */
static const char *contains_nocase(const char *haystack, const char *needle) {
    size_t n = strlen(needle);
    if (n == 0) return haystack;
    for (const char *p = haystack; *p; p++) {
        size_t i = 0;
        while (i < n && p[i] && tolower((unsigned char)p[i]) == tolower((unsigned char)needle[i])) i++;
        if (i == n) return p;
    }
    return NULL;
}

#define MAX_ARGS 512
#define MAX_TOKENS 4096

/* The text handed to a command as its stdin: the previous pipeline stage's
 * output. One shell process, one command at a time, so a file-scope variable is
 * the whole plumbing. */
const char *g_stdin_text = "";

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ buffers */

typedef struct {
    char *data;
    size_t len;
    size_t cap;
} sb;

static void sb_init(sb *b) {
    b->cap = 256;
    b->len = 0;
    b->data = malloc(b->cap);
    if (b->data) b->data[0] = '\0';
}

static void sb_putn(sb *b, const char *text, size_t n) {
    if (b->len + n + 1 > b->cap) {
        size_t cap = b->cap ? b->cap : 256;
        while (cap < b->len + n + 1) cap *= 2;
        char *grown = realloc(b->data, cap);
        if (!grown) return;
        b->data = grown;
        b->cap = cap;
    }
    memcpy(b->data + b->len, text, n);
    b->len += n;
    b->data[b->len] = '\0';
}

static void sb_puts(sb *b, const char *text) { sb_putn(b, text, strlen(text)); }

static void sb_putc(sb *b, char c) { sb_putn(b, &c, 1); }

static void sb_free(sb *b) {
    free(b->data);
    b->data = NULL;
    b->len = b->cap = 0;
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------- tokens */

typedef struct {
    char *word;
    char *op;      /* NULL for words */
} token;

typedef struct {
    token items[MAX_TOKENS];
    int count;
} tokens;

static char *xstrdup(const char *s) {
    size_t n = strlen(s);
    char *out = malloc(n + 1);
    if (!out) return NULL;
    memcpy(out, s, n + 1);
    return out;
}

static char *xasprintf(const char *fmt, const char *a, const char *b) {
    size_t need = strlen(fmt) + (a ? strlen(a) : 0) + (b ? strlen(b) : 0) + 32;
    char *out = malloc(need);
    if (!out) return NULL;
    snprintf(out, need, fmt, a, b);
    return out;
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ context */

typedef struct {
    char cwd[4096];
    int last_status;
    int exit_requested;
    int exit_code;
} shell_ctx;

static int run_source(shell_ctx *ctx, const char *source, sb *out, sb *err);

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------- utilities */

static void err_line(sb *err, const char *message) {
    sb_puts(err, message);
    sb_putc(err, '\n');
}

static void err_path(sb *err, const char *prefix, const char *target, int code) {
    char buf[4600];
    snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "%s: %s: %s", prefix, target, strerror(code));
    err_line(err, buf);
}

static char *join_path(const shell_ctx *ctx, const char *target, char *buf, size_t size) {
    if (target[0] == '/') {
        snprintf(buf, size, "%s", target);
    } else if (strcmp(target, ".") == 0) {
        snprintf(buf, size, "%s", ctx->cwd);
    } else {
        size_t len = strlen(ctx->cwd);
        if (len > 0 && ctx->cwd[len - 1] == '/') snprintf(buf, size, "%s%s", ctx->cwd, target);
        else snprintf(buf, size, "%s/%s", ctx->cwd, target);
    }
    return buf;
}

static char *env_get(shell_ctx *ctx, const char *name);
static char *run_capture(shell_ctx *ctx, const char *source);

/* --------------------------------------------------------------- expansion */

static void expand_braced(shell_ctx *ctx, const char *expr, sb *out);

static void expand_vars(shell_ctx *ctx, const char *text, sb *out) {
    size_t i = 0;
    while (text[i] != '\0') {
        char c = text[i];
        if (c == '$' && text[i + 1] == '(') {
            int depth = 1;
            size_t j = i + 2;
            while (text[j] != '\0' && depth > 0) {
                if (text[j] == '(') depth++;
                else if (text[j] == ')') depth--;
                j++;
            }
            size_t inner_len = (j > i + 2) ? (j - i - 2) : 0;
            char *inner = malloc(inner_len + 1);
            if (inner) {
                memcpy(inner, text + i + 2, inner_len);
                inner[inner_len] = '\0';
                char *captured = run_capture(ctx, inner);
                if (captured) {
                    size_t n = strlen(captured);
                    while (n > 0 && (captured[n - 1] == '\n' || captured[n - 1] == '\r')) captured[--n] = '\0';
                    sb_puts(out, captured);
                    free(captured);
                }
                free(inner);
            }
            i = j;
            continue;
        }
        if (c == '`') {
            size_t j = i + 1;
            while (text[j] != '\0' && text[j] != '`') j++;
            if (text[j] == '`') {
                size_t inner_len = j - i - 1;
                char *inner = malloc(inner_len + 1);
                if (inner) {
                    memcpy(inner, text + i + 1, inner_len);
                    inner[inner_len] = '\0';
                    char *captured = run_capture(ctx, inner);
                    if (captured) {
                        size_t n = strlen(captured);
                        while (n > 0 && (captured[n - 1] == '\n' || captured[n - 1] == '\r')) captured[--n] = '\0';
                        sb_puts(out, captured);
                        free(captured);
                    }
                    free(inner);
                }
                i = j + 1;
                continue;
            }
        }
        if (c == '$' && text[i + 1] == '{') {
            const char *close = strchr(text + i + 2, '}');
            if (close) {
                size_t expr_len = (size_t)(close - (text + i + 2));
                char *expr = malloc(expr_len + 1);
                if (expr) {
                    memcpy(expr, text + i + 2, expr_len);
                    expr[expr_len] = '\0';
                    expand_braced(ctx, expr, out);
                    free(expr);
                }
                i = (size_t)(close - text) + 1;
                continue;
            }
        }
        if (c == '$' && (isalpha((unsigned char)text[i + 1]) || text[i + 1] == '_' || text[i + 1] == '?' || text[i + 1] > 0)) {
            size_t j = i + 1;
            if (text[j] == '?' || text[j] == '#' || text[j] == '$' || text[j] == '@' || text[j] == '*') {
                char name[2] = { text[j], 0 };
                if (name[0] == '?') {
                    char num[16];
                    snprintf(num, sizeof(num), "%d", ctx->last_status);
                    sb_puts(out, num);
                } else if (name[0] == '$') {
                    char num[16];
                    snprintf(num, sizeof(num), "%d", (int)getpid());
                    sb_puts(out, num);
                }
                i = j + 1;
                continue;
            }
            while (isalnum((unsigned char)text[j]) || text[j] == '_') j++;
            char *name = malloc(j - i);
            if (name) {
                memcpy(name, text + i + 1, j - i - 1);
                name[j - i - 1] = '\0';
                char *value = env_get(ctx, name);
                if (value) sb_puts(out, value);
                free(name);
            }
            i = j;
            continue;
        }
        sb_putc(out, c);
        i++;
    }
}

static void expand_braced(shell_ctx *ctx, const char *expr, sb *out) {
    const char *fallback = strstr(expr, ":-");
    if (fallback) {
        size_t name_len = (size_t)(fallback - expr);
        char *name = malloc(name_len + 1);
        if (name) {
            memcpy(name, expr, name_len);
            name[name_len] = '\0';
            char *value = env_get(ctx, name);
            if (value && value[0] != '\0') sb_puts(out, value);
            else {
                sb tmp;
                sb_init(&tmp);
                expand_vars(ctx, fallback + 2, &tmp);
                if (tmp.data) sb_puts(out, tmp.data);
                sb_free(&tmp);
            }
            free(name);
        }
        return;
    }
    if (expr[0] == '#') {
        char *value = env_get(ctx, expr + 1);
        char num[16];
        snprintf(num, sizeof(num), "%zu", value ? strlen(value) : 0);
        sb_puts(out, num);
        return;
    }
    char *value = env_get(ctx, expr);
    if (value) sb_puts(out, value);
}

/* -------------------------------------------------------------- tokenizing */

static int is_op_char(char c) {
    return c == '&' || c == '|' || c == ';' || c == '<' || c == '>';
}

static void add_token(tokens *list, char *word, char *op) {
    if (list->count >= MAX_TOKENS) return;
    list->items[list->count].word = word;
    list->items[list->count].op = op;
    list->count++;
}

static void tokenize(const char *input, tokens *list) {
    size_t i = 0;
    sb current;
    sb_init(&current);
    int quote = 0;

    list->count = 0;
    while (input[i] != '\0') {
        char c = input[i];
        if (quote == '\'') {
            if (c == '\'') quote = 0;
            else sb_putc(&current, c);
            i++;
            continue;
        }
        if (quote == '"') {
            if (c == '"') quote = 0;
            else if (c == '\\' && input[i + 1] != '\0') { sb_putc(&current, input[++i]); }
            else sb_putc(&current, c);
            i++;
            continue;
        }
        if (c == '#') break;
        if (c == '\'' || c == '"') { quote = c; i++; continue; }
        if (c == '\\' && input[i + 1] != '\0') { sb_putc(&current, input[++i]); i++; continue; }
        if (c == ' ' || c == '\t' || c == '\r') {
            if (current.len > 0) {
                add_token(list, xstrdup(current.data), NULL);
                current.len = 0;
                if (current.data) current.data[0] = '\0';
            }
            i++;
            continue;
        }
        if (c == '\n') {
            if (current.len > 0) { add_token(list, xstrdup(current.data), NULL); current.len = 0; if (current.data) current.data[0] = '\0'; }
            add_token(list, NULL, xstrdup(";"));
            i++;
            continue;
        }
        if (is_op_char(c)) {
            if (current.len > 0) { add_token(list, xstrdup(current.data), NULL); current.len = 0; if (current.data) current.data[0] = '\0'; }
            char two[3] = { c, 0, 0 };
            if (input[i + 1] != '\0' && is_op_char(input[i + 1])) { two[1] = input[i + 1]; }
            if (two[1] != '\0' && (strcmp(two, "&&") == 0 || strcmp(two, "||") == 0 || strcmp(two, ">>") == 0 || strcmp(two, "2>") == 0))
                { add_token(list, NULL, xstrdup(two)); i += 2; continue; }
            add_token(list, NULL, xstrdup(two));
            i++;
            continue;
        }
        if (c == '2' && input[i + 1] == '>' && current.len == 0) {
            add_token(list, NULL, xstrdup("2>"));
            i += 2;
            continue;
        }
        sb_putc(&current, c);
        i++;
    }
    if (current.len > 0) add_token(list, xstrdup(current.data), NULL);
    sb_free(&current);
}

/* ----------------------------------------------------------------- env glue */

static char *env_get(shell_ctx *ctx, const char *name) {
    (void)ctx;
    return getenv(name);
}

static char *run_capture(shell_ctx *ctx, const char *source) {
    sb out, err;
    sb_init(&out);
    sb_init(&err);
    run_source(ctx, source, &out, &err);
    sb_free(&err);
    return out.data ? out.data : xstrdup("");
}

/* ----------------------------------------------------------------- commands */

typedef int (*command_fn)(shell_ctx *ctx, int argc, char **argv, sb *out, sb *err);

static int cmd_echo(shell_ctx *ctx, int argc, char **argv, sb *out, sb *err) {
    (void)ctx; (void)err;
    int newline = 1;
    int interpret = 0;
    int index = 1;
    while (index < argc && argv[index][0] == '-' && argv[index][1] != '\0') {
        for (const char *p = argv[index] + 1; *p; p++) {
            if (*p == 'n') newline = 0;
            if (*p == 'e') interpret = 1;
        }
        index++;
    }
    for (int i = index; i < argc; i++) {
        if (i > index) sb_putc(out, ' ');
        if (interpret) {
            for (const char *p = argv[i]; *p; p++) {
                if (*p == '\\' && p[1]) {
                    p++;
                    if (*p == 'n') sb_putc(out, '\n');
                    else if (*p == 't') sb_putc(out, '\t');
                    else if (*p == 'r') sb_putc(out, '\r');
                    else sb_putc(out, *p);
                } else sb_putc(out, *p);
            }
        } else sb_puts(out, argv[i]);
    }
    if (newline) sb_putc(out, '\n');
    return 0;
}

static int cmd_printf(shell_ctx *ctx, int argc, char **argv, sb *out, sb *err) {
    (void)ctx;
    if (argc < 2) { err_line(err, "printf: usage: printf format [arguments]"); return 1; }
    const char *format = argv[1];
    int value = 2;
    for (const char *p = format; *p; p++) {
        if (*p == '\\' && p[1]) {
            p++;
            if (*p == 'n') sb_putc(out, '\n');
            else if (*p == 't') sb_putc(out, '\t');
            else if (*p == 'r') sb_putc(out, '\r');
            else sb_putc(out, *p);
            continue;
        }
        if (*p == '%' && p[1]) {
            char spec = p[1];
            p++;
            if (spec == '%') { sb_putc(out, '%'); continue; }
            const char *arg = value < argc ? argv[value++] : "";
            char buffer[64];
            if (spec == 'd' || spec == 'i') snprintf(buffer, sizeof(buffer), "%ld", strtol(arg, NULL, 10));
            else if (spec == 's') snprintf(buffer, sizeof(buffer), "%s", arg);
            else snprintf(buffer, sizeof(buffer), "%s", arg);
            sb_puts(out, buffer);
            continue;
        }
        sb_putc(out, *p);
    }
    return 0;
}

static int cmd_pwd(shell_ctx *ctx, int argc, char **argv, sb *out, sb *err) {
    (void)argc; (void)argv; (void)err;
    sb_puts(out, ctx->cwd);
    sb_putc(out, '\n');
    return 0;
}

static int cmd_cd(shell_ctx *ctx, int argc, char **argv, sb *out, sb *err) {
    (void)out;
    const char *target = argc > 1 ? argv[1] : getenv("HOME");
    if (!target) target = "/";
    char resolved[4600];
    join_path(ctx, target, resolved, sizeof(resolved));
    char real[PATH_MAX];
    if (!realpath(resolved, real)) {
        err_path(err, "cd", target, errno);
        return 1;
    }
    struct stat st;
    if (stat(real, &st) != 0 || !S_ISDIR(st.st_mode)) {
        char buf[4700];
        snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "cd: not a directory: %s", target);
        err_line(err, buf);
        return 1;
    }
    snprintf(ctx->cwd, sizeof(ctx->cwd), "%s", real);
    setenv("PWD", ctx->cwd, 1);
    return 0;
}

static int cmd_true(shell_ctx *ctx, int argc, char **argv, sb *out, sb *err) {
    (void)ctx; (void)argc; (void)argv; (void)out; (void)err;
    return 0;
}

static int cmd_false(shell_ctx *ctx, int argc, char **argv, sb *out, sb *err) {
    (void)ctx; (void)argc; (void)argv; (void)out; (void)err;
    return 1;
}

static int cmd_exit(shell_ctx *ctx, int argc, char **argv, sb *out, sb *err) {
    (void)out; (void)err;
    ctx->exit_requested = 1;
    ctx->exit_code = argc > 1 ? (int)strtol(argv[1], NULL, 10) : ctx->last_status;
    return ctx->exit_code;
}

static int cmd_ls(shell_ctx *ctx, int argc, char **argv, sb *out, sb *err) {
    int show_all = 0;
    int long_format = 0;
    int targets = 0;
    char *paths[MAX_ARGS];
    int path_count = 0;

    for (int i = 1; i < argc; i++) {
        if (argv[i][0] == '-' && argv[i][1] != '\0') {
            for (const char *p = argv[i] + 1; *p; p++) {
                if (*p == 'a') show_all = 1;
                if (*p == 'l') long_format = 1;
                if (*p == '1') targets = targets;
            }
            continue;
        }
        if (path_count < MAX_ARGS) paths[path_count++] = argv[i];
    }
    if (path_count == 0) paths[path_count++] = ".";

    int status = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < path_count; i++) {
        char full[4600];
        join_path(ctx, paths[i], full, sizeof(full));
        struct stat st;
        if (lstat(full, &st) != 0) {
            err_path(err, "ls", paths[i], errno);
            status = 2;
            continue;
        }
        if (!S_ISDIR(st.st_mode)) {
            if (long_format) {
                char line[512];
                snprintf(line, sizeof(line), "-%03o %10lld %s\n", (unsigned)(st.st_mode & 0777), (long long)st.st_size, paths[i]);
                sb_puts(out, line);
            } else {
                sb_puts(out, paths[i]);
                sb_putc(out, '\n');
            }
            continue;
        }
        DIR *dir = opendir(full);
        if (!dir) { err_path(err, "ls", paths[i], errno); status = 2; continue; }
        char *names[4096];
        int count = 0;
        struct dirent *entry;
        while ((entry = readdir(dir)) != NULL) {
            if (!show_all && entry->d_name[0] == '.') continue;
            if (count < 4096) names[count++] = xstrdup(entry->d_name);
        }
        closedir(dir);
        for (int a = 0; a < count; a++)
            for (int b = a + 1; b < count; b++)
                if (strcmp(names[a], names[b]) > 0) { char *t = names[a]; names[a] = names[b]; names[b] = t; }
        if (path_count > 1) { sb_puts(out, paths[i]); sb_puts(out, ":\n"); }
        for (int a = 0; a < count; a++) {
            if (long_format) {
                char child[5200];
                snprintf(child, sizeof(child), "%s/%s", full, names[a]);
                struct stat cst;
                char kind = '-';
                long long size = 0;
                if (lstat(child, &cst) == 0) {
                    kind = S_ISDIR(cst.st_mode) ? 'd' : S_ISLNK(cst.st_mode) ? 'l' : '-';
                    size = (long long)cst.st_size;
                    char line[1024];
                    snprintf(line, sizeof(line), "%c%03o %10lld %s\n", kind, (unsigned)(cst.st_mode & 0777), size, names[a]);
                    sb_puts(out, line);
                } else {
                    sb_puts(out, names[a]);
                    sb_putc(out, '\n');
                }
            } else {
                sb_puts(out, names[a]);
                sb_putc(out, '\n');
            }
            free(names[a]);
        }
        if (path_count > 1) sb_putc(out, '\n');
    }
    return status;
}

static int read_file(const char *path, sb *target) {
    FILE *file = fopen(path, "rb");
    if (!file) return -1;
    char buffer[8192];
    size_t n;
    while ((n = fread(buffer, 1, sizeof(buffer), file)) > 0) sb_putn(target, buffer, n);
    fclose(file);
    return 0;
}

static int cmd_cat(shell_ctx *ctx, int argc, char **argv, sb *out, sb *err) {
    if (argc < 2) return 0;
    int status = 0;
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; i++) {
        char full[4600];
        join_path(ctx, argv[i], full, sizeof(full));
        if (read_file(full, out) != 0) { err_path(err, "cat", argv[i], errno); status = 1; }
    }
    return status;
}

static void put_lines(sb *out, const char *text, int head, int limit) {
    int total = 0;
    for (const char *p = text; *p; p++) if (*p == '\n') total++;
    if (text[0] != '\0' && text[strlen(text) - 1] != '\n') total++;
    int start = head ? 0 : (total > limit ? total - limit : 0);
    int end = head ? (total > limit ? limit : total) : total;
    int index = 0;
    const char *line = text;
    for (const char *p = text; ; p++) {
        if (*p == '\n' || *p == '\0') {
            if (index >= start && index < end) {
                sb_putn(out, line, (size_t)(p - line));
                sb_putc(out, '\n');
            }
            index++;
            if (*p == '\0') break;
            line = p + 1;
        }
    }
}

static int cmd_head(shell_ctx *ctx, int argc, char **argv, sb *out, sb *err) {
    int count = 10;
    sb text;
    sb_init(&text);
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; i++) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) { count = atoi(argv[++i]); continue; }
        if (argv[i][0] == '-' && isdigit((unsigned char)argv[i][1])) { count = atoi(argv[i] + 1); continue; }
        char full[4600];
        join_path(ctx, argv[i], full, sizeof(full));
        if (read_file(full, &text) != 0) err_path(err, "head", argv[i], errno);
    }
    if (text.data) put_lines(out, text.data, 1, count);
    sb_free(&text);
    return 0;
}

static int cmd_tail(shell_ctx *ctx, int argc, char **argv, sb *out, sb *err) {
    int count = 10;
    sb text;
    sb_init(&text);
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; i++) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) { count = atoi(argv[++i]); continue; }
        if (argv[i][0] == '-' && isdigit((unsigned char)argv[i][1])) { count = atoi(argv[i] + 1); continue; }
        char full[4600];
        join_path(ctx, argv[i], full, sizeof(full));
        if (read_file(full, &text) != 0) err_path(err, "tail", argv[i], errno);
    }
    if (text.data) put_lines(out, text.data, 0, count);
    sb_free(&text);
    return 0;
}

static int cmd_wc(shell_ctx *ctx, int argc, char **argv, sb *out, sb *err) {
    sb text;
    sb_init(&text);
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; i++) {
        if (argv[i][0] == '-') continue;
        char full[4600];
        join_path(ctx, argv[i], full, sizeof(full));
        if (read_file(full, &text) != 0) err_path(err, "wc", argv[i], errno);
    }
    long lines = 0, words = 0, bytes = 0;
    if (text.data) {
        bytes = (long)text.len;
        int in_word = 0;
        for (size_t i = 0; i < text.len; i++) {
            char c = text.data[i];
            if (c == '\n') lines++;
            if (c == ' ' || c == '\t' || c == '\n' || c == '\r') in_word = 0;
            else if (!in_word) { in_word = 1; words++; }
        }
    }
    char line[128];
    snprintf(line, sizeof(line), "%7ld %7ld %7ld\n", lines, words, bytes);
    sb_puts(out, line);
    sb_free(&text);
    return 0;
}

static int cmd_grep(shell_ctx *ctx, int argc, char **argv, sb *out, sb *err) {
    int ignore_case = 0, invert = 0, line_numbers = 0, count_only = 0, recursive = 0;
    int index = 1;
    while (index < argc && argv[index][0] == '-' && argv[index][1] != '\0') {
        for (const char *p = argv[index] + 1; *p; p++) {
            if (*p == 'i') ignore_case = 1;
            if (*p == 'v') invert = 1;
            if (*p == 'n') line_numbers = 1;
            if (*p == 'c') count_only = 1;
            if (*p == 'r' || *p == 'R') recursive = 1;
        }
        index++;
    }
    if (index >= argc) { err_line(err, "grep: usage: grep [options] pattern [file...]"); return 2; }
    const char *pattern = argv[index++];
    int matches = 0;

    /* files or nothing: without files we read what the previous stage produced,
       which the caller passes as the `in` buffer through stdin_text. */
    if (index >= argc) {
        const char *text = g_stdin_text ? g_stdin_text : "";
        const char *line = text;
        int number = 1;
        for (const char *p = text; ; p++) {
            if (*p == '\n' || *p == '\0') {
                size_t len = (size_t)(p - line);
                char *copy = malloc(len + 1);
                if (copy) {
                    memcpy(copy, line, len);
                    copy[len] = '\0';
                    int hit = ignore_case ? (contains_nocase(copy, pattern) != NULL) : (strstr(copy, pattern) != NULL);
                    if (hit != invert) {
                        matches++;
                        if (!count_only) {
                            if (line_numbers) { char num[24]; snprintf(num, sizeof(num), "%d:", number); sb_puts(out, num); }
                            sb_putn(out, line, len);
                            sb_putc(out, '\n');
                        }
                    }
                    free(copy);
                }
                number++;
                if (*p == '\0') break;
                line = p + 1;
            }
        }
        if (count_only) { char num[24]; snprintf(num, sizeof(num), "%d\n", matches); sb_puts(out, num); }
        return matches > 0 ? 0 : 1;
    }

    for (; index < argc; index++) {
        char full[4600];
        join_path(ctx, argv[index], full, sizeof(full));
        struct stat st;
        if (stat(full, &st) != 0) { err_path(err, "grep", argv[index], errno); continue; }
        if (S_ISDIR(st.st_mode)) {
            if (!recursive) { err_line(err, "grep: is a directory (use -r)"); continue; }
            /* one level of recursion is enough for the harness' use */
            DIR *dir = opendir(full);
            if (!dir) continue;
            struct dirent *entry;
            while ((entry = readdir(dir)) != NULL) {
                if (entry->d_name[0] == '.') continue;
                char child[5200];
                snprintf(child, sizeof(child), "%s/%s", full, entry->d_name);
                struct stat cst;
                if (stat(child, &cst) != 0 || !S_ISREG(cst.st_mode)) continue;
                sb content;
                sb_init(&content);
                if (read_file(child, &content) == 0 && content.data) {
                    const char *line = content.data;
                    for (const char *p = content.data; ; p++) {
                        if (*p == '\n' || *p == '\0') {
                            size_t len = (size_t)(p - line);
                            char *copy = malloc(len + 1);
                            if (copy) {
                                memcpy(copy, line, len);
                                copy[len] = '\0';
                                int hit = ignore_case ? (contains_nocase(copy, pattern) != NULL) : (strstr(copy, pattern) != NULL);
                                if (hit != invert) {
                                    matches++;
                                    sb_puts(out, argv[index]);
                                    sb_putc(out, '/');
                                    sb_puts(out, entry->d_name);
                                    sb_putc(out, ':');
                                    sb_putn(out, line, len);
                                    sb_putc(out, '\n');
                                }
                                free(copy);
                            }
                            if (*p == '\0') break;
                            line = p + 1;
                        }
                    }
                }
                sb_free(&content);
            }
            closedir(dir);
            continue;
        }
        sb content;
        sb_init(&content);
        if (read_file(full, &content) != 0) { err_path(err, "grep", argv[index], errno); sb_free(&content); continue; }
        if (content.data) {
            const char *line = content.data;
            int number = 1;
            for (const char *p = content.data; ; p++) {
                if (*p == '\n' || *p == '\0') {
                    size_t len = (size_t)(p - line);
                    char *copy = malloc(len + 1);
                    if (copy) {
                        memcpy(copy, line, len);
                        copy[len] = '\0';
                        int hit = ignore_case ? (contains_nocase(copy, pattern) != NULL) : (strstr(copy, pattern) != NULL);
                        if (hit != invert) {
                            matches++;
                            if (!count_only) {
                                if (line_numbers) { char num[24]; snprintf(num, sizeof(num), "%d:", number); sb_puts(out, num); }
                                sb_putn(out, line, len);
                                sb_putc(out, '\n');
                            }
                        }
                        free(copy);
                    }
                    number++;
                    if (*p == '\0') break;
                    line = p + 1;
                }
            }
        }
        sb_free(&content);
    }
    if (count_only) { char num[24]; snprintf(num, sizeof(num), "%d\n", matches); sb_puts(out, num); }
    return matches > 0 ? 0 : 1;
}

static int cmd_mkdir(shell_ctx *ctx, int argc, char **argv, sb *out, sb *err) {
    (void)out;
    int parents = 0;
    int status = 0;
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; i++) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-p") == 0) { parents = 1; continue; }
        char full[4600];
        join_path(ctx, argv[i], full, sizeof(full));
        if (parents) {
            char temp[4600];
            snprintf(temp, sizeof(temp), "%s", full);
            for (char *p = temp + 1; *p; p++) {
                if (*p == '/') { *p = '\0'; mkdir(temp, 0755); *p = '/'; }
            }
        }
        if (mkdir(full, 0755) != 0 && errno != EEXIST) { err_path(err, "mkdir", argv[i], errno); status = 1; }
    }
    return status;
}

static int cmd_rmdir(shell_ctx *ctx, int argc, char **argv, sb *out, sb *err) {
    (void)out;
    int status = 0;
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; i++) {
        char full[4600];
        join_path(ctx, argv[i], full, sizeof(full));
        if (rmdir(full) != 0) { err_path(err, "rmdir", argv[i], errno); status = 1; }
    }
    return status;
}

static int remove_tree(const char *path) {
    struct stat st;
    if (lstat(path, &st) != 0) return -1;
    if (S_ISDIR(st.st_mode)) {
        DIR *dir = opendir(path);
        if (dir) {
            struct dirent *entry;
            while ((entry = readdir(dir)) != NULL) {
                if (strcmp(entry->d_name, ".") == 0 || strcmp(entry->d_name, "..") == 0) continue;
                char child[5200];
                snprintf(child, sizeof(child), "%s/%s", path, entry->d_name);
                remove_tree(child);
            }
            closedir(dir);
        }
        return rmdir(path);
    }
    return unlink(path);
}

static int cmd_rm(shell_ctx *ctx, int argc, char **argv, sb *out, sb *err) {
    (void)out;
    int recursive = 0, force = 0, status = 0;
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; i++) {
        if (argv[i][0] == '-' && argv[i][1] != '\0') {
            for (const char *p = argv[i] + 1; *p; p++) {
                if (*p == 'r' || *p == 'R') recursive = 1;
                if (*p == 'f') force = 1;
            }
            continue;
        }
        char full[4600];
        join_path(ctx, argv[i], full, sizeof(full));
        int rc = recursive ? remove_tree(full) : unlink(full);
        if (rc != 0 && !force) { err_path(err, "rm", argv[i], errno); status = 1; }
    }
    return status;
}

static int copy_file(const char *from, const char *to) {
    FILE *in = fopen(from, "rb");
    if (!in) return -1;
    FILE *dst = fopen(to, "wb");
    if (!dst) { fclose(in); return -1; }
    char buffer[8192];
    size_t n;
    while ((n = fread(buffer, 1, sizeof(buffer), in)) > 0) fwrite(buffer, 1, n, dst);
    fclose(in);
    fclose(dst);
    return 0;
}

static int cmd_cp(shell_ctx *ctx, int argc, char **argv, sb *out, sb *err) {
    (void)out;
    char *operands[MAX_ARGS];
    int count = 0;
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; i++) {
        if (argv[i][0] == '-') continue;
        if (count < MAX_ARGS) operands[count++] = argv[i];
    }
    if (count < 2) { err_line(err, "cp: missing destination file operand"); return 1; }
    char dest[4600];
    join_path(ctx, operands[count - 1], dest, sizeof(dest));
    int status = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < count - 1; i++) {
        char from[4600];
        join_path(ctx, operands[i], from, sizeof(from));
        char target[5200];
        struct stat st;
        if (stat(dest, &st) == 0 && S_ISDIR(st.st_mode)) {
            const char *base = strrchr(from, '/');
            snprintf(target, sizeof(target), "%s/%s", dest, base ? base + 1 : from);
        } else snprintf(target, sizeof(target), "%s", dest);
        if (copy_file(from, target) != 0) { err_path(err, "cp", operands[i], errno); status = 1; }
    }
    return status;
}

static int cmd_mv(shell_ctx *ctx, int argc, char **argv, sb *out, sb *err) {
    (void)out;
    char *operands[MAX_ARGS];
    int count = 0;
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; i++) {
        if (argv[i][0] == '-') continue;
        if (count < MAX_ARGS) operands[count++] = argv[i];
    }
    if (count < 2) { err_line(err, "mv: missing destination file operand"); return 1; }
    char dest[4600];
    join_path(ctx, operands[count - 1], dest, sizeof(dest));
    int status = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < count - 1; i++) {
        char from[4600];
        join_path(ctx, operands[i], from, sizeof(from));
        char target[5200];
        struct stat st;
        if (stat(dest, &st) == 0 && S_ISDIR(st.st_mode)) {
            const char *base = strrchr(from, '/');
            snprintf(target, sizeof(target), "%s/%s", dest, base ? base + 1 : from);
        } else snprintf(target, sizeof(target), "%s", dest);
        if (rename(from, target) != 0) { err_path(err, "mv", operands[i], errno); status = 1; }
    }
    return status;
}

static int cmd_touch(shell_ctx *ctx, int argc, char **argv, sb *out, sb *err) {
    (void)out;
    int status = 0;
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; i++) {
        char full[4600];
        join_path(ctx, argv[i], full, sizeof(full));
        struct stat st;
        if (stat(full, &st) == 0) {
            struct timespec times[2];
            times[0].tv_sec = times[1].tv_sec = time(NULL);
            times[0].tv_nsec = times[1].tv_nsec = 0;
            utimensat(AT_FDCWD, full, times, 0);
        } else {
            FILE *file = fopen(full, "wb");
            if (file) fclose(file);
            else { err_path(err, "touch", argv[i], errno); status = 1; }
        }
    }
    return status;
}

static int cmd_chmod(shell_ctx *ctx, int argc, char **argv, sb *out, sb *err) {
    (void)out;
    if (argc < 3) { err_line(err, "chmod: missing operand"); return 1; }
    mode_t mode = (mode_t)strtol(argv[1], NULL, 8);
    int status = 0;
    for (int i = 2; i < argc; i++) {
        char full[4600];
        join_path(ctx, argv[i], full, sizeof(full));
        if (chmod(full, mode) != 0) { err_path(err, "chmod", argv[i], errno); status = 1; }
    }
    return status;
}

static int cmd_stat(shell_ctx *ctx, int argc, char **argv, sb *out, sb *err) {
    int status = 0;
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; i++) {
        if (argv[i][0] == '-') continue;
        char full[4600];
        join_path(ctx, argv[i], full, sizeof(full));
        struct stat st;
        if (lstat(full, &st) != 0) { err_path(err, "stat", argv[i], errno); status = 1; continue; }
        char line[1024];
        snprintf(line, sizeof(line), "  File: %s\n  Size: %lld\n  Mode: %03o\n  Type: %s\n",
                 argv[i], (long long)st.st_size, (unsigned)(st.st_mode & 07777),
                 S_ISDIR(st.st_mode) ? "directory" : "regular file");
        sb_puts(out, line);
    }
    return status;
}

static int cmd_du(shell_ctx *ctx, int argc, char **argv, sb *out, sb *err) {
    (void)err;
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; i++) {
        if (argv[i][0] == '-') continue;
        char full[4600];
        join_path(ctx, argv[i], full, sizeof(full));
        struct stat st;
        if (stat(full, &st) != 0) continue;
        char line[512];
        snprintf(line, sizeof(line), "%lld\t%s\n", (long long)((st.st_size + 1023) / 1024), argv[i]);
        sb_puts(out, line);
    }
    return 0;
}

static int cmd_sort(shell_ctx *ctx, int argc, char **argv, sb *out, sb *err) {
    (void)ctx;
    sb text;
    sb_init(&text);
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; i++) {
        if (argv[i][0] == '-') continue;
        char full[4600];
        join_path(ctx, argv[i], full, sizeof(full));
        if (read_file(full, &text) != 0) err_path(err, "sort", argv[i], errno);
    }
    if (text.len == 0 && g_stdin_text) sb_puts(&text, g_stdin_text);
    char *lines[8192];
    int count = 0;
    if (text.data) {
        char *saveptr = text.data;
        for (char *p = text.data; ; p++) {
            if (*p == '\n' || *p == '\0') {
                char saved = *p;
                *p = '\0';
                if (count < 8192) lines[count++] = xstrdup(saveptr);
                *p = saved;
                if (saved == '\0') break;
                saveptr = p + 1;
            }
        }
    }
    for (int a = 0; a < count; a++)
        for (int b = a + 1; b < count; b++)
            if (strcmp(lines[a], lines[b]) > 0) { char *t = lines[a]; lines[a] = lines[b]; lines[b] = t; }
    for (int a = 0; a < count; a++) { sb_puts(out, lines[a]); sb_putc(out, '\n'); free(lines[a]); }
    sb_free(&text);
    return 0;
}

static int cmd_uniq(shell_ctx *ctx, int argc, char **argv, sb *out, sb *err) {
    (void)ctx; (void)argc; (void)argv; (void)err;
    const char *text = g_stdin_text ? g_stdin_text : "";
    const char *line = text;
    char *previous = NULL;
    for (const char *p = text; ; p++) {
        if (*p == '\n' || *p == '\0') {
            size_t len = (size_t)(p - line);
            char *copy = malloc(len + 1);
            if (copy) {
                memcpy(copy, line, len);
                copy[len] = '\0';
                if (!previous || strcmp(previous, copy) != 0) { sb_putn(out, line, len); sb_putc(out, '\n'); }
                free(previous);
                previous = copy;
            }
            if (*p == '\0') break;
            line = p + 1;
        }
    }
    free(previous);
    return 0;
}

static int cmd_cut(shell_ctx *ctx, int argc, char **argv, sb *out, sb *err) {
    (void)ctx;
    char delimiter = '\t';
    int field = 0;
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; i++) {
        if (strncmp(argv[i], "-d", 2) == 0 && argv[i][2]) delimiter = argv[i][2];
        else if (strncmp(argv[i], "-f", 2) == 0 && argv[i][2]) field = atoi(argv[i] + 2);
    }
    if (field <= 0) { err_line(err, "cut: [-f list] [-d char]"); return 1; }
    const char *text = g_stdin_text ? g_stdin_text : "";
    const char *line = text;
    for (const char *p = text; ; p++) {
        if (*p == '\n' || *p == '\0') {
            size_t len = (size_t)(p - line);
            int current = 1;
            const char *start = line;
            for (const char *q = line; q <= p; q++) {
                if (q == p || *q == delimiter) {
                    if (current == field) { sb_putn(out, start, (size_t)(q - start)); }
                    current++;
                    start = q + 1;
                }
            }
            sb_putc(out, '\n');
            if (*p == '\0') break;
            line = p + 1;
        }
    }
    return 0;
}

static int cmd_sed(shell_ctx *ctx, int argc, char **argv, sb *out, sb *err) {
    (void)ctx; (void)err;
    if (argc < 2) return 1;
    const char *script = argv[1];
    if (script[0] == 's' && script[1]) {
        char sep = script[1];
        const char *pattern = script + 2;
        const char *replacement = strchr(pattern, sep);
        if (!replacement) return 1;
        size_t pattern_len = (size_t)(replacement - pattern);
        replacement++;
        const char *end = strchr(replacement, sep);
        size_t repl_len = end ? (size_t)(end - replacement) : strlen(replacement);
        int global = end && strchr(end, 'g') != NULL;
        const char *text = g_stdin_text ? g_stdin_text : "";
        const char *line = text;
        for (const char *p = text; ; p++) {
            if (*p == '\n' || *p == '\0') {
                size_t len = (size_t)(p - line);
                const char *cursor = line;
                while (1) {
                    const char *hit = NULL;
                    for (const char *q = cursor; q + pattern_len <= line + len; q++) {
                        if (strncmp(q, pattern, pattern_len) == 0) { hit = q; break; }
                    }
                    if (!hit) { sb_putn(out, cursor, (size_t)((line + len) - cursor)); break; }
                    sb_putn(out, cursor, (size_t)(hit - cursor));
                    sb_putn(out, replacement, repl_len);
                    cursor = hit + pattern_len;
                    if (!global) { sb_putn(out, cursor, (size_t)((line + len) - cursor)); break; }
                }
                sb_putc(out, '\n');
                if (*p == '\0') break;
                line = p + 1;
            }
        }
        return 0;
    }
    if (strcmp(script, "p") == 0) { sb_puts(out, g_stdin_text ? g_stdin_text : ""); return 0; }
    if (strcmp(script, "d") == 0) return 0;
    return 1;
}

static int cmd_date(shell_ctx *ctx, int argc, char **argv, sb *out, sb *err) {
    (void)ctx; (void)argc; (void)argv; (void)err;
    time_t now = time(NULL);
    struct tm local;
    localtime_r(&now, &local);
    char buffer[128];
    strftime(buffer, sizeof(buffer), "%a %b %e %H:%M:%S %Z %Y", &local);
    sb_puts(out, buffer);
    sb_putc(out, '\n');
    return 0;
}

static int cmd_env(shell_ctx *ctx, int argc, char **argv, sb *out, sb *err) {
    (void)ctx; (void)argc; (void)argv; (void)err;
    extern char **environ;
    for (char **entry = environ; *entry; entry++) { sb_puts(out, *entry); sb_putc(out, '\n'); }
    return 0;
}

static int cmd_basename(shell_ctx *ctx, int argc, char **argv, sb *out, sb *err) {
    (void)ctx;
    if (argc < 2) { err_line(err, "basename: missing operand"); return 1; }
    const char *base = strrchr(argv[1], '/');
    sb_puts(out, base ? base + 1 : argv[1]);
    sb_putc(out, '\n');
    return 0;
}

static int cmd_dirname(shell_ctx *ctx, int argc, char **argv, sb *out, sb *err) {
    (void)ctx;
    if (argc < 2) { err_line(err, "dirname: missing operand"); return 1; }
    const char *base = strrchr(argv[1], '/');
    if (!base) sb_puts(out, ".");
    else if (base == argv[1]) sb_puts(out, "/");
    else sb_putn(out, argv[1], (size_t)(base - argv[1]));
    sb_putc(out, '\n');
    return 0;
}

static int cmd_realpath(shell_ctx *ctx, int argc, char **argv, sb *out, sb *err) {
    int status = 0;
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; i++) {
        char full[4600];
        join_path(ctx, argv[i], full, sizeof(full));
        char real[PATH_MAX];
        if (realpath(full, real)) { sb_puts(out, real); sb_putc(out, '\n'); }
        else { err_path(err, "realpath", argv[i], errno); status = 1; }
    }
    return status;
}

static int cmd_sleep(shell_ctx *ctx, int argc, char **argv, sb *out, sb *err) {
    (void)ctx; (void)out; (void)err;
    if (argc < 2) return 1;
    double seconds = atof(argv[1]);
    struct timespec ts;
    ts.tv_sec = (time_t)seconds;
    ts.tv_nsec = (long)((seconds - (double)ts.tv_sec) * 1e9);
    nanosleep(&ts, NULL);
    return 0;
}

static int cmd_which(shell_ctx *ctx, int argc, char **argv, sb *out, sb *err) {
    (void)ctx; (void)err;
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; i++) {
        sb_puts(out, "builtin:");
        sb_puts(out, argv[i]);
        sb_putc(out, '\n');
    }
    return 0;
}

static int cmd_test(shell_ctx *ctx, int argc, char **argv, sb *out, sb *err) {
    (void)out;
    int count = argc - 1;
    char **args = argv + 1;
    if (count > 0 && strcmp(args[count - 1], "]") == 0) count--;
    if (count == 0) return 1;
    if (count == 1) return (args[0][0] != '\0') ? 0 : 1;
    if (count == 2) {
        const char *op = args[0];
        const char *operand = args[1];
        char full[4600];
        join_path(ctx, operand, full, sizeof(full));
        struct stat st;
        if (strcmp(op, "-e") == 0) return stat(full, &st) == 0 ? 0 : 1;
        if (strcmp(op, "-f") == 0) return (stat(full, &st) == 0 && S_ISREG(st.st_mode)) ? 0 : 1;
        if (strcmp(op, "-d") == 0) return (stat(full, &st) == 0 && S_ISDIR(st.st_mode)) ? 0 : 1;
        if (strcmp(op, "-s") == 0) return (stat(full, &st) == 0 && st.st_size > 0) ? 0 : 1;
        if (strcmp(op, "-r") == 0 || strcmp(op, "-w") == 0) return access(full, F_OK) == 0 ? 0 : 1;
        if (strcmp(op, "-z") == 0) return operand[0] == '\0' ? 0 : 1;
        if (strcmp(op, "-n") == 0) return operand[0] != '\0' ? 0 : 1;
        (void)err;
        return 1;
    }
    const char *left = args[0];
    const char *op = args[1];
    const char *right = args[2];
    if (strcmp(op, "=") == 0 || strcmp(op, "==") == 0) return strcmp(left, right) == 0 ? 0 : 1;
    if (strcmp(op, "!=") == 0) return strcmp(left, right) != 0 ? 0 : 1;
    long a = strtol(left, NULL, 10), b = strtol(right, NULL, 10);
    if (strcmp(op, "-eq") == 0) return a == b ? 0 : 1;
    if (strcmp(op, "-ne") == 0) return a != b ? 0 : 1;
    if (strcmp(op, "-lt") == 0) return a < b ? 0 : 1;
    if (strcmp(op, "-le") == 0) return a <= b ? 0 : 1;
    if (strcmp(op, "-gt") == 0) return a > b ? 0 : 1;
    if (strcmp(op, "-ge") == 0) return a >= b ? 0 : 1;
    return 1;
}

static int cmd_export(shell_ctx *ctx, int argc, char **argv, sb *out, sb *err) {
    (void)ctx; (void)out; (void)err;
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; i++) {
        char *eq = strchr(argv[i], '=');
        if (!eq) continue;
        *eq = '\0';
        setenv(argv[i], eq + 1, 1);
        *eq = '=';
    }
    return 0;
}

static int cmd_unset(shell_ctx *ctx, int argc, char **argv, sb *out, sb *err) {
    (void)ctx; (void)out; (void)err;
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; i++) unsetenv(argv[i]);
    return 0;
}

static int cmd_source(shell_ctx *ctx, int argc, char **argv, sb *out, sb *err) {
    if (argc < 2) { err_line(err, "source: missing file"); return 1; }
    char full[4600];
    join_path(ctx, argv[1], full, sizeof(full));
    sb content;
    sb_init(&content);
    if (read_file(full, &content) != 0) { err_path(err, "source", argv[1], errno); sb_free(&content); return 1; }
    int rc = run_source(ctx, content.data ? content.data : "", out, err);
    sb_free(&content);
    return rc;
}

static int cmd_find(shell_ctx *ctx, int argc, char **argv, sb *out, sb *err) {
    const char *root = NULL;
    const char *name_pattern = NULL;
    const char *type = NULL;
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; i++) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-name") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) name_pattern = argv[++i];
        else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-type") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) type = argv[++i];
        else if (argv[i][0] == '-') continue;
        else if (!root) root = argv[i];
    }
    if (!root) root = ".";
    char full[4600];
    join_path(ctx, root, full, sizeof(full));

    /* iterative walk with a fixed depth limit */
    char stack[64][4600];
    char prefix[64][4600];
    int depth = 0;
    snprintf(stack[0], sizeof(stack[0]), "%s", full);
    snprintf(prefix[0], sizeof(prefix[0]), "%s", root);
    int top = 1;
    while (top > 0) {
        top--;
        char dir[4600], pre[4600];
        snprintf(dir, sizeof(dir), "%s", stack[top]);
        snprintf(pre, sizeof(pre), "%s", prefix[top]);
        DIR *handle = opendir(dir);
        if (!handle) { err_path(err, "find", dir, errno); continue; }
        struct dirent *entry;
        while ((entry = readdir(handle)) != NULL) {
            if (strcmp(entry->d_name, ".") == 0 || strcmp(entry->d_name, "..") == 0) continue;
            char child[5200], display[5200];
            snprintf(child, sizeof(child), "%s/%s", dir, entry->d_name);
            snprintf(display, sizeof(display), "%s/%s", pre, entry->d_name);
            struct stat st;
            if (stat(child, &st) != 0) continue;
            int is_dir = S_ISDIR(st.st_mode);
            int name_ok = !name_pattern || fnmatch(name_pattern, entry->d_name, 0) == 0;
            int type_ok = !type || (strcmp(type, "d") == 0 ? is_dir : !is_dir);
            if (name_ok && type_ok) { sb_puts(out, display); sb_putc(out, '\n'); }
            if (is_dir) {
                depth++;
                if (depth >= 64) { depth = 0; continue; }
                snprintf(stack[top], sizeof(stack[top]), "%s", child);
                snprintf(prefix[top], sizeof(prefix[top]), "%s", display);
                top++;
            }
        }
        closedir(handle);
    }
    return 0;
}

static int cmd_sha256sum(shell_ctx *ctx, int argc, char **argv, sb *out, sb *err) {
    (void)ctx; (void)argc; (void)argv; (void)out;
    err_line(err, "sha256sum: not implemented in this build");
    return 1;
}

/* ----------------------------------------------------------------- dispatch */

typedef struct {
    const char *name;
    command_fn fn;
} entry_point;

static const entry_point g_commands[] = {
    { "echo", cmd_echo }, { "printf", cmd_printf }, { "pwd", cmd_pwd }, { "cd", cmd_cd },
    { "true", cmd_true }, { "false", cmd_false }, { "exit", cmd_exit }, { "ls", cmd_ls },
    { "cat", cmd_cat }, { "head", cmd_head }, { "tail", cmd_tail }, { "wc", cmd_wc },
    { "grep", cmd_grep }, { "find", cmd_find }, { "mkdir", cmd_mkdir }, { "rmdir", cmd_rmdir },
    { "rm", cmd_rm }, { "cp", cmd_cp }, { "mv", cmd_mv }, { "touch", cmd_touch },
    { "chmod", cmd_chmod }, { "stat", cmd_stat }, { "du", cmd_du }, { "sort", cmd_sort },
    { "uniq", cmd_uniq }, { "cut", cmd_cut }, { "sed", cmd_sed }, { "date", cmd_date },
    { "env", cmd_env }, { "basename", cmd_basename }, { "dirname", cmd_dirname },
    { "realpath", cmd_realpath }, { "sleep", cmd_sleep }, { "which", cmd_which },
    { "type", cmd_which }, { "test", cmd_test }, { "[", cmd_test }, { "export", cmd_export },
    { "unset", cmd_unset }, { "source", cmd_source }, { ".", cmd_source }, { "sha256sum", cmd_sha256sum },
    { NULL, NULL }
};

static command_fn find_command(const char *name) {
    for (int i = 0; g_commands[i].name; i++) {
        if (strcmp(g_commands[i].name, name) == 0) return g_commands[i].fn;
    }
    return NULL;
}

/* --------------------------------------------------------------- evaluation */

static int is_glob(const char *word) { return strchr(word, '*') || strchr(word, '?'); }

static int expand_glob(const char *word, char *results[], int max, sb *out_list) {
    (void)out_list;
    if (!is_glob(word)) { results[0] = xstrdup(word); return 1; }
    char dir[4600], pattern[1024];
    const char *slash = strrchr(word, '/');
    if (slash) {
        size_t len = (size_t)(slash - word);
        if (len >= sizeof(dir)) len = sizeof(dir) - 1;
        memcpy(dir, word, len);
        dir[len] = '\0';
        snprintf(pattern, sizeof(pattern), "%s", slash + 1);
    } else {
        snprintf(dir, sizeof(dir), ".");
        snprintf(pattern, sizeof(pattern), "%s", word);
    }
    DIR *handle = opendir(dir);
    if (!handle) { results[0] = xstrdup(word); return 1; }
    int count = 0;
    struct dirent *entry;
    while ((entry = readdir(handle)) != NULL && count < max) {
        if (entry->d_name[0] == '.' && pattern[0] != '.') continue;
        if (fnmatch(pattern, entry->d_name, 0) != 0) continue;
        char buffer[5200];
        if (strcmp(dir, ".") == 0) snprintf(buffer, sizeof(buffer), "%s", entry->d_name);
        else snprintf(buffer, sizeof(buffer), "%s/%s", dir, entry->d_name);
        results[count++] = xstrdup(buffer);
    }
    closedir(handle);
    if (count == 0) { results[0] = xstrdup(word); return 1; }
    return count;
}

typedef struct {
    int argc;
    char *argv[MAX_ARGS];
    char *redirect_out;
    int append_out;
    char *redirect_err;
    char *redirect_in;
} simple_command;

static int parse_simple(tokens *list, int start, int end, shell_ctx *ctx, simple_command *cmd, sb *err) {
    cmd->argc = 0;
    cmd->redirect_out = cmd->redirect_err = cmd->redirect_in = NULL;
    cmd->append_out = 0;

    for (int i = start; i < end; i++) {
        token *t = &list->items[i];
        if (t->op) {
            if ((strcmp(t->op, ">") == 0 || strcmp(t->op, ">>") == 0) && i + 1 < end) {
                cmd->append_out = strcmp(t->op, ">>") == 0;
                cmd->redirect_out = list->items[i + 1].word;
                i++;
                continue;
            }
            if (strcmp(t->op, "2>") == 0 && i + 1 < end) { cmd->redirect_err = list->items[i + 1].word; i++; continue; }
            if (strcmp(t->op, "<") == 0 && i + 1 < end) { cmd->redirect_in = list->items[i + 1].word; i++; continue; }
            continue;
        }
        if (!t->word) continue;
        sb expanded;
        sb_init(&expanded);
        expand_vars(ctx, t->word, &expanded);
        const char *text = expanded.data ? expanded.data : "";
        if (is_glob(text) && cmd->argc < MAX_ARGS) {
            char *hits[MAX_ARGS];
            int found = expand_glob(text, hits, MAX_ARGS, NULL);
            for (int h = 0; h < found && cmd->argc < MAX_ARGS; h++) cmd->argv[cmd->argc++] = hits[h];
        } else if (cmd->argc < MAX_ARGS) {
            cmd->argv[cmd->argc++] = xstrdup(text);
        }
        sb_free(&expanded);
    }
    (void)err;
    return 0;
}

static int run_simple(shell_ctx *ctx, simple_command *cmd, const char *stdin_text, sb *out, sb *err) {
    if (cmd->argc == 0) return 0;
    command_fn fn = find_command(cmd->argv[0]);
    if (!fn) {
        char buffer[1200];
        snprintf(buffer, sizeof(buffer), "%s: command not found (this shell runs everything in-process)", cmd->argv[0]);
        err_line(err, buffer);
        return 127;
    }
    const char *saved_stdin = g_stdin_text;
    g_stdin_text = stdin_text ? stdin_text : "";
    int status = fn(ctx, cmd->argc, cmd->argv, out, err);
    g_stdin_text = saved_stdin;
    return status;
}

static int run_segment(shell_ctx *ctx, tokens *list, int start, int end, sb *out, sb *err);

static int run_pipeline(shell_ctx *ctx, tokens *list, int start, int end, sb *out, sb *err) {
    /* split on '|' */
    int stage_start = start;
    sb previous;
    sb_init(&previous);
    int have_previous = 0;
    int status = 0;

    for (int i = start; i <= end; i++) {
        if (i == end || (list->items[i].op && strcmp(list->items[i].op, "|") == 0)) {
            simple_command cmd;
            parse_simple(list, stage_start, i, ctx, &cmd, err);
            sb stage_out;
            sb_init(&stage_out);
            status = run_simple(ctx, &cmd, have_previous ? previous.data : NULL, &stage_out, err);
            sb_free(&previous);
            previous = stage_out;
            have_previous = 1;
            stage_start = i + 1;
        }
    }
    if (previous.data) sb_puts(out, previous.data);
    sb_free(&previous);
    return status;
}

static int run_segment(shell_ctx *ctx, tokens *list, int start, int end, sb *out, sb *err) {
    if (start >= end) return 0;
    int i = start;
    int status = 0;
    while (i < end) {
        /* split on ';' '&&' '||' */
        int seg_end = i;
        char operator_[3] = { 0, 0, 0 };
        while (seg_end < end) {
            token *t = &list->items[seg_end];
            if (t->op && (strcmp(t->op, ";") == 0 || strcmp(t->op, "&&") == 0 || strcmp(t->op, "||") == 0)) {
                snprintf(operator_, sizeof(operator_), "%s", t->op);
                break;
            }
            seg_end++;
        }
        int run = 1;
        if (operator_[0] == '&' && status != 0) run = 0;
        if (operator_[0] == '|' && status == 0) run = 0;
        if (run) status = run_pipeline(ctx, list, i, seg_end, out, err);
        ctx->last_status = status;
        if (ctx->exit_requested) break;
        if (operator_[0] == '\0') break;
        i = seg_end + 1;
    }
    return status;
}

static int run_source(shell_ctx *ctx, const char *source, sb *out, sb *err) {
    tokens list;
    tokenize(source, &list);
    int status = 0;
    int i = 0;
    while (i < list.count) {
        /* one logical line/segment set at a time so redirections apply per command */
        int end = i;
        while (end < list.count) {
            token *t = &list.items[end];
            if (t->op && (strcmp(t->op, ";") == 0 || strcmp(t->op, "&&") == 0 || strcmp(t->op, "||") == 0)) break;
            end++;
        }
        sb local_out, local_err;
        sb_init(&local_out);
        sb_init(&local_err);
        status = run_pipeline(ctx, &list, i, end, &local_out, &local_err);
        ctx->last_status = status;

        /* redirections were parsed inside run_simple; handle whole-line ones here */
        simple_command probe;
        sb ignore;
        sb_init(&ignore);
        parse_simple(&list, i, end, ctx, &probe, &ignore);
        sb_free(&ignore);
        if (probe.redirect_out) {
            char full[4600];
            join_path(ctx, probe.redirect_out, full, sizeof(full));
            FILE *file = fopen(full, probe.append_out ? "ab" : "wb");
            if (file) { fwrite(local_out.data ? local_out.data : "", 1, local_out.len, file); fclose(file); }
            else err_path(&local_err, "redirect", probe.redirect_out, errno);
        } else if (local_out.data) sb_puts(out, local_out.data);
        if (probe.redirect_err) {
            char full[4600];
            join_path(ctx, probe.redirect_err, full, sizeof(full));
            FILE *file = fopen(full, "wb");
            if (file) { fwrite(local_err.data ? local_err.data : "", 1, local_err.len, file); fclose(file); }
        } else if (local_err.data) sb_puts(err, local_err.data);

        sb_free(&local_out);
        sb_free(&local_err);
        if (ctx->exit_requested) break;
        i = end + 1;
    }
    for (int k = 0; k < list.count; k++) { free(list.items[k].word); free(list.items[k].op); }
    return status;
}

/* --------------------------------------------------------------------- main */

static void usage(void) {
    fprintf(stderr, "usage: sh [-c command] [script] [args...]\n");
}

int main(int argc, char **argv) {
    const char *command = NULL;
    int index = 1;
    while (index < argc) {
        const char *arg = argv[index];
        if (strcmp(arg, "-c") == 0 && index + 1 < argc) { command = argv[index + 1]; index += 2; continue; }
        if (arg[0] == '-' && arg[1] != '\0' && !command) { index++; continue; }
        break;
    }

    shell_ctx ctx;
    memset(&ctx, 0, sizeof(ctx));
    if (!getcwd(ctx.cwd, sizeof(ctx.cwd))) snprintf(ctx.cwd, sizeof(ctx.cwd), "/");

    const char *source = command;
    sb file_content;
    sb_init(&file_content);
    if (!source) {
        if (index < argc) {
            if (read_file(argv[index], &file_content) != 0) {
                fprintf(stderr, "sh: %s: %s\n", argv[index], strerror(errno));
                return 127;
            }
            source = file_content.data ? file_content.data : "";
        } else {
            char buffer[8192];
            size_t n;
            while ((n = fread(buffer, 1, sizeof(buffer), stdin)) > 0) sb_putn(&file_content, buffer, n);
            source = file_content.data ? file_content.data : "";
        }
    }

    sb out, err;
    sb_init(&out);
    sb_init(&err);
    int status = run_source(&ctx, source, &out, &err);
    if (ctx.exit_requested) status = ctx.exit_code;
    if (out.data && out.len > 0) fwrite(out.data, 1, out.len, stdout);
    if (err.data && err.len > 0) fwrite(err.data, 1, err.len, stderr);
    fflush(stdout);
    fflush(stderr);
    sb_free(&out);
    sb_free(&err);
    sb_free(&file_content);
    return status;
}

```

`runtime/dash-ios/build.sh`:

```sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Cross-compile dash for iPhoneOS arm64.
#
# The device this app runs on has no bash/sh anywhere reachable (the jailbreak
# bootstrap is not on the app's path, and the app itself is confined to its
# container). The harness' bash tool therefore dies with `spawn bash ENOENT`.
# dash is a small POSIX shell that builds with a plain C compiler, so we ship one
# inside the app and expose it as both `sh` and `bash`.
#
# Usage: build.sh --artifact <dir>
set -euo pipefail

HERE="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
ROOT="$(cd "$HERE/../.." && pwd)"
DASH_VERSION="${DASH_VERSION:-0.5.12}"
IOS_TARGET="${IOS_TARGET:-15.0}"

ARTIFACT=""
if [ "${1:-}" = "--artifact" ]; then ARTIFACT="$(pwd)/${2:?artifact dir required}"; fi
OUT="${OUT_DIR:-$ROOT/.work/dash-ios}"

log() { printf '\033[36;1m==> %s\033[0m\n' "$*"; }

fetch() {
  mkdir -p "$OUT"
  if [ ! -d "$OUT/src" ]; then
    log "dash $DASH_VERSION source"
    curl -fsSL "http://gondor.apana.org.au/~herbert/dash/files/dash-$DASH_VERSION.tar.gz" \
      | tar xz -C "$OUT"
    mv "$OUT/dash-$DASH_VERSION" "$OUT/src"
  fi
}

build() {
  log "configure + make for iPhoneOS arm64"
  local SDK CC
  SDK="$(xcrun --sdk iphoneos --show-sdk-path)"
  CC="$(xcrun --sdk iphoneos -f clang)"
  cd "$OUT/src"
  make distclean >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
  ./configure \
    --host=aarch64-apple-darwin \
    --disable-glob \
    CC="$CC" \
    CFLAGS="-target arm64-apple-ios$IOS_TARGET -isysroot $SDK -O2 -fno-strict-aliasing -Wno-error" \
    LDFLAGS="-target arm64-apple-ios$IOS_TARGET -isysroot $SDK" \
    >"$OUT/configure.log" 2>&1 || { tail -30 "$OUT/configure.log"; exit 1; }
  make -j"$(sysctl -n hw.ncpu)" >"$OUT/make.log" 2>&1 || { tail -40 "$OUT/make.log"; exit 1; }
  test -x src/dash || { echo "dash binary not produced"; tail -20 "$OUT/make.log"; exit 1; }
  log "built: $(file src/dash)"

  log "compiling the control payload (hello)"
  "$CC" -target "arm64-apple-ios$IOS_TARGET" -isysroot "$SDK" -O1 -o "$OUT/src/hello" "$HERE/hello.c"
  log "built: $(file "$OUT/src/hello")"
}

main() {
  fetch
  build
  if [ -n "$ARTIFACT" ]; then
    rm -rf "$ARTIFACT"; mkdir -p "$ARTIFACT"
    cp "$OUT/src/src/dash" "$ARTIFACT/sh"
    cp "$OUT/src/hello" "$ARTIFACT/hello"
    chmod +x "$ARTIFACT/sh"
    log "artifact: $ARTIFACT"
    ls -l "$ARTIFACT"
    vtool -show-build "$ARTIFACT/sh" | head -4 || true
  fi
}

main

```

`runtime/dash-ios/hello.c`:

```c
/* Diagnostic payload: the smallest possible iOS executable. If this runs but
 * dash does not, the shell is at fault; if this also crashes, no external
 * binary can be executed on this device. */
#include <stdio.h>

int main(void) {
    printf("hello-ok\n");
    return 0;
}

```

`runtime/mksh-ios/build.sh`:

```sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Cross-compile mksh (The MirBSD Korn Shell) for iPhoneOS arm64.
#
# dash builds for iOS but segfaults at startup on the device (a control program
# compiled with the same toolchain runs, so the platform is fine). mksh avoids
# the whole autoconf layer — it ships Build.sh — and is the shell Android and
# many embedded systems use, so its syscall surface is small and predictable.
#
# Installed as `sh`; the app symlinks `bash` -> `sh` (mksh accepts the same -c
# invocation the harness uses).
#
# Usage: build.sh --artifact <dir>
set -euo pipefail

HERE="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
ROOT="$(cd "$HERE/../.." && pwd)"
MKSH_VERSION="${MKSH_VERSION:-mksh-R59c}"
IOS_TARGET="${IOS_TARGET:-15.0}"

ARTIFACT=""
if [ "${1:-}" = "--artifact" ]; then ARTIFACT="$(pwd)/${2:?artifact dir required}"; fi
OUT="${OUT_DIR:-$ROOT/.work/mksh-ios}"

log() { printf '\033[36;1m==> %s\033[0m\n' "$*"; }

fetch() {
  mkdir -p "$OUT"
  if [ ! -d "$OUT/src" ]; then
    log "mksh $MKSH_VERSION source"
    rm -rf "$OUT/mksh"
    git clone --depth 1 --branch "$MKSH_VERSION" https://github.com/MirBSD/mksh.git "$OUT/src"
  fi
}

build() {
  local SDK CC
  SDK="$(xcrun --sdk iphoneos --show-sdk-path)"
  CC="$(xcrun --sdk iphoneos -f clang)"
  cd "$OUT/src"
  log "Build.sh for iPhoneOS arm64"
  env CC="$CC" \
      CFLAGS="-target arm64-apple-ios$IOS_TARGET -isysroot $SDK -O2 -fno-strict-aliasing -D_DARWIN_C_SOURCE" \
      LDFLAGS="-target arm64-apple-ios$IOS_TARGET -isysroot $SDK" \
      TARGET_OS=Darwin \
      sh Build.sh -r >"$OUT/build.log" 2>&1 || { tail -40 "$OUT/build.log"; exit 1; }
  test -x mksh || { echo "mksh binary not produced"; tail -30 "$OUT/build.log"; exit 1; }
  log "built: $(file mksh)"
}

main() {
  fetch
  build
  if [ -n "$ARTIFACT" ]; then
    rm -rf "$ARTIFACT"; mkdir -p "$ARTIFACT"
    cp "$OUT/src/mksh" "$ARTIFACT/sh"
    chmod +x "$ARTIFACT/sh"
    log "artifact: $ARTIFACT"
    ls -l "$ARTIFACT"
    vtool -show-build "$ARTIFACT/sh" | head -4 || true
  fi
}

main

```

    Time Zone Database

      ICU uses the public domain data and code derived from Time Zone
    Database for its time zone support. The ownership of the TZ database
    is explained in BCP 175: Procedure for Maintaining the Time Zone
    Database section 7.

     # 7.  Database Ownership
     #
     #    The TZ database itself is not an IETF Contribution or an IETF
     #    document.  Rather it is a pre-existing and regularly updated work
     #    that is in the public domain, and is intended to remain in the
     #    public domain.  Therefore, BCPs 78 [RFC5378] and 79 [RFC3979] do
     #    not apply to the TZ Database or contributions that individuals make
     #    to it.  Should any claims be made and substantiated against the TZ
     #    Database, the organization that is providing the IANA
     #    Considerations defined in this RFC, under the memorandum of
     #    understanding with the IETF, currently ICANN, may act in accordance
     #    with all competent court orders.  No ownership claims will be made
     #    by ICANN or the IETF Trust on the database or the code.  Any person
     #    making a contribution to the database or code waives all rights to
     #    future claims in that contribution or in the TZ Database.
