`runtime/node-ios/NOTICE.md`:

```md
# NOTICE

This repository is a community port of **Node.js 26.13.0** to iPhoneOS (iOS) on
jailbroken ARM64 devices.

- Node.js is licensed under the MIT license; its `LICENSE` file is included
  verbatim (`LICENSE`, from the pinned `node-v26.13.0` source tarball).
- The cross-compilation support in `patches/nodejs26/` (configure/gyp iOS
  flavor support, host-toolset fixes, c-ares darwin config, crypto macOS-only
  API guards, and the V8 iOS executable-memory model) is original work for this
  port and is provided under the same MIT terms.
- No nodejs-mobile code is used: nodejs-mobile has no Node 22+/26 branch, so
  the iOS build patches are derived on the pristine upstream Node.js tree.
- The V8 W^X memory model follows the approach device-verified in the
  OpenCode iOS port (mprotect RW<->RX toggling); see docs/RUNTIME_BUILD.md.
```

`runtime/node-ios/compat/ios-posix-compat.c`:

```c
// iPhoneOS POSIX compatibility shim for the Node 26 runtime.
//
// iOS userland does not implement unnamed POSIX semaphores (sem_init returns
// ENOSYS) and the kernel sandbox terminates processes that call System V shared
// memory (shmget traps with SIGSYS). This dylib is loaded into the Node process
// via DYLD_INSERT_LIBRARIES by the node26 launcher and:
//
//   * interposes sem_init/sem_wait/sem_trywait/sem_post/sem_getvalue/sem_destroy
//     with process-local implementations backed by a mutex + condition variable;
//     calls for named semaphores (sem_open) fall through to libSystem.
//   * interposes shmget/shmat/shmdt/shmctl with an emulation backed by POSIX
//     shared memory (shm_open + mmap MAP_SHARED), which is implemented on iOS.
//     Segments created with IPC_PRIVATE get a deterministic name derived from
//     their id so another process that receives the id can attach as well.
//
// Scope/limits: unnamed semaphores are process-local (pshared=0/1 accepted but
// not shared across processes); System V segments are emulated, not kernel
// objects (no SELinux-style permissions, no System V semaphores/msg queues).
#include <dlfcn.h>
#include <errno.h>
#include <fcntl.h>
#include <pthread.h>
#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <sys/mman.h>
#include <sys/stat.h>
#include <unistd.h>

// Opaque ABI types: callers pass a pointer to their own sem_t storage; the
// shim only uses the pointer as a key and never dereferences it.
typedef struct ios_posix_sem { long opaque[8]; } ios_posix_sem_t;

// libSystem exposes these names but iOS rejects sem_init and SIGSYS-traps shmget.
// Declare them locally so this shim can interpose the symbols without including
// the SDK headers that mark them unavailable.
extern int sem_init(ios_posix_sem_t *sem, int pshared, unsigned int value);
extern int sem_destroy(ios_posix_sem_t *sem);
extern int sem_wait(ios_posix_sem_t *sem);
extern int sem_trywait(ios_posix_sem_t *sem);
extern int sem_post(ios_posix_sem_t *sem);
extern int sem_getvalue(ios_posix_sem_t *sem, int *value);
extern int shmget(int key, unsigned long size, int shmflg);
extern void *shmat(int shmid, const void *shmaddr, int shmflg);
extern int shmdt(const void *shmaddr);
extern int shmctl(int shmid, int cmd, void *buf);

#define IOS_SEM_MAX 256
#define IOS_SHM_MAX 128
#define IOS_SHM_NAME_MAX 64

typedef struct {
  int used;
  const ios_posix_sem_t *key;
  unsigned value;  // current count
} ios_sem_entry;

static ios_sem_entry ios_sems[IOS_SEM_MAX];
static pthread_mutex_t ios_sem_lock = PTHREAD_MUTEX_INITIALIZER;
static pthread_cond_t ios_sem_cond = PTHREAD_COND_INITIALIZER;

static ios_sem_entry *ios_sem_find_locked(const ios_posix_sem_t *key) {
  for (int i = 0; i < IOS_SEM_MAX; i++) {
    if (ios_sems[i].used && ios_sems[i].key == key) return &ios_sems[i];
  }
  return NULL;
}

static int ios_sem_init(ios_posix_sem_t *sem, int pshared, unsigned value) {
  if (sem == NULL) { errno = EINVAL; return -1; }
  if (pshared != 0 && pshared != 1) { errno = EINVAL; return -1; }
  pthread_mutex_lock(&ios_sem_lock);
  ios_sem_entry *existing = ios_sem_find_locked(sem);
  if (existing != NULL) {
    pthread_mutex_unlock(&ios_sem_lock);
    errno = EBUSY;
    return -1;
  }
  for (int i = 0; i < IOS_SEM_MAX; i++) {
    if (!ios_sems[i].used) {
      ios_sems[i].used = 1;
      ios_sems[i].key = sem;
      ios_sems[i].value = value;
      pthread_mutex_unlock(&ios_sem_lock);
      return 0;
    }
  }
  pthread_mutex_unlock(&ios_sem_lock);
  errno = ENOSPC;
  return -1;
}

static int ios_sem_destroy(ios_posix_sem_t *sem) {
  if (sem == NULL) { errno = EINVAL; return -1; }
  pthread_mutex_lock(&ios_sem_lock);
  ios_sem_entry *entry = ios_sem_find_locked(sem);
  if (entry == NULL) {
    pthread_mutex_unlock(&ios_sem_lock);
    // Possibly a named semaphore: calls from this interposing image are exempt
    // from dyld interposition, so this reaches libSystem's real sem_destroy.
    return sem_destroy(sem);
  }
  entry->used = 0;
  pthread_cond_broadcast(&ios_sem_cond);
  pthread_mutex_unlock(&ios_sem_lock);
  return 0;
}

static int ios_sem_wait(ios_posix_sem_t *sem) {
  if (sem == NULL) { errno = EINVAL; return -1; }
  pthread_mutex_lock(&ios_sem_lock);
  ios_sem_entry *entry = ios_sem_find_locked(sem);
  if (entry == NULL) {
    pthread_mutex_unlock(&ios_sem_lock);
    return sem_wait(sem);
  }
  while (entry->value == 0) pthread_cond_wait(&ios_sem_cond, &ios_sem_lock);
  entry->value -= 1;
  pthread_mutex_unlock(&ios_sem_lock);
  return 0;
}

static int ios_sem_trywait(ios_posix_sem_t *sem) {
  if (sem == NULL) { errno = EINVAL; return -1; }
  pthread_mutex_lock(&ios_sem_lock);
  ios_sem_entry *entry = ios_sem_find_locked(sem);
  if (entry == NULL) {
    pthread_mutex_unlock(&ios_sem_lock);
    return sem_trywait(sem);
  }
  if (entry->value == 0) {
    pthread_mutex_unlock(&ios_sem_lock);
    errno = EAGAIN;
    return -1;
  }
  entry->value -= 1;
  pthread_mutex_unlock(&ios_sem_lock);
  return 0;
}

static int ios_sem_post(ios_posix_sem_t *sem) {
  if (sem == NULL) { errno = EINVAL; return -1; }
  pthread_mutex_lock(&ios_sem_lock);
  ios_sem_entry *entry = ios_sem_find_locked(sem);
  if (entry == NULL) {
    pthread_mutex_unlock(&ios_sem_lock);
    return sem_post(sem);
  }
  entry->value += 1;
  pthread_cond_signal(&ios_sem_cond);
  pthread_mutex_unlock(&ios_sem_lock);
  return 0;
}

static int ios_sem_getvalue(ios_posix_sem_t *sem, int *value) {
  if (sem == NULL || value == NULL) { errno = EINVAL; return -1; }
  pthread_mutex_lock(&ios_sem_lock);
  ios_sem_entry *entry = ios_sem_find_locked(sem);
  if (entry == NULL) {
    pthread_mutex_unlock(&ios_sem_lock);
    return sem_getvalue(sem, value);
  }
  *value = (int)entry->value;
  pthread_mutex_unlock(&ios_sem_lock);
  return 0;
}

// ---- System V shared memory emulation -------------------------------------

#define IOS_IPC_PRIVATE 0
#define IOS_IPC_CREAT 01000
#define IOS_IPC_RMID 0
#define IOS_SHM_PRIVATE_FLAG 0x40000000
#define IOS_SHM_ANON_MAX (128 * 1024 * 1024)
#define IOS_SHM_ANON_MAP ((void *)-1)

typedef struct {
  int used;
  int created;
  int id;
  size_t size;
  void *mapping;
} ios_shm_segment;

static ios_shm_segment ios_shms[IOS_SHM_MAX];
static pthread_mutex_t ios_shm_lock = PTHREAD_MUTEX_INITIALIZER;
static unsigned int ios_shm_counter = 0;

static void ios_shm_name_for_id(int id, char out[IOS_SHM_NAME_MAX]) {
  if ((id & IOS_SHM_PRIVATE_FLAG) != 0) {
    snprintf(out, IOS_SHM_NAME_MAX, "/n26sv-%d", id & ~IOS_SHM_PRIVATE_FLAG);
  } else {
    snprintf(out, IOS_SHM_NAME_MAX, "/n26svk-%d", id);
  }
}

static int ios_shm_create(const char *name, size_t size) {
  int fd = shm_open(name, O_CREAT | O_RDWR | O_EXCL, 0600);
  if (fd < 0) return -1;
  if (ftruncate(fd, (off_t)size) != 0) {
    int saved = errno;
    close(fd);
    shm_unlink(name);
    errno = saved;
    return -1;
  }
  close(fd);
  return 0;
}

static int ios_shmget(int key, size_t size, int shmflg) {
  (void)shmflg;
  if (size == 0 || size > IOS_SHM_ANON_MAX) { errno = EINVAL; return -1; }
  pthread_mutex_lock(&ios_shm_lock);
  char name[IOS_SHM_NAME_MAX];
  int id;
  if (key == IOS_IPC_PRIVATE) {
    // Keep the id a positive 31-bit value: callers treat negative returns as errors
    // and the high bit is also used to identify private segments in the name.
    id = (int)((((unsigned)getpid() & 0x7ffffu) << 8) ^ (ios_shm_counter++ & 0xffu));
    id = (id & ~IOS_SHM_PRIVATE_FLAG) | IOS_SHM_PRIVATE_FLAG;
    ios_shm_name_for_id(id, name);
    if (ios_shm_create(name, size) != 0) {
      pthread_mutex_unlock(&ios_shm_lock);
      return -1;
    }
  } else {
    // Keyed segments: reuse an existing segment when present, otherwise create
    // it only when IPC_CREAT was requested.
    id = key & 0x3fffffff;
    ios_shm_name_for_id(id, name);
    int fd = shm_open(name, O_RDWR, 0);
    if (fd >= 0) {
      close(fd);
      pthread_mutex_unlock(&ios_shm_lock);
      return id;
    }
    if ((shmflg & IOS_IPC_CREAT) == 0) {
      pthread_mutex_unlock(&ios_shm_lock);
      errno = ENOENT;
      return -1;
    }
    if (ios_shm_create(name, size) != 0) {
      pthread_mutex_unlock(&ios_shm_lock);
      return -1;
    }
  }
  for (int i = 0; i < IOS_SHM_MAX; i++) {
    if (!ios_shms[i].used) {
      ios_shms[i].used = 1;
      ios_shms[i].created = 1;
      ios_shms[i].id = id;
      ios_shms[i].size = size;
      ios_shms[i].mapping = IOS_SHM_ANON_MAP;
      pthread_mutex_unlock(&ios_shm_lock);
      return id;
    }
  }
  pthread_mutex_unlock(&ios_shm_lock);
  shm_unlink(name);
  errno = ENOSPC;
  return -1;
}

static void *ios_shmat(int id, const void *addr, int shmflg) {
  (void)addr;
  (void)shmflg;
  char name[IOS_SHM_NAME_MAX];
  ios_shm_name_for_id(id, name);
  pthread_mutex_lock(&ios_shm_lock);
  for (int i = 0; i < IOS_SHM_MAX; i++) {
    if (ios_shms[i].used && ios_shms[i].id == id && ios_shms[i].mapping != IOS_SHM_ANON_MAP) {
      void *mapping = ios_shms[i].mapping;
      pthread_mutex_unlock(&ios_shm_lock);
      return mapping;
    }
  }
  pthread_mutex_unlock(&ios_shm_lock);
  int fd = shm_open(name, O_RDWR, 0);
  if (fd < 0) return IOS_SHM_ANON_MAP;
  struct stat status;
  if (fstat(fd, &status) != 0 || status.st_size <= 0) {
    int saved = errno ? errno : EINVAL;
    close(fd);
    errno = saved;
    return IOS_SHM_ANON_MAP;
  }
  size_t length = (size_t)status.st_size;
  void *mapping = mmap(NULL, length, PROT_READ | PROT_WRITE, MAP_SHARED, fd, 0);
  close(fd);
  if (mapping == MAP_FAILED) return IOS_SHM_ANON_MAP;
  pthread_mutex_lock(&ios_shm_lock);
  int stored = 0;
  for (int i = 0; i < IOS_SHM_MAX; i++) {
    if (!ios_shms[i].used) {
      ios_shms[i].used = 1;
      ios_shms[i].created = 0;
      ios_shms[i].id = id;
      ios_shms[i].size = length;
      ios_shms[i].mapping = mapping;
      stored = 1;
      break;
    }
  }
  pthread_mutex_unlock(&ios_shm_lock);
  if (!stored) {
    munmap(mapping, length);
    errno = ENOSPC;
    return IOS_SHM_ANON_MAP;
  }
  return mapping;
}

static int ios_shmdt(const void *addr) {
  if (addr == NULL || addr == MAP_FAILED) { errno = EINVAL; return -1; }
  pthread_mutex_lock(&ios_shm_lock);
  for (int i = 0; i < IOS_SHM_MAX; i++) {
    if (ios_shms[i].used && ios_shms[i].mapping == addr) {
      void *mapping = ios_shms[i].mapping;
      size_t size = ios_shms[i].size;
      ios_shms[i].used = 0;
      ios_shms[i].created = 0;
      ios_shms[i].mapping = IOS_SHM_ANON_MAP;
      pthread_mutex_unlock(&ios_shm_lock);
      if (munmap(mapping, size) != 0) return -1;
      return 0;
    }
  }
  pthread_mutex_unlock(&ios_shm_lock);
  errno = EINVAL;
  return -1;
}

static int ios_shmctl(int id, int cmd, void *buf) {
  (void)buf;
  if (cmd != IOS_IPC_RMID) { errno = EINVAL; return -1; }
  char name[IOS_SHM_NAME_MAX];
  ios_shm_name_for_id(id, name);
  if (shm_unlink(name) != 0) {
    if (errno == ENOENT) { errno = EINVAL; }
    return -1;
  }
  pthread_mutex_lock(&ios_shm_lock);
  for (int i = 0; i < IOS_SHM_MAX; i++) {
    if (ios_shms[i].used && ios_shms[i].id == id && ios_shms[i].mapping == IOS_SHM_ANON_MAP) {
      ios_shms[i].used = 0;
      break;
    }
  }
  pthread_mutex_unlock(&ios_shm_lock);
  return 0;
}

// ---- dyld interposing -----------------------------------------------------

#define DYLD_INTERPOSE(_replacement, _replacee)                                                 \
  __attribute__((used)) static struct {                                                         \
    const void *replacement;                                                                    \
    const void *replacee;                                                                       \
  } _interpose_##_replacee __attribute__((section("__DATA,__interpose"))) = {                   \
      (const void *)(unsigned long)&_replacement, (const void *)(unsigned long)&_replacee};

DYLD_INTERPOSE(ios_sem_init, sem_init)
DYLD_INTERPOSE(ios_sem_destroy, sem_destroy)
DYLD_INTERPOSE(ios_sem_wait, sem_wait)
DYLD_INTERPOSE(ios_sem_trywait, sem_trywait)
DYLD_INTERPOSE(ios_sem_post, sem_post)
DYLD_INTERPOSE(ios_sem_getvalue, sem_getvalue)
DYLD_INTERPOSE(ios_shmget, shmget)
DYLD_INTERPOSE(ios_shmat, shmat)
DYLD_INTERPOSE(ios_shmdt, shmdt)
DYLD_INTERPOSE(ios_shmctl, shmctl)

static void ios_posix_compat_cleanup(void) {
  // Best-effort cleanup of POSIX shm names created for System V emulation.
  for (int i = 0; i < IOS_SHM_MAX; i++) {
    if (ios_shms[i].used && ios_shms[i].created) {
      char name[IOS_SHM_NAME_MAX];
      ios_shm_name_for_id(ios_shms[i].id, name);
      shm_unlink(name);
    }
  }
}

__attribute__((constructor)) static void ios_posix_compat_init(void) {
  atexit(ios_posix_compat_cleanup);
}

```

`runtime/node-ios/docs/POSIX_JIT_WASM_DEVICE_RESULTS.md`:

```md
# Node.js 26.13.0：JIT / wasm 与 POSIX 系统操作真机报告（26.13.0-2，全部 PASS）

2026-11-03，iPhone SE 2（`iPhone12,8`）、iOS 16.1.1、Darwin 22.1.0、Dopamine rootless。
以 `mobile`（uid 501）运行，终端为 EverTerm 1.5-2（真实 SSH PTY）。

**21 PASS / 0 UNSUPPORTED / 0 RESTRICTED / 0 FAIL，主测试退出码 0。**
上一版报告中两项 iOS 限制已被修复：

- 未命名信号量 `sem_init`（此前 ENOSYS=78）→ **PASS**
- System V `shmget`（此前内核 SIGSYS 终止进程）→ **PASS**（shmget/shmat/shmdt/IPC_RMID 跨进程完整可用）

修复方式是 `node26` `26.13.0-2` 引入的 **POSIX 兼容 dylib**：`node` 启动器通过
`DYLD_INSERT_LIBRARIES` 注入 `libios-posix-compat.dylib`，用 dyld interposing 替换
`sem_init/sem_wait/sem_trywait/sem_post/sem_getvalue/sem_destroy` 与
`shmget/shmat/shmdt/shmctl`。未命名信号量以进程内 mutex/cond 实现（命名信号量回退到
libSystem）；System V 共享内存以 POSIX `shm_open` + `mmap(MAP_SHARED)` 模拟，
`IPC_PRIVATE` 段使用由 id 派生的确定性名字，因此另一进程可通过 id 附加。

## 工件与方法

| 项目 | 实测身份 |
| --- | --- |
| node26 deb | [run 41648623126](https://github.com/Justintunsday/nodejs26-ios/actions/runs/41648623126)，`node26_26.13.0-2_iphoneos-arm64.deb`，25,899,420 B，SHA-256 `8e9979639645fb57262653c84dd705a0fab12acf3e8e4e8b9cb5f7ef793f63b9`（9 个 payload 文件，26 项静态检查 PASS） |
| Node 可执行文件 | [run 41549633128](https://github.com/Justintunsday/nodejs26-ios/actions/runs/41549633128)，134,710,336 B，SHA-256 `84613100b3d31a747a55d1132906bfef9417b15a1e33a4088ff1da5956e61ef9`（与上一版相同，未重新编译） |
| POSIX 兼容 dylib | [run 41647498001](https://github.com/Justintunsday/nodejs26-ios/actions/runs/41647498001)，86,928 B，SHA-256 `5ac7fcb88ce61ece5a2d5acf6bb8373940767e5bfa7babbcc0dec78c92310895`，Apple codesign ad-hoc |
| 原生测试库 | [run 41650208508](https://github.com/Justintunsday/nodejs26-ios/actions/runs/41650208508)，87,840 B，SHA-256 `cd2f8a9e694b335346f738747f7df425fb16933615b1f2430318436e36900a0a`，clang/ld64 + Apple codesign ad-hoc |
| 测试脚本 | [`tests/posix-jit-wasm.cjs`](../tests/posix-jit-wasm.cjs)，SHA-256 `b82a7532d0cbfdfaea80ca65272d64605d84042a7aa13a320895e7351ba4fc3a` |
| 本轮矩阵原始日志 | 本地 `posix-final/matrix.log`，SHA-256 `120b5d960b363d67735def8a98b69f03b5bd6a6bc4953a5cc5b6902fac15d235` |

Node V8 `14.6.202.34-node.40`；主进程参数
`--single-threaded --allow-natives-syntax --no-warnings --max-old-space-size=256`。
原生库位于 root:wheel / 0755 的 `/var/jb` 专用目录；测试库源码是
[`native-posix-probe.c`](../tests/native-posix-probe.c)，由脚本通过 `node:ffi` 调用。
主进程累计 **141 次已返回原生调用**、**104 轮引擎脉冲**；`%GetOptimizationStatus(jit) = 41`
（function + optimized + TurboFan）。兼容 dylib 只在 `node` 启动器（或 OpenCode 启动器）注入，
原生二进制路径直调用者需自行设置 `DYLD_INSERT_LIBRARIES`（见“限制”）。

## 操作结果

| 操作 | 结论 | 实际证据 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| JavaScript JIT + wasm | PASS | 优化状态 41；wasm 返回 42；Memory 分配成功 |
| libc open/write/fsync/read/close/unlink | PASS | UTF-8 内容 21 B，写入/读取长度相同，内容一致 |
| open 不存在的文件 | PASS（错误路径） | -1，errno 2 / ENOENT |
| Node fs.open 异步操作 | PASS | 写入、sync、关闭、读回、删除 |
| sysctlbyname / sysctl MIB，只读 | PASS | `kern.osrelease=22.1.0`、`hw.machine=iPhone12,8`、页 16384、memsize；CTL_KERN/KERN_OSRELEASE |
| libc pipe | PASS | 双 fd，16 B 往返一致 |
| mmap/mprotect/munmap | PASS | 16 KiB 匿名映射，RW → R → RW |
| Node spawnSync / spawn / execFile / exec | PASS | 子进程 JIT 41、wasm 42、退出 0；shell 输出 `NODE_EXEC_SHELL_OK` |
| 原生 posix_spawn/waitpid | PASS | spawn 0，waitpid 子 pid，wait status 4352（退出码 17） |
| 原生 execve | PASS，附 stdout 限制 | 替换前后同 pid；新进程 JIT 41 / wasm 42、退出 0；采集 stdout 仍为 0 B（见限制） |
| execve 错误路径 | PASS | -1，errno 2 / ENOENT |
| POSIX shm_open + mmap | PASS | 16 KiB MAP_SHARED；父写 0x12345678，子进程读并改 0x23456789，父读回一致 |
| POSIX 命名 semaphore | PASS | 空空 trywait EAGAIN=35；post 后子进程取得并 post；close/unlink 0 |
| **POSIX 未命名 sem_init/sem_destroy** | **PASS** | `sem_init=0`、`sem_trywait=0`、`sem_post=0`、`sem_destroy=0`（兼容 dylib） |
| **System V shmget/shmat/shmdt/IPC_RMID** | **PASS** | `shmget` 返回正 id；`shmat` 映射成功；父写、独立子进程读改、父读回；`shmdt` / `shmctl(IPC_RMID)` 返回 0 |
| 24 轮交错压力 | PASS | JIT / wasm / sysctl / open-write-close 结果一致 |
| 命名 IPC 清理 | PASS | shm_open 与 sem_open 再次打开均 ENOENT |

`sem_init` 的 `pshared` 参数兼容 0/1，但实现是进程内状态，不跨进程共享未命名信号量。
System V 段为 POSIX-shm 模拟：无内核 SysV 权限/键空间语义，也不提供 System V 信号量与消息队列。

### execve 的 stdout 限制（已确认范围，不再列为 UNSUPPORTED）

`execve` 本身工作正常：同 pid、引擎状态与退出码均正确，文件落盘证据完整；但被替换的
Node 进程写入继承的 stdout 管道的数据未被父进程采集（同时试过 `console.log` 与
`fs.writeSync(1,...)`，均 0 B；直接 execve 到 `/var/jb/bin/sh` 也未采集到输出）。
根因未隔离到单一机制，属于测试采集/终端继承边界；需要输出的程序请使用已验证的
`child_process.spawn/execFile/exec` 路径。

## 无沙盒 wasm 补充测试

独立测试 rc=0：`v8_enable_sandbox=0`；运行中的 Node 二进制签名含
`com.apple.private.security.no-sandbox=true`。wasm add 42；内存扩至 131072 B；
6,000,000 次调用校验和 3074877120；wasm 经宿主导入写私有文件；24 轮 wasm 编译与 HTTP 请求
全部成功（374 ms）；越界访问仍抛出预期异常。关闭 V8 sandbox、iOS entitlement 与 wasm 线性
内存边界是不同机制，以上未证明 wasm 可绕过内存边界。

## 崩溃记录

本轮兼容矩阵后 **没有新增 Node 崩溃报告**。安装前基线 5 份 Node `.ips`，其中：

- `node-2026-11-03-202540.ips`、`202743.ips`：旧版 `shmget` 能力探测的 SIGSYS。
- `node-2026-11-03-213932.ips`、`213933.ips`、`214709.ips`：为证明旧行为而**故意**在
  未注入兼容 dylib 的基线/调试运行中触发的 `shmget` SIGSYS，不是兼容矩阵产生，也不是 JIT SIGBUS。

兼容矩阵运行前后 Node 崩溃数均为 5，未出现新的 SIGSYS/JIT SIGBUS。
**旧报告中“shmget 会 SIGSYS 终止进程”的结论仅适用于 26.13.0-1 及未注入兼容 dylib 的调用。**

## 复验

```sh
gh workflow run build-posix-probe.yml -R Justintunsday/nodejs26-ios
gh run download 41650208508 -R Justintunsday/nodejs26-ios -n ios-posix-test-bridge
sha256sum -c SHA256SUMS
```

把测试库安装到 `/var/jb` 下 root:wheel / 0755 的目录（`/var/mobile` 路径会被 dyld 拒绝执行映射），
脚本放在自己拥有的目录，然后用 `node` 命令（启动器会注入兼容 dylib）：

```sh
NODE26_POSIX_BRIDGE=/var/jb/usr/lib/node26-tests/libnode26-posix-probe.dylib \
  node --allow-natives-syntax --no-warnings --max-old-space-size=256 \
  /absolute/test/posix-jit-wasm.cjs main /absolute/test
```

脚本输出 `posix-jit-wasm-results.json`，摘要行应为
`POSIX_JIT_WASM_SUMMARY {"PASS":21,"UNSUPPORTED":0,"RESTRICTED":0,"FAIL":0}`。

## 限制

- 未命名信号量仅在进程内有效；命名信号量仍由 libSystem 提供，兼容 dylib 只负责透传。
- System V 共享内存为 POSIX-shm 模拟，不是内核 SysV 对象；System V 信号量、消息队列未实现。
- 兼容 dylib 由 `node` 启动器注入；直接执行 `/var/jb/usr/lib/node26/node` 的程序需要自行
  `export DYLD_INSERT_LIBRARIES=/var/jb/usr/lib/node26/libios-posix-compat.dylib`
  （OpenCode 的启动器已注入）。
- `execve` 的 stdout 采集限制仍存在（见上）。
- 覆盖范围仅限本设备、运行时、权限与所列操作；未测试写 sysctl、裸 fork、用户
  `worker_threads`、任意可执行页权限或其他系统版本。`--single-threaded` 仍是必需条件。

结构化证据：[`evidence/posix-jit-wasm-20261103-compat.json`](evidence/posix-jit-wasm-20261103-compat.json)。

```

`runtime/node-ios/docs/ROOTLESS_PACKAGE.md`:

```md
# Standalone Node.js 26 rootless package

The experimental `node26` package installs the `node` command and a complete
Node.js 26.13.0 iPhoneOS ARM64 executable under `/var/jb/usr/lib/node26/`.
It can be used independently of OpenCode. The package owns its own files; it provides the default `node` command. OpenCode can depend on this package without
embedding another runtime. The package author is `nodejs`.

## Pinned runtime

- Runtime artifact: `ios-node26-runtime-ffi`, run
  [41549633128](https://github.com/Justintunsday/nodejs26-ios/actions/runs/41549633128).
- Runtime source: `7cd359f377d9d37dfe13d929927cf67274451ec2`.
- `node`: 134,710,336 bytes, SHA-256
  `84613100b3d31a747a55d1132906bfef9417b15a1e33a4088ff1da5956e61ef9`.
- Includes iOS W^X, wasm trap-handler/allocator, libffi callback 0014/0015 and
  foreground V8 JobHandle 0016 fixes. The already signed executable is packaged
  without modification or resigning.

## Accepted package and device scope

Package [run 41648623126](https://github.com/Justintunsday/nodejs26-ios/actions/runs/41648623126),
artifact `node26-rootless`: `node26_26.13.0-2_iphoneos-arm64.deb`,
**25,899,420 bytes**, SHA-256 `8e9979639645fb57262653c84dd705a0fab12acf3e8e4e8b9cb5f7ef793f63b9`. Control metadata: `Package: node26`,
`Version: 26.13.0-2`, `Author: nodejs`, maintainer display name `nodejs`.
CI passed 26 runtime/package checks plus native Debian metadata/extraction checks.

The package also ships **`/var/jb/usr/lib/node26/libios-posix-compat.dylib`**
(86,928 B, SHA-256 `5ac7fcb88ce61ece5a2d5acf6bb8373940767e5bfa7babbcc0dec78c92310895`,
Apple codesign ad-hoc). The `node` launcher exports
`DYLD_INSERT_LIBRARIES` before exec; dyld interposition replaces the iOS-limited
`sem_init/sem_wait/sem_trywait/sem_post/sem_getvalue/sem_destroy` and
`shmget/shmat/shmdt/shmctl`. Unnamed semaphores are process-local (named
semaphores still use libSystem); System V shared memory is emulated with POSIX
`shm_open`+`mmap` and IPC_PRIVATE ids map to deterministic names so another
process can attach by id.

**DEVICE-VERIFIED**, iPhone SE 2 / iOS 16.1.1 / Dopamine rootless, 2026-11-03:
installation rc=0; command `node` and independent executable identity passed;
11 checks cover JIT checksum 5999999, wasm function 42/memory, ICU/Unicode, FFI
native/reentrant callback, SQLite persistence, ESM/stdin, fork flag inheritance,
HTTP/HTTPS 200 and exact signed binary SHA. Stress passed 192 HTTP requests and
24 wasm modules; arguments with spaces/stdin passed; real Node REPL `2+3` → `5`,
`.exit` rc=0. `dpkg --verify node26 opencode` returned 0.

OpenCode [package run 41649519712](https://github.com/Justintunsday/opencode-ios/actions/runs/41649519712)
installs app `2.2.5-5` with exact dependency `node26 (= 26.13.0-2)` and no embedded
Node executable. CLI 8/0, strict JSON/tool E2E, normal TUI rc=0 and three-turn
121.600-second TUI rc=0 passed using the independent runtime. Source docs and
[full device evidence](https://github.com/Justintunsday/opencode-ios/blob/main/docs/NODE26_EXTERNAL_DEVICE_RESULTS.md)
record the exact package pair, exits and raw PTY checksum.

CrashReporter changed 0 → 3 system Jetsam reports; none lists a terminated Node
victim and there was no Node crash report. System daemon memory-pressure/per-process
terminations were observed. Runtime verification is scoped to these executed tests;
indefinite uptime and other devices remain unverified. Static CI reports cannot
verify device execution.

## Installation and use

On a rootless jailbroken iPhone with `/var/jb` and Procursus `dpkg`:

```sh
sudo dpkg -i node26_26.13.0-2_iphoneos-arm64.deb
node --version
node -p 'process.platform'
node -e 'console.log(2 + 3)'
node script.cjs
node --input-type=module -e 'console.log(await Promise.resolve(42))'
```

The launcher always passes `--single-threaded`. The executable retains its
`platform-application` and `com.apple.private.security.no-sandbox` entitlements.
JIT, WebAssembly, full ICU, native SQLite and `node:ffi` are included.

## Limits

The later [JIT/wasm + POSIX report](POSIX_JIT_WASM_DEVICE_RESULTS.md) verifies native
open, read-only sysctl, Node/native exec/spawn, POSIX shared-memory mapping, named
semaphore handoff, **unnamed semaphores** and **System V shared memory** on this
device: 21 PASS / 0 UNSUPPORTED / 0 FAIL. The compatibility shim is injected by the
`node` launcher; directly executing `/var/jb/usr/lib/node26/node` bypasses it unless
`DYLD_INSERT_LIBRARIES=/var/jb/usr/lib/node26/libios-posix-compat.dylib` is set.
A no-sandbox entitlement does not make every dylib path loadable: the same signed
test library was rejected under `/var/mobile` and worked after root-owned installation
under `/var/jb`. Native execve replaced the process successfully, but its original
stdout capture was not preserved (use child_process for output). These are measured
limits, not claims about all POSIX calls or other iOS versions.

This is an experimental runtime for jailbroken iPhoneOS, with device coverage on
iPhone SE 2 / iOS 16.1.1 / Dopamine rootless. The binary minimum is iOS 15.0;
other devices and system versions require their own acceptance.

- **Use `node` for launching programs.** It also injects the POSIX compatibility
  dylib. Direct execution of the underlying binary requires `--single-threaded`
  and `DYLD_INSERT_LIBRARIES=/var/jb/usr/lib/node26/libios-posix-compat.dylib`
  for sem_init/System V shm: iOS code-page permission changes apply to the entire
  process, so background V8 execution can race JIT writes.
- **Node user `worker_threads` are unsupported.** The flag does not disable user
  worker isolates. OpenCode separately disables its tree-sitter worker; this
  standalone runtime does not patch arbitrary applications.
- Forward `--single-threaded` when explicitly spawning `process.execPath`.
  Standard `child_process.fork()` inherits `process.execArgv` unless overridden.
- Temporal was disabled when this runtime was built. Native addons require an
  iPhoneOS build. npm/npx are separate from this runtime-only package.
- `--jitless` is a diagnostic option; it disables the WebAssembly path needed by
  built-in fetch, so it is unsuitable for normal network applications.

Remove dependent OpenCode first, or switch to an OpenCode variant without this
dependency, before `sudo dpkg -r node26`. The runtime owns no user data and does
not terminate running applications. The earlier prototype package `nodejs26-ios`
must be removed before installing `node26`; it provided the old `node26` command.
The new package keeps a relative internal `usr/lib/nodejs26/node` compatibility
link for processes already running from that prototype path.

## Build the deb

The package workflow consumes the exact runtime in `packaging/runtime-pin.json`.
Assembly uses Python's standard library and does not need Xcode or another native
compilation. Linux CI additionally checks the deb with `dpkg-deb`.

```sh
python3 scripts/package-rootless-node26.py --runtime /path/to/downloaded/runtime
```

Output: `dist/node26-rootless/`, containing the deb, `package-report.json`
and `SHA256SUMS`. File timestamps/ownership are fixed to the pinned source epoch.

```

`runtime/node-ios/docs/RUNTIME_BUILD.md`:

```md
# Node.js 26.13.0 iPhoneOS runtime — Phase A (build feasibility gate)

> Current acceptance includes the standalone Node 26.13.0 package (26.13.0-2)
> and the [JIT/wasm + POSIX device matrix](POSIX_JIT_WASM_DEVICE_RESULTS.md):
> 21 PASS / 0 UNSUPPORTED / 0 FAIL. The phase notes below preserve build history.

Status: **Phase A in progress** — goal is a CI-green native iPhoneOS arm64 Node
26.13.0 `node` executable plus static Mach-O/entitlement validation. V8 14.6's
iOS executable-memory model is **not** adapted to iOS 16.1.1 yet (Phase B), so
JIT/WebAssembly execution on the device is expected to need that follow-up work.

## Why Node 26

The real OpenTUI TUI loads its native Zig core through Node's `node:ffi`, which
exists since Node 26.1 and requires Node >= 26.4. The device-verified runtime is
Node 22.9.0, which has neither `--experimental-ffi` nor `node:ffi`
(device-confirmed: `bad option: --experimental-ffi`). Node 26 is therefore the
Phase A prerequisite; Phase B then ports our V8 W^X work to V8 14.6, and Phase C
covers the OpenTUI Zig kernel/FFI bring-up.

## Pins

| Item | Value |
| --- | --- |
| source | `https://nodejs.org/dist/v26.13.0/node-v26.13.0.tar.gz` (upstream nodejs/node; no nodejs-mobile branch exists for 24/26) |
| tarball sha256 | `986ead18ef18256831bae3e57d94aef680a7b84cfd4044f3e8471f833fbd7191` (127,162,302 B) |
| Node version | 26.13.0 (V8 14.6.202.34) |
| Xcode / SDK | 16.4 / iPhoneOS SDK 18.5 |
| target | iPhoneOS arm64, `IPHONEOS_DEPLOYMENT_TARGET=15.0` |

## Patches (`patches/nodejs26/`)

`0011-wasm-ios-platform-page-allocator.patch` -- **Phase B wasm fix**: with 0009
in place, device testing confirmed JIT works but WebAssembly trapped with
`V8_Fatal` from `WasmCodeManager::TryAllocate`. V8 14.6's Chrome-iOS-only
`GetPageAllocator()` branch requires `IsolateGroup::current()->GetCodeRange()`
(the Chrome assumption: MAP_JIT is usable once and the CodeRange consumed it) and
otherwise hits `UNREACHABLE()`. This Node build has no pointer compression, so
there is no CodeRange, and MAP_JIT is disabled entirely (0005/0007). Patch 0011
falls back to `GetPlatformPageAllocator()` on iOS when no CodeRange allocator
exists and reserves wasm code space with plain `kNoAccess` instead of the
`kNoAccessWillJitLater` MAP_JIT hint; `WasmCodeManager::Commit()` (0007) already
grants RW then RX and registers the range for the write scopes.

`0007-v8-ios-mprotect-wx.patch` -- **Phase B**: V8 14.6 assumes Chrome-iOS
semantics for executable memory (MAP_JIT / RWX code pages / BrowserEngineCore
for iOS 17.4+), none of which work on the target iPhone (iOS 16.1.1: MAP_JIT mmap
= EINVAL, RWX requests silently drop PROT_EXEC, `pthread_jit_write_protect_np`
absent). The patch ports the **device-verified Node 22.9 model** to V8 14.6:
`V8_HAS_PTHREAD_JIT_WRITE_PROTECT=1` on iOS arm64; a new
`src/base/platform/platform-ios.cc` implements `RegisterIOSCodeRange()` and
`SetJitWriteProtected()` as a plain `mprotect` RW<->RX toggler over registered
code ranges; the code range is reserved `PROT_NONE` and each executable chunk is
committed RW and registered (`memory-allocator.cc`); re-embedded builtins and
wasm code spaces commit RW->RX and register; `platform-posix.cc` never requests
MAP_JIT on iOS; `mutable-page.h` never requests RWX when write scopes are
enabled; `code-range.cc`/`wasm-code-manager.cc` iOS branches are adjusted
accordingly; `tools/v8_gypfiles/v8.gyp` compiles `platform-ios.cc` for the iOS
target. Non-iOS behaviour is unchanged (all edits are
`V8_OS_IOS`/`V8_TARGET_OS_IOS`-guarded). This keeps the same launcher constraint
as the Node 22 runtime: **`--single-threaded`** is required for JIT/wasm because
the mprotect toggle is process-wide.

`0006-node-crypto-ios-no-sectrustsettings.patch` — `src/crypto/crypto_context.cc`
guards the macOS keychain/trust-settings certificate reader with
`#ifdef __APPLE__` (also true on iOS), but the `SecTrustSettings*` APIs only
exist in the macOS Security framework. The iOS build failed with `use of
undeclared identifier 'kSecTrustSettingsApplication'` (and the related
`kSecTrustSettings*`/`SecTrustSettings*` symbols). The patch guards the Apple
trust-store implementation with `defined(__APPLE__) && !TARGET_OS_IPHONE` and
makes the system-certificate dispatch fall through to
`GetOpenSSLSystemCertificates()` on iOS (bundled/default root certs unchanged;
only `--use-system-ca` finds no Apple keychain on iOS).

`0005-cares-ios-no-sys-random.patch` — Node 26's c-ares ships one
`config/darwin/ares_config.h` for macOS and iOS, and it defines
`HAVE_SYS_RANDOM_H` (present in the macOS SDK but NOT in the iPhoneOS SDK). The
iOS build failed with `../deps/cares/src/lib/util/ares_rand.c:37:12: fatal error:
'sys/random.h' file not found`. iOS uses `arc4random_buf()` (already enabled via
`HAVE_ARC4RANDOM_BUF` in that config), so the patch leaves
`HAVE_SYS_RANDOM_H` undefined (matching Node 22's c-ares darwin config).

`0004-gyp-empty-ios-deployment-target.patch` — Node 26's `xcode_emulation.py`
emitted `-miphoneos-version-min=` (empty value) whenever
`IPHONEOS_DEPLOYMENT_TARGET` was present in the merged settings. The
`_toolset=="host"` override intentionally sets it to `''` so ICU/node host tools
build for macOS, which combined with our `-mmacosx-version-min=13.5` and made
clang fail with `error: invalid argument '-mmacosx-version-min=13.5' not allowed
with '-miphoneos-version-min='` (host abseil objects). The patch only emits the
iPhoneOS flag when the deployment target value is non-empty (matching the
truthiness check `xcode_emulation` already uses for `isIOS`).

`0003-gyp-make-ios.patch` (provided by the Lead, verified against pristine
v26.13.0) — Node 26 bundles a newer Chromium gyp whose **make generator** only
recognizes the `mac` flavor (only the Xcode generator understands iOS, and
Chromium does not build iOS through gyp; nodejs-mobile's Node 22 gyp fork carried
the iOS handling). Without this, `--dest-os=ios` emits no `-isysroot`,
`-miphoneos-version-min`, `-arch` or `-std=gnu++20` flags and bundled abseil
fails with `"C++ versions less than C++17 are not supported."`. The patch ports
the minimal `ios` flavor handling into `tools/gyp/pylib/gyp/generator/make.py`
(17 sites: OS default, XcodeSettings creation, cflags/cflags_cc/ldflags from
xcode_settings, action env expansion, library/rpath/install_name handling, flock
helper) and `tools/gyp/pylib/gyp/common.py` (mac_toolchain_dir). No
`xcode_emulation.py` change is needed: Node 26's copy already understands iOS
(`_archs` includes iphoneos/iossim, IPHONEOS_DEPLOYMENT_TARGET and SDKROOT
handling).

`0001-configure-ios.patch` — Node 26 lists `ios` in `valid_os` but only maps
android to a gyp `OS` value. This sets `o['variables']['OS']='ios'` and
`iossim=false` for `--dest-os=ios`, selecting the upstream iOS gyp branches in
`tools/v8_gypfiles/toolchain.gypi`, `tools/v8_gypfiles/v8.gyp`, `deps/uv`,
`deps/zlib`, `deps/cares`, `tools/icu` and `node.gyp(i)`.

`0002-common-gypi-ios.patch` — adds the missing iPhoneOS `target_defaults` block
to `common.gypi` (the equivalent of nodejs-mobile Node 22's `OS=="ios"` block):
Xcode settings (`SDKROOT=iphoneos`, `IPHONEOS_DEPLOYMENT_TARGET=15.0`,
`ARCHS=arm64`, `CLANG_CXX_LANGUAGE_STANDARD=gnu++20`, `-Wl,-no_pie`), plus
`_toolset=="host"` overrides that keep ICU/node host tools as runnable macOS
binaries during the cross build. It also adds `iossim` and includes `ios` in the
V8 archive path variables.

## Build recipe

`scripts/build-ios-runtime-node26.sh` (used by
`.github/workflows/build-runtime.yml`):

```
GYP_DEFINES="target_arch=arm64 host_os=mac target_os=ios"
./configure --dest-os=ios --dest-cpu=arm64 --cross-compiling \
  --with-intl=full-icu --openssl-no-asm \
  --without-node-code-cache --without-node-snapshot \
  --without-ffi --v8-disable-temporal-support
make -j3
```

- `--without-ffi` defers libffi/iOS bring-up to Phase C (FFI is what OpenTUI
  needs, but it is not needed for the Phase A build gate).
- `--v8-disable-temporal-support` avoids a Rust toolchain: Node 26's Temporal
  support (ICU4X) needs cargo/rustc >= 1.86 and cross-compiling Rust to
  `aarch64-apple-ios` is out of scope for Phase A/Phase C's JS-only TUI goal.

The job restores a cached `nodejs26/out/Release` tree (keyed on the patch
series sha256 + cache epoch) so iterations are incremental; a cache miss falls
back to a clean cold build.

## Validation (CI, static only)

- Mach-O iPhoneOS arm64 executable (scripts/check_macho.py --max-minos 16.1.1)
- `xcrun vtool` platform IOS, minos <= 16.1.1
- `xcrun otool -L` allowlist: only `/usr/lib/` and
  `/System/Library/Frameworks/` dependencies
- full-ICU present (`v8_enable_i18n_support=1`, multi-signal audit)
- WebAssembly compiled in (`v8_enable_webassembly=1`)
- ldid ad-hoc signature with the same device-verified **2-key** entitlement set
  used by the Node 22 runtime (`platform-application` +
  `com.apple.private.security.no-sandbox`), exact-set gate
- `runtime-manifest.json` (node sha256/size/dylibs, pins, patch hashes) and
  `SHA256SUMS`

GitHub-hosted runners cannot execute iPhoneOS binaries, so the artifact is
marked `UNVERIFIED ON DEVICE`. Device testing of the Phase A artifact checks the
minimum: `node --version`, `node --jitless -e 'console.log(1+1)'`,
`process.platform`, and what JIT/wasm do before the Phase B W^X work.

Failure classes fixed along the way (all device-diagnosed, patches in the series):
0007 (mprotect RW<->RX write-scope machinery + registered ranges), 0009 (register every
executable chunk and start it RX in `AllocateAlignedMemory`; the 0007 registration site was
dead code in V8 14.6), 0011 (wasm code space from the platform page allocator instead of the
Chrome-iOS `UNREACHABLE()` CodeRange/MAP_JIT branch). The crash logs and exact stacks are
summarised in the device-evidence notes; none of them required entitlement changes.

bundled `deps/libffi` cross-built for iPhoneOS, manifest variant `node26-phaseab-ffi`,

| Check | Device result |
| --- | --- |
| `node --version` / `--jitless` | v26.13.0 / rc=0 |

This is the runtime side of Phase D: `node:ffi` (default-on in Node 26.13, libffi closures/trampolines
working on iOS) can load the OpenTUI native renderer, so the CLI/TUI layer (patched OpenTUI asset
resolver for `ios-arm64`, node26 CLI variant, node26 .deb variant) is what remains for the real TUI.

## Remaining work (Phase B/C risks)

- **Phase B -- V8 14.6 iOS W^X: implemented in patch 0007**, pending CI +
  device verification. V8 14.6's iOS model expects MAP_JIT/RWX code pages
  (BrowserEngineCore, iOS 17.4+); iOS 16.1.1 only supports plain `mprotect`
  RW<->RX toggling, so `RwxMemoryWriteScope` is implemented over registered
  ranges with the same design as the device-verified Node 22.9 runtime. Expected
  device constraint: `--single-threaded` for JIT/wasm (process-wide toggle).
- **Phase C — node:ffi + OpenTUI Zig kernel:** enable `--experimental-ffi`
  without `--without-ffi`, cross-build `deps/libffi` for iPhoneOS, adapt
  `src/ffi/jit_memory.cc` to the iOS 16.1.1 mprotect model, then cross-build
  OpenTUI's Zig core (native renderer packages are currently darwin/linux/win32
  only) and load it via `node:ffi`.

{
  "repository": "Justintunsday/nodejs26-ios",
  "artifact": "ios-posix-compat",
  "run_id": "41647498001",
  "commit": "12efac8f",
  "dylib_sha256": "5ac7fcb88ce61ece5a2d5acf6bb8373940767e5bfa7babbcc0dec78c92310895",
  "scope": "Interposes unnamed POSIX semaphores (process-local mutex/cond) and emulates System V shmget/shmat/shmdt/shmctl with POSIX shm_open+mmap; named semaphores pass through to libSystem."

`runtime/node-ios/packaging/node`:

```
#!/var/jb/bin/sh
# V8's iPhoneOS mprotect W^X policy requires foreground compilation.
# Load the iOS POSIX compatibility shim before Node starts: iOS rejects
# sem_init (ENOSYS) and the kernel SIGSYS-traps System V shmget, so the shim
# interposes those calls with local/POSIX-shm-backed implementations.
COMPAT=/var/jb/usr/lib/node26/libios-posix-compat.dylib
if [ -f "$COMPAT" ]; then
  DYLD_INSERT_LIBRARIES="$COMPAT${DYLD_INSERT_LIBRARIES:+:$DYLD_INSERT_LIBRARIES}"
  export DYLD_INSERT_LIBRARIES
fi
exec /var/jb/usr/lib/node26/node --single-threaded "$@"

```

`runtime/node-ios/patches/nodejs26/0001-configure-ios.patch`:

```patch
# =============================================================================
# nodejs 26.13.0 iPhoneOS arm64 Phase A build fix
# configure.py: map --dest-os=ios to gyp OS=ios and iossim=false
#
# Base: upstream node-v26.13.0 from https://nodejs.org/dist/v26.13.0/
#       (sha256 986ead18ef18256831bae3e57d94aef680a7b84cfd4044f3e8471f833fbd7191)
# Applied with `patch -p1` / `git apply` after extracting the tarball.
# =============================================================================

--- a/configure.py
+++ b/configure.py
@@ -1901,6 +1901,12 @@

`runtime/node-ios/patches/nodejs26/0003-gyp-make-ios.patch`:

```patch
# Node.js 26.13.0 iPhoneOS build: restore gyp 'ios' flavor support in the make generator
# =============================================================================
# Base:    nodejs/node v26.13.0
# Applied: by scripts/build-ios-runtime-node26.sh with `git apply`, after 0001/0002.
#
# Why: upstream Node 26 bundles a newer Chromium gyp whose *make* generator only
# knows the 'mac' flavor (only the Xcode generator understands iOS, and Chromium
# iOS builds do not use gyp). nodejs-mobile's Node 22 gyp fork carried the 'ios'
# handling in tools/gyp/pylib/gyp. Without it, `--dest-os=ios` makes gyp emit no
# Xcode settings for the iPhoneOS target, so:
#   - no -isysroot / -miphoneos-version-min / -arch flags are emitted,
#   - no -std=gnu++20 from CLANG_CXX_LANGUAGE_STANDARD is emitted, and
#   - V8's bundled abseil fails with:
#       error: "C++ versions less than C++17 are not supported."
#
# This patch ports the minimal 'ios' handling into the make generator: every
# place that treats flavor 'mac' as Apple/Xcode (XcodeSettings creation,
# cflags/cflags_cc/ldflags from xcode_settings, env expansion for actions,
# library dir/lib name adjustment, flock helper, OS defaults) now treats 'ios'
# the same way. It mirrors the nodejs-mobile Node 22 gyp tree's ios changes.
# =============================================================================

`runtime/node-ios/patches/nodejs26/0004-gyp-empty-ios-deployment-target.patch`:

```patch
# =============================================================================
# nodejs 26.13.0 iPhoneOS build: do not emit an empty -miphoneos-version-min
# =============================================================================
# Base:    nodejs/node v26.13.0
# Applied: by scripts/build-ios-runtime-node26.sh after 0001/0002/0003.
#
# Why: the common.gypi OS=="ios" target_defaults set
# IPHONEOS_DEPLOYMENT_TARGET=15.0 for the iPhoneOS target, and the
# _toolset=="host" override sets it to '' so host tools (ICU genccode,
# node_js2c, abseil host libs, ...) are built for macOS instead. gyp's
# make generator appended "-miphoneos-version-min=%s" whenever the key was
# present, so host compiles got BOTH "-mmacosx-version-min=13.5" and the
# empty "-miphoneos-version-min=", which clang rejects:
#   clang++: error: invalid argument '-mmacosx-version-min=13.5' not allowed
#            with '-miphoneos-version-min='
# Guard the flag on a non-empty deployment target (xcode_emulation already
# uses the truthy check when deciding isIOS at line ~181).
# =============================================================================

--- a/tools/gyp/pylib/gyp/xcode_emulation.py
+++ b/tools/gyp/pylib/gyp/xcode_emulation.py
@@ -553,7 +553,14 @@
 
     def _AppendPlatformVersionMinFlags(self, lst):
         self._Appendf(lst, "MACOSX_DEPLOYMENT_TARGET", "-mmacosx-version-min=%s")
-        if "IPHONEOS_DEPLOYMENT_TARGET" in self._Settings():
+        # nodejs26-build-fixes: only emit -miphoneos-version-min when the value
+        # is non-empty. The iOS target_defaults block sets a real deployment
+        # target, while the _toolset=="host" override deliberately sets
+        # IPHONEOS_DEPLOYMENT_TARGET:'' so mac host tools get macOS flags; the
+        # empty string must not produce "-miphoneos-version-min=" (clang errors
+        # with: invalid argument '-mmacosx-version-min=...' not allowed with
+        # '-miphoneos-version-min=').
+        if self._Settings().get("IPHONEOS_DEPLOYMENT_TARGET"):
             # TODO: Implement this better?
             sdk_path_basename = os.path.basename(self._SdkPath())
             if sdk_path_basename.lower().startswith("iphonesimulator"):

`runtime/node-ios/patches/nodejs26/0005-cares-ios-no-sys-random.patch`:

```patch
# =============================================================================
# nodejs 26.13.0 iPhoneOS build: c-ares darwin config must not require
# <sys/random.h> on iOS
# =============================================================================
# Base:    nodejs/node v26.13.0
# Applied: by scripts/build-ios-runtime-node26.sh after 0001..0004.
#
# Why: Node 26's c-ares ships one config/darwin/ares_config.h for macOS and
# iOS, and it defines HAVE_SYS_RANDOM_H (present in macOS SDK >= 10.15 but
# NOT in the iPhoneOS SDK). The iOS build failed with:
#   ../deps/cares/src/lib/util/ares_rand.c:37:12: fatal error:
#       'sys/random.h' file not found
# iOS uses arc4random_buf() (HAVE_ARC4RANDOM_BUF is already 1 in this
# config), so simply leave HAVE_SYS_RANDOM_H undefined for this build.
# Node 22's c-ares darwin config also left it undefined.
# =============================================================================

--- a/deps/cares/config/darwin/ares_config.h
+++ b/deps/cares/config/darwin/ares_config.h
@@ -329,7 +329,11 @@
 #define HAVE_SYS_PARAM_H 1
 
 /* Define to 1 if you have the <sys/random.h> header file. */
-#define HAVE_SYS_RANDOM_H 1
+/* nodejs26-build-fixes: the darwin config is shared by macOS and iOS, but the
+ * iPhoneOS SDK has no <sys/random.h>; iOS uses arc4random_buf instead (that
+ * path is already enabled through HAVE_ARC4RANDOM_BUF in this config), so
+ * leave it undefined for the iOS target build. */
+/* #undef HAVE_SYS_RANDOM_H */
 
 /* Define to 1 if you have the <sys/select.h> header file. */
 #define HAVE_SYS_SELECT_H 1

`runtime/node-ios/patches/nodejs26/0006-node-crypto-ios-no-sectrustsettings.patch`:

```patch
# =============================================================================
# nodejs 26.13.0 iPhoneOS build: crypto trust store is macOS-only in the
# Apple branch; iOS must skip SecTrustSettings and use the OpenSSL path
# =============================================================================
# Base:    nodejs/node v26.13.0
# Applied: by scripts/build-ios-runtime-node26.sh after 0001..0005.
#
# Why: src/crypto/crypto_context.cc guards the macOS keychain/trust-settings
# certificate reader with `#ifdef __APPLE__`, which is also true on iOS, but
# the SecTrustSettings* APIs (kSecTrustSettingsApplication,
# kSecTrustSettingsPolicy, kSecTrustSettingsResult, SecTrustSettingsCopy*
# TrustSettings, ...) only exist in the macOS Security framework. The iOS
# build failed with:
#   ../src/crypto/crypto_context.cc:328:43: error: use of undeclared
#       identifier 'kSecTrustSettingsApplication'
#   ... (same for kSecTrustSettingsPolicy/String/Result*, SecTrustSettings*)
# Guard the Apple-only trust-store implementation with
# `defined(__APPLE__) && !TARGET_OS_IPHONE` and make the system-certificate
# dispatch fall through to GetOpenSSLSystemCertificates() on iOS. Node's
# default/bundled root certificates are unaffected; only --use-system-ca on
# iOS finds no Apple keychain certificates (the macOS-only API is absent).
# =============================================================================

   return X509_NAME_cmp(subject, issuer) == 0;

`runtime/node-ios/patches/nodejs26/0007-v8-ios-mprotect-wx.patch`:

```patch
# =============================================================================
# nodejs 26.13.0 iPhoneOS Phase B: V8 14.6 mprotect RW<->RX W^X for iOS
# =============================================================================
# Base:    nodejs/node v26.13.0 (V8 14.6.202.34)
# Applied: by scripts/build-ios-runtime-node26.sh after 0001..0006.
#
# Device-proven constraints (iPhone SE 2, iOS 16.1.1, same as Node 22 v6):
#   plain RW -> write -> mprotect RX -> execute : WORKS
#   mmap RWX / mprotect RWX + execute           : SIGBUS (X dropped)
#   mmap MAP_JIT                               : EINVAL
#   pthread_jit_write_protect_np               : absent
#   BrowserEngineCore W^X (V8's iOS 17.4+ path): unavailable
#
# V8 14.6 assumes for iOS that the code range is already RWX/MAP_JIT
# (Chrome iOS model). This patch replaces that with the Node 22.9
# device-verified model: plain PROT_NONE reservation, per-chunk RW commit,
# and RwxMemoryWriteScope implemented as mprotect RW<->RX over ranges
# registered by RegisterIOSCodeRange().
#
# Changes:
#   * build_config.h: V8_HAS_PTHREAD_JIT_WRITE_PROTECT=1 on iOS arm64 so
#     the write-scope machinery compiles and SetJitWriteProtected is used.
#   * platform.h/platform-ios.cc: RegisterIOSCodeRange registry +
#     SetJitWriteProtected() mprotect RW/RX toggler (new source in v8.gyp).
#   * code-range.cc: plain PROT_NONE reservation on iOS; re-embedded
#     builtins copy commits RW -> memcpy -> RX instead of assuming RWX.
#   * memory-allocator.cc: iOS commits code chunks RW and registers them.
#   * mutable-page.h: never request RWX when write scopes are enabled.
#   * platform-posix.cc: never request MAP_JIT on iOS.
#   * wasm-code-manager.cc: commit wasm code spaces RW->RX and register.
#
# Non-iOS behaviour is unchanged (all changes are V8_OS_IOS/
# V8_TARGET_OS_IOS guarded; macOS keeps pthread_jit_write_protect).
# Entitlements: unchanged device-verified 2-key set, no MAP_JIT keys.
# =============================================================================

--- a/deps/v8/src/base/build_config.h
+++ b/deps/v8/src/base/build_config.h

--- /dev/null
+++ b/deps/v8/src/base/platform/platform-ios.cc
@@ -0,0 +1,69 @@
+// Copyright 2026 the V8 project authors. All rights reserved.
+// Use of this source code is governed by a BSD-style license that can be
+// found in the LICENSE file.
+
+#include <sys/mman.h>
+
+#include <mutex>
+#include <vector>
+
+#include "src/base/base-export.h"
+#include "src/base/build_config.h"
+#include "src/base/macros.h"
+#include "src/base/platform/platform.h"
+
+// nodejs26 Phase B: iOS device JIT memory model.
+//
+// Device probing (iPhone SE 2, iOS 16.1.1) shows that on iOS:
+//   * mmap(MAP_JIT) is denied (EINVAL) and pthread_jit_write_protect_np is
+//     absent, so neither the macOS APRR model nor MAP_JIT can be used;
+//     BrowserEngineCore is iOS 17.4+ only and unavailable on this device;
+//   * ordinary mappings DO support mprotect(PROT_READ|PROT_WRITE) and
+//     mprotect(PROT_READ|PROT_EXEC), i.e. explicit W^X toggling works;
+//   * requesting RWX silently drops PROT_EXEC.
+// V8 therefore runs its RwxMemoryWriteScope machinery over mprotect:
+// RegisterIOSCodeRange() is called by the heap and wasm code for every
+// committed executable range; SetJitWriteProtected(0) makes them writable
+// (RW, while code is written) and SetJitWriteProtected(1) makes them
+// executable (RX) again. This mirrors the Node 22.9 device-verified runtime.
+namespace v8::base {
+
+#if V8_OS_IOS
+namespace {
+struct CodeRange {
+  void* base;
+  size_t size;
+};
+
+std::mutex& CodeRangesMutex() {
+  static std::mutex mutex;
+  return mutex;
+}
+
+std::vector<CodeRange>& CodeRanges() {
+  static std::vector<CodeRange> ranges;
+  return ranges;
+}
+}  // namespace
+
+V8_BASE_EXPORT void RegisterIOSCodeRange(void* base, size_t size) {
+  if (base == nullptr || size == 0) return;
+  std::lock_guard<std::mutex> guard(CodeRangesMutex());
+  CodeRanges().push_back({base, size});
+}
+
+#if V8_HAS_PTHREAD_JIT_WRITE_PROTECT
+V8_BASE_EXPORT void SetJitWriteProtected(int enable) {
+  const int prot = enable ? (PROT_READ | PROT_EXEC) : (PROT_READ | PROT_WRITE);
+  std::lock_guard<std::mutex> guard(CodeRangesMutex());
+  for (const CodeRange& range : CodeRanges()) {
+    // Ignore failures: a range may have been decommitted or reused; subsequent
+    // toggles keep the mapping consistent.
+    int result = mprotect(range.base, range.size, prot);
+    USE(result);
+  }
+}
+#endif  // V8_HAS_PTHREAD_JIT_WRITE_PROTECT
+#endif  // V8_OS_IOS
+
+}  // namespace v8::base

```

`runtime/node-ios/patches/nodejs26/0009-v8-ios-register-allocated-code-chunks.patch`:

```patch
# =============================================================================
# nodejs 26.13.0 iPhoneOS Phase B fix: register + make RX every allocated
# executable code chunk (page-aligned whole chunk)
# =============================================================================
# Base:    nodejs/node v26.13.0 + patches 0001..0008
# Applied: by scripts/build-ios-runtime-node26.sh after 0001..0008.
#
# Device evidence round 1 (run 41354422221 artifact, iPhone SE 2 / iOS 16.1.1):
#   JIT/wasm trapped with EXC_BAD_ACCESS (SIGBUS) executing
#   Builtins_InterpreterEntryTrampoline; the fault address was in a code-range
#   chunk mapped rw- (should be r-x). Root cause: the registry in patch 0007
#   was empty because MemoryAllocator::SetPermissionsOnExecutableMemoryChunk
#   (where 0007 registered ranges) has NO callers in V8 14.6.
#
# Device evidence round 2 (initial 0009, code-area split): SIGBUS class gone,
# but V8 died with `Check failed: 12 == (*__error())` in OS::SetPermissions
# called from AllocateAlignedMemory: the code-area split is not page aligned
# (ObjectStartOffsetInMemoryChunk is a small header offset), so mprotect()
# returned EINVAL, which V8's OS::SetPermissions does not allow (only ENOMEM).
#
# Fix: register and set permissions on the whole page-aligned chunk
# (base, chunk_size). This matches the macOS pthread_jit_write_protect
# contract: writes to any code-range page happen inside RwxMemoryWriteScope,
# which toggles the registered chunk RW<->RX. Header/metadata pages are only
# written from within those scopes as well.
#
# Real V8 14.6 executable-chunk commit path (for future reference):
#   MemoryAllocator::AllocateAlignedMemory()
#     VirtualMemory reservation(..., MutablePage::GetCodeModificationPermission())
#     -> BoundedPageAllocator commits pages RW (our 0007 returns kReadWrite)
#     -> chunk created; pages were never registered and never made RX.
#
# Known limitation (deferred, cheap to revisit): ranges are not unregistered
# when a code chunk is freed/partially freed; SetJitWriteProtected ignores
# mprotect errors on stale ranges, so this is benign for correctness but may
# briefly recommit discarded pages on scope toggles.
# =============================================================================

--- a/deps/v8/src/heap/memory-allocator.cc
+++ b/deps/v8/src/heap/memory-allocator.cc
@@ -148,6 +148,28 @@
   }
 
   Address base = reservation.address();
+
+#if defined(V8_OS_IOS)
+  // nodejs26 Phase B fix (0009): every executable chunk must be registered with
+  // the mprotect W^X toggler and start out executable. In V8 14.6 the
+  // per-chunk commit happens here in AllocateAlignedMemory (via the
+  // VirtualMemory reservation with MutablePage::GetCodeModificationPermission()
+  // == kReadWrite under V8_HAS_PTHREAD_JIT_WRITE_PROTECT), NOT through
+  // SetPermissionsOnExecutableMemoryChunk (which has no callers in 14.6).
+  //
+  // Use the whole page-aligned chunk (base/chunk_size are commit-page aligned);
+  // a code-area split would not be page aligned and mprotect() rejects it.
+  // This mirrors the macOS pthread_jit_write_protect contract: any write to a
+  // code-range page happens inside RwxMemoryWriteScope, which toggles the whole
+  // registered chunk RW while writing and RX afterwards.
+  if (executable == EXECUTABLE) {
+    base::RegisterIOSCodeRange(reinterpret_cast<void*>(base), chunk_size);
+    if (!reservation.SetPermissions(base, chunk_size,
+                                    PageAllocator::kReadExecute)) {
+      return HandleAllocationFailure(isolate_->heap(), executable);
+    }
+  }
+#endif  // V8_OS_IOS

# =============================================================================

# =============================================================================
# Base:    nodejs/node v26.13.0 + patches 0001..0009
# Applied: by scripts/build-ios-runtime-node26.sh after 0001..0009.
#
# Device evidence (artifact sha 627f12d3..., iPhone SE 2 / iOS 16.1.1):
#   Node 26 JIT is now device-verified: --version/--jitless OK, and with
#   --single-threaded the JIT loop (jit_loop=5999999), built-in fetch
#   (HTTP 200), TLS and the event loop all work. WebAssembly however traps:

+  // iOS (patches 0005/0007) - there is no "only one MAP_JIT per process"
+  // constraint to satisfy. Wasm code space is therefore reserved as plain
+  // PROT_NONE memory from the platform page allocator;

`runtime/node-ios/patches/nodejs26/0012-ios-node-platform.patch`:

```patch
# =============================================================================
# nodejs 26.13.0 iPhoneOS: split the node.gypi macOS framework block from a
# dedicated iOS block so process.platform is "ios"
# =============================================================================
# Base:    nodejs/node v26.13.0 (on top of patches 0001..0011)
# Applied: by scripts/build-ios-runtime-node26.sh after 0001..0011.
#
# Why: 0008 extended the upstream macOS block to `OS=="mac" or OS=="ios"`,
# which also applies its defines, including NODE_PLATFORM="darwin". Node's
# process.platform is built from NODE_PLATFORM (src/node_metadata.cc), so the
# iOS runtime started reporting platform=darwin. The OpenTUI JS asset
# resolver selects the native renderer by platform (core-ios-arm64), and
# CLI code must treat iOS as its own platform, so the iOS build must keep
# process.platform === "ios".
#
# Change: restore the original macOS-only block (NODE_PLATFORM="darwin") and
# add a separate OS=="ios" block with the same CoreFoundation/Security
# framework linkage and explicit NODE_PLATFORM="ios".

`runtime/node-ios/patches/nodejs26/0013-wasm-ios-no-trap-handler.patch`:

```patch
# =============================================================================
# nodejs 26.13.0 iPhoneOS: disable the V8 WebAssembly trap handler on iOS so
# wasm memory does not reserve an 8 GB guard region
# =============================================================================
# Base:    nodejs/node v26.13.0 (on top of patches 0001..0012)
# Applied: by scripts/build-ios-runtime-node26.sh after 0001..0012.
#
# Device evidence (node26test.2 package on iPhone SE 2 / iOS 16.1.1):
#   `new WebAssembly.Memory({initial: 1})` always fails with
#   "WebAssembly.Memory(): could not allocate memory", even with a tiny
#   maximum; `new ArrayBuffer(4GB/5GB)` works and 6GB fails (device VA limit
#   ~5GB). Consequently undici/llhttp wasm fails and http(s) fetch fails with
#   RangeError: WebAssembly.Instance(): Out of memory: Cannot allocate Wasm
#   memory for new instance, breaking the TUI server-connect path.
#
# Root cause (V8 14.6): with the wasm trap handler enabled,
# BackingStore::AllocateWasmMemory sets has_guard_regions and
# GetWasmReservationSize returns the fixed kFullGuardSize32 = 8 GB
# reservation, which the iOS kernel denies. Node enables the trap handler
# because CanEnableWebAssemblyTrapHandler() only inspects RLIMIT_AS, which is
# RLIM_INFINITY on iOS (the real limit is the process VA space).
#
# Fix: return false on Apple iOS (TARGET_OS_IPHONE), so V8 never enables the
# trap handler, guard regions are disabled and wasm memory reservations match
# the requested capacity. macOS behavior is unchanged. Explicit
# --disable-wasm-trap-handler still works on the other platforms.
# =============================================================================

--- a/src/node.cc
+++ b/src/node.cc
@@ -127,6 +127,10 @@
 
 #include "absl/synchronization/mutex.h"
 
+#if defined(__APPLE__)
+#include <TargetConditionals.h>
+#endif  // __APPLE__
+
 // ========== global C++ headers ==========
 
 #include <cerrno>
@@ -1136,6 +1140,19 @@
 
 #if NODE_USE_V8_WASM_TRAP_HANDLER
 bool CanEnableWebAssemblyTrapHandler() {
+#if defined(__APPLE__) && TARGET_OS_IPHONE
+  // nodejs26 iOS (patch 0013): the process virtual address space on the target
+  // device is limited to roughly 5 GB, while V8's trap-handler configuration
+  // reserves a fixed 8 GB guard region for wasm memory
+  // (BackingStore::AllocateWasmMemory has_guard_regions / kFullGuardSize32).
+  // The iOS kernel refuses that reservation, so every WebAssembly.Memory
+  // allocation fails ("could not allocate memory") and undici's llhttp wasm
+  // makes http fetch fail with "Cannot allocate Wasm memory for new instance".
+  // Without the trap handler V8 disables guard regions and wasm memory only
+  // reserves the requested capacity. `--disable-wasm-trap-handler` still works
+  // as an explicit CLI opt-out on other platforms.
+  return false;
+#else
 // On POSIX, the machine may have a limit on the amount of virtual memory
 // available, if it's not enough to allocate at least one cage for WASM,
 // then the trap-handler-based bound checks cannot be used.
@@ -1170,6 +1187,7 @@
 #else
   return true;
 #endif  // __POSIX__
+#endif  // defined(__APPLE__) && TARGET_OS_IPHONE
 }
 #endif  // NODE_USE_V8_WASM_TRAP_HANDLER
 

```

```patch
# Node 26.13 iPhoneOS: use libffi's Apple ARM executable trampoline table.
# Device regression: node:ffi registerCallback() returned a MALLOC_TINY rw-
# address. A native edit event jumped there and SIGBUSed; in the TUI its signal
# handling appeared as an input hang. Plain dlopen/function calls passed.

# upstream libffi configure.ac's *arm*-apple-* / aarch64-apple-* rule.

         return '0'
 
     if target_arch == 'arm':

+    return os_name in ('ios', 'mac') and target_arch in ('arm', 'arm64')
 
 
 def render_ffi_header(base_dir, os_name, target_arch, target):

`runtime/node-ios/patches/nodejs26/0015-libffi-ios-rw-rx-trampoline-table.patch`:

```patch
# Node 26.13 iPhoneOS: libffi trampoline table with anonymous RW -> RX code.
# After patch 0014, callback allocation failed because iOS remaps code pages
# without executable current/max protection. Device probe: vm_remap R/R;
# copying the instruction page to anonymous memory then protecting RX succeeds.
# Trampolines use PC-relative access to the preceding writable config page.
# Keep that layout, flush instruction cache, and seal code RX exactly once.
# No malloc execution, MAP_JIT or RWX. macOS retains its original remap path.

diff --git a/deps/libffi/src/closures.c b/deps/libffi/src/closures.c
--- a/deps/libffi/src/closures.c
+++ b/deps/libffi/src/closures.c

+#if TARGET_OS_IPHONE
+#include <libkern/OSCacheControl.h>
+#include <string.h>
+#endif
 #include <pthread.h>
 #ifdef HAVE_ARM64E_PTRAUTH
 #include <ptrauth.h>

+#endif
   kern_return_t kt;
   uint16_t i;
 
@@ -233,15 +240,29 @@

   trampoline_page_template &= ~1UL;
 #endif
+#if TARGET_OS_IPHONE
+  /* iOS can strip execute permission (including max_prot) from a remapped
+     code page. Keep the adjacent writable config page, but copy the fixed,
+     position-independent trampoline instructions into anonymous memory and
+     seal only that page RX. ffi_prep_closure_loc writes metadata in config,
+     never this code page. No MAP_JIT or RWX mapping is requested. */
+  memcpy ((void *) trampoline_page, (const void *) trampoline_page_template,
+          PAGE_MAX_SIZE);
+  sys_icache_invalidate ((void *) trampoline_page, PAGE_MAX_SIZE);
+  kt = vm_protect (mach_task_self (), trampoline_page, PAGE_MAX_SIZE,
+                   FALSE, VM_PROT_READ | VM_PROT_EXECUTE);
+#else
   kt = vm_remap (mach_task_self (), &trampoline_page, PAGE_MAX_SIZE, 0x0,
 		 VM_FLAGS_OVERWRITE, mach_task_self (), trampoline_page_template,
 		 FALSE, &cur_prot, &max_prot, VM_INHERIT_SHARE);
+#endif
   if (kt != KERN_SUCCESS)
     {
       vm_deallocate (mach_task_self (), config_page, PAGE_MAX_SIZE * 2);
       return NULL;
     }

     {
       /* If VM_PROT_EXECUTE isn't set on the remapped trampoline page, set it */
@@ -253,6 +274,8 @@

         }

`runtime/node-ios/patches/nodejs26/0016-ios-v8-jobs-join-only.patch`:

```patch
# Node 26.13 iPhoneOS: keep V8 JobTask execution on the joining thread.
#
# Device evidence: packaged run 41546616774 inherited --single-threaded in
# serve --service, but node-2026-11-03-074108.ips caught a node-V8Worker inside
# wasm::NativeModule::PublishCode -> SetJitWriteProtected while the main thread
# executed JIT code from a now-RW page. V8's wasm_num_compilation_tasks=0 still
# allows BackgroundCompileJob::GetMaxConcurrency to return one; Node's platform
# supplies four workers to NewDefaultJobHandle independently of that V8 flag.
#
# Pass zero background workers for iPhoneOS JobHandles. DefaultJobState::Join
# adds the current thread as its sole worker, so synchronous wasm compilation
# still completes without publishing code concurrently with JS execution.
# --single-threaded remains mandatory for the other V8 compilation/GC tasks.
# macOS and all other targets retain their original worker count.

--- a/src/node_platform.cc
+++ b/src/node_platform.cc
@@ -6,6 +6,10 @@
 #include <cmath>  // llround()
 #include <memory>  // unique_ptr(), shared_ptr(), make_shared()
 
+#ifdef __APPLE__
+#include <TargetConditionals.h>
+#endif
+
 namespace node {
 
 using v8::Isolate;
@@ -717,8 +721,16 @@
     }
     fflush(stderr);
   }
+#if defined(__APPLE__) && TARGET_OS_IPHONE
+  // iOS mprotect W^X is process-wide. Even with --single-threaded, wasm
+  // BackgroundCompileJob can request one worker. JobHandle::Join supplies
+  // the foreground participant; never publish code on a background worker.
+  constexpr size_t job_worker_threads = 0;
+#else
+  const size_t job_worker_threads = NumberOfWorkerThreads();
+#endif

-      this, priority, std::move(job_task), NumberOfWorkerThreads());
