```cjs
  });
  await test('native execve error path ENOENT',()=>{
    assert.equal(native('execve',path.join(directory,'missing-executable'),script,'node-child',directory),-1);assert.equal(traces.at(-1).errno,C('ENOENT'));return traces.at(-1);
  });
  const sharedName='/n26m'+process.pid,semName='/n26s'+process.pid;
  await test('POSIX shm_open and cross-process shared mapping',()=>{
    const fd=success(native('shm_open',sharedName,C('O_RDWR')|C('O_CREAT')|C('O_EXCL'),0o600),'shm_open',true);let pointer;
    try {success(native('ftruncate',fd,16384n),'shm ftruncate');pointer=mapped(fd,C('MAP_SHARED'));f.probe_store(pointer,0x12345678);fs.writeFileSync(path.join(directory,'shm-name.txt'),sharedName);const result=child('shm-child');assert.equal(f.probe_load(pointer),0x23456789);return {name:sharedName,bytes:16384,child:result};}
    finally {if(pointer)success(native('munmap',pointer,16384n),'shm munmap');success(native('close',fd),'shm close');success(native('shm_unlink',sharedName),'shm unlink');}
  });
  await test('POSIX named semaphore across processes',()=>{
    const semaphore=native('sem_open',semName,C('O_CREAT')|C('O_EXCL'),0o600,0);
    if(f.probe_sem_failed(semaphore)) {const errno=traces.at(-1).errno;if([C('ENOSYS'),C('EPERM'),C('EACCES')].includes(errno))throw new CapabilityError('sem_open',errno);assert.fail(json(traces.at(-1)));}
    try {assert.equal(native('sem_trywait',semaphore),-1);assert.equal(traces.at(-1).errno,C('EAGAIN'));success(native('sem_post',semaphore),'sem_post');fs.writeFileSync(path.join(directory,'sem-name.txt'),semName);const result=child('sem-child');success(native('sem_trywait',semaphore),'sem_trywait after child');return {name:semName,emptyErrno:C('EAGAIN'),child:result};}
    finally {success(native('sem_close',semaphore),'sem_close');success(native('sem_unlink',semName),'sem_unlink');}
  });
  await test('POSIX unnamed sem_init/sem_destroy',()=>{
    const storage=Buffer.alloc(f.probe_sem_size());success(native('sem_init',storage,1),'sem_init',true);
    try {success(native('sem_trywait',storage),'unnamed sem_trywait');success(native('sem_post',storage),'unnamed sem_post');}
    finally {success(native('sem_destroy',storage),'sem_destroy');}
    return {storage:storage.length};
  });
  await test('System V shmget/shmat/shmdt/IPC_RMID',()=>{
    const result=cp.spawnSync(process.execPath,childArgs('sysv-probe'),{encoding:'utf8',timeout:20000});
    assert.equal(result.error,undefined,result.error?.message);
    if(result.signal==='SIGSYS') {
      const attempt=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(directory,'sysv-attempt.json'),'utf8'));
      throw new NativeSignalError(result.signal,{signal:result.signal,status:result.status,attempt,stderr:result.stderr});
    }
    assert.equal(result.status,0,result.stderr);assert.equal(result.signal,null);
    const record=JSON.parse(result.stdout.trim());
    if(!record.supported) throw new CapabilityError('shmget',record.calls.at(-1).errno);
    return record;
  });
  await test('24 rounds interleaved JIT/wasm, sysctl and file open',()=>{
    const filename=path.join(directory,'stress-open.txt');
    for(let round=0;round<24;round++) {pulse();sysctl('kern.osrelease');const fd=success(native('open',filename,C('O_RDWR')|C('O_CREAT')|C('O_TRUNC'),0o600),'stress open');try {const value=Buffer.from(String(wasm.exports.foo()));assert.equal(native('write',fd,value,BigInt(value.length)),BigInt(value.length));} finally {success(native('close',fd),'stress close');}pulse();}
    success(native('unlink',filename),'stress unlink');return {rounds:24,engine:engine()};
  });
  await test('named IPC resources removed',()=>{
    const shared=rows.find(row=>row.name.startsWith('POSIX shm_open'));
    if(shared.status==='PASS') {assert.equal(native('shm_open',sharedName,C('O_RDWR'),0),-1);assert.equal(traces.at(-1).errno,C('ENOENT'));}
    const semaphore=rows.find(row=>row.name.startsWith('POSIX named'));
    if(semaphore.status==='PASS') {const missing=native('sem_open',semName,0,0,0);assert.equal(f.probe_sem_failed(missing),1);assert.equal(traces.at(-1).errno,C('ENOENT'));}
    return {sharedName,semName,remainingNamedResources:0};
  });
  const report={startedAt,finishedAt:new Date().toISOString(),runtime:{version:process.version,platform:process.platform,pid:process.pid,uid:process.getuid(),flags:process.execArgv,executable:process.execPath,sha256:crypto.createHash('sha256').update(fs.readFileSync(process.execPath)).digest('hex'),v8:process.versions.v8,v8_enable_sandbox:process.config.variables.v8_enable_sandbox},bridge:{file:bridge,sha256:crypto.createHash('sha256').update(fs.readFileSync(bridge)).digest('hex')},engine:engine(),summary:Object.fromEntries(['PASS','UNSUPPORTED','RESTRICTED','FAIL'].map(status=>[status,rows.filter(row=>row.status===status).length])),nativeCalls:traces.length,rows};
  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(directory,'posix-jit-wasm-results.json'),json(report)+'\n');
  console.log('POSIX_JIT_WASM_SUMMARY '+json(report.summary)+' calls='+traces.length+' pulses='+pulses);
  process.exitCode=report.summary.FAIL?1:0;
}
run().catch(error=>{console.error(error);process.exitCode=1;}).finally(()=>lib.close());

```

`runtime/node-ios/tests/standalone-smoke.cjs`:

```cjs
// Run on the iPhone as: node standalone-smoke.cjs <writable evidence directory>.
const assert = require('node:assert/strict');
const fs = require('node:fs');
const path = require('node:path');
const http = require('node:http');
const {spawnSync, fork} = require('node:child_process');
const crypto = require('node:crypto');

async function main() {
  assert.equal(process.platform, 'ios');
  assert.equal(process.version, 'v26.13.0');
  assert.ok(process.execArgv.includes('--single-threaded'));
  assert.ok(fs.realpathSync(process.execPath).endsWith('/usr/lib/node26/node'));
  const directory = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(process.argv[2], 'standalone-'));
  const checks = [];
  const pass = name => { checks.push(name); console.log('PASS ' + name); };
  pass('independent executable, version, platform and required flags');
  const sha = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(fs.readFileSync(process.execPath)).digest('hex');
  assert.equal(sha, '84613100b3d31a747a55d1132906bfef9417b15a1e33a4088ff1da5956e61ef9');
  pass('signed executable bytes preserved');
  let jit = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < 3000000; i++) jit += i * i % 7;
  assert.equal(jit, 5999999);
  pass('JIT checksum 5999999');
  const module = new WebAssembly.Module(Buffer.from('0061736d010000000105016000017f0302010007070103666f6f00000a06010400412a0b', 'hex'));
  assert.equal(new WebAssembly.Instance(module).exports.foo(), 42);
  assert.equal(new WebAssembly.Memory({initial:1}).buffer.byteLength, 65536);
  pass('WebAssembly function and memory');
  assert.equal([...new Intl.Segmenter('en', {granularity:'word'}).segment('hello world')].filter(x => x.isWordLike).length, 2);
  assert.ok(/\p{L}/u.test('中'));
  pass('ICU, Intl.Segmenter and Unicode properties');
  const ffi = require('node:ffi');
  const {lib, functions} = ffi.dlopen('/usr/lib/libSystem.B.dylib', {
    getpid: {arguments:[], return:'i32'},
    qsort: {arguments:['pointer','u64','u64','pointer'], return:'void'},
  });
  assert.equal(functions.getpid(), process.pid);
  let calls = 0;
  const callback = lib.registerCallback({arguments:['pointer','pointer'], return:'i32'}, () => {
    assert.equal(functions.getpid(), process.pid);
    calls++;
    return 0;
  });
  functions.qsort(new Uint32Array([2,1]), 2n, 4n, callback);
  assert.ok(calls > 0);
  lib.unregisterCallback(callback);
  lib.close();
  pass('node:ffi native call and reentrant callback');
  const sqlite = require('node:sqlite');
  const database = path.join(directory, 'probe.sqlite');
  const db = new sqlite.DatabaseSync(database);
  db.exec('CREATE TABLE probe(value INTEGER); INSERT INTO probe VALUES(42)');
  assert.equal(db.prepare('SELECT value FROM probe').get().value, 42);
  db.close();
  const sqlChild = spawnSync(process.execPath, [...process.execArgv, '-e', 'const{DatabaseSync}=require("node:sqlite");const d=new DatabaseSync(process.argv[1]);console.log(d.prepare("SELECT value FROM probe").get().value);d.close()', database], {encoding:'utf8',timeout:20000});
  assert.equal(sqlChild.status, 0, sqlChild.stderr);
  assert.equal(sqlChild.stdout.trim(), '42');
  pass('native SQLite persistence across processes');
  const esm = spawnSync(process.execPath, [...process.execArgv, '--input-type=module', '-'], {input:'console.log("ESM_STDIN_OK", await Promise.resolve(42), process.platform)',encoding:'utf8',timeout:20000});
  assert.equal(esm.status, 0, esm.stderr);
  assert.equal(esm.stdout.trim(), 'ESM_STDIN_OK 42 ios');
  pass('ES modules and stdin');
  const childFile = path.join(directory, 'fork.cjs');
  fs.writeFileSync(childFile, 'process.send({platform:process.platform,argv:process.execArgv});process.disconnect();');
  await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const child = fork(childFile, [], {stdio:['ignore','ignore','pipe','ipc']});
    let message;
    const timer = setTimeout(() => { child.kill('SIGKILL'); reject(new Error('fork timed out')); }, 20000);
    child.on('message', data => message = data);
    child.on('error', error => { clearTimeout(timer); reject(error); });
    child.on('exit', code => {
      clearTimeout(timer);
      try { assert.equal(code, 0); assert.equal(message.platform, 'ios'); assert.ok(message.argv.includes('--single-threaded')); resolve(); }
      catch (error) { reject(error); }
    });
  });
  pass('fork inherits required runtime flag');
  const server = http.createServer((req,res) => res.end('NODE26_HTTP_OK'));
  await new Promise(resolve => server.listen(0, '127.0.0.1', resolve));
  try {
    const response = await fetch('http://127.0.0.1:' + server.address().port, {signal:AbortSignal.timeout(10000)});
    assert.equal(response.status, 200);
    assert.equal(await response.text(), 'NODE26_HTTP_OK');
  } finally { await new Promise(resolve => server.close(resolve)); }
  pass('HTTP fetch and wasm-backed parser');
  const https = await fetch('https://example.com', {signal:AbortSignal.timeout(20000)});
  assert.equal(https.status, 200);
  await https.arrayBuffer();
  pass('HTTPS fetch status 200');
  const report = {status:'PASS', checks, executable:process.execPath, execArgv:process.execArgv, sha256:sha, jit, callbackCalls:calls, directory};
  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(directory, 'report.json'), JSON.stringify(report,null,2)+'\n');
  console.log('STANDALONE_NODE26_PASS ' + JSON.stringify(report));
}
main().catch(error => { console.error(error); process.exitCode = 1; });

```

`runtime/node-ios/tests/wasm-http-stress.cjs`:

```cjs
// Regression for iOS process-wide W^X: wasm code publication must not race
// main-thread JIT execution, even when Node owns a V8 platform worker pool.
const assert = require('node:assert/strict');
const http = require('node:http');

function uleb(value) {
  const bytes = [];
  do { bytes.push((value & 127) | (value >= 128 ? 128 : 0)); value >>>= 7; } while (value);
  return bytes;
}
function section(id, bytes) { return [id, ...uleb(bytes.length), ...bytes]; }
function wasmFunctions(count) {
  const types = [1, 0x60, 0, 1, 0x7f];
  const funcs = [...uleb(count), ...Array(count).fill(0)];
  const exports = [1, 3, 0x66, 0x6f, 0x6f, 0, 0];
  const code = [...uleb(count), ...Array.from({ length: count }, () => [4, 0, 0x41, 42, 0x0b]).flat()];
  return Uint8Array.from([0, 97, 115, 109, 1, 0, 0, 0,
    ...section(1, types), ...section(3, funcs), ...section(7, exports), ...section(10, code)]);
}
function hotLoop(n) { let sum = 0; for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) sum += i; return sum; }

(async () => {
  let requests = 0;
  const server = http.createServer((_req, res) => { requests++; res.end('ok'); });
  await new Promise(resolve => server.listen(0, '127.0.0.1', resolve));
  const url = `http://127.0.0.1:${server.address().port}`;
  try {
    for (let batch = 0; batch < 24; batch++) {
      const responses = Array.from({ length: 8 }, async () => {
        const response = await fetch(url);
        assert.equal(response.status, 200);
        assert.equal(await response.text(), 'ok');
      });
      // Vary the module to avoid merely exercising the wasm native-module cache.
      const instance = new WebAssembly.Instance(new WebAssembly.Module(wasmFunctions(256 + batch)));
      assert.equal(instance.exports.foo(), 42);
      assert.equal(hotLoop(100000), 4999950000);
      await Promise.all(responses);
    }
    assert.equal(requests, 192);
    console.log(`WASM_HTTP_STRESS_OK requests=${requests} modules=24`);
  } finally {
    server.closeAllConnections();
    await new Promise(resolve => server.close(resolve));
  }
})().catch(error => { console.error(error); process.exitCode = 1; });

```

`runtime/node-pty-ios/build.sh`:

```sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Cross-compile node-pty's native binding (pty.node) for iPhoneOS arm64.
#
# This is not "the terminal feature": `dsh-subprocess-local` depends on node-pty,
# so the whole tool stack sits on it —
#   pty.node missing -> subprocess service missing -> ptcRuntime never starts
#   -> workflow-ptc never starts -> the standard/PTC agent presets cannot load.
#
# node-pty is MIT, single translation unit (src/unix/pty.cc) plus node-addon-api
# headers. iOS is Darwin and has forkpty(2), so the same source builds here.
#
# Usage: build.sh --artifact <dir>
set -euo pipefail

HERE="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
ROOT="$(cd "$HERE/../.." && pwd)"
NODE_PTY_VERSION="${NODE_PTY_VERSION:-1.2.0-beta.15}"
NODE_ADDON_API_VERSION="${NODE_ADDON_API_VERSION:-7.1.1}"
NODE_VERSION="26.13.0"
NAPI_VERSION="8"
IOS_TARGET="${IOS_TARGET:-15.0}"

ARTIFACT=""
if [ "${1:-}" = "--artifact" ]; then ARTIFACT="${2:?artifact dir required}"; fi
OUT="${OUT_DIR:-$ROOT/.work/node-pty-ios}"

log() { printf '\033[36;1m==> %s\033[0m\n' "$*"; }

fetch() {
  log "node-pty $NODE_PTY_VERSION source"
  rm -rf "$OUT/src"; mkdir -p "$OUT"
  curl -fsSL "https://registry.npmjs.org/node-pty/-/node-pty-$NODE_PTY_VERSION.tgz" | tar xz -C "$OUT"
  mv "$OUT/package" "$OUT/src"

  log "node-addon-api $NODE_ADDON_API_VERSION headers"
  if [ ! -d "$OUT/node-addon-api" ]; then
    curl -fsSL "https://registry.npmjs.org/node-addon-api/-/node-addon-api-$NODE_ADDON_API_VERSION.tgz" | tar xz -C "$OUT"
    mv "$OUT/package" "$OUT/node-addon-api"
  fi

  log "node v$NODE_VERSION headers"
  if [ ! -d "$OUT/node-headers/include/node" ]; then
    curl -fsSL "https://nodejs.org/dist/v$NODE_VERSION/node-v$NODE_VERSION-headers.tar.gz" | tar xz -C "$OUT"
    mv "$OUT/node-v$NODE_VERSION" "$OUT/node-headers"
  fi
}

compile() {
  log "compiling pty.node for iPhoneOS arm64"
  mkdir -p "$OUT/prebuilds/darwin-arm64"
  local SDK CLANGXX
  SDK="$(xcrun --sdk iphoneos --show-sdk-path)"
  CLANGXX="$(xcrun --sdk iphoneos -f clang++)"
  "$CLANGXX" \
    -target "arm64-apple-ios$IOS_TARGET" \
    -isysroot "$SDK" \
    -std=c++17 -O2 -fexceptions -fvisibility=hidden \
    -bundle -undefined dynamic_lookup \
    -DNAPI_VERSION="$NAPI_VERSION" \
    -DNAPI_CPP_EXCEPTIONS \
    -I"$HERE/compat" \
    -I"$OUT/node-headers/include/node" \
    -I"$OUT/node-addon-api" \
    "$OUT/src/src/unix/pty.cc" \
    -o "$OUT/prebuilds/darwin-arm64/pty.node"
  log "built: $(file "$OUT/prebuilds/darwin-arm64/pty.node")"
}

host_check() {
  [ "${SKIP_HOST_CHECK:-0}" = "1" ] && return 0
  log "host self-check (same source must build for macOS)"
  mkdir -p "$OUT/host"
  "$(xcrun --sdk macosx -f clang++)" -std=c++17 -O1 -fexceptions -fvisibility=hidden \
    -isysroot "$(xcrun --sdk macosx --show-sdk-path)" \
    -bundle -undefined dynamic_lookup -DNAPI_VERSION="$NAPI_VERSION" \
    -DNAPI_CPP_EXCEPTIONS \
    -I"$OUT/node-headers/include/node" -I"$OUT/node-addon-api" \
    "$OUT/src/src/unix/pty.cc" -o "$OUT/host/pty.node"
  node -e '
    const target = { exports: {} };
    process.dlopen(target, process.argv[1]);
    const api = target.exports || {};
    const keys = Object.keys(api);
    console.log("host self-check: loaded, exports = " + keys.join(","));
    if (keys.length === 0) { console.error("binding exported nothing"); process.exit(1); }
  ' "$OUT/host/pty.node"
}

main() {
  fetch
  compile
  host_check
  if [ -n "$ARTIFACT" ]; then
    rm -rf "$ARTIFACT"; mkdir -p "$ARTIFACT/prebuilds/darwin-arm64"
    cp "$OUT/prebuilds/darwin-arm64/pty.node" "$ARTIFACT/prebuilds/darwin-arm64/pty.node"
    log "artifact: $ARTIFACT"
    find "$ARTIFACT" -type f -exec ls -l {} \;
  fi
}

main

```

```h
 * is placed first on the include path for the iOS build only. */
```

`runtime/require-builtin-ios/build.sh`:

```sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Cross-compile the official DeepSeek native addon (`node-addon-require-builtin`)
# for iPhoneOS arm64 and install it into the shipped runtime tree.
#
# The harness refuses to boot without this addon:
#   dsh: host preparation failed: No usable native binding found for
#   node-addon-require-builtin-<platform> (auto)
# npm only publishes darwin/linux/win32 prebuilds, so iOS must be built here from
# the pinned official source. Nothing is reimplemented.
#
# Usage: build.sh <dsh-tree-root>        e.g. deepseek/out/runtime/dsh
#        build.sh --artifact <dir>       only produce the platform package
set -euo pipefail

HERE="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
ROOT="$(cd "$HERE/../.." && pwd)"
# shellcheck disable=SC1091
source "$HERE/pin.env"

MODE="install"
TREE=""
if [ "${1:-}" = "--artifact" ]; then MODE="artifact"; OUTDIR="${2:?artifact dir required}"; else TREE="${1:?tree root required}"; fi

WORK="${WORK:-$ROOT/.work/require-builtin-ios}"
SRC="$WORK/src"
PKG="$WORK/node-addon-require-builtin-ios-arm64"
BIN="$WORK/require_builtin.node"

log() { printf '\033[36;1m==> %s\033[0m\n' "$*"; }

SDK="$(xcrun --sdk iphoneos --show-sdk-path)"
CLANGXX="$(xcrun --sdk iphoneos -f clang++)"

fetch_sources() {
  log "official addon source $ADDON_REPO @ $ADDON_COMMIT"
  rm -rf "$SRC"; mkdir -p "$WORK"
  git init -q "$SRC"
  git -C "$SRC" remote add origin "$ADDON_REPO"
  git -C "$SRC" fetch -q --depth 1 origin "$ADDON_COMMIT"
  git -C "$SRC" checkout -q FETCH_HEAD
  local got; got="$(git -C "$SRC" rev-parse HEAD)"
  [ "$got" = "$ADDON_COMMIT" ] || { echo "addon commit mismatch: $got"; exit 1; }

  log "node v$NODE_VERSION headers"
  if [ ! -d "$WORK/node-headers/include/node" ]; then
    curl -fsSL "https://nodejs.org/dist/v$NODE_VERSION/node-v$NODE_VERSION-headers.tar.gz" \
      | tar xz -C "$WORK"
    mv "$WORK/node-v$NODE_VERSION" "$WORK/node-headers"
  fi

  log "node-addon-api $NODE_ADDON_API"
  if [ ! -d "$WORK/node-addon-api" ]; then
    curl -fsSL "https://registry.npmjs.org/node-addon-api/-/node-addon-api-$NODE_ADDON_API.tgz" \
      | tar xz -C "$WORK"
    mv "$WORK/package" "$WORK/node-addon-api"
  fi
}

compile() {
  log "compiling require_builtin.node for iPhoneOS arm64 (N-API backend)"
  local N="$SRC/packages/native"
  # napi backend sources from binding.gyp (narb_backend=napi, narb_product=internal-loader)
  local SOURCES=(
    "$N/src/node_api_addon.cc"
    "$N/src/debug_trace.cc"
    "$N/src/native_types.cc"
    "$N/src/runtime_symbol.cc"
    "$N/src/runtime_context/helper.cc"
    "$N/src/runtime_context/platform.cc"
    "$N/src/runtime_context/darwin_arm64.cc"
    "$N/src/runtime_context/darwin_x64.cc"
    "$N/src/runtime_context/linux_arm64.cc"
    "$N/src/runtime_context/linux_x64.cc"
    "$N/src/runtime_context/win32_arm64.cc"
    "$N/src/runtime_context/win32_x64.cc"
    "$N/src/runtime_context/win32_ia32.cc"
    "$N/src/runtime_context/embedder_data.cc"
    "$N/src/runtime_context/runtime_profile.cc"
    "$N/src/runtime_context/runtime_profile_napi.cc"
    "$N/src/runtime_compat_napi.cc"
    "$N/src/runtime_probe/helper.cc"
    "$N/src/runtime_probe/platform.cc"
    "$N/src/runtime_probe/getter_decoder.cc"
    "$N/src/runtime_probe/posix.cc"
    "$N/src/runtime_probe/win32_common.cc"
    "$N/src/runtime_probe/darwin_arm64.cc"
    "$N/src/runtime_probe/darwin_x64.cc"
    "$N/src/runtime_probe/linux_arm64.cc"
    "$N/src/runtime_probe/linux_x64.cc"
    "$N/src/runtime_probe/win32_arm64.cc"
    "$N/src/runtime_probe/win32_x64.cc"
    "$N/src/runtime_probe/win32_ia32.cc"
    "$N/src/require_builtin_probe.cc"
  )
  set -- "${SOURCES[@]}"
  "$CLANGXX" \
    -target "arm64-apple-ios${IOS_DEPLOYMENT_TARGET}" \
    -isysroot "$SDK" \
    -std=c++17 -O2 -fno-exceptions -fvisibility=hidden \
    -bundle -undefined dynamic_lookup \
    -DNAPI_VERSION=9 \
    -DNODE_ADDON_API_DISABLE_CPP_EXCEPTIONS \
    -DNARB_BACKEND=1 \
    -DNARB_PRODUCT=1 \
    -I"$WORK/node-headers/include/node" \
    -I"$WORK/node-addon-api" \
    -Wall -Wextra -Wno-unused-parameter -Wno-cast-function-type-mismatch \
    "$@" \
    -o "$BIN" \
    -lc++
  log "built: $(file "$BIN")"
}

package_platform() {
  log "assembling node-addon-require-builtin-ios-arm64"
  rm -rf "$PKG"; mkdir -p "$PKG/prebuilt"
  cp "$BIN" "$PKG/prebuilt/ios-arm64-napi-v9.node"

  cat > "$PKG/package.json" <<JSON
{
  "name": "node-addon-require-builtin-ios-arm64",
  "version": "$ADDON_VERSION",
  "description": "Prebuilt ios-arm64 native addon package for node-addon-require-builtin (built from official source for dsh-ios).",
  "main": "./index.js",
  "license": "MIT",
  "dependencies": {
    "node-addon-native-custom-loader": "0.1.9"
  }
}
JSON
  cat > "$PKG/index.js" <<'JS'
'use strict';

const { loadPrebuild } = require('node-addon-native-custom-loader');

module.exports = loadPrebuild(__dirname);
JS
  cat > "$PKG/prebuilds.json" <<JSON
{
  "platform": "ios-arm64",
  "binaries": [
    {
      "backend": "napi",
      "abi": "napi-v9",
      "binaryTag": "napi-v9",
      "node": ">=20",
      "path": "prebuilt/ios-arm64-napi-v9.node"
    }
  ]
}
JSON
  ls -l "$PKG" "$PKG/prebuilt"
}

sign() {
  local f="$1"
  if command -v ldid >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    ldid -S"$ROOT/apple/entitlements.plist" "$f" 2>/dev/null || ldid -S "$f"
  fi
}

install_into_tree() {
  local tree="$1"
  log "installing addon into $tree"
  # 1) optional package resolvable from the loader (top level + pnpm sibling)
  local loader_root
  loader_root="$(find "$tree/node_modules" -maxdepth 4 -type d -name 'node-addon-require-builtin' -path '*/node_modules/*' | head -1)"
  rm -rf "$tree/node_modules/node-addon-require-builtin-ios-arm64"
  cp -a "$PKG" "$tree/node_modules/node-addon-require-builtin-ios-arm64"
  if [ -n "${loader_root:-}" ]; then
    local parent; parent="$(dirname "$loader_root")"
    rm -rf "$parent/node-addon-require-builtin-ios-arm64"
    cp -a "$PKG" "$parent/node-addon-require-builtin-ios-arm64"
    # 2) local-build fallback paths
    mkdir -p "$loader_root/build/napi/napi-v9-ios-arm64"
    cp "$BIN" "$loader_root/build/napi/napi-v9-ios-arm64/require_builtin.node"
  else
    echo "warning: node-addon-require-builtin not found in tree; only the top-level package was installed" >&2
  fi
  # 3) hoisted fallback dir used by pnpm resolution
  if [ -d "$tree/node_modules/.pnpm/node_modules" ]; then
    rm -rf "$tree/node_modules/.pnpm/node_modules/node-addon-require-builtin-ios-arm64"
    cp -a "$PKG" "$tree/node_modules/.pnpm/node_modules/node-addon-require-builtin-ios-arm64"
  fi

  # every copy of the binary must be signed for a jailbroken device
  while IFS= read -r f; do sign "$f"; done < <(find "$tree" -name 'require_builtin.node' -newer "$WORK" 2>/dev/null; find "$tree" -name 'ios-arm64-napi-v9.node')
  log "installed:"; find "$tree" -name '*ios-arm64*' -o -name 'require_builtin.node' | sed "s|$tree|<tree>|"
}


# Builds the same sources for the build host and loads them with the host node.
# The harness rejects a binding whose advertised product/backend/abi differ, so
# this catches a wrong -DNARB_* before an ipa ever ships.
host_check() {
  [ "${SKIP_HOST_CHECK:-0}" = "1" ] && { log "host check skipped"; return 0; }
  log "host self-check of the binding contract"
  local N="$SRC/packages/native" hostnode="$WORK/require_builtin_host.node"
  "$(xcrun --sdk macosx -f clang++)" -std=c++17 -O1 -fno-exceptions -fvisibility=hidden \
    -isysroot "$(xcrun --sdk macosx --show-sdk-path)" \
    -bundle -undefined dynamic_lookup \
    -DNAPI_VERSION=9 -DNODE_ADDON_API_DISABLE_CPP_EXCEPTIONS -DNARB_BACKEND=1 -DNARB_PRODUCT=1 \
    -I"$WORK/node-headers/include/node" -I"$WORK/node-addon-api" \
    "$N/src/node_api_addon.cc" "$N/src/debug_trace.cc" "$N/src/native_types.cc" "$N/src/runtime_symbol.cc" \
    "$N/src/runtime_context/helper.cc" "$N/src/runtime_context/platform.cc" \
    "$N/src/runtime_context/darwin_arm64.cc" "$N/src/runtime_context/darwin_x64.cc" \
    "$N/src/runtime_context/linux_arm64.cc" "$N/src/runtime_context/linux_x64.cc" \
    "$N/src/runtime_context/win32_arm64.cc" "$N/src/runtime_context/win32_x64.cc" "$N/src/runtime_context/win32_ia32.cc" \
    "$N/src/runtime_context/embedder_data.cc" "$N/src/runtime_context/runtime_profile.cc" \
    "$N/src/runtime_context/runtime_profile_napi.cc" "$N/src/runtime_compat_napi.cc" \
    "$N/src/runtime_probe/helper.cc" "$N/src/runtime_probe/platform.cc" \
    "$N/src/runtime_probe/getter_decoder.cc" "$N/src/runtime_probe/posix.cc" \
    "$N/src/runtime_probe/win32_common.cc" "$N/src/runtime_probe/darwin_arm64.cc" \
    "$N/src/runtime_probe/darwin_x64.cc" "$N/src/runtime_probe/linux_arm64.cc" \
    "$N/src/runtime_probe/linux_x64.cc" "$N/src/runtime_probe/win32_arm64.cc" \
    "$N/src/runtime_probe/win32_x64.cc" "$N/src/runtime_probe/win32_ia32.cc" \
    "$N/src/require_builtin_probe.cc" \
    -o "$hostnode" -lc++
  node -e '
    const b = require(process.argv[1]);
    const info = b.getNativeBindingInfo();   // the actual contract
    console.log("nativeBindingInfo:", JSON.stringify(info));
    for (const fn of ["requireBuiltin", "isAllowedInternalId", "getNativeBindingInfo"]) {
      if (typeof b[fn] !== "function") { console.error("missing export:", fn); process.exit(1); }
    }
    if (String(info.product) !== "require-builtin") {
      console.error("wrong product:", info.product, "(need require-builtin)");
      process.exit(1);
    }
    if (info.backend !== "napi" || info.abi !== "napi-v9") {
      console.error("wrong backend/abi:", info.backend, info.abi);
      process.exit(1);
    }
  ' "$hostnode"
  log "host self-check OK"
}

main() {
  fetch_sources
  compile
  host_check
  package_platform
  if [ "$MODE" = "artifact" ]; then
    rm -rf "$OUTDIR"; mkdir -p "$OUTDIR"
    cp -a "$PKG" "$OUTDIR/"; cp "$BIN" "$OUTDIR/require_builtin.node"
    log "artifact at $OUTDIR"
  else
    install_into_tree "$TREE"
  fi
}

main
```

`runtime/ripgrep-ios/build.sh`:

```sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Cross-compile ripgrep for iPhoneOS arm64.
#
# `dsh-tool-fs-search` (glob/grep) spawns the binary shipped by
# `@vscode/ripgrep`, which has no iOS build — so both search tools fail with
# "ripgrep launch failed". ripgrep is pure Rust, so a normal cross build works.
#
# Usage: build.sh --artifact <dir>
set -euo pipefail

HERE="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
ROOT="$(cd "$HERE/../.." && pwd)"
RIPGREP_VERSION="${RIPGREP_VERSION:-14.1.1}"
TARGET="aarch64-apple-ios"

ARTIFACT=""
if [ "${1:-}" = "--artifact" ]; then ARTIFACT="${2:?artifact dir required}"; fi
OUT="${OUT_DIR:-$ROOT/.work/ripgrep-ios}"

log() { printf '\033[36;1m==> %s\033[0m\n' "$*"; }

fetch() {
  mkdir -p "$OUT"
  if [ ! -d "$OUT/src" ]; then
    log "ripgrep $RIPGREP_VERSION source"
    curl -fsSL "https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/archive/refs/tags/$RIPGREP_VERSION.tar.gz" \
      | tar xz -C "$OUT"
    mv "$OUT/ripgrep-$RIPGREP_VERSION" "$OUT/src"
  fi
}

build() {
  log "rust target $TARGET"
  rustup target add "$TARGET" >/dev/null
  export IPHONEOS_DEPLOYMENT_TARGET="${IOS_TARGET:-15.0}"
  log "cargo build --release --target $TARGET"
  ( cd "$OUT/src" && cargo build --release --target "$TARGET" --features pcre2 2>&1 | tail -20 ) || \
    ( cd "$OUT/src" && cargo build --release --target "$TARGET" 2>&1 | tail -20 )

  local bin="$OUT/src/target/$TARGET/release/rg"
  test -x "$bin" || { echo "rg binary not produced"; exit 1; }
  log "built: $(file "$bin")"
  printf '%s\n' "$bin" > "$OUT/rg-path.txt"
}

main() {
  fetch
  build
  if [ -n "$ARTIFACT" ]; then
    rm -rf "$ARTIFACT"; mkdir -p "$ARTIFACT"
    cp "$(cat "$OUT/rg-path.txt")" "$ARTIFACT/rg"
    chmod +x "$ARTIFACT/rg"
    log "artifact: $ARTIFACT"
    ls -l "$ARTIFACT"
    vtool -show-build "$ARTIFACT/rg" | head -5 || true
  fi
}

main

```

`runtime/runner-ios/build.sh`:

```sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Build the sandbox pass-through runner for iPhoneOS arm64.
#
# A script cannot do this job: the device has no /bin/sh, so a shebang fails
# before exec — which is exactly the "every spawn SIGSEGVs" failure.
set -euo pipefail

HERE="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
IOS_TARGET="${IOS_TARGET:-15.0}"
ARTIFACT=""
if [ "${1:-}" = "--artifact" ]; then ARTIFACT="$(pwd)/${2:?artifact dir required}"; fi

SDK="$(xcrun --sdk iphoneos --show-sdk-path)"
CLANG="$(xcrun --sdk iphoneos -f clang)"
OUT="$(mktemp -d)"

echo "==> compiling runner for iPhoneOS arm64"
"$CLANG" -target "arm64-apple-ios$IOS_TARGET" -isysroot "$SDK" \
    -O2 -Wall -Wextra -o "$OUT/runner" "$HERE/runner.c"
file "$OUT/runner"

if [ -n "$ARTIFACT" ]; then
    rm -rf "$ARTIFACT"; mkdir -p "$ARTIFACT"
    cp "$OUT/runner" "$ARTIFACT/runner"
    chmod +x "$ARTIFACT/runner"
    echo "==> artifact: $ARTIFACT"
    ls -l "$ARTIFACT"
    vtool -show-build "$ARTIFACT/runner" | head -4 || true
fi
rm -rf "$OUT"

```

`runtime/runner-ios/runner.c`:

```c
/*
 * DeepSeek for iOS — sandbox pass-through runner.
 *
 * `dsh-sandbox-local` wraps every child process with its configured
 * `runnerCommand`: [runner, ...profileArgs, "--", ...command]. iOS has no
 * bwrap/Landlock/Seatbelt, so this binary just discards the profile arguments
 * and execs the command unchanged.
 *
 * It must be a real executable, not a shell script: the device has no /bin/sh,
 * so a script's shebang fails before the command ever runs (which surfaced as
 * a spawn SIGSEGV for every tool).
 */
#include <stdio.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <unistd.h>

extern char **environ;

int main(int argc, char **argv) {
    int i = 1;
    while (i < argc && strcmp(argv[i], "--") != 0) {
        i++;
    }
    if (i + 1 >= argc) {
        fputs("deepseek-ios-runner: fatal: no command after --\n", stderr);
        return 125;
    }
    execv(argv[i + 1], &argv[i + 1]);
    perror("deepseek-ios-runner: fatal: exec");
    return 125;
}

```

`runtime/shell-launcher/build.sh`:

```sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Build the Mach-O launcher that execs node with the in-process shell script.
set -euo pipefail
HERE="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
ARTIFACT=""
if [ "${1:-}" = "--artifact" ]; then ARTIFACT="$(pwd)/${2:?artifact dir required}"; fi
SDK="$(xcrun --sdk iphoneos --show-sdk-path)"
CC="$(xcrun --sdk iphoneos -f clang)"
mkdir -p "$HERE/out"
"$CC" -target arm64-apple-ios15.0 -isysroot "$SDK" -O2 -Wall -Wextra \
  "$HERE/main.c" -o "$HERE/out/sh-launch"
echo "==> built: $(file "$HERE/out/sh-launch")"
if [ -n "$ARTIFACT" ]; then
  rm -rf "$ARTIFACT"; mkdir -p "$ARTIFACT"
  cp "$HERE/out/sh-launch" "$ARTIFACT/sh-launch"
  chmod +x "$ARTIFACT/sh-launch"
  vtool -show-build "$ARTIFACT/sh-launch" | head -4
fi

```

`runtime/shell-launcher/main.c`:

```c
/*
 * dsh-shell launcher.
 *
 * iOS refuses to exec a script file ("Operation not permitted") but runs a
 * signed Mach-O from the app bundle happily — which is how the harness itself
 * starts node. So this tiny launcher is a Mach-O: it execs the bundled node with
 * the in-process shell script (both paths arrive through the environment the app
 * sets), forwarding the harness' arguments (`-c "<command>"`).
 *
 * execv of a bundle binary is allowed, so no fork and no shell rewriting is
 * needed: the actual shell logic stays in JavaScript.
 */
#include <errno.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <unistd.h>

int main(int argc, char **argv) {
    const char *node = getenv("DSH_SHELL_NODE");
    const char *script = getenv("DSH_SHELL_JS");

    if (node == NULL || node[0] == '\0' || script == NULL || script[0] == '\0') {
        fprintf(stderr, "dsh-shell: DSH_SHELL_NODE / DSH_SHELL_JS are not set\n");
        return 127;
    }
    if (access(node, X_OK) != 0) {
        fprintf(stderr, "dsh-shell: cannot run %s: %s\n", node, strerror(errno));
        return 126;
    }
    if (access(script, R_OK) != 0) {
        fprintf(stderr, "dsh-shell: cannot read %s: %s\n", script, strerror(errno));
        return 126;
    }

    char **args = calloc((size_t)argc + 3, sizeof(char *));
    if (args == NULL) {
        fprintf(stderr, "dsh-shell: out of memory\n");
        return 127;
    }

    int next = 0;
    args[next++] = (char *)node;
    args[next++] = (char *)script;
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; i++) {
        args[next++] = argv[i];
    }
    args[next] = NULL;

    execv(node, args);
    fprintf(stderr, "dsh-shell: execv(%s) failed: %s\n", node, strerror(errno));
    return 126;
}

```

`runtime/system-flock-ios/build.sh`:

```sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Cross-compile the official POSIX flock binding for iPhoneOS arm64.
#
# `@deepseek-ai/node-addon-system/flock` refuses any platform other than
# linux/darwin ("flock is not supported on ios-arm64"), and the JSONL session
# store needs it on every write. iOS has the POSIX flock(2) syscall, and the
# binding is a single C file, so we compile the official source for iOS and ship
# it under the darwin platform package name — together with a tiny CommonJS
# preload that reports `process.platform === 'darwin'`.
#
# Usage: build.sh --artifact <dir>
set -euo pipefail

HERE="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
ROOT="$(cd "$HERE/../.." && pwd)"
HOOK_COMMIT="$(sed -n 's/^UPSTREAM_COMMIT=//p' "$ROOT/deepseek/upstream.env" | tr -d '" ')"
NODE_VERSION="26.13.0"
NAPI_VERSION="8"
IOS_TARGET="${IOS_TARGET:-15.0}"

ARTIFACT=""
if [ "${1:-}" = "--artifact" ]; then ARTIFACT="${2:?artifact dir required}"; fi
OUT="${OUT_DIR:-$ROOT/.work/system-flock-ios}"
SRC="$OUT/src"
PKG="$OUT/node-addon-system-darwin-arm64"
BIN="$PKG/bin/system.node"

log() { printf '\033[36;1m==> %s\033[0m\n' "$*"; }

fetch() {
  log "official harness source @ $HOOK_COMMIT (native/system)"
  rm -rf "$SRC"; mkdir -p "$OUT"
  git init -q "$SRC"
  git -C "$SRC" remote add origin https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness.git
  git -C "$SRC" fetch -q --depth 1 origin "$HOOK_COMMIT"
  git -C "$SRC" checkout -q FETCH_HEAD
  local got; got="$(git -C "$SRC" rev-parse HEAD)"
  [ "$got" = "$HOOK_COMMIT" ] || { echo "commit mismatch: $got"; exit 1; }

  log "node v$NODE_VERSION headers (node_api.h)"
  if [ ! -d "$OUT/node-headers/include/node" ]; then
    curl -fsSL "https://nodejs.org/dist/v$NODE_VERSION/node-v$NODE_VERSION-headers.tar.gz" | tar xz -C "$OUT"
    mv "$OUT/node-v$NODE_VERSION" "$OUT/node-headers"
  fi
}

compile() {
  log "compiling system.node for iPhoneOS arm64 (N-API $NAPI_VERSION)"
  mkdir -p "$(dirname "$BIN")"
  local SDK CLANG
  SDK="$(xcrun --sdk iphoneos --show-sdk-path)"
  CLANG="$(xcrun --sdk iphoneos -f clang)"
  "$CLANG" \
    -target "arm64-apple-ios$IOS_TARGET" \
    -isysroot "$SDK" \
    -std=c11 -O2 -fno-exceptions -fvisibility=hidden \
    -bundle -undefined dynamic_lookup \
    -DNAPI_VERSION="$NAPI_VERSION" \
    -I"$OUT/node-headers/include/node" \
    "$SRC/native/system/packages/entry/src/flock.c" \
    -o "$BIN"
  log "built: $(file "$BIN")"
}

package() {
  log "assembling node-addon-system-darwin-arm64"
  cat > "$PKG/package.json" <<JSON
{
  "name": "@deepseek-ai/node-addon-system-darwin-arm64",
  "version": "0.1.2",
  "description": "Prebuilt POSIX flock Node-API binding (built from official source for iPhoneOS arm64 by dsh-ios).",
  "os": ["darwin"],
  "cpu": ["arm64"],
  "engines": { "node": ">=20" },
  "files": ["bin/", "prebuilds.json", "README.md"],
  "license": "BSD-3-Clause"
}
JSON
  cat > "$PKG/prebuilds.json" <<'JSON'
{
  "platform": "darwin-arm64",
  "binaries": [
    { "tool": "flock", "kind": "node-api", "napi": 8, "path": "bin/system.node" }
  ]
}
JSON
  cat > "$PKG/README.md" <<'MD'
Prebuilt POSIX flock Node-API binding.

Built from `native/system/packages/entry/src/flock.c` of the official
`deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness` tree, cross-compiled for iPhoneOS arm64 by the
dsh-ios project (Apple ships no Node.js, and the harness' flock entry only
accepts linux/darwin).
MD
  ls -l "$PKG" "$PKG/bin"
}

host_check() {
  [ "${SKIP_HOST_CHECK:-0}" = "1" ] && return 0
  log "host self-check: the same source must build and load on macOS"
  local hostnode="$OUT/system.host.node"
  "$(xcrun --sdk macosx -f clang)" -std=c11 -O1 -fno-exceptions -fvisibility=hidden \
    -isysroot "$(xcrun --sdk macosx --show-sdk-path)" \
    -bundle -undefined dynamic_lookup -DNAPI_VERSION="$NAPI_VERSION" \
    -I"$OUT/node-headers/include/node" \
    "$SRC/native/system/packages/entry/src/flock.c" -o "$hostnode"
  node -e '
    const target = { exports: {} };
    process.dlopen(target, process.argv[1]);
    if (typeof target.exports.tryLock !== "function") {
      console.error("missing tryLock");
      process.exit(1);
    }
    console.log("host self-check OK: tryLock exported");
  ' "$hostnode"
}

main() {
  fetch
  compile
  package
  host_check
  if [ -n "$ARTIFACT" ]; then
    rm -rf "$ARTIFACT"; mkdir -p "$ARTIFACT"
    cp -a "$PKG" "$ARTIFACT/"
    cp "$BIN" "$ARTIFACT/system.node"
    log "artifact: $ARTIFACT"
    ls -l "$ARTIFACT"
  fi
}

main

```

`tools/install-native-compat.sh`:

```sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Install the two iOS-built native modules under their **darwin** platform names.
#
# The harness' native layer only accepts linux/darwin; the app reports darwin
# (see apple/Sources/RuntimeShims.swift) and these packages are what it then
# resolves. Everything here is compiled from official DeepSeek source for
# iPhoneOS arm64 by the other workflows — nothing is reimplemented.
#
# Usage: tools/install-native-compat.sh <app-dir> <require-builtin.node> <system-addon-pkg-dir>
set -euo pipefail

APP="${1:?app dir}"
RB_BIN="${2:?require-builtin ios binary}"
SYS_PKG="${3:?node-addon-system-darwin-arm64 package dir}"
PTY_BIN="${4:-}"
RG_BIN="${5:-}"

TREE="$APP/Runtime/dsh"
NM="$TREE/node_modules"
log() { printf '\033[36;1m==> %s\033[0m\n' "$*"; }

log "node-addon-require-builtin-darwin-arm64"
RB_DST="$NM/node-addon-require-builtin-darwin-arm64"
rm -rf "$RB_DST"
mkdir -p "$RB_DST/prebuilt"
cp "$RB_BIN" "$RB_DST/prebuilt/darwin-arm64-napi-v9.node"
cat > "$RB_DST/package.json" <<'JSON'
{
  "name": "node-addon-require-builtin-darwin-arm64",
  "version": "0.1.9",
  "description": "Prebuilt darwin-arm64 native addon for node-addon-require-builtin (built for iPhoneOS arm64 by dsh-ios).",
  "main": "./index.js",
  "license": "MIT",
  "dependencies": { "node-addon-native-custom-loader": "0.1.9" }
}
JSON
cat > "$RB_DST/index.js" <<'JS'
'use strict';

const { loadPrebuild } = require('node-addon-native-custom-loader');

module.exports = loadPrebuild(__dirname);
JS
cat > "$RB_DST/prebuilds.json" <<'JSON'
{
  "platform": "darwin-arm64",
  "binaries": [
    { "backend": "napi", "abi": "napi-v9", "binaryTag": "napi-v9", "node": ">=20", "path": "prebuilt/darwin-arm64-napi-v9.node" }
  ]
}
JSON

# Make it resolvable from the loader (pnpm hoisted dir) and beside the entry.
if [ -d "$NM/.pnpm/node_modules" ]; then
  rm -rf "$NM/.pnpm/node_modules/node-addon-require-builtin-darwin-arm64"
  cp -a "$RB_DST" "$NM/.pnpm/node_modules/node-addon-require-builtin-darwin-arm64"
fi
while IFS= read -r entry; do
  [ -n "$entry" ] || continue
  parent="$(dirname "$entry")"
  rm -rf "$parent/node-addon-require-builtin-darwin-arm64"
  cp -a "$RB_DST" "$parent/node-addon-require-builtin-darwin-arm64"
  mkdir -p "$entry/build/napi/napi-v9-darwin-arm64"
  cp "$RB_BIN" "$entry/build/napi/napi-v9-darwin-arm64/require_builtin.node"
done < <(find "$NM" -type d -name 'node-addon-require-builtin' -path '*/node_modules/*')

log "@deepseek-ai/node-addon-system-darwin-arm64"
mkdir -p "$NM/@deepseek-ai"
rm -rf "$NM/@deepseek-ai/node-addon-system-darwin-arm64"
cp -a "$SYS_PKG" "$NM/@deepseek-ai/node-addon-system-darwin-arm64"
if [ -d "$NM/.pnpm/node_modules/@deepseek-ai" ]; then
  rm -rf "$NM/.pnpm/node_modules/@deepseek-ai/node-addon-system-darwin-arm64"
  cp -a "$SYS_PKG" "$NM/.pnpm/node_modules/@deepseek-ai/node-addon-system-darwin-arm64"
fi
while IFS= read -r entry; do
  [ -n "$entry" ] || continue
  parent="$(dirname "$entry")"
  rm -rf "$parent/node-addon-system-darwin-arm64"
  cp -a "$SYS_PKG" "$parent/node-addon-system-darwin-arm64"
done < <(find "$NM" -type d -name 'node-addon-system' -path '*/@deepseek-ai/*')

if [ -n "$PTY_BIN" ] && [ -f "$PTY_BIN" ]; then
  log "node-pty prebuild (darwin-arm64, built for iOS)"
  installed=0
  while IFS= read -r pkg; do
    [ -n "$pkg" ] || continue
    mkdir -p "$pkg/prebuilds/darwin-arm64"
    cp "$PTY_BIN" "$pkg/prebuilds/darwin-arm64/pty.node"
    installed=$((installed+1))
  done < <(find "$NM" -type d -name 'node-pty' -path '*/node_modules/*')
  log "pty.node installed into $installed node-pty package(s)"
  if [ "$installed" -eq 0 ]; then echo "warning: no node-pty package found"; fi
fi

if [ -n "$RG_BIN" ] && [ -f "$RG_BIN" ]; then
  log "ripgrep for iOS (@vscode/ripgrep expects bin/rg)"
  count=0
  while IFS= read -r pkgdir; do
    [ -n "$pkgdir" ] || continue
    mkdir -p "$pkgdir/bin"
    cp "$RG_BIN" "$pkgdir/bin/rg"
    chmod 755 "$pkgdir/bin/rg"
    count=$((count+1))
  done < <(find "$NM" -type d -name 'ripgrep' -path '*@vscode*' 2>/dev/null)
  log "rg installed into $count @vscode/ripgrep package(s)"
  [ "$count" -eq 0 ] && echo "warning: @vscode/ripgrep not found in the tree"
fi

log "signing every shipped .node"
signed=0; kept=0
while IFS= read -r f; do
  if ldid -S"$APP/../entitlements.plist" "$f" 2>/dev/null; then signed=$((signed+1)); continue; fi
  if ldid -S "$f" 2>/dev/null; then signed=$((signed+1)); continue; fi
  if codesign --force --sign - "$f" 2>/dev/null; then signed=$((signed+1)); continue; fi
  kept=$((kept+1))
done < <(find "$APP/Runtime" \( -name '*.node' -o -name 'rg' -o -name 'sh*' -o -name 'bash-inproc' -o -name 'sh-launch' -o -name 'hello' \) -type f)
log "signed=$signed unsigned-kept=$kept"

log "validation"
for f in "$NM/node-addon-require-builtin-darwin-arm64/prebuilt/darwin-arm64-napi-v9.node" \
         "$NM/@deepseek-ai/node-addon-system-darwin-arm64/bin/system.node"; do
  test -f "$f" || { echo "missing $f"; exit 1; }
  file "$f"
  vtool -show-build "$f" | grep -E "platform|minos"
done
echo "installed darwin-native packages:"
find "$NM" -maxdepth 4 -type d \( -name 'node-addon-require-builtin-darwin-arm64' -o -name 'node-addon-system-darwin-arm64' \) | sed "s|$NM|<tree>/node_modules|"

```

`tools/make-icon.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Generate the app icon (no Pillow needed: raw PNG writer over zlib).

Design: deep indigo→blue vertical gradient, white rounded speech bubble with a
tail, three blue "typing" dots. 1024x1024, single-size iOS app icon.
"""
import struct
import zlib
import math
import os

W = H = 1024
OUT = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "apple", "Resources",
                   "Assets.xcassets", "AppIcon.appiconset", "icon-1024.png")


def lerp(a, b, t):
    return a + (b - a) * t


def rounded_rect_alpha(x, y, x0, y0, x1, y1, r):
    """Signed coverage in [0,1] for a rounded rect (antialiased by 1px)."""
    cx = min(max(x, x0 + r), x1 - r)
    cy = min(max(y, y0 + r), y1 - r)
    d = math.hypot(x - cx, y - cy)
    if x0 <= x <= x1 and y0 <= y <= y1:
        return max(0.0, min(1.0, (r - d) + 0.5)) if d > r else 1.0
    return 0.0


def main():
    px = bytearray()
    # brand gradient
    top = (0x22, 0x2B, 0x6B)
    bottom = (0x4C, 0x6C, 0xFF)
    bubble = (0xF7, 0xF9, 0xFF)
    dot = (0x22, 0x2B, 0x6B)

    # bubble geometry
    bx0, by0, bx1, by1 = 214, 300, 810, 700
    br = 96
    # tail (triangle) below-left of the bubble
    tx, ty = 330, 690          # apex
    tail_pts = [(330, 660), (470, 660), (tx, ty + 78)]

    def in_triangle(x, y, pts):
        def sign(p1, p2, p3):
            return (p1[0] - p3[0]) * (p2[1] - p3[1]) - (p2[0] - p3[0]) * (p1[1] - p3[1])
        d1 = sign((x, y), pts[0], pts[1])
        d2 = sign((x, y), pts[1], pts[2])
        d3 = sign((x, y), pts[2], pts[0])
        neg = (d1 < 0) or (d2 < 0) or (d3 < 0)
        pos = (d1 > 0) or (d2 > 0) or (d3 > 0)
        return not (neg and pos)

    dots = [(400, 500), (512, 500), (624, 500)]

    for y in range(H):
        row = bytearray()
        t = y / (H - 1)
        bg = (lerp(top[0], bottom[0], t), lerp(top[1], bottom[1], t), lerp(top[2], bottom[2], t))
        for x in range(W):
            r, g, b = bg
            # subtle diagonal sheen
            sheen = max(0.0, 1.0 - abs((x * 0.6 + y * 0.4) / 1180.0 - 0.45)) * 0.10
            r, g, b = r + 255 * sheen * 0.3, g + 255 * sheen * 0.3, b + 255 * sheen * 0.35

            a = rounded_rect_alpha(x, y, bx0, by0, bx1, by1, br)
            if a <= 0 and in_triangle(x, y, tail_pts):
                a = 1.0
            if a > 0:
                r = lerp(r, bubble[0], a)
                g = lerp(g, bubble[1], a)
                b = lerp(b, bubble[2], a)
                for cx, cy in dots:
                    d = math.hypot(x - cx, y - cy)
                    da = max(0.0, min(1.0, 34 - d + 0.5))
                    if da > 0:
                        r = lerp(r, dot[0], da)
                        g = lerp(g, dot[1], da)
                        b = lerp(b, dot[2], da)
            row += bytes((int(max(0, min(255, r))), int(max(0, min(255, g))), int(max(0, min(255, b)))))
        px += b"\x00" + row  # filter type 0

    def chunk(tag, data):
        return (struct.pack(">I", len(data)) + tag + data +
                struct.pack(">I", zlib.crc32(tag + data) & 0xFFFFFFFF))

    png = (b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n"
           + chunk(b"IHDR", struct.pack(">IIBBBBB", W, H, 8, 2, 0, 0, 0))
           + chunk(b"IDAT", zlib.compress(bytes(px), 9))
           + chunk(b"IEND", b""))
    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(OUT), exist_ok=True)
    with open(OUT, "wb") as fh:
        fh.write(png)
    print("wrote", OUT, len(png), "bytes")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`tools/make-preset-overlay.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Generate the iOS preset overlay: the `standard` preset minus workflow-ptc.

`workflow-ptc` never starts on this platform (its subtree needs services that do
not exist on iOS), and a preset with one dead plugin is reported as
"加载失败" even though every other tool is fine. The profile patch lets a later
layer override a preset's `config.plugins` by id, so we re-emit the same preset
with that subtree removed. Upstream source is untouched.

Usage: make-preset-overlay.py --out <file> [--file <local yaml> | --commit <sha>]
"""
import argparse
import sys
import urllib.request

import yaml

DROP_IDS = {"workflow-ptc", "tool-workflow"}
PRESET_ID = "preset-standard"
RAW = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/{commit}/packages/bundle/web-app/presets/standard.patch.yml"


class JsTag:
    """`!!js` scalars are opaque to us; keep them verbatim."""

    def __init__(self, text: str) -> None:
        self.text = text


class Loader(yaml.SafeLoader):
    pass


Loader.add_multi_constructor("tag:yaml.org,2002:js", lambda loader, suffix, node: JsTag(node.value))


class Dumper(yaml.SafeDumper):
    pass


Dumper.add_representer(JsTag, lambda dumper, value: dumper.represent_scalar("tag:yaml.org,2002:js", value.text))


def strip_plugins(plugins, removed):
    out = []
    for plugin in plugins:
        if isinstance(plugin, dict) and plugin.get("id") in DROP_IDS:
            removed.append(plugin.get("id"))
            continue
        if isinstance(plugin, dict) and isinstance(plugin.get("config"), list):
            plugin["config"] = strip_plugins(plugin["config"], removed)
        out.append(plugin)
    return out


def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--out", required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--file")
    parser.add_argument("--commit")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    if args.file:
        source = open(args.file, encoding="utf-8").read()
    elif args.commit:
        with urllib.request.urlopen(RAW.format(commit=args.commit), timeout=60) as response:
            source = response.read().decode()
    else:
        print("need --file or --commit", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2

    document = yaml.load(source, Loader=Loader)
    entry = None
    for item in document:
        for candidate in (item.get("insert") or []):
            if candidate.get("id") == PRESET_ID:
                entry = candidate
    if entry is None:
        print(f"{PRESET_ID} not found in the bundle patch", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1

    config = entry["config"]
    removed = []
    config["plugins"] = strip_plugins(config["plugins"], removed)
    if not removed:
        print("warning: nothing removed; the preset layout changed", file=sys.stderr)

    header = (
        "# Generated by dsh-ios: the `standard` preset with its workflow-ptc subtree removed,\n"
        "# because that subtree cannot start on iOS. Overrides config.plugins by id — the\n"
        "# documented behaviour of the profile patch. Upstream source is untouched.\n"
    )
    body = yaml.dump([{"id": PRESET_ID, "config": config}], Dumper=Dumper,
                     sort_keys=False, allow_unicode=True, width=1000, default_flow_style=False)
    with open(args.out, "w", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        handle.write(header + body)
    print(f"wrote {args.out}: removed {', '.join(removed) or 'nothing'}")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

```
